### [75] [Long-Lived Characters, Local Inference: Incremental Memory Maintenance for Game NPCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18935)
*Zimu Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 面向长期游戏NPC，研究在量化Qwen混合循环-注意力模型中的增量记忆维护，以避免每次对话重算整个前缀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角色对话应即时应答，而修改少量记忆会使长可复用前缀失效，准备成本与对话及多角色维护冲突；且错误记忆会污染确定性规则输入。

Method: 运行时删除被取代的注意力KV，在真实序列尾部计算替换记录，并保留持续循环状态与未变KV；用多更新对话回放、固定输入放置消融和注意力诊断实验。

Result: 独立块组合削弱查询条件记忆选择但无统一块首注意力崩塌；真实尾部更新在八轮脚本维护中保留当前状态与历史绑定；某放置案例三次重建恢复全量刷新量，而保留槽位方案重复双减错误；注意力分布接近不能解释语义差异。

Conclusion: 角色推理状态应视为被维护、依赖历史资源，而非仅最新记忆文本的一次性编码。

Abstract: A game character should not have to reread its entire life before every conversation. For locally deployed language-model characters, however, revising a few memories can invalidate a long reusable prefix. The resulting preparation cost competes with both foreground dialogue and the maintenance of other characters. This matters especially when dialogue feeds game-defined actions and value judgments: a fluent but incorrect account of who owns an item, or whether a transfer has already happened, can corrupt the input to otherwise deterministic rules. We study incremental memory maintenance for long-lived game NPCs in a quantized Qwen hybrid recurrent-attention model. Our runtime removes superseded attention KV entries, computes replacement records at the true sequence tail, and preserves the continuing recurrent state and unchanged KV. Existing local experiments combine multi-update dialogue replays, fixed-input placement ablations, and attention diagnostics. Independent block composition weakens query-conditioned memory selection without a uniform chunk-initial attention collapse. True-tail updates preserve important current-state and historical bindings across eight scripted maintenance rounds; a placement case recovers the full-refill quantity in three reconstructions, while slot-preserving alternatives repeat a double-subtraction error. Attention-distribution proximity alone does not explain these semantic differences. The results motivate treating a character's inference state as a maintained, history-dependent resource, rather than only a disposable encoding of its latest memory text.

</details>


### [76] [LangSelect: Cost-Aware Target-Language Routing for LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18959)
*Son Ha Xuan,Phat T. Tran-Truong,Xuan-Bach Le,Nghia Duong-Trung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出LangSelect——一个验证感知的目标语言路由器，在语言灵活的可验证编程任务中，生成前选择目标语言并在失败时回退，从而以较少的生成token维持高通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成通常把目标语言当作固定选择，忽略了在多种语言均可通过同一测试时，不同语言的生成token长度差异巨大，这一选择本身是可量化的成本杠杆。

Method: 提出LangSelect验证感知路由器，在生成前选择目标语言并在首次失败时回退。评估分两种设置：离线replay在已接受语料解中选择；在线使用GPT-5生成，对每次尝试（含失败与回退）计费。构建MultiLang-Bench（3000任务、8语言的验证语料），在450个留出任务上比较Domain启发式与CodeBERT+元数据选择器。

Result: replay显示显著的语言路由空间。在线评测中，train-split Domain启发式基线将harness-proxy token（含wrapper与入口开销）降低50.3%，回退后通过率92.9%；学习的CodeBERT+元数据选择器达到最高回退后通过率93.8%，token增加3.7%。

Conclusion: 输出语言路由能够为可通过单元测试验证的代码生成定义一条实际的成本-正确率前沿，语言选择应被视为可优化的决策而非固定前提。

Abstract: LLM code-generation systems usually choose a target programming language before decoding and treat that choice as fixed. We show that, for language-flexible programming tasks -- tasks where several target languages are acceptable and checkable by the same tests -- this choice is a measurable cost lever: verified implementations of the same task can differ substantially in generated-token length. We introduce LangSelect, a verification-aware router that selects the target language before generation and falls back when the first attempt fails. To separate offline routing opportunity from end-to-end behavior, we evaluate verified-solution replay, which chooses among already accepted corpus solutions, and live GPT-5 generation, which charges every generation attempt, including failures and fallbacks. On MultiLang-Bench, a 3,000-task, 8-language verified corpus, replay shows substantial language-routing headroom. In live evaluation on 450 held-out tasks, a train-split Domain heuristic baseline reduces harness-proxy tokens, which include wrapper and entrypoint overhead, by 50.3\% at 92.9\% pass after fallback, while a learned CodeBERT+metadata selector reaches the highest pass after fallback, 93.8\%, with a 3.7\% token increase. These results show that output-language routing can define a practical cost-correctness frontier for unit-test-verifiable code generation.

</details>


### [77] [When Audit Quality Fails to Predict Downstream Utility: A Counterfactual Study of Synthetic-Data Selectors for Low-Resource African NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18960)
*Son Ha Xuan,Phat T. Tran-Truong,Xuan-Bach Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过低资源非洲语言分类的可控复现实验，发现LLM评判的合成数据质量与其下游效用存在系统性脱节：审计排名与下游Macro-F1排名不一致，即"审计质量"并不保证下游收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质量感知的合成数据选择依赖一个代理假设：LLM评判为"好"的样本也应有助于下游模型学习。作者要在受控的低资源场景中检验该代理假设是否成立。

Method: 在低资源非洲语言分类中做可控复现：4种语言（阿姆哈拉语、豪萨语、斯瓦希里语、约鲁巴语）、2个分类任务（MasakhaNEWS、AfriSenti）、5个等预算选择器；比较LLM审计排名（如评判标签正确性）与下游Macro-F1排名，并比较\method{}-V2与AlpaGasus、naive等选择器。\method{}-V2为反事实审计框架。

Result: 每个单元格内，评判标签正确性与选择器Macro-F1的Spearman相关平均仅ρ=0.04（中位数0.00），说明错配非聚合假象。\method{}-V2在三条审计通道上最优：评判标签正确性0.904（naive为0.767，相对提升17.9%）、最低捷径分、硬拒率0.162（naive为0.486）。但AlpaGasus下游Macro-F1更高（0.202 vs. \method{}-V2的0.163），且反转在5个非退化单元格中持续存在。

Conclusion: 在此受控设定下，审计质量是所选池的属性，并不保证下游效用。合成数据评测应在同一保留集上同时报告审计指标与下游指标。作者公开审计表、各选择器保留池及逐条数字溯源的主张台账。

Abstract: Quality-aware synthetic-data selection rests on a proxy: examples that an LLM judge rates as good should also help a downstream model learn. In a controlled replay in low-resource African-language classification, we show that this proxy breaks. Across four languages (Amharic, Hausa, Swahili, Yoruba), two classification tasks (MasakhaNEWS, AfriSenti), and five matched-budget selectors, audit rankings and downstream rankings diverge. Within each cell, the Spearman between judged label correctness and Macro-F1 across selectors has mean $ρ{=}0.04$ (median $0.00$), showing that the mismatch is not an aggregation artifact. \method{}-V2, our counterfactual audit framework, produces the cleanest selected pool on three audit channels at once: highest judged label correctness ($0.904$ vs.\ $0.767$ for naive, a $17.9\%$ relative gain), lowest shortcut score, and a hard-reject rate of $0.162$ vs.\ $0.486$ for naive. AlpaGasus nevertheless leads downstream Macro-F1 ($0.202$ vs.\ $0.163$ for \method{}-V2), and the inversion persists on the five non-degenerate cells. The lesson is methodological: in this controlled setting, audit quality is a property of the selected pool, not a guarantee of downstream utility. Synthetic-data evaluation should therefore report audit and downstream metrics on the same retained sets. We release the audit tables, per-selector retained pools, and a claim ledger that links every reported number to its source row.

</details>


### [78] [MechSparse: Mechanism-Guided Sparse PEFT Selection Is Task-Shaped](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18961)
*Son Ha Xuan,Phat T. Tran-Truong,Xuan-Bach Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究检验因果可解释性信号能否比廉价启发式更有效指导小规模PEFT预算的放置，发现在三个测试场景中因果选择器均未在主要指标上取胜，并给出任务相关的选择诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性已识别出承载特定行为的稀疏注意力头和MLP块的因果子集。作者想探究这些因果信号是否能比 practitioners 常用的廉价启发式（随机、magnitude、activation-norm、gradient/Fisher）更有效地指导小型PEFT（如LoRA/QLoRA）参数预算的放置位置。

Method: 提出基于归一化activation-patching恢复分数来给注意力头和MLP块打分，仅在选中的位置训练LoRA/QLoRA；另加一个变体对层内小规模联合子集给予有界奖励。在Ministral-8B/NF4上比较随机、magnitude、activation-norm、gradient/Fisher，覆盖三种设置：Swahili span-JSON信息抽取（IE，b=0.25%和1.0%）与英→斯瓦希里机器翻译（MT，b=1.0%）。

Result: 因果选择器从未赢得主要指标。在核心IE设置（3个种子，600条预测的paired-bootstrap置信区间）中，方法变体比random高+0.079 span+type F1，比gradient/Fisher高+0.174，但比activation-norm低0.028，且跨种子方差最小（±0.003）。MT上四种选择器均在0.30 BLEU以内，且所有配对置信区间包含零。schema-versus-span分解解释了IE差距：activation-norm捕获刚性JSON流程，而因果分数追踪内容敏感位置。

Conclusion: 给出初步诊断：当输出结构主导时优先使用activation-norm；将因果选择器视为内容主导任务的一个假设。并发布masks、scores、预测与评估文件以供直接复现。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability identifies sparse subsets of heads and MLP blocks that carry specific behaviors. We ask whether such causal signals can guide where to place a small PEFT budget more effectively than the cheap heuristics practitioners already use. \method{} scores attention heads and MLP blocks by normalized activation-patching recovery on clean/corrupted probes and trains LoRA/QLoRA only on the selected sites; \methodc{} adds bounded credit for small within-layer joint subsets.
  We compare against random, magnitude, activation-norm, and gradient/Fisher on Ministral-8B/NF4 in three cells: Swahili span-JSON information extraction (IE) at $b{=}0.25\%$ and $1.0\%$, and English$\to$Swahili machine translation (MT) at $b{=}1.0\%$. The causal selectors never win the primary metric. On the headline IE cell (3 seeds, paired-bootstrap CIs over $600$ predictions), \methodc{} beats random by $+0.079$ span+type F1 and gradient/Fisher by $+0.174$, but trails activation-norm by $0.028$, with the smallest cross-seed std ($\pm 0.003$). On MT all four selectors lie within $0.30$ BLEU and every paired CI includes zero. A schema-versus-span decomposition explains the IE gap: activation-norm captures the rigid JSON routine, while causal scores track content-sensitive sites. We distill a preliminary diagnostic -- prefer activation-norm when output structure dominates, treat causal selectors as a hypothesis for content-dominated tasks -- and release masks, scores, predictions, and evaluation files for direct replay.

</details>


### [79] [A Benchmark Suite and Ground-Truth Methodology for Formal Verification of IEC 61131-3 Ladder Diagram Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18994)
*Pierre Dantas,Lucas Cordeiro,Waldir Junior*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向PLC程序形式化验证的基准测试套件，结合可控真值与ST（文本）和LD（图形）两种IEC 61131-3编码，包含50个程序、83个变体，覆盖10个工业领域，并提供形式属性、机器可校验判定及SV-COMP格式反例见证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域缺乏标准评测基准：现有语料或缺形式属性、或缺图形方言，且私有程序集阻碍可复现的进展度量。此外存在一个具体失效模式——明显安全属性因不可见非终止将两个公开逻辑炸弹语料的攻击全部误判为安全。

Method: 核心方法为“三方真值纪律”：判定通过构造、故障注入或经审计的跨工具共识三种方式确立。程序以PLCopen XML和ST提供。用ESBMC v8.4获取参考判定，并用决策过程无关的模型检查器nuXmv在有限状态片段上交叉验证。

Result: ESBMC：全部25个图形基准可执行，45个接受变体中43个与记录判定一致。nuXmv在21个有限状态基准上与全部24个互锁变体一致，并解决了ESBMC-PLC无法判定的两个基准，确认了工具中立真值与判别力。同时暴露格式与语义碎片化（前端接受不同序列化、定时器语义各异）。

Conclusion: 该套件为PLC形式化验证提供了可复现、工具中立的标准基准，能揭示工具间的格式与语义差异，语料、模式、校验器与复检工具链已作为开放产物发布。

Abstract: We present the first benchmark suite for formal verification of Programmable Logic Controller (PLC) programs that combines controlled ground truth with coverage of both textual (Structured Text, ST) and graphical (Ladder Diagram, LD) IEC 61131-3 encodings. Despite growing support for tools, the field lacks standard evaluation benchmarks: existing corpora omit formal properties or graphical dialects, and private program sets preclude reproducible measurement of progress. Our suite comprises 50 programs in 83 variants across ten industrial domains, provided in PLCopen Extensible Markup Language (XML) and ST, each paired with a formal property, machine-checkable expected verdict, and violation witness in the Software Verification Competition (SV-COMP) format. The central methodological contribution is a tripartite ground-truth discipline - verdicts are established by construction, fault injection, or audited cross-tool consensus - motivated by a concrete failure mode where the obvious safety property misclassifies all attacks from two public logic-bomb corpora as safe due to invisible non-termination. Reference verdicts are obtained with the Efficient SMT-Based Context-Bounded Model Checker (ESBMC) v8.4 from source: all 25 graphical benchmarks execute, and 43 of 45 accepted variants match recorded verdicts. On the finite-state fragment (21 benchmarks), nuXmv - a model checker with unrelated decision procedures - agrees on all 24 interlock variants and resolves two benchmarks ESBMC-PLC leaves unknown, confirming tool-neutral ground truth and discriminative power. Porting exposes format and semantics fragmentation: front-ends accept different serializations, and timer semantics vary across tools - phenomena the suite is designed to reveal. The corpus, schema, validator, and recheck harness are released as open artifacts.

</details>


### [80] [WordPolo: Evaluating Language Models Through Iterative Semantic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19006)
*Tyler McDonald,Ali Emami*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出WordPolo猜词任务，通过语义相似度反馈评估LLM/LRM的推理过程，超越单一准确率指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM和LRM仅通过数据集准确率评估，无法反映其推理过程的质量与忠实性，需要能同时测试推理过程与结果的基准。

Method: 设计WordPolo词查找任务：玩家零知识起步，通过猜测获得语义距离分数（1=正确，越大越远），逐步缩小搜索空间。评估GPT-4.1、Llama 4、Claude 3.5 Haiku、Qwen 3、o4-mini、Deepseek-R1、人类及一种启发式方法，共1500个谜题，并引入基于进展的指标。

Result: 解决率从4%到62%不等；基于进展的指标显示模型常能取得有意义进展，这是准确率所无法揭示的。推理模型受"过度思考"和"思考不足"影响，成功模型展现类人策略。

Conclusion: WordPolo表明需要同时测试推理过程与结果的基准，以更全面衡量模型能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) are typically evaluated on challenging benchmarks through dataset accuracy alone, providing no insight into the quality or faithfulness of their reasoning processes. We present WordPolo, a word-finding task where participants must discover an unknown target word using semantic similarity feedback. Players start with zero knowledge, make guesses, and receive distance scores (1 = correct, higher = further away). Success requires interpreting scores to navigate semantic space and systematically narrow the search. This design makes iterative reasoning and adaptive search strategies both directly observable and necessary for success. We evaluate recent LLMs (GPT-4.1, Llama 4, Claude 3.5 Haiku, Qwen 3), LRMs (o4-mini, Deepseek-R1), humans, and a novel heuristic on 1,500 puzzles. Beyond solve rates (which range from 4% to 62%), we introduce progression-based metrics that reveal models often make meaningful progress, insights that accuracy alone would miss. Our analysis shows how reasoning models can be hindered by overthinking and underthinking, while successful models exhibit human-like strategies. WordPolo demonstrates the need for benchmarks that test both reasoning process and outcomes, providing holistic measurements of model capabilities. Our code and dataset can be found at https://wordpolo-demo.vercel.app/.

</details>


### [81] [TalkMatrix: Generating Character Dialogue that is Both Consistent and Diverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19022)
*Ayuto Tsutsumi,Yuu Jinnai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化多提示多完成选择问题，并针对角色对话场景设计了TalkMatrix方法，通过生成多候选并联合选择整矩阵来满足全局不可分解约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于候选的解码方法独立地为每个提示选择完成，无法满足跨多个输出的全局、不可分解需求，例如角色对话中要求角色跨情境保持一致、每句台词契合情境、角色与情境可区分。

Method: 将问题形式化为structure multi-prompt multi-completion selection；TalkMatrix为每个角色—情境对生成多个候选，并用四个基于嵌入的一致性/多样性目标联合选择完整矩阵。由于加权和会导致维度间权衡，采用两层minimax最大化最差目标，并通过多起点坐标上升近似优化该离散目标。

Result: 在50个合成角色扮演场景和25个精选桌游场景中，LLM-as-a-judge评分显示矩阵级选择优于随机和逐单元格独立选择基线。

Conclusion: 结构化选择对全局可控对话生成具有价值，但实证验证仍局限于角色扮演场景。

Abstract: Candidate-based decoding typically selects a completion for each prompt independently, but many applications require a collection of outputs that satisfies global, non-decomposable requirements. We formulate this setting as structured multi-prompt, multi-completion selection: given a candidate pool for every prompt, select one completion per prompt to optimize a collection-level objective. We instantiate the problem in character dialogue, where each character should remain consistent across situations, each line should fit its situation, and characters and situations should remain distinguishable. Our method, TalkMatrix, generates multiple candidates for every character--situation pair and jointly selects a complete matrix using four embedding-based consistency and diversity objectives. Because a weighted sum can improve some dimensions by sacrificing another, TalkMatrix maximizes the worst-performing objective through a two-level minimax formulation. We approximately optimize the resulting discrete objective with multi-start coordinate ascent, and compare it with local, partial-matrix, and generic combinatorial search baselines. We run experiments on $50$ synthetic role-playing scenarios and $25$ curated board game scenarios where multiple characters interact in predefined situations. An LLM-as-a-judge rates matrix-level selection higher than random and independent cell-level selection baselines. These results show the value of structured selection for globally controlled dialogue generation, while our empirical validation remains specific to role-playing scenarios.

</details>


### [82] [The Death of Schema Linking? Text-to-SQL in the Age of Well-Reasoned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.07702)
*Karime Maamari,Fadhil Abubaker,Daniel Jaroslawicz,Amine Mhedhbi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视Text-to-SQL中的schema linking，发现最新LLM能有效利用大量无关schema元素，因此在schema可容纳于上下文窗口时完全跳过schema linking，改用增强、选择与修正技术，在BIRD基准上以71.83%准确率排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: schema linking是Text-to-SQL的关键步骤，旨在检索用户查询相关的表和列。但不完善的schema linking常会错误排除生成准确查询所需的列，导致准确率下降。作者希望重新评估在最新LLM背景下schema linking的必要性。

Method: 实证发现新模型即便面对大量无关schema元素也能有效利用相关元素。因此，当schema能放入模型上下文窗口时，完全跳过schema linking；并以增强（augmentation）、选择（selection）和修正（correction）等技术替代上下文过滤，提升Text-to-SQL流水线准确率。

Result: 该方法在BIRD基准上取得71.83%的准确率，排名第一。

Conclusion: 在最新LLM时代，schema linking可能不再必要，甚至有害；直接提供完整schema并辅以增强、选择和修正技术，可获得更优的Text-to-SQL性能。

Abstract: Schema linking is a crucial step in Text-to-SQL pipelines. Its goal is to retrieve the relevant tables and columns of a target database for a user's query while disregarding irrelevant ones. However, imperfect schema linking can often exclude required columns needed for accurate query generation. In this work, we revisit schema linking when using the latest generation of large language models (LLMs). We find empirically that newer models are adept at utilizing relevant schema elements during generation even in the presence of large numbers of irrelevant ones. As such, our Text-to-SQL pipeline entirely forgoes schema linking in cases where the schema fits within the model's context window in order to minimize issues due to filtering required schema elements. Furthermore, instead of filtering contextual information, we highlight techniques such as augmentation, selection, and correction, and adopt them to improve the accuracy of our Text-to-SQL pipeline. Our approach ranks first on the BIRD benchmark achieving an accuracy of 71.83%.

</details>


### [83] [Entropy in Conversational AI: Structured Unpredictability as Inferrable Interiority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19044)
*Sebastian Cochinescu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出"结构化不可预测性"这一设计目标，即输出与持久隐藏状态之间的条件依赖超出观察者从文本可推断的范围。作者用选择层更新低维风格-注意力状态，并选择新颖且与状态契合的回复。合成实验通过四项预注册特征，但真实模型网格中联合新颖性-一致性标准与累积标准均未通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样虽能提升回复多样性，却不产生依赖历史的行为。作者希望形式化一个不同的设计目标——结构化不可预测性，使输出与持久隐藏状态产生条件依赖，从而让跨轮次依赖存在于包装层状态与选择器中，而非对话历史本身。

Method: 选择层从容量受限的流中更新低维"风格-注意力"状态；固定基座模型生成多个回复，再依据新颖性与状态契合度进行选择。评估采用脚本化的独立提示轮次序列：基座模型只接收当前轮次和渲染状态，不接收前序对话。合成实现验证流程，并做真实模型网格实验（Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct-4bit，每臂56条序列）。

Result: 真实模型网格中，该机制在词汇新颖性上比低方差与仅一致性对照分别提高0.073和0.023。但与新颖性匹配的采样相比，风格一致性对比在注册最小效应规则下等价于零，联合新颖性-一致性标准失败。原始两部分累积标准亦失败；修订后的最终网格对比显示一致性高于记忆重置消融（0.028，95% CI [0.018,0.039]），但不足以确立路径依赖。孪生分离未成立（0.003，95% CI [-0.011,0.019]），均值曲线的饱和曲率与冻结预测吻合，但无分离则不支持路径依赖。探针级能力等价性在近天花板测试集上处于±0.10内，输出质量未评估。

Conclusion: 结构化不可预测性的机制能提升词汇新颖性，但关键的新颖性-一致性联合标准与路径依赖均未获证据支持。所有结果均为机器评分，未涉及对感知心智或意识的任何主张。

Abstract: Sampling can increase response diversity without producing history-dependent behavior. We formalize a different design target, structured unpredictability, as conditional dependence between an output and a persistent hidden state beyond what an observer can infer from the transcript. A selection layer updates a low-dimensional style-and-attention state from a capacity-limited stream, generates several responses with a fixed base model, and selects for novelty and state affinity. Evaluation uses scripted sequences of independent prompt turns: the base model receives the current turn and rendered state, but not the preceding dialogue; cross-turn dependence resides in the wrapper state and response selector. A synthetic implementation validates the pipeline and matches four prospectively hash-frozen divergence features at point level. In the final real-model grid (mlx-community/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct-4bit; 56 sequences per arm), the mechanism increased lexical novelty over the low-variance and consistency-only controls by 0.073 and 0.023, respectively. Its stylometric-consistency contrast with novelty-matched sampling was equivalent to zero under the registered smallest-effect rule, so the joint novelty-consistency criterion failed. The original two-part accumulation criterion also failed; a revised final-grid contrast, frozen after the powered grid, found higher consistency than the memory-reset ablation (0.028, 95% CI [0.018,0.039]), but does not establish path dependence. Twin separation was not established (0.003, 95% CI [-0.011,0.019]); the mean curve's saturating curvature matched the frozen prediction, which without separation does not support path dependence. Probe-level capability equivalence held within +/-0.10 on a near-ceiling battery, while output quality was not evaluated. All outcomes are machine-scored; no claims about perceived mind or consciousness are tested.

</details>


### [84] [MIRAGE: How Conversation State Shapes Historical Evidence Use in Multimodal Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19059)
*Yu Liu,Wenxiao Zhang,Cheng Hu,Cong Cao,Fangfang Yuan,Xinyu Wang,Jin B. Hong,Yanbing Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MIRAGE，控制对话状态变化来评估多模态LLM智能体的历史证据使用，发现仅看结果会高估真实证据利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM智能体用于长任务个人助理，其连续性依赖跨对话、文件、工作区状态检索早期证据；但即便历史访问已退化，智能体仍能生成看似合理的答案，导致结果导向评估高估证据使用。

Method: MIRAGE保持证据对象、问题与评分不变，仅改变对话状态，评估智能体能否判断可答性、恢复正确来源并据此作答；在七种前沿与开源多模态骨干模型上测试。

Result: 发现：1) 压缩前深度与压缩后续接构成不同的非单调失败区间；2) 开源模型高度依赖上下文连续性，来源失效时不愿自发转向工具检索；3) 检索压力在深压缩前状态改善来源归因（工具合规模型），但压缩后一致退步。

Conclusion: 历史证据使用应在状态变化下评估，而非仅从结果正确性推断。

Abstract: Multimodal large language model (MLLM) agents are increasingly used as personal assistants for long-running tasks. Their utility depends on continuity: agents must retrieve and use earlier evidence across dialogue, files, and workspace state. However, agents can generate plausible answers even when access to that history has degraded, causing outcome-only evaluation to overestimate true evidence use. We present MIRAGE (Multimodal Interaction Retrieval, Attribution, and Grounding Evaluation), a controlled study of historical evidence use under conversation-state variation in multimodal personal agents. MIRAGE holds evidence objects, questions, and scoring fixed while varying only conversation state, and evaluates whether an agent can determine answerability, recover the correct source, and answer from it. Across seven frontier and open-weight multimodal backbones, we find that: 1) pre-compaction depth and post-compaction continuation form distinct, non-monotonic failure regimes rather than a single degradation curve; 2) open-weight models rely heavily on context continuity and are reluctant to spontaneously switch to tool-mediated retrieval when provenance fails; and 3) retrieval pressure improves source attribution in deep pre-compaction states for tool-compliant models, but consistently regresses after compaction, where stored evidence has already degraded. These findings show that historical evidence use should be evaluated under state variation, rather than inferred from outcome-only correctness.

</details>


### [85] [Reading Between the Lines: Can LLMs Discover the Question Behind the Text?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19070)
*Claudiu Creanga,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"问题考古"(question archaeology)任务：从完整文本中推断出激发该文本创作的唯一真实"起源问题"，并发布配套数据集与基准评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分于面向任意合理问题的"问题生成"和建模话语行为的会话框架，本任务旨在评估模型对作者意图的理解能力，填补对深层写作动机推断评测的空白。

Method: 构建新数据集，将委托创作的文本与其原始研究问题及合理的干扰项配对；在该数据集上评测Gemini Flash/Pro等商业模型、Mistral、Qwen等开源模型及BERT类模型。

Result: 新版本模型表现优于旧版本，BERT类模型表现较差；当前LLM在该任务上超过人类水平，显示出对作者意图的较强理解。

Conclusion: 该能力对AI在需要细致解读人类沟通的任务中具有重要意义，本文提供了新框架与具挑战性的基准供未来模型评测。

Abstract: This paper introduces ``question archaeology'', a specific evaluation task focused on inferring the single, authentic "genesis question" that motivated the creation of a complete text. Distinct from question generation, which targets any plausible question, or discourse frameworks that model utterance-level acts, our task assesses a model's grasp of authorial intent. We present a new dataset of commissioned texts paired with their original research questions and plausible distractors. Our evaluation of both proprietary models, like Gemini Flash and Pro, as well as open source models like Mistral and Qwen, reveals significant progress in this task, with the newer versions outperforming the earlier ones, while BERT-based models performed poorly. Notably, our findings indicate that current LLMs surpass human performance on this task, suggesting advanced understanding of authorial intent. This capability has important implications for AI's role in tasks requiring nuanced interpretation of human communication. Our work thus provides a new framework and a challenging benchmark for future models.

</details>


### [86] [Benchmarking Large Language Models for Biomedical Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19071)
*Claudiu Creanga,Teodor Marchitan,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文基准测试了多种NLP模型（包括MLM、混合架构及先进LLM）在SNPPhenA语料库上提取SNP-表型关联的三个任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从生物医学文献中提取SNP-表型关联很重要但具有挑战性，需要评估现代NLP/LLM方法在此任务上的能力。

Method: 在SNPPhenA语料库上，对多种模型（MLM、混合架构和Gemini 2.0、OpenAI O系列、Qwen、Mistral等LLM）进行基准测试，涵盖句子级、摘要级和关联强度分类三个任务。

Result: OpenAI O1在少样本句子级分类达到SOTA（F1 0.89），并在摘要级分类创下新SOTA（F1 0.82）；关联强度分类较难，微调Gemini 2.0 Pro最佳（F1 0.60），为该任务首次LLM评估。

Conclusion: 专有LLM（尤其少样本O1或微调Gemini 2.0 Pro）显著优于其他模型，证实了现代LLM在基因组知识提取中的强大能力。

Abstract: Extracting SNP-phenotype associations from biomedical literature is vital but challenging. We benchmarked diverse NLP models, including MLMs, hybrid architectures, and state-of-the-art LLMs (Gemini 2.0, OpenAI O-series, Qwen, Mistral), on the SNPPhenA corpus across three tasks: sentence-level, abstract-level, and association strength classification. OpenAI O1 achieved state-of-the-art (SOTA) results using few-shot learning for non-finetuned sentence-level classification (F1 0.89) and established a new SOTA for abstract-level classification (F1 0.82). Association strength classification proved difficult, though fine-tuned Gemini 2.0 Pro performed best (F1 0.60) in the first LLM evaluation of this task. Proprietary LLMs, especially in few-shot (O1) or fine-tuned (Gemini 2.0 Pro) settings, significantly outperformed other models. These findings confirm the power of modern LLMs for genomic knowledge extraction.

</details>


### [87] [Reporting Practice Matters: The Impact of Reference Choice on Chest X-ray Report Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19093)
*Daniel P. Jeong,Charles Q. Li,Hossein Hosseiny,Nitya M. Bhalla,Fatma Uyar Morency,Pradeep Ravikumar,Zachary C. Lipton,Michael Oberst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文量化了放射报告书写习惯差异对AI报告生成模型评估指标的影响，提出反映书写差异的分类体系与重写方法ReRef，并发布验证数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科医生报告习惯各异，同一图像同一发现可能写出表面不同的报告；而AI放射报告生成模型的评估通常依赖与人类参考报告的一致性，这种书写习惯差异是被低估的评估障碍。

Method: 提出放射科医生参与制定的报告书写差异分类体系，以及ReRef方法：在保持临床解读不变的前提下，沿分类体系各维度重写参考报告。

Result: 现有评估指标对书写习惯差异高度敏感，足以改变模型排名。例如在MIMIC-CXR上用RadCliQ-v1比较九个模型时，压缩正常发现讨论使Libra从第一降至第二，CheXOne从第三升至第一。

Conclusion: 许多现有指标无法将临床解读与对书写习惯的符合度解耦，选择合适的参考报告很重要；发布MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef数据集（120对经放射科医生验证的参考报告对）以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Radiologists follow heterogeneous reporting practices. Two radiologists examining the same image and identifying the same clinical findings might nevertheless compose superficially distinct reports, varying in terminology, shorthand, formatting, and level of detail. These variations in reporting norms represent an under-appreciated obstacle in efforts to evaluate AI-based radiology report generation (RRG) models, where machine-generated reports are typically assessed based on their concordance with human-generated references. In this paper, we quantify the sensitivity of established evaluation metrics to variations in reporting practices, revealing impacts large enough to alter the rankings of models. We introduce a radiologist-informed taxonomy of variations in radiology reporting practice and a method (ReRef) that rewrites reference reports along the axes of our taxonomy while preserving clinical interpretation. For instance, when comparing the performance of nine RRG models on MIMIC-CXR using RadCliQ-v1, condensing the discussion of normal findings in the reference reports causes Libra to drop from first to second place while CheXOne rises from third to first. Our results suggest that many current metrics fail to decouple clinical interpretation from conformity to reporting practices and that choosing the ``right'' references that accurately reflect the desired reporting practices can be important in practice. To support future research, we release MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef, a radiologist-validated dataset of 120 (original, alternative) reference report pairs derived from MIMIC-CXR.

</details>


### [88] [Monitoring and Discovering Reward Hacking with Internal Representations during LLM Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19101)
*Leon Bergen,Usha Bhalla,Andrew Lee,Barak Widawsky,Linas Nasvytis,Connor Watts,Siddharth Boppana,Sidharth Baskaran,Dron Hazra,Michael Byun,Atticus Geiger,Owen Lewis,Matthew Kowal,Vasudev Shyam,Thomas Fel,Thomas McGrath,Ekdeep Singh Lubana,Jack Merullo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究用简单"均值差向量（DoM）"在开源前沿LLM内部表征中捕捉reward hacking，发现其通用、可解释、可低成本在线检测hacking行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随模型规模扩大，reward hacking愈发频繁、复杂且具后果性。作者想探究hacking是否在模型内部表征中留下可识别的特征，从而廉价地理解、发现和监测这类行为，替代昂贵但有效的LLM监控器。

Method: 在Kimi K3、GLM 5.2、Qwen 3.8 Max上，用简单的差异均值（DoM）向量表示reward hacking，覆盖常见评测中的多种行为；在DeepSWE、SWE-bench上评估hacking频率；将DoM向量用于检测及对思维链（CoT）在线预测。

Result: 模型在基准中hack严重：GLM 5.2在DeepSWE 57.2%、SWE-bench 73%的rollout中hack。DoM向量在相同误报率下几乎零成本且效果与LLM监控器相当：Kimi K3多抓3.1%的hack，GLM 5.2少抓7.9%。DoM跑在CoT上还能预测后续动作中的hack，实现提前在线拦截。探针还发现LLM监控器漏掉的hack及其他不良行为，并可迁移到非SWE评测。

Conclusion: 简单的白盒方法可规模化地研究和监控前沿开源模型的reward hacking行为。

Abstract: As models scale, reward hacking becomes more frequent, more sophisticated, and more consequential. Does it leave a telltale signature in model representations? This work analyzes how reward hacking is represented internally in frontier open source LLMs, and how those representations can be used to understand and discover the range of hacking behaviors a model displays. In particular, we find that simple difference of means vectors coherently represent reward hacking in Kimi K3, GLM 5.2, and Qwen 3.8 Max across a variety of behaviors in common evaluations. Despite their simplicity, these vectors are both generalizable and interpretable, and we can use them to reliably detect reward hacking. We first evaluate reward hacking in commonly reported benchmarks like DeepSWE and SWE-bench, finding that models reward hack excessively in these environments; GLM 5.2 hacks in 57.2% of rollouts on DeepSWE and in 73% of rollouts on SWE-bench. Catching these requires monitors; LLM monitors are effective, but expensive detectors. We show that DoM vectors are similarly effective but virtually free, catching 3.1% more hacks in Kimi K3 and 7.9% fewer hacks in GLM 5.2 on DeepSWE at a monitor matched false positive rate. DoM vectors run on the chain-of-thought also predict reward hacks in the model's subsequent actions, meaning we can run them online and catch potential hacks before they occur. Finally, we analyze probe-hits that LLM monitors do not catch and discover other undesirable behaviors, as well as show transfer to finding hacks in non-SWE evaluations. Together, these results provide evidence that simple, white-box methods can be used to scalably study and monitor reward hacking behaviors in frontier open source models

</details>


### [89] [Playing log(N)-Questions over Wikipedia Abstracts: Communication Efficiency Between Paired Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19113)
*Peter Potash*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 六款前沿模型在两智能体log(N)-Questions游戏中自问自答，通过每轮可靠度参数刻画沟通能力，胜率随集合规模以 win=p^{log2 N}（p=0.928）衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量同一模型在信息不对称下与"自己"沟通的能力：提问者只看N个维基段落，回答者只看目标，二者需用恰好log2 N个是非问题完成识别，从而隔离模棱两可沟通的失效来源。

Method: 在4至1024段落的文档集上运行408局游戏（总成本363美元），评测6款前沿模型；由答案平衡度估计每问信息量，对最弱模型的全部一致性答案错误进行人工检查，并记录推理token消耗。

Result: Claude Opus 5以68局28胜显著落后，其余五款（GLM-5.3、GPT-5.6 Sol、Grok 4.6、Gemini 3.8 Flash、Kimi K3）胜率45–56且差异微小；五款合并后胜率与集合规模 r=-0.973，拟合为单一可靠度参数 p=0.928 的 win=p^{log2 N}。失败约等分为答案错误与区分失败，模型几乎不会指认被自身证据排除的文档；最弱模型34次一致答案错误中32次为"否"，均针对首句陈述的属性且在明确警告勿默认"否"的指令下发生。每问信息量与胜率 r=+0.88。仅两款模型达到每问1比特，恰是唯二按标题划分的模型（N≥32时约占四分之一题目）。推理token消耗跨模型相差4.5倍且与成功关联弱。

Conclusion: 模型自我沟通能力随集合规模按单一可靠度参数衰减，答案错误比区分失败更可诊断；划分策略（如按标题）与每问信息量、胜率强相关，而推理长度并非成功关键。

Abstract: We evaluate six frontier language models on the two-agent $\log(N)$-Questions game. A questioner sees $N$ Wikipedia lead paragraphs and must identify a secretly chosen target using exactly $\log_2 N$ yes/no questions. An answerer sees only the target and the question, and replies with one word. Both roles run on the same provider, so the game measures how well a model communicates with itself across an information asymmetry. We run 408 games over document sets of 4 to 1024 paragraphs at a total API cost of \$363. One model finishes well behind the others: Claude Opus 5 wins 28 of 68 games, against 45 to 56 for GLM-5.3, GPT-5.6 Sol, Grok 4.6, Gemini 3.8 Flash and Kimi K3. The leading five are only marginally separable. Pooling those five, win rate declines with set size at $r=-0.973$ and is fit by a single per-round reliability parameter. The form is $\text{win}=p^{\log_2 N}$ with $p=0.928$. Losses divide into answer errors and discrimination failures in roughly equal measure, and models almost never name a document their own evidence excludes. Every unanimous answer error from the weakest model was inspected: 32 of 34 are ``No'' answers, on properties stated in the document's first sentence, under an instruction that explicitly warns against defaulting to ``No''. Information per question, estimated from answer balance, correlates with win rate at $r=+0.88$. The only two models to extract a full bit per question are the only two that partition on document titles, a strategy absent below $N{=}32$ and used in a quarter of questions above it. Reasoning-token expenditure varies $4.5\times$ across models with little relation to success, and the trace grows as the candidate set shrinks without a matching gain in reliability.

</details>


### [90] [A Zeroth-Order Paradigm for LLM Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19144)
*Peter Chen,Xi Chen,Wotao Yin,Tianyi Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ComPO，一种基于比较oracle的零阶偏好对齐方法，避免直接优化可微偏好损失，理论证明离线与在线方案收敛性，并在多个LLM上超越现有直接对齐方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接偏好对齐方法虽高效，但存在likelihood displacement问题，即对小似然边际的偏好对信息提取不佳，需要新的信息利用方式。

Method: 提出Comparison-based Preference Optimization (ComPO)：从偏好对中提取方向信息而非直接优化可微偏好损失；离线方案在平滑性、梯度稀疏与oracle-潜在目标兼容性下有收敛保证；在线ComPO保留离线比较机制，用无标注策略生成做相对参考策略的反向KL控制。

Result: 在Mistral、Llama、Gemma-2、Qwen3、Gemma-3上优于现有直接对齐方法，包括长度控制胜率提升，配对级诊断显示缓解likelihood displacement的证据；并在局部覆盖与分布内配对奖励准确率下证明受限方案性能保证。

Conclusion: ComPO通过比较oracle的零阶对齐有效缓解likelihood displacement，兼具理论保证与实证优势，为偏好对齐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Direct preference alignment methods are widely used to align large language models (LLMs) with human preferences because of their computational and memory efficiency. However, likelihood displacement motivates alternative ways to extract information from preference pairs with small likelihood margins. In this paper, we propose and analyze Comparison-based Preference Optimization (ComPO), a zeroth-order alignment method based on comparison oracles. ComPO extracts directional information from these pairs without directly optimizing a differentiable preference loss on them. We establish a convergence guarantee for its basic offline scheme under smoothness, gradient sparsity, and compatibility between the oracle and a latent objective. We further introduce online ComPO, which retains the offline comparison mechanism and uses unlabeled policy generations for reverse-KL control relative to a reference policy. Following the coverage perspective of preference fine-tuning, we establish a performance guarantee for a basic constrained scheme under local coverage and in-distribution pairwise reward accuracy. Experiments on Mistral, Llama, Gemma-2, Qwen3, and Gemma-3 models demonstrate improvements over existing direct alignment methods, including length-controlled win rates, with pair-level diagnostics providing evidence consistent with mitigating likelihood displacement.

</details>


### [91] [Objective vs. Search: Decomposing What Makes a Good Tokeniser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19145)
*Ahmetcan Yavuz,Clara Meister,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过引入两种新分词算法（BottomUpLL和TopDownComp）补全2x2设计空间，解耦了分词优化的目标（压缩vs似然）与搜索方式（自底向上合并vs自顶向下剪枝）两个因素。实验表明，搜索方式而非优化目标是语言建模性能（bits-per-byte）的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型常用BPE和UnigramLM两种分词算法，它们在优化目标（压缩vs对数似然）和搜索过程（自底向上合并vs自顶向下剪枝）两个正交维度上都不同。现有比较混淆了这两个维度，使人无法判断性能差异究竟来自"优化什么"还是"如何优化"。

Method: 引入两种新分词算法补全2x2设计空间：BottomUpLL（自底向上的基于似然的分词器）和TopDownComp（自顶向下的基于压缩的分词器）。用每种算法生成的分词器训练语言模型，并变化模型规模、词表大小和领域（纯英文vs多语言），在bits-per-byte和BLiMP任务上评估。

Result: 在bits-per-byte评估中，搜索过程（而非优化目标）是主导因素：自底向上分词器在多数设置下稳定取得更低的bits-per-byte。但在BLiMP任务上，设计选择与性能之间没有一致关系。

Conclusion: 研究解耦了分词器设计选择对语言建模性能的影响，为更合理地构建分词器提供了具体指导。

Abstract: Two dominant tokenisation algorithms are used by modern language models: byte-pair encoding (BPE) and UnigramLM. These differ along two orthogonal axes: their optimisation objective (compression vs. log-likelihood) and their search procedure (bottom-up merging vs. top-down pruning). Existing comparisons confound these axes, making it unclear whether their observed differences stem from what is being optimised vs. how it is being optimised. We disentangle the two by introducing two new tokenisation algorithms that complete this 2x2 design space: BottomUpLL, a bottom-up likelihood-based tokeniser, and TopDownComp, a top-down compression-based tokeniser. We train language models with tokenisers produced by each algorithm, varying: model size, vocabulary sizes, and domain (English-only vs. multilingual). Evaluating models on bits-per-byte, we find that the search procedure -- not the objective -- is the dominant factor: bottom-up tokenisers consistently achieve lower bits-per-byte in most settings. Evaluating models on the BLiMP task, however, shows no consistent relationship between design choice and performance. Overall, our results disentangle the effect of tokeniser design choices on language modelling performance, offering concrete guidance for their more principled construction.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [Pay Only for Disagreement: Certified No-Regression Verdicts for Model Updates with Matching Label-Complexity Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17560)
*Vishnu Bindu Balachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"更新上线审批"形式化为配对风险差审计，提出DISCERN两阶段协议：零标签层仅用未标注流量证明差异率低于容差，审计层仅对采样的分歧样本打标签，实现随时有效的置信序列。理论上达到rho^2/eps^2的标签复杂度，比配对盲审计节省1/rho，并可在一个误差预算下跨无限次上线组合保证。在14000+审计流、785对更新上，误覆盖0.0002、功效0.986、零误报，56%良性更新零标签通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产模型不断通过重训、微调、量化或供应商静默替换而更新，每次更新都可能比被替换的版本更差。现有上线审批缺乏对"新模型是否更差"的严格、可组合的统计保证，且打标签成本高。

Method: 核心是支撑集恒等式：两模型的风险差只存在于它们预测分歧的输入上，且该分歧无需标签即可观测。据此设计DISCERN顺序两阶段协议：零标签层依据未标注流量的分歧率认证良性更新；审计层仅对采样的分歧样本打标签，通过随时有效置信序列完成检验，对任意停止时间和任意标签路由规则（含对抗性评审）均有效。

Result: 证明有限样本有效性，并给出速率层面rho^2/eps^2的匹配标签复杂度界，说明利用免费分歧可比配对盲审计者节省1/rho因子，且保证可在单一误差预算下跨无界上线序列组合。在785对更新、14000+回放审计流（含至1.4B参数的LoRA微调语言模型）上，误覆盖0.0002（名义5%）、功效0.986、零误报，56%良性更新零标签认证，每次审计输出可机器校验的证据记录用于上市后监测。

Conclusion: 将模型更新上线审批转化为可认证的配对风险差审计，利用无需标签的分歧信息显著降低标签成本，并提供随时有效、可组合的统计保证。实验验证其高功效、零误报与零标签认证能力，输出可机器校验的证据记录，支撑上市后持续监测。

Abstract: Every production model is updated, by retraining, fine-tuning, quantization, or a silent vendor swap, and each update risks being worse than what it replaced. We formalize update promotion as certified paired risk-difference auditing. Our starting point is a support identity: the risk difference between two models lives on the inputs where they disagree, observable without labels. We build DISCERN, a sequential two-tier protocol. A zero-label tier certifies benign updates whose disagreement rate is below tolerance from unlabeled traffic alone. An audited tier labels only sampled disagreements through an anytime-valid confidence sequence, valid at every stopping time and under any label-routing rule, even an adversarial judge. We prove finite-sample validity and matching label-complexity bounds of order rho^2/eps^2 at the rate level, so exploiting free disagreement provably saves a factor 1/rho over any pairing-blind auditor, and the guarantee composes across an unbounded sequence of promotions from one error budget. Across 14,000+ replayed audit streams over 785 update pairs, including LoRA fine-tunes of language models up to 1.4B parameters, miscoverage is 0.0002 (nominal 5%), power 0.986 with zero false alarms, and 56% of benign updates certify with zero labels. Each audit emits a machine-checkable evidence record for post-market monitoring.

</details>


### [93] [Beyond Static RAG: An Adaptive, Tri-Metric Routing Framework for Efficient Long-Context Inference on Commodity GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17564)
*Saipraveen Vabbilisetty,Ajay Kumar Boddepalli,Deep Narayan Mishra,Shashank Kapadia,Haoan Wang,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"压缩悖论"问题，并设计Tri-Metric Router在T4等低显存GPU上动态选择Raw/Neural/Lexical三种RAG流水线，实现0% OOM且无需额外训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在16GB显存的消费级GPU上部署RAG存在两难：神经提示压缩会加剧KV缓存竞争并增加预处理延迟，其收益被抵消；而不压缩又会导致长上下文OOM。现有方法多基于语义自适应，未考虑显存与延迟的硬件物理约束。

Method: 作者识别出vLLM服务的LLM与PyTorch压缩器共部署时的两种失效机制，提出Tri-Metric Router：一种确定性、免训练的策略，依据三个CPU端信号（空间复杂度L、句法密度ρ_key、类型-标记比TTR）在Raw、Neural(LLMLingua-2)、Lexical(BM25)三种流水线间选择。调度信号基于显存余量与延迟交叉点，阈值通过在LongBench qasper上的剖析校准（T4上约4,332词为操作交叉点）。

Result: 在分布外留出集上，方法取得0% OOM失败率、88.5±4.4%的oracle对齐度、49.3%的组合F1，比始终开启词法压缩提升5.2个点，且不增加显存或训练开销。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了低显存GPU上RAG压缩的硬件物理约束，提出的Tri-Metric Router通过免训练、确定性的调度策略有效规避OOM并提升性能；其核心贡献在于阈值校准方法论而非特定硬件常数。

Abstract: Deploying retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) on commodity GPUs such as the NVIDIA T4 (16 GB VRAM) exposes a practical failure mode we call the Compression Paradox: neural prompt compression can add key-value (KV) cache contention and preprocessing latency that outweigh generation-time savings, while skipping compression can cause out-of-memory (OOM) failures on long contexts. We identify two distinct failure mechanisms when a vLLM-served LLM and a PyTorch-based compressor are co-deployed under tight memory budgets, and introduce the Tri-Metric Router, a deterministic, training-free policy that selects among Raw, Neural (LLMLingua-2), and Lexical (BM25) pipelines. The router uses three CPU-side signals: spatial complexity ($L$), syntactic density ($ρ_{key}$), and type-token ratio (TTR). Unlike prior semantic-only adaptation, our dispatch signal is hardware-physical, based on VRAM headroom and a latency crossover point. Thresholds are calibrated from profiling on LongBench qasper, yielding an operating crossover near 4,332 words on T4; our contribution is this calibration methodology rather than a hardware-specific constant. On out-of-distribution holdouts, the method achieves 0% OOM failures, 88.5 $\pm$ 4.4% oracle alignment, and 49.3% Combined F1, improving over always-on lexical compression by 5.2 points without additional VRAM or training cost.

</details>


### [94] [Where Grokking Happens: Distributed Utility and Fourier Recoding Without a Module Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17571)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过"Transition Games"行为对齐的精确激活博弈，探究Transformer中从记忆到泛化的功能变化位置，发现泛化源于已有分布式电路的频谱重编码，而非某个模块的切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者想定位Transformer中"从记忆到泛化"这一转变在功能上于何处表达，并检验流行的"MLP记忆、注意力泛化"假说是否成立。

Method: 引入Transition Games——行为对齐的精确激活博弈，并配合成对的非泛化对照；使用替换博弈(replacement games)分析度二模式(degree-two modes)的贡献；另用独立的精确路径(exact path)研究检验下游路径的作用。

Result: 发现分布式效用增益，与block-0注意力的前瞻性偏置相关；选定的度二模式在替换博弈中贡献其加法对比的67–92%；block-1 MLP介导的作用多于其他下游路径(12/12对)；而"MLP记忆、注意力泛化"预测反向(-.331 bits/example，0/12符合预测方向)；路由起始、全局秩崩塌、素数不变架构脊等假说均不成立。

Conclusion: grokking在此表现为已有分布式电路的频谱重编码(spectral recoding)，而非模块切换(module switch)。

Abstract: Where in a Transformer is the change from memorization to generalization functionally expressed? We introduce Transition Games--behavior-aligned exact activation games with paired non-generalizing controls--and find distributed utility gain with a prospective block-0 attention bias; selected degree-two modes account for 67--92% of its addition contrast across replacement games, and a disjoint exact path study confirms that block-1 MLP mediates more of their effect than all other tested downstream paths in 12/12 pairs. The sharper "MLP memorizes, attention generalizes" prediction instead reverses (-.331 bits/example at the memory anchor; 0/12 in the predicted direction), while routing onset, global rank collapse, and a prime-invariant architecture ridge also fail, identifying grokking here as spectral recoding of an existing distributed circuit rather than a module switch.

</details>


### [95] [Disentangling Algorithmic Bias from Archival Artifacts: A Controlled Audit of Vision-Language Model Valuation in Metropolitan Museum Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17572)
*Manpreet Singh,Rhythm Bhatia,Rahul Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出量化审计框架,用大都会艺术博物馆艺术品元数据审计CLIP模型的性别偏见。零样本logit差分显示性别主效应不显著且具统计等价性,OLS回归(控制媒介、年代、宽高比)后条件效应也不显著(R²<0.02)。作者强调零样本指标粗糙、易受嵌入噪声影响,并提示去属性样本遗漏造成的机构存活偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审计视觉语言模型(VLM)的社会偏见时,需区分算法本身的估值差异与档案元数据中的混杂因素。作者希望以历史艺术品元数据为切入口,检验CLIP是否存在性别偏见,并建立更严谨的审计方法论。

Method: 使用大都会艺术博物馆开放获取藏品元数据(N=1500,其中有署名作品743件:男性534、女性209,匿名618件)。构建量化审计框架,在"masterpiece、quality、influence"三组语义提示对上计算零样本CLIP logit差分;进行未调整检验、双单侧检验(TOST,Cohen's d≥0.25)以及控制艺术品媒介、创作年代和宽高比的多元OLS回归。

Result: 未调整评估显示得分高度收敛,性别主效应不显著(OpenAI CLIP: μ_F=-0.0067 vs μ_M=-0.0035,p=0.1829;OpenCLIP: μ_F=0.0171 vs μ_M=0.0237,p=0.1224)。TOST确认在Cohen's d≥0.25范围内统计等价(pTOST<0.005)。OLS回归中性别条件效应仍不显著(p>0.20,R²<0.02),残差嵌入方差高,表明全局零样本估值指标接近嵌入噪声下限。

Conclusion: 宏观层面的得分等价反映的是指标对细粒度视觉-语义特征不敏感,而非证明模型绝对公平;不能排除局部微观视觉偏见。此外,排除41.2%未署名藏品会引入机构存活偏差。评估文化遗产领域AI公平性时,必须结合多元混杂控制、等价检验与档案来源审计。

Abstract: Auditing vision-language models (VLMs) for societal bias requires distinguishing direct algorithmic valuation disparities from confounders embedded within archival metadata. In this study, we audit Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) models using historical artwork metadata from the Metropolitan Museum of Art Open Access collection (N = 1,500 total objects; N = 743 attributed works: Male n = 534, Female n = 209; n = 618 anonymous).
  We establish a quantitative audit framework evaluating zero-shot CLIP logit differential scores across three semantic prompt pairs (masterpiece, quality, and influence). Unadjusted evaluations demonstrate high score convergence without a statistically significant main gender effect under OpenAI CLIP (mu_F = -0.0067 vs mu_M = -0.0035, p = 0.1829) or OpenCLIP (mu_F = 0.0171 vs mu_M = 0.0237, p = 0.1224). Two One-Sided Tests (TOST) confirm statistical equivalence across Cohen's d >= 0.25 bounds (pTOST < 0.005).
  Multivariate OLS regression controlling for artwork medium, creation era, and aspect ratio (R^2 < 0.02) confirms that artist gender has no statistically significant conditional effect (p > 0.20). High residual embedding variance (R^2 < 2%) indicates that global zero-shot valuation metrics operate near an embedding noise floor, showing that broad zero-shot prompt logit differentials are a coarse measurement instrument rather than proving absolute model fairness.
  We highlight two key caveats: (i) macro-level score equivalence reflects metric insensitivity to fine-grained visual-semantic features and does not preclude localized micro-level visual biases, and (ii) excluding 41.2% unattributed holdings reflects institutional survival bias. These results demonstrate the necessity of multivariate confound control, equivalence testing, and archival provenance auditing when assessing AI fairness in cultural heritage collections.

</details>


### [96] [Temperon: Full-Time SAM Quality at a Third Less Wall-Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17575)
*Stamatis Mastromichalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Temperon：前 43% epoch 用普通 SGD 探索，末期移交整个 cosine anneal 给 SAM 包裹的 Muon 精修，在几乎不损失精度的前提下大幅缩短训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM 每步训练代价翻倍，但其收益主要集中在训练末期。因此应研究昂贵的训练模式该花在何时。

Method: 前 43% epoch 预算使用普通 SGD 探索，然后一次计划性移交，将全部末期 cosine 退火交给 SAM 包裹的 Muon 精修器。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10/100、SVHN、Tiny ImageNet 上匹配全时 SAM 最优精度，三个数据集上最快目标提前 35%/34%/32%；消融显示 Muon 精修贡献 +0.85pp，探索器形态与重启无贡献；可迁移到 GPT-2 预训练（-29% 墙钟）与 GLUE 微调。

Conclusion: 在 cosine 调度下精度曲线呈平台后跃升，移交时点信息来自调度而非轨迹，故计划性切换是有依据的；方法存在边界（如 Tiny ImageNet 上 Muon 无增益时不如对手）。

Abstract: Sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) doubles the cost of every training step, yet its benefit concentrates where training ends. We study where an expensive training mode should be spent and propose Temperon: a plain-SGD explorer for the first 43% of the epoch budget, then one scheduled hand-off that gives the entire final cosine anneal to a SAM-wrapped Muon refiner. On CIFAR-10/100, SVHN and Tiny ImageNet (five seeds, times reported as epochs-to-target times an idle-GPU-calibrated epoch cost), Temperon matches the best full-time-SAM recipe on accuracy everywhere while reaching the hardest common target 35%, 34% and 32% sooner on three of the four, and sits a tier above the published SAM+SGD recipe at level cost. Ablations make the attribution exact: the Muon refiner is worth +0.85pp with everything else fixed; the explorer's shape and its restarts are worth nothing, and we withdraw them as contributions. Re-running the closest rival, late-phase SAM, at matched budget shows the frontier: it is fastest to every mid-level target, but the tier the Muon refiner buys (0.83 on CIFAR-100, 0.97 on CIFAR-10) is reached by no SGD-refined method in any seed, and on Tiny ImageNet, where Muon buys no tier, the rival simply wins -- the measured boundary of the method. The allocation law transfers to GPT-2 pretraining (full-SAM quality at -29% wall-clock) and GLUE fine-tuning (never worse than full-time SAM at a third of its SAM cost). Two constants organize the economics: skipping SAM early buys a fixed credit, and a Muon epoch costs 1.50x a SAM+SGD epoch on all four datasets. Finally, the hand-off cannot be timed from the trajectory: under cosine schedules the accuracy curve is plateau-then-surge, so the information lives in the schedule, making the scheduled switch principled rather than convenient. Code and a pip-installable implementation are released.

</details>


### [97] [When the Gradient Sees Rank: Provable Necessity, Causal Recruitment, and Composition in Trained Matrix Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17594)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究基于梯度的训练能否学习矩阵记忆中存储与组合关联所需的秩，通过需 rank ≥ K 的键值绑定任务加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的矩阵增强推理器任务仅存在 rank-1 解，无法回答梯度训练能否学出存储 K 个关联所需的秩这一问题。

Method: 在 K 个新键值绑定上训练矩阵记忆，其精确线性恢复需 rank(Z) ≥ K；用固定线性读出在不访问原始绑定的情况下查询单一矩阵状态；设置训练时秩上限以观察恢复突变。

Result: 有效秩随 K 增长（d=16 时 Spearman ρ=1.0）；d=8、K=4 时秩上限 3 恢复≤0.0004，秩 4 达 0.97；5 个种子中 4 个经 21 次自应用仍保持≥0.9996 恢复；算子有效秩接近 K 并逼近理想循环，预测余弦误差在 0.008 内；但矩阵维度增大且编码器宽度固定时恢复会下降。

Conclusion: 梯度训练能学到接近所需 K 的有效秩并在秩上限 K 处出现恢复突变，但随矩阵维度增大恢复仍会退化。

Abstract: Can gradient-based training learn the rank needed to store and compose associations in a matrix memory? In our earlier study, we used a matrix-augmented reasoner on a task that admits a rank-1 solution, leaving this question open. We train matrix memories on $K$ fresh key-value bindings whose exact linear recovery requires $\mathrm{rank}(Z) \geq K$. A fixed linear readout queries a single matrix state without access to the original bindings. Experiments measure recovery by cosine similarity greater than 0.9, a threshold distinct from mathematical equality. Learned effective rank increases with $K$ across the tested grid (Spearman $ρ= 1.0$ at $d = 16$). Training-time rank caps produce a recovery transition near $k = K$: at $d = 8$, $K = 4$, rank 3 gives at most 0.0004 recovery and rank 4 gives 0.97. Four of five seeds retain at least 0.9996 recovery through 21-fold self-application of the trained operator. On the entity subspace, the learned operator has effective rank close to $K$ and approximates the ideal cycle. For the single converged seed capped below $K$, a calculation using the entity-subspace operator and ideal cycle predicts the measured cosine within 0.008 through seven applications. Extending training resolves several initial failures, but recovery still declines at larger matrix dimensions with encoder width fixed.

</details>


### [98] [Prior-Free Competitive Ratios for Improving Bandits: Scale, Curvature and Horizon Are Free, but Not Jointly Under Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17595)
*Xuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 改进型多臂老虎机中，无需先验知识即可达到最优竞争比：探针-提交算法给出 Θ(√k+k/T)，无噪声时可同时适配所有 β 与时间视野，噪声下自适应代价为 Θ(√(log k/log log k))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blum 与 Ravichandran (ALT 2025) 在已知尺度 m 时得 O(√k)、未知时得 O(√k log k) 竞争比，而下界为 Ω(√k)。本文旨在消除多余的 log k 因子，并探究在未知尺度、未知凹性指数 β、未知时间视野 T 乃至未知噪声水平时是否仍能达到最优。

Method: 提出"探针-提交"算法（一页篇幅）：先随机探针估计最优臂尺度，再提交。无噪声时采用随机边缘探针算法，不读尺度 m、不读 β、不读 T，即可同步适配。有噪声时采用嵌套随机排列探针算法处理自适应代价。

Result: 探针-提交算法在 T≥2⌊√k⌋ 时达到竞争比 4√3·√k，且无需知道尺度；对每个时间视野给出最优比 Θ(√k+k/T)。无噪声时算法对每个 β 和每个视野同时达到最优 Θ(k^{β/(1+β)}+k/T)。噪声下保持 Θ(√k+k/T)，但先验代价上升：均匀自适应代价 φ_ε(k)=Θ_ε(√(log k/log log k))（k 固定 ε 时的渐近下界），仅知 m 或 β 之一则可恢复常数代价。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了改进型多臂老虎机中先验知识与竞争比之间的精确权衡：多数情形下先验并非必需，无噪声时完全不需先验；但噪声环境下，不知道尺度或凹性指数会带来不可避免的 √(log k/log log k) 级代价，而已知任一参数即可消除该代价。

Abstract: In the improving multi-armed bandits problem, each of $k$ arms has an unknown nondecreasing, discretely concave reward curve $f_i$, and pulling arm $i$ for the $t$-th time yields $f_i(t)$. For sufficiently long horizons, Blum and Ravichandran (ALT 2025) proved that randomized algorithms achieve an $O(\sqrt k)$ approximation to the best single arm when the scale $m=f^*(T)$ of the optimal arm is known ($T\ge2k$), and $O(\sqrt k\log k)$ when it is not ($T>4k$), against an $Ω(\sqrt k)$ lower bound. The logarithmic factor is unnecessary: a one-page \emph{probe-and-commit} algorithm achieves competitive ratio $4\sqrt3\,\sqrt k$ for $T\ge2\lfloor\sqrt k\rfloor$, without any knowledge of the scale, and we determine the optimal ratio for every horizon, $Θ(\sqrt k+k/T)$, also for unknown horizons. Without noise, \emph{no prior is needed at all}: a random-marginal probing algorithm reading neither the scale $m$, nor the concavity-envelope exponent $β$ of Blum, Garicano, Ravichandran and Sharma (UAI 2026), nor the horizon $T$, achieves the optimal $Θ(k^{β/(1+β)}+k/T)$ simultaneously for every $β$ and every horizon. Under the multiplicative noise model of Blum and Ravichandran, probe-and-commit keeps the same all-horizon order $Θ(\sqrt k+k/T)$ without knowing the noise level (and $Θ(\sqrt k)$ on the same range), but the price of priors jumps: for any fixed noise level $\varepsilon\in(0,1/2]$, the uniform price of adaptation $φ_\varepsilon(k)$ --- the worst case over horizons $T\ge16k$ of the loss relative to $k^{β/(1+β)}$ for algorithms knowing neither $m$ nor $β$ --- is $Θ_\varepsilon(\sqrt{\log k/\log\log k})$, the lower bound asymptotic in $k$ at fixed positive $\varepsilon$ and matched by a nested random-permutation probing algorithm, whereas knowing either $m$ or $β$ alone restores a constant price.

</details>


### [99] [Lecture notes on Physics Informed Neural Networks, Neural Operators, and their applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17638)
*Alessandro Bombini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一份博岑/博尔扎诺大学2025/2026学年"物理信息神经网络"博士课程的讲义，介绍PINN与神经算子的概念、PyTorch从零实现及NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo等库的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为博士生提供PINN与神经算子的系统性教学材料，帮助其掌握解决工程、物理、石油储层等实际问题的先进深度学习方法。

Method: 以课程讲义形式组织内容：从零用PyTorch实现PINN和神经算子，并结合成熟的领域开源库（如NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo）进行实践讲解。

Result: 涵盖Mixture-of-Models、傅里叶神经算子（FNO）、物理信息Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（PIKANs）等前沿主题。

Conclusion: 该讲义整合了PINN与神经算子的基础理论、编程实现及跨领域应用，可作为相关研究与教学参考。

Abstract: This is the set of lecture notes for the PhD course \href{https://www.unibz.it/en/faculties/engineering/phd-computer-science/study-course-offering/2025/36967}{\textit{Physics Informed Neural Network}, held at the University of Bozen/Bolzano} in the academic year 2025/2026.
  The goal of the course was to introduce the concept of Physics Informed Deep Neural Networks (PINN) and Neural Operators (NOs), discuss their implementation from scratch in PyTorch and using advanced ad-hoc developed open-source libraries such as NVIDia PhysicsNeMo to address real-world problems in various fields (engineering, physics, petroleum reservoir). We discuss recent topics such as Mixture-of-Models, Fourier Neural Operators, Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (PIKANs) and Fourier Neural Operators.

</details>


### [100] [Fathom: Per-Query Read Depth for Sparse Decoding over Offloaded KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17652)
*Vivek Kalyanarangan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Fathom，一种按查询动态决定读取每位 key 通道多少比特的 key 扫描方法，将 4-bit K cache 以位平面按通道存储，实现百万 token 场景下更快的 decode。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 百万 token 的 agentic 会话同时驻留多个 session，KV cache 及其排序索引存于主机内存，为 top-k 步骤对所有 n 个 key 排序的扫描成为 decode 的瓶颈（内存流量受限）。

Method: 4-bit K cache 按通道主序存为位平面，前 t 个平面恰好等价于该通道的 t-bit 量化器；查询根据各通道方差加权重要性的逆向注水（reverse water-filling）分配位预算，决定每个 key 通道读取多少比特。

Result: 百万 token、Qwen3-8B 上，GPU 时间比 Double Sparsity/Loki/SparQ r=32 的 136-bit 扫描快 1.67x；与 SparQ 68-bit（r=16）同 GPU 时间下少读 18% 字节，7 个设置中 6 个注意力误差更低；RULER 任务每 token 扫描与精确 top-k 一致；真实编码会话中 92 bits 达到 136-bit 扫描的步一致率。存储复用量化服务栈已有的 4-bit K 副本。

Conclusion: 在索引驻留主机内存时 Fathom 有效降低扫描流量并提速；但若索引常驻 GPU 显存则不带来加速。

Abstract: When agentic sessions run to a million tokens with many sessions resident at once, the KV cache and the index that ranks it live in host memory, and the scan that ranks all n keys for a top-k step becomes the traffic that bounds decoding. We present Fathom, a key scan in which each query decides how many bits of each key channel to read. The 4-bit K cache is stored channel-major as bit planes, so a prefix of t planes is exactly the channel's t-bit quantizer, and the query spends its bit budget by reverse water-filling over the variance-weighted importance of its channels. At one million tokens on Qwen3-8B a decode step is 1.67x faster in GPU time than with the 136-bit scans of Double Sparsity, Loki and SparQ r=32, and in the same GPU time as SparQ's 68-bit read (r=16) Fathom reads 18% fewer bytes with lower attention error on six of seven model and context settings. On RULER-style tasks every per-token scan matches exact top-k decoding, and on real coding-agent sessions Fathom reaches the step agreement of the most accurate 136-bit scan at 92 bits. The store is the 4-bit K copy a quantized serving stack already holds, and the method is not faster when the index is resident in GPU memory.

</details>


### [101] [Reflect, Revise, Reuse: Training-Free Skill Evolution for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17653)
*Bofan Chen,Boxuan Zhang,Fei Tang,Zhengxi Lu,Yong Du,Tongbo Chen,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvoSkill-GUI：免训练框架，将技能视为可在部署时通过执行反馈修订的活知识，采用reflect-revise-reuse循环提升GUI智能体表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI智能体需在动态界面执行长程任务，弹窗、延迟加载、控件移位常使执行前固定的计划失效。现有技能框架未针对执行动态设计，且将技能视为部署前产生的静态产物，而非可在部署中改进的活知识。

Method: 免训练框架，每个技能是结构化多文件包，含检索元数据、可执行计划、备份定位、失败恢复规则、无障碍工具和失败案例。通过reflect-revise-reuse循环运行：执行器在rollout内即时修订，隔离的critic在严格信息隔离下诊断失败轨迹，执行器通过受限工具接口编辑特定技能文件。

Result: 在MobileWorld、AndroidWorld、OSWorld三个主流GUI基准上，无需训练即持续提升多个基座模型，最大增益分别为+16.2%、+6.0%、+10.5%，且进化后的技能库能持续受益于相关任务而非从零重建。

Conclusion: GUI智能体需要的不是更好的静态技能，而是能在部署时从执行反馈中修订、无需额外训练的技能，EvoSkill-GUI验证了该思路的有效性与可迁移性。

Abstract: GUI agents execute long-horizon tasks on dynamic graphical user interfaces, where pop-ups, delayed loads, and relocated widgets routinely invalidate plans fixed before execution. Recent agent-skill frameworks encapsulate reusable procedural knowledge to mitigate this, yet existing skill designs are largely developed without targeting GUI execution dynamics and treat skills as static artifacts produced before deployment rather than living procedural knowledge that improves through it. We argue that what GUI agents need is not better static skills, but skills that can be revised from execution feedback at deployment time, without additional training. We propose \textbf{EvoSkill-GUI}, a training-free framework in which each skill is a structured multi-file package containing retrieval metadata, executable plans, backup localization, failure-recovery rules, accessibility utilities, and failure cases. EvoSkill-GUI operates through a \textbf{\emph{reflect-revise-reuse}} loop: the executor performs instant in-rollout revisions, an isolated critic diagnoses failed trajectories under strict information isolation, and the executor edits specific skill files through a restricted tool interface. Across MobileWorld, AndroidWorld, and OSWorld, three mainstream GUI benchmarks spanning mobile and desktop platforms, EvoSkill-GUI consistently improves multiple base models without any training, with maximum gains of $+16.2\%$, $+6.0\%$, and $+10.5\%$ respectively, and evolved skill libraries continue to benefit related tasks rather than being rebuilt from scratch. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/EvoSkill-GUI.

</details>


### [102] [Regularized Least Squares Training of Quadratic Neural Networks with Applications to System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17654)
*Luis Rodrigues,Zachary Yetman Van Egmond,Mohammad R. Amiri Fard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种带正则化的二次神经网络最小二乘训练方法，可得到训练优化问题解的下界、近似解的闭式表达式及其对数据误差的灵敏度，并在非线性系统辨识中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代数值方法（如反向传播）计算耗时长且易陷入局部极小值，因此需要一种能给出闭式解、降低计算成本并具有理论保证的训练方法。

Method: 针对带正则化的二次神经网络，采用最小二乘方法进行训练。当正则化系数为正时，给出训练优化问题解的下界；同时给出近似解及其灵敏度的闭式表达式。当正则化系数为零时，该下界是紧的，近似解即为最优解。此外，建立该下界优化与核范数最小化之间的联系。

Result: 获得三项主要成果：(i) 权重的解析表达式；(ii) 权重对数据误差灵敏度的解析表达式；(iii) 下界优化与核范数最小化的联系。在非线性系统辨识示例中成功应用，并将所提下界与最优值进行比较。

Conclusion: 所提最小二乘训练方法能为带正则化的二次神经网络提供闭式权重与灵敏度表达，显著降低计算时间，避免迭代方法陷入局部极小值，并通过系统辨识实验验证了有效性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a least squares approach for the training of quadratic neural networks with regularization. The proposed methodology yields a lower bound on the solution of the training optimization problem for the case where the regularization coefficient is positive. Moreover, it yields closed-form expressions for the approximate solution and its sensitivity The lower bound is tight and the approximate solution is the optimal solution when the regularization coefficient is zero. Having a closed-form expression for the weights reduces considerably the computational time when compared with iterative numerical methods such as backpropagation that can get stuck in local minima. The proposed approach has three main contributions, namely, (i) it yields an analytical expression for the weights, (ii) an analytical expression for the sensitivity of the weights to errors in the data is also provided, (iii) it establishes a connection between the optimization to compute a lower bound and nuclear norm minimization. The proposed least squares training is successfully applied to a nonlinear system identification example where the proposed lower bound is compared with the optimal value.

</details>


### [103] [DSD: Learning Diverse and Reusable Motor Skills via Diffusion Skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17682)
*Sun Woo Kim,Xue Bin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Diffusion Skill Discovery (DSD)，用扩散模型通过score matching近似策略诱导状态分布的熵梯度，从而在高维人形控制中学习多样且可复用的运动技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟角色要高效完成新任务，需要复用多样化、可复用的运动技能库。常用方法通过最大化技能隐变量与状态间的互信息来学技能，但直接估计高维控制下的边际状态熵不可行，现有方法依赖间接隐空间近似或粗糙状态分布估计，导致技能多样性不足、下游任务效用低。

Method: DSD使用扩散模型通过score matching近似策略诱导状态分布的熵梯度，据此构造目标函数，鼓励发现行为覆盖更广的技能。

Result: 技能被复用于两种下游设置：带任务特定高层策略的层级控制，以及通过离线轨迹隐选择实现的零样本控制。实验表明DSD比已有技能发现方法学到更广泛的可复用运动技能库，并涌现出可跨下游任务复用的复杂、敏捷行为。

Conclusion: 用扩散模型估计状态分布熵梯度，可有效促进高维控制下的多样化技能发现，提升技能库的复用性与下游任务表现。

Abstract: Humans efficiently learn new tasks by reusing a rich repertoire of motor skills across different goals and contexts. A similar strategy can also be used to enable simulated characters to efficiently perform new tasks by leveraging reusable motor skills. To support a wide range of downstream tasks, the learned repertoire should be diverse, consisting of distinct behaviors as well as spatial and temporal variation within each behavior. A commonly used method for learning diverse skills is by maximizing the mutual information between skill latents and the states produced by a policy. The marginal state entropy promotes broad behavioral coverage, while the conditional entropy encourages consistent behaviors from each latent. However, directly estimating the marginal state entropy is intractable in high-dimensional control problems. Prior methods therefore rely on indirect latent-space approximations or coarse estimators of the state distribution. These approximations may not effectively promote broad coverage of the state space, resulting in skills with limited behavioral diversity and reduced utility for downstream tasks. In this work, we propose Diffusion Skill Discovery (DSD), a skill discovery method that uses a diffusion model to approximate the entropy gradient of the policy-induced state distribution through score matching. The resulting objective encourages the discovery of skills that produce a broader range of behaviors for high-dimensional humanoid control. The learned skills are reused in two downstream control settings: hierarchical control with a task-specific high-level policy and zero-shot control through latent selection from offline trajectories. Our experiments show that DSD discovers a broader repertoire of reusable motor skills than prior skill discovery methods, leading to the emergence of complex and agile behaviors that can be reused across downstream tasks.

</details>


### [104] [The Missing "I Don't Know": Why Three Reasoning-Reliability Findings Converge on Calibrated Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17686)
*Srijith Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 三项看似无关的LLM可靠性研究（工具表示坍缩、安全生成中的改写/截断、无"我不知道"函数必致幻觉）共同指向同一缺失能力：校准弃答，而现有基准对该能力零奖励，需改革评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三篇近期论文分别记录了LLM的可靠性缺陷：推理RL破坏工具可靠性表示、安全约束生成下大小模型分别改写或截断被标记片段、以及任何无隐式"我不知道"功能的一致推理系统必然在广泛问题上无限次幻觉。作者希望说明这些表面无关的问题实则汇聚于同一根因，并指出评测体系的结构性缺陷阻碍其解决。

Method: 本文为立场/分析性论文，通过概念综合比较三项结果，辨析其"能力缺口、策略缺口、递归论缺口"三种不同来源，论证三者共同指向校准弃答（calibrated abstention）这一缺失能力，并据此提出评测改革方案。

Result: 三项发现均独立地将校准弃答识别为缺失能力，但成因各异。诚实性后训练已缩小部署模型中的差距，然而Bastounis所指的缺口无法仅靠此闭合：主流基准对"拒绝回答"给予零奖励，导致排行榜梯度不存在，无法选择出校准弃答函数。

Conclusion: 提出四项评测改革：三重打分、弃答率报告、能力分层评测、强制性校准指标。基准改革是闭合该定理所揭示缺口的必要条件，但并非充分条件。

Abstract: Three recent results describe what look like unrelated LLM reliability problems. Yin et al. (2026) show reasoning RL collapses tool-reliability representations. Suleymanov et al. (2026) show that under safety-constrained generation, large models rewrite flagged spans while small models truncate. Bastounis et al. (2024) prove any consistent-reasoning system without an implicit "I don't know" function must hallucinate infinitely often on broad problem classes. We argue these findings converge on a single intervention: calibrated abstention is what each independently identifies as the missing capability, even though the unavailability they document, a capability gap, a policy gap, and a recursion-theoretic gap, has a different source in each case. Honesty post-training has narrowed the gap in deployed models, but principled closure of the class Bastounis identifies requires a calibrated abstention function whose training signal at the leaderboard level is absent: dominant benchmarks assign zero reward to decline, so the leaderboard gradient that would select for the function does not exist. We propose four changes to evaluation: triple-scoring, abstention-rate reporting, capability-stratified evaluation, and mandatory calibration metrics. Benchmark reform is necessary, not sufficient, for closing the gap the theorem identifies.

</details>


### [105] [Accelerating Diffusion Sampling via Speculative Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17691)
*Marcello Bullo,Yanxiao Liu,Öykü Sıla Güner,Arpan Mukherjee,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将扩散模型的投机采样与相对熵编码(REC)建立联系，指出前瞻草稿不必是线性的，由此提出"草稿树"(draft trees)，并采用贪心拒绝采样作为草稿-目标耦合，实现精确目标分布采样并加速生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散采样器（如基于反射最大耦合的方法）存在拓扑约束：其前瞻草稿形成链式图（单一线性序列），本质上限制了每次目标评估的接受率。作者希望通过打破线性结构来提升接受率、减少昂贵的目标函数评估。

Method: 1) 将扩散模型中的投机采样与相对熵编码(REC)建立理论联系，证明前瞻草稿无需线性；2) 提出核心贡献"草稿树"(draft trees)，每轮丰富候选状态以降低目标函数评估数；3) 采用贪心拒绝采样（一种REC算法）作为草稿-目标耦合，在保证精确目标样本的同时提升接受率。

Result: 在不同目标模型与草稿模型的实验上，相比反射耦合基线，在实际设置中实现了最高达8.3%的加速。

Conclusion: 通过REC视角重新审视扩散投机采样，草稿树与贪心拒绝采样耦合打破了传统线性链式结构限制，提升了接受率并减少目标评估，在保持精确采样的前提下有效加速扩散模型生成。

Abstract: Speculative sampling accelerates diffusion model generation by drafting inexpensive candidate states and correcting them under a coupling that preserves the target distribution exactly, reducing the number of expensive target evaluations. Existing diffusion samplers, notably those based on reflection maximal coupling, are topologically constrained: their lookahead drafts form a chain graph, a single linear sequence, which inherently limits the acceptance rate per target evaluation. We connect speculative sampling in diffusion models to relative entropy coding (REC). This perspective shows the lookahead need not be linear and motivates our central contribution, draft trees, which enrich the candidates considered per round and lower the target function evaluations. We further adopt greedy rejection sampling, an REC algorithm, as the draft-target coupling, improving acceptance while guaranteeing exact target samples. Experiments across diverse target and draft models demonstrate up to 8.3% acceleration over the reflection coupling baseline in practical settings.

</details>


### [106] [TabPFN-3.5: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17895)
*Benjamin Jäger,Nick Erickson,Léo Grinsztajn,Felix Birkel,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Kürşat Kaya,Jonas Kübler,Adèle Frankel,Tobias Schröder,Anurag Garg,Jan Hendrik Metzen,David Salinas,Simon Bing,Kristina Collins,Tuana Çelik,Vahid Balazadeh,Lydia Sidhoum,Tomás Pereda,Brendan Roof,Andrej Tschalzev,Siyuan Guo,Philipp Singer,Lennart Purucker,Jake Robertson,Marie Salmon,Philipp Jund,Jerry Chen,Diana Kriuchkova,Arthur Cahu,Eliott Kalfon,Adrian Hayler,Georg Grab,Vitor Monteiro,Lilly Wehrhahn,Dominik Safaric,Clara Cornu,Alan Arazi,Rylee Grace,Simone Alessi,Mihir Manium,Bernhard Schölkopf,Yann LeCun,Madelon Hulsebos,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 发布TabPFN-3.5旗舰表格基础模型，在标准与真实场景表格预测上刷新SOTA，并提供快速、多模态与Thinking变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型在标准基准上表现有限，且难以应对实践中的非i.i.d.数据、混合模态、高基数类别与宽表等复杂情形，需要更强的通用模型。

Method: 升级基础模型至TabPFN-3.5，扩展至时序/分组切分、字符串文本与图像、高基数特征和宽表；推出TabPFN-3.5-Fast加速推理、TabPFN-3.5-Plus增强多模态与推理优化、TabPFN-3.5-Thinking扩展推理时计算。

Result: 在TabArena等标准基准上取得SOTA；在关系数据与时间序列预测任务上也达SOTA；Fast版本比TabPFN-3快3倍且保留大部分精度；Thinking版本比TabPFN-3-Thinking快达12倍并进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3.5显著超越前代与现有基线，覆盖更真实多样的表格场景，并通过Fast/Plus/Thinking变体兼顾速度、多模态与推理时扩展能力。

Abstract: We introduce TabPFN-3.5, our new flagship Tabular Foundation Model. It significantly outperforms its predecessor, TabPFN-3, and all existing baselines across a broad range of tabular problems. TabPFN-3.5 sets a new state of the art on standard tabular prediction in TabArena, and extends it to the data practitioners encounter in practice: non-i.i.d. data with temporal or grouped splits, tables with strings, text and images, high-cardinality categorical features, and wide tables with many features. These gains carry over to our task-specific harnesses: state of the art on relational data and stronger time-series forecasting. For faster inference, our variant TabPFN-3.5-Fast runs up to 3x faster than TabPFN-3 while keeping most of the accuracy gains. In addition, we upgrade TabPFN-3.5-Plus, expanding our multimodal capabilities with advanced text and date handling alongside proprietary inference optimizations. Finally, we release a new version of our Thinking mode, TabPFN-3.5-Thinking, which scales inference-time computation to push the state of the art further. It benefits from our stronger base model and from inference-time improvements that make it up to 12x faster than TabPFN-3-Thinking.

</details>


### [107] [Composite-Gradient Learning for Shared Control Authority Between Deep Reinforcement Learning and Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17697)
*Giray Önür,Azita Dabiri,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出复合梯度学习(CGL)方法，将MPC控制器显式纳入DRL训练过程，把DRL与MPC的控制输入视为联合动作并考虑二者的交互作用，在多类高速公路交通网络上验证其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享控制权的DRL-MPC框架中，DRL智能体与MPC各自决定部分控制输入。但常见的学习公式将MPC视为环境的一部分，未显式考虑MPC对控制的贡献及其与DRL智能体的交互，从而限制了学习效果。

Method: 提出复合梯度学习(CGL)：将DRL与MPC的控制输入表示为联合动作，并在训练更新DRL智能体时考虑二者的交互作用，从而将MPC控制器集成到学习过程中。

Result: 在两个具有不同交互强度的多类高速公路交通网络上评估CGL，并与将MPC视为环境一部分或仅部分纳入MPC的替代方法比较。结果显示：弱交互下CGL收益有限；强交互下在部分训练运行中能学到性能更高的控制策略，但平均控制性能提升仍较为有限。

Conclusion: 显式建模MPC与DRL的交互在某些条件下可提升控制策略性能，尤其在强交互场景的部分运行中效果更好，但整体收益并不显著，CGL的适用性依赖于DRL与MPC之间的交互强度。

Abstract: Integrated deep reinforcement learning (DRL) and model predictive control (MPC) methods are increasingly used to control autonomous systems by combining their complementary capabilities. DRL learns control policies through interaction with the environment. MPC uses a system model to optimize control inputs while accounting for constraints. In DRL-MPC frameworks with shared control authority, both the DRL agent and the MPC controller each determine part of the control inputs. However, common learning formulations treat MPC as part of the environment and therefore do not explicitly account for MPC's contribution to control or its interaction with the DRL agent. This paper proposes a novel composite-gradient learning (CGL) method that integrates the MPC controller into the learning process by representing the DRL and MPC control inputs as a joint action and accounting for their interaction when updating the DRL agent during training. CGL is evaluated on two multi-class freeway traffic networks with different strengths of interaction between the DRL and MPC control inputs and it is compared with alternative methods that treat MPC as part of the environment or that only partially incorporate MPC into learning. The results show that CGL offers limited benefit under weak interaction, but learns higher-performing control policies than the alternative methods in a subset of training runs under strong interaction, although the average control-performance gains remain modest.

</details>


### [108] [Symmetry without a manifold: intrinsic dimension on orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17926)
*Chon-Fai Kam,Miloud Bessafi,Frédéric Cadet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在有限等距群轨道上，标准神经标度指数估计器失效，报告的是探测分辨率而非维度；损失的幂律被隐藏宽度的指数律取代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准几何推导以数据流形的内在维度为输入，但在 Z_p 模加法上该推导没有输入：精确代数解是 Z_p 的等距作用轨道，传递性使维度估计器所依赖的比值统计量成为点质量，估计器无定义（两个最近邻距离恰好相等）。作者旨在揭示这一失效的普遍性并给出替代描述。

Method: 分析 Z_p 模加法中 Z_p 等距作用轨道的最近邻距离比统计量；在尺度 ε 处破缺对称性；将失效推广到任意有限等距群作用轨道；拟合损失随隐藏宽度的曲线，比较指数律 L(h)=L_∞+A exp(-c h^α) 与带相同下限的幂律，并测试正则化（权重衰减、群阶）与 α 对速率 c 的影响。

Result: 估计器在任意有限等距群轨道上报告的是探测分辨率而非维度；指数律拟合 R² 为 0.982–0.995，优于幂律的 0.857–0.906；权重衰减使 c 变化 47 倍，而群阶仅使其变化 1.10（低于种子间分辨率）；临界宽度随群阶下降而非上升，与按不可约表示分配固定神经元的容量计数相矛盾。

Conclusion: 在有限等距群轨道上维度估计本质上无定义，标度行为由隐藏宽度的指数律而非幂律描述；数据充足时速率由正则化器而非群结构决定，且临界宽度随群阶下降，挑战了固定神经元分配的传统容量观点。

Abstract: The standard geometric derivation of neural scaling exponents takes the intrinsic dimension of a data manifold as its input. On modular addition in $\mathbb{Z}_p$ that derivation has no input. The exact algebraic solution is an orbit of $\mathbb{Z}_p$ acting by isometries. Transitivity alone makes the ratio statistic underlying the standard dimension estimator a point mass, so the estimator is undefined, and here the two nearest neighbour distances coincide exactly. Breaking the symmetry at scale $ε$ returns a number, but one that tracks $1/ε$ with no scale free plateau. We show that the failure is general, since on any finite orbit of a group acting by isometries the estimator reports the resolution at which the set is probed rather than a dimension. What replaces the power law is exponential in hidden width, $L(h)=L_\infty+A\exp(-c\,h^α)$, with $R^2$ between 0.982 and 0.995 against 0.857 to 0.906 for a power law admitting the same floor and fitted under the same protocol. Where the data supply is sufficient the rate belongs to the regulariser rather than to the group, since weight decay moves $c$ by a factor of 47 while group order moves it by 1.10, a residual below seed to seed resolution, for every fixed $α$ between 0.75 and 2. The critical width falls with group order rather than rising, against capacity counting that assigns a fixed number of neurons to each irreducible representation.

</details>


### [109] [REVERSAL-BENCH: A Reversibility Axis and Reset Oracle for Measuring the Reset-Free RL Cliff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17745)
*Riyaaz Shaik,Chandru Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出REVERSAL-BENCH基准，通过可控可逆性参数ρ揭示无重置强化学习在不可逆操作任务中的致命"可逆性悬崖"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主RL的核心目标是无需外部重置的连续策略训练，但现有范式大多依赖环境可逆性。现实操作任务（如推物体下桌、洒颗粒物）不可逆，缺少对此类场景的系统评估。

Method: 构建REVERSAL-BENCH，用连续参数ρ∈[0,1]控制可逆性，提供重置oracle与真实状态可恢复性验证机制，覆盖5个物理引擎、8种操作设置。评测多种策略架构（标准actor-critic、safe RL、专门的无重置框架），并与几何相同的可逆任务对照，还评估了安全shield干预机制。

Result: 发现明显的"可逆性悬崖"：随着ρ增大，无重置智能体持续被吸收到不可恢复状态，学习停滞；而情节式智能体保持稳定学习。对照组证明该崩溃由不可逆性而非障碍复杂度因果导致。安全shield能准确预测可恢复性，但主动恢复仅在智能体能物理避开陷阱时成功。

Conclusion: 无重置RL在不可逆环境中存在根本性吸收失效问题。作者发布基准套件、带可恢复性标注的多模拟器数据集及重置oracle，指出需要能避免或逃逸不可逆陷阱的新方法。

Abstract: A central goal of autonomous reinforcement learning is continuous policy training without external resets. However, existing paradigms largely depend on underlying environmental reversibility, a property absent in real world manipulation, where events such as pushing objects off tables or spilling granular substances cannot be undone. We introduce REVERSAL-BENCH, a benchmark that controls reversibility via a continuous parameter $ρ\in [0, 1]$ and provides a reset oracle, a ground-truth verification mechanism to test state recoverability across eight manipulation settings in five physics engines. Evaluating a broad spectrum of policy architectures, including standard actor-critic algorithms, safe RL, and specialized reset-free frameworks, reveals a sharp reversibility cliff: reset-free agents are consistently absorbed into irrecoverable states as $ρ$ increases, whereas episodic agents maintain steady learning. We see this failure mode across autonomous reset-free baselines and constrained RL.
