Because reset-free agents lack external resets, any transition into an irrecoverable state results in permanent absorption, leaving the agent trapped where further learning halts. We show that this absorption phenomenon persists in full physics simulations under learned manipulation policies. By evaluating against geometrically identical reversible counterparts, we confirm that this breakdown is causally driven by irreversibility rather than obstacle complexity. We release the benchmark suite, a large multi-simulator dataset labeled with recoverability and a reset oracle. We also evaluate a safety shield that intervenes before irreversible failures occur, showing that while recoverability can be predicted accurately, active recovery primarily succeeds only when the agent can physically steer clear of the trap

</details>


### [110] [On the Identifiability of Mixed Ordinal and Exponential Family Causal DAGs under Linear Parametric Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17942)
*Sambit Mishra,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究线性参数模型中，有序logit节点与单参数指数族节点之间边的方向可识别性，证明在充分条件下方向可由联合分布唯一确定，并给出必要性反面结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结构方程模型及同质分布族的可识别性结果无法覆盖混合类型节点（有序变量与指数族变量）的线性参数模型，需明确这类边的方向能否由联合分布识别。

Method: 在节点服从有序logit模型或正则单参数指数族的线性参数模型中，从联合分布出发分析边的方向可识别性，并给出充要条件的正面与反面证明。

Result: 若有序节点至少3个类别、指数族节点至少3个支撑点，则任意连接二者的边在每个参数值下方向均可由联合分布识别，且对充分统计量无额外限制；反之，三类别要求仅在仿射充分统计量时必要，三支撑点要求仅在典范链接下必要；该结论可扩展到d节点无向骨架中所有此类混合边。

Conclusion: 混合有序-指数族的边方向具有可识别性，数值实验也成功区分了仅凭条件独立性无法区分的马尔可夫等价类内方向，验证了理论结果。

Abstract: The problem of identifiability in linear parametric models (LPMs) whose nodes follow either an ordered logit model or a regular one-parameter exponential family is evaluated. The results go beyond classical structural equation models as well as results for nodes with observations from a homogeneous family of distributions. The main result establishes that the orientation of every edge joining an ordinal node to an exponential-family node is identifiable from the joint distribution alone at every parameter value, provided the ordinal node has at least three categories and the exponential-family node at least three points of support, with no restriction on the sufficient statistic. Converses show that both requirements are necessary: the three-category requirement is binding only for affine sufficient statistics, and the three-point requirement is binding under the canonical link. The guarantee extends to orienting every such mixed ordinal-exponential family edge of a given $d$-node undirected skeleton. Numerical experiments illustrate the theoretical results by successfully separating orientations within a Markov equivalence class, which are indistinguishable by conditional independence alone.

</details>


### [111] [SAM-on-the-Curve: Sharpness-Aware Mode Connectivity for Robust Weight-Space Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17748)
*Alejandro Calatrava,Xu Zhang,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出模式连通性（MC）仅约束一维轨迹上的损失，忽视邻域，导致曲线脆弱。提出 Sharp Mode Connectivity（SMC），将MC重构为邻域鲁棒的路径优化，使整条曲线邻域保持平坦低损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MC作为低损失连通性只控制一维路径上的损失，未约束周围权重空间邻域，优化的曲线可能穿过尖锐脊，在分布偏移下脆弱。因此需将MC重构为邻域鲁棒的路径优化问题。

Method: 提出SMC，对极小极大泛函应用一阶锐度感知近似，要求整条曲线而非仅曲线本身平坦，并推导该锐度感知目标下连通路径的实际优化算法。

Result: 在CIFAR-10-C严重模糊损坏下，SMC比标准MC绝对精度提升达6.09%。SMC产生负损失壁垒，路径内点模型可优于端点平均损失；在ResNet-18、VGG16-BN、ViT-Tiny及CIFAR-10、ImageNet-100上验证有效。

Conclusion: 路径级平坦性可作为鲁棒权重空间插值的实用原则，SMC证明邻域鲁棒性比单纯低损失连通性更有效。

Abstract: Deep neural networks that are independently trained to similar performance can be connected by low-loss parametric curves in weight space, a phenomenon known as Mode Connectivity (MC). This geometric property underpins practical techniques such as weight averaging, model ensembling, and model merging. We argue that low-loss connectivity is an incomplete geometric criterion: it controls loss only along a one-dimensional trajectory while leaving the surrounding weight-space neighborhood unconstrained, so the optimized curve may traverse sharp ridges that become fragile under distribution shift. We therefore reformulate mode connectivity as a neighborhood-robust path optimization problem, seeking a curve whose entire local neighborhood maintains low loss. We propose Sharp Mode Connectivity (SMC), which applies a first-order sharpness-aware approximation to the resulting minimax functional, enforcing flatness along the entire curve rather than only on it. We derive a practical optimization algorithm for connectivity paths under this sharpness-aware objective. Under severe blur corruptions from CIFAR-10-C, SMC achieves up to 6.09\% absolute accuracy improvement over standard MC. Remarkably, SMC produces negative loss barriers, meaning that models obtained at interior points of the optimized path can outperform the average endpoint loss. These results, validated across ResNet-18, VGG16-BN, and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-100, establish path-wise flatness as a practical principle for robust weight-space interpolation.

</details>


### [112] [Modular Deep Learning Mechanisms for Auditable Next-Day Wildfire Spread Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17763)
*Miguel Esparza,Aydin Ayanzadeh Ahmad Mousavi,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究为次日活跃火预测提出三种模块化增强（风/坡注意力偏置、物理特征检索增强输出校正、火条件双流门控），在Next Day Wildfire Spread基准上跨五种骨干网络进行消融与审计评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次日野火预测需模型预测可与其计算所用假设及历史证据一同检验；仅凭深度学习从遥感数据学到的空间模式与预测性能，无法保证物理保真度与业务可信度。

Method: 提出三种模块化增强：风向坡向条件注意力偏置（显式规定方向偏好）、基于九维环境与火状态描述子检索历史图块并对冻结模型logits做学习式校正的检索增强输出模块、火条件双流门控；在五种骨干网络上使用分阶段消融、方向审计、检索扰动、校准度量与计算量对比进行评估。

Result: 三增强的SwinUNETR三种子平均F1为0.4216、AUC-PR为0.3673；混合集成（两个增强架构+一个非增强架构）达0.4292与0.3790。增益因架构而异，检索带来的AUC-PR提升未稳定转化为F1提升；所构造风偏置与输入风高度一致，但与观测到的次日火位移一致性明显弱得多，区分了先验可检验性与预测物理保真度。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个暴露并评估野火预测模型中选定领域知识组件的框架，表明预测性能、业务可信度与计算实用性并非相互竞争的目标。

Abstract: Next-day wildfire prediction requires models whose forecasts can be evaluated alongside the assumptions and historical evidence used in their computation. Although deep learning can learn spatial patterns from remote-sensing data, predictive performance alone does not establish physical fidelity or operational trustworthiness. This study investigates three modular augmentations for next-day active-fire prediction: wind- and slope-conditioned attention biases, physics-feature retrieval-augmented output correction, and fire conditioned dual-stream gating. The attention biases expose prescribed directional preferences, while the retrieval module selects historical tiles using a nine-dimensional environmental and fire-state descriptor and applies a learned correction to a frozen model's logits. The modules are evaluated across five backbones on the Next Day Wildfire Spread benchmark, using staged ablations, directional audits, retrieval perturbations, calibration measures, and computational comparisons. The three-seed mean F1 score and area under the precision--recall curve (AUC-PR) of a SwinUNETR model with all three augmentations are 0.4216 and 0.3673. Then, a mixed ensemble (two augmented architectures and one non-augmented architecture) model achieves 0.4292 and 0.3790. Benefits vary across architectures, and retrieval-related improvements in AUC-PR do not consistently translate into higher F1. The constructed wind bias aligns closely with input wind, but its alignment with observed next-day fire displacement is much weaker, distinguishing prior inspectability from predictive physical fidelity. The study contributes a framework for exposing and evaluating selected domain-informed components within wildfire prediction models. Together, the results presented show that predictive performance, operational trustworthiness, and computational practicality need not be competing objectives.

</details>


### [113] [Beyond Quadratic Loss: The Stability Phase Diagram of Adam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18314)
*Gaoxiang Tang,Huanran Chen,Ziming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究Adam优化器在(β1,β2)平面上损失尖峰的产生机制，揭示动量时间尺度失配与有限尺度超二次损失几何之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 损失尖峰是神经网络训练中的反复出现的不稳定性，对Adam而言其与优化器动力学有关，但两个动量时间尺度(β1,β2)如何决定尖峰尚不清楚。

Method: 在(β1,β2)平面上映射训练动力学；分析一维二次损失和超二次损失L(x)∝|x|^n；研究置信交叉熵损失形成的"核心-墙"损失景观。

Result: 发现近似线性边界1-β2=C(1-β1)区分尖峰与非尖峰动力学；一维二次损失给出近似三次斜率；超二次损失恢复近线性缩放并联系边界系数与有效损失指数n；交叉熵损失具有窄二次核心与陡墙结构，在优化器更新尺度上产生有效超二次行为。

Conclusion: Adam损失尖峰源于动量时间尺度失配与超出Hessian的有限尺度超二次损失几何的共同作用。

Abstract: Loss spikes are recurrent instabilities in neural-network training and can arise from multiple mechanisms. For Adam in particular, macroscopic loss spikes have been linked to optimizer dynamics, yet how its two momentum timescales govern them remains unclear. We investigate this dependence by mapping training dynamics across the $(β_1,β_2)$ plane. Across a range of model--task settings, an approximately linear boundary, $1-β_2=C(1-β_1)$, separates spiky from non-spiky dynamics, whereas a one-dimensional quadratic loss produces approximately cubic slope. A one-dimensional superquadratic loss $L(x)\propto|x|^n$ recovers the near-linear scaling and links the boundary coefficient to the effective loss exponent $n$. We further show that confident cross-entropy losses develop a core--wall landscape comprising a narrow quadratic core followed by a steep wall, which produces effective superquadratic behavior at the scale of an optimizer update. Together, these results connect Adam loss spikes to both the mismatch between momentum timescales and finite-scale superquadratic loss geometry beyond the Hessian.

</details>


### [114] [Principled Koopman Representations with Kalman Inference for Efficient Time-Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17815)
*Ruiquan Li,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出K²SVD，通过优化Hilbert-Schmidt目标显式学习Koopman算子的主奇异函数，构建有效低秩Koopman空间，实现高效准确的时间序列预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用神经网络学习潜在Koopman空间的方法常无法构建有效的Koopman空间：表示与算子理论公式在数学上不一致，且未能捕捉系统动态的内在低秩结构，导致预测受限。

Method: K²SVD显式学习Koopman算子的主导奇异函数，通过优化Hilbert-Schmidt目标得到定义良好的低秩近似，具有可解释的线性组合与紧凑潜在空间（维度不到先前工作的10%）。在该Koopman空间中，用线性高斯状态空间模型刻画时间演化，并用Kalman滤波进行推理，缓解多步预测中的噪声累积。

Result: K²SVD在多个数据集上超越最先进方法，预测速度显著更快，计算成本低于此前专注效率的模型。

Conclusion: 结果体现了有原则的低秩Koopman表示的优势，为更广泛的应用开辟了潜力。

Abstract: The Koopman operator has been widely used for time-series prediction in dynamical systems. However, prior work that learns latent ``Koopman spaces'' using neural networks often did not construct a valid Koopman space for forecasting, as these representations may be mathematically inconsistent with the operator-theoretic formulation and fail to capture the intrinsic low-rank structure of system dynamics. To address this issue, we introduce K$^2$SVD, a method that explicitly learns the leading singular functions of the Koopman operator by optimizing a Hilbert-Schmidt objective. This yields a well-defined low-rank approximation of the Koopman operator with an interpretable linear combination, featuring a compact latent space with less than $10\%$ of the dimensions used in previous work. In the learned Koopman space, K$^2$SVD further captures temporal evolution with a linear Gaussian state-space model and performs inference via Kalman filtering, mitigating noise accumulation during multi-step prediction. Empirical results show that K$^2$SVD outperforms state-of-the-art methods across multiple datasets, with significantly faster prediction speeds and lower computational cost than previous efficiency-focused models. This highlights the benefits of principled low-rank Koopman representations and opens up broader potential for applications.

</details>


### [115] [The Free Inference Dimension: Complexity Measure for Zero-Collision Navigation under Hypothesis Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17816)
*Luiz Carlos Castro Guedes,Edward Hermann Haeusler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限元强化学习中嵌套约束族下 value-mixture (VM) 智能体的 "Free Inference" 现象，提出 Free Inference 维度 dFI 刻画其可处理的环境复杂度，并给出泛化界与最优混合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Solomonoff 归纳将预测视为可计算假设的混合，通常导向对真实环境的识别。作者此前工作观察到不同机制：VM 智能体无需识别真实环境即可实现近优、零碰撞导航，且该现象在密度阈值后失效，需转向 posterior-mode selection。本文旨在形式化并解释这一现象。

Method: 定义 Free Inference 维度 dFI(S,N)，作为 VM 智能体保持轨迹连贯性时能处理的环境复杂度的组合度量；证明 dFI 严格小于 VC 维，并与 Natarajan 维通过路径长度因子相关联（刻画非可分解损失的代价）；引入 PAC 式松弛得到由 dFI^(epsilon,delta) 驱动的泛化界；定义互补的 PMS 识别维度，提出"先平均、首次碰撞后切换到选择"的混合策略，并与 Littlestone 型维度建立联系。

Result: 证明 dFI 严格小于 VC 维；dFI 与 Natarajan 维在路径长度因子内相关；获得基于 dFI^(epsilon,delta) 的 PAC 式泛化界；证明混合策略（平均直到首次碰撞再选模）最优；通过 grid-world 实验支持与 Littlestone 型维度的联系。

Conclusion: Free Inference 是一种可被维度理论刻画的机制：VM 智能体在一定环境复杂度阈值内无需识别真实环境即可近优导航。dFI 及 PMS 识别维度为理解混合与选择策略的权衡提供了理论框架，最优策略是先混合、首次碰撞后转选择。

Abstract: Solomonoff induction frames prediction as a mixture over computable hypotheses, typically leading to identification of the true environment. In a finite meta-reinforcement learning setting with nested constraint families, in our previous work, we observe a different regime: a value-mixture (VM) agent achieves near-optimal, zero-collision navigation without identifying the true environment, a phenomenon we call Free Inference. This regime persists up to a sharp density threshold, beyond which performance degrades and posterior-mode selection (PMS) becomes preferable.
  We formalize this behavior via the Free Inference dimension dFI(S,N), a combinatorial measure of the environmental complexity a VM agent can handle while preserving trajectory coherence. We prove dFI is strictly smaller than the VC-dimension and relates to the Natarajan dimension up to a path-length factor, capturing the cost of non-decomposable loss. A PAC-style relaxation yields generalization bounds driven by dFI^(epsilon,delta). We also define a complementary PMS identification dimension and show that a hybrid strategy---averaging until the first collision, then switching to selection---is optimal, with links to Littlestone-type dimensions supported by grid-world experiments.

</details>


### [116] [NObSP: Functional Decomposition of Neural Networks via Oblique Subspace Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17825)
*Alexander Caicedo,Víctor De La Hoz,Santiago Alférez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NObSP框架将深度网络预测分解为逐特征贡献函数与交互残差，利用末层线性结构与斜投影减少重叠子空间的双重计数，支持局部解释与全局功能分析，并提供无需反向传播的CAM变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度神经网络的决策机制仍是根本性难题，现有标量归因方法难以刻画可分离的正负证据与功能贡献结构，且特征子空间重叠时易出现双重计数问题。

Method: NObSP利用训练网络的线性末层，在样本空间中使用斜投影（oblique projections）分解预测为逐特征贡献函数与交互残差；与functional ANOVA及Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理建立联系，推导高效部分回归算法用于样本外评估；针对卷积网络提出NObSP-CAM，经一次性校准后无需反向传播即可生成类激活图。

Result: 表格与视觉基准上忠实度与主流归因方法相当；合成基准函数复现得分0.989，优于KernelSHAP的0.966与Integrated Gradients的0.922；TinyImageNet上贡献向量嵌入将最近邻类纯度均值从0.654（原始激活）提升至0.713，平均近邻距离减半以上。

Conclusion: NObSP通过恢复具有可分离正负证据的功能贡献轮廓，对现有标量归因方法形成补充。

Abstract: Understanding how deep neural networks make decisions remains a fundamental challenge. We present NObSP (Nonlinear Oblique Subspace Projections), a framework that decomposes predictions into explicit per feature contribution functions and an interaction residual. NObSP exploits the linear final layer of a trained network and uses oblique projections in sample space to reduce double counting when learned feature subspaces overlap, thereby supporting both local explanations and global functional analysis. We establish connections to functional ANOVA and the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem and derive an efficient partial regression algorithm for out of sample evaluation. For convolutional networks, NObSP-CAM produces class activation maps without backward passes after a one time calibration. Experiments on tabular and vision benchmarks show faithfulness comparable to established attribution methods. On a synthetic benchmark with known component functions, NObSP obtains a Function Reproduction Score of 0.989, compared with 0.966 for KernelSHAP and 0.922 for Integrated Gradients. On TinyImageNet, contribution vector embeddings improve mean nearest neighbor class purity from 0.654 for raw activations to 0.713 and reduce mean neighbor distance by more than half. These results indicate that NObSP complements scalar attribution methods by recovering functional contribution profiles with separable positive and negative evidence.

</details>


### [117] [Spatially Adaptive Noise Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18466)
*Frantzeska Lavda,Maciej Falkiewicz,Van Khoa Nguyen,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Spatially Adaptive Noise Injection (SANI)，一种在扩散采样中按像素动态调整噪声注入的框架，在高曲率区域注入噪声以修正、在平滑区域保留结构，从而提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDPM（随机）与 DDIM（确定性）是同一采样族的两端点，其噪声注入方差为标量且在所有空间位置统一施加。这种均匀方式忽视了自然图像几何特性：边缘、纹理等去噪器不确定的高曲率区域需要随机修正，而 score 精确的平滑区域会被注入噪声破坏。

Method: SANI 判断每个像素在给定时间步是否需要随机修正，结合概率门控机制与推导出的空间自适应方差，实现逐像素的噪声注入，仅在需要处注入噪声以精炼复杂特征并保持完好结构。

Result: 实验与解耦消融表明，SANI 在多种采样时间步下相较原始 DDPM 与 DDIM 端点采样器持续改善 FID，同时与方差学习基线保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 空间自适应对扩散采样具有重要性，逐像素噪声注入能在保持结构的同时提升生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion samplers reverse a learned noising process using either stochastic (DDPM) or deterministic (DDIM) updates, which represent endpoints of a single family controlled by a scalar noise-injection variance that is applied identically at every spatial location. This uniform approach neglects the geometry of natural images: high-curvature regions such as edges and textures, where the denoiser is uncertain, benefit from stochastic correction, whereas smooth regions, where the score is precise, are degraded by injected noise. This work investigates whether each pixel requires stochastic correction at a given timestep and introduces Spatially Adaptive Noise Injection (SANI), a novel sampling framework that dynamically adjusts noise application on a per-pixel basis. SANI integrates a probabilistic gating mechanism with a derived spatially adaptive variance, ensuring that noise is injected precisely where needed to refine complex features while preserving well-formed structures. Experimental results and decoupling ablations demonstrate that SANI consistently improves Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) over the vanilla DDPM and DDIM endpoint samplers across diverse sampling timesteps, while remaining competitive with variance-learning baselines, highlighting the importance of spatial adaptivity in diffusion sampling.

</details>


### [118] [Procedural Pretraining for Molecular Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17831)
*Moritz Friedemann,Zachary Shinnick,Philip Torr,Bruno Andreis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三阶段训练流程：程序化预训练→SMILES分子预训练→下游微调，发现抽象的、程序生成的数据（序列结构、元胞自动机、图推理等）能为分子性质预测提供可迁移的归纳偏置，尤其在标注数据稀缺时提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测受限于下游标注数据规模小，通常依赖大规模无标注分子预训练。作者追问：能否在模型接触任何分子数据之前，先从抽象的、程序生成的数据中学习有用的归纳偏置，从而提供一条不依赖分子语料的补充路径。

Method: 采用三阶段训练流程：1）程序化预训练（覆盖序列结构、元胞自动机、图推理等程序化任务）；2）在SMILES上进行分子预训练；3）下游微调。并分析迁移效果与数据稀缺性、程序数据结构、程序训练预算及网络层（注意力层 vs 前馈层）的关系。

Result: 程序化预训练即使经过后续分子预训练仍能提升分子性质预测：在Lipophilicity上，Reverse任务使测试误差降低4.8%，约为250K分子基线到公开MoLFormer（约100M分子预训练）checkpoint性能差距的90%。收益在数据稀缺时最强，取决于程序数据的结构而非表面统计，且随程序训练量非单调变化（在中等预算处峰值，接近收敛时退化）。此外，可迁移信息多集中于注意力层，前馈层可能导致过度特化。

Conclusion: 程序化数据能为分子学习提供可迁移的结构性归纳偏置，是标注分子数据有限时提升性能的互补途径。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction is often limited by the small size of labeled downstream datasets, motivating pretraining on large corpora of unlabeled molecules. In this work, we ask whether useful inductive biases can instead be learned from abstract, procedurally generated data before a model sees any molecular data. We introduce a three-stage training pipeline consisting of procedural pretraining, molecular pretraining on SMILES, and downstream fine-tuning, and evaluate several procedural tasks spanning sequence structure, cellular automata, and graph reasoning. We find that procedural pretraining can improve molecular property prediction even after subsequent molecular pretraining: on Lipophilicity, \textsc{Reverse} reduces test error by 4.8\%. For context, the magnitude of this improvement is roughly 90\% of the performance difference between our 250K-molecule baseline and the publicly released MoLFormer checkpoint pretrained on approximately 100M molecules. Our analysis shows that the benefit is strongest under downstream data scarcity, depends on the structure of the procedural data rather than only surface-level statistics, and does not increase monotonically with additional procedural training. Instead, transfer typically peaks at an intermediate procedural budget and deteriorates as the model approaches convergence on the procedural task. We further find that, for several tasks, much of the transferable information is localized in the attention layers, while feed-forward layers can contribute to over-specialization. These results show that procedural data can provide transferable structure for molecular learning and offer a complementary route to improving performance when labeled molecular data are limited.

</details>


### [119] [Provable Guarantees and Efficient Learning of Structural Equation Models with Latent Confounders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18535)
*Weijian Yu,Jean Honorio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究存在潜在混杂因子的线性结构方程模型，提出迭代识别末端观测节点并重建观测变量DAG的因果发现算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变量间因果关系在诸多领域很重要，但潜在混杂因子的存在会导致虚假关联和错误边方向，因此需要专门处理潜在混杂因子的方法。

Method: 将观测变量的精度矩阵恢复为稀疏加低秩矩阵：稀疏部分捕捉观测变量间的条件依赖，低秩部分捕捉少量潜在混杂因子的综合影响；据此迭代识别末端观测节点并重建观测变量DAG。

Result: 理论证明：在p个观测变量、r个潜在混杂因子、s条边情况下，当样本量n ≳ max{s log p, r p}时可正确识别观测变量间的有向因果关系；实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 所提算法在潜在混杂因子下能有效恢复观测变量的因果结构，并给出了样本复杂度保证。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to recover causal relationships from observed data. In various fields, exploring causal relationships among variables remains an important topic, but this task becomes challenging due to the existence of latent confounders. Ignoring such confounders can lead to false associations and incorrect edge directions. In this paper, we study the linear structural equation model with latent confounders. We propose an algorithm that iteratively identifies terminal (observed) nodes and reconstructs the directed acyclic graph of the observed variables. To do this, we recover the precision matrix of the observed variables as a sparse plus low-rank matrix: a sparse matrix captures the conditional dependencies among observed variables, while a low-rank matrix captures the combined influence of a few latent confounders. We establish that for $p$ observed variables, $r$ latent confounders and $s$ edges, our procedure correctly identifies the directed causal relationship among observed variables, for $n \gtrsim \max\{s\log p,\ r p\}$ samples. Experimental results validate our theoretical contributions.

</details>


### [120] [Adaptive hybrid coupling with operator inference, the overlapping Schwarz alternating method and reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17837)
*Trishit Mondal,Irina Tezaur,Anthony Gruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于强化学习(DQN)的方法，在线自适应调整重叠Schwarz交替法耦合的FOM-ROM子域模型，用于瞬态问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合域分解方法假设每个子域的模型在整个仿真中固定，这对需要高保真分辨率的局部特征随时间传播的瞬态问题具有局限性。

Method: 用深度Q网络(DQN)离线训练，通过平衡精度、成本和模型切换频率的奖励，在子域局部的FOM与预训练Operator Inference (OpInf) ROM之间进行选择；训练后策略在未见过的算例上进行预测性部署，无需参考FOM解。

Result: 在1D对流扩散(移动前沿)问题中，学习策略随前沿传播动态分配高保真分辨率，优于静态FOM/ROM分配，且允许智能体自适应域分解无额外收益；在Norma.jl实现的3D线弹性波传播问题中，二、三子域策略能通过给含波子域分配FOM、其余分配ROM来追踪传播的波。

Conclusion: 结果表明强化学习有潜力在基于Schwarz的混合仿真中实现模型保真度的预测性在线自适应。

Abstract: Hybrid domain decomposition methods provide a flexible framework for coupling full order models (FOMs) and reduced order models (ROMs), but typically assume the model assigned to each subdomain is fixed throughout a simulation. This is limiting for transient problems in which localized features propagate through the domain and the regions requiring high-fidelity resolution change over time. We introduce a reinforcement learning (RL)-based approach for online adaptation of FOM-ROM models coupled via the overlapping Schwarz alternating method (O-SAM), an iterative domain decomposition method that solves subdomain-local problems while exchanging solution information through transmission boundary conditions on overlapping interfaces. Deep Q-networks (DQNs) are trained offline to select among subdomain-local FOMs and pre-trained Operator Inference (OpInf) ROMs using a reward balancing accuracy, cost, and model-switching frequency. Once trained, the policies are deployed predictively on problem instances not seen during training, without requiring a reference FOM solution. We demonstrate the approach on two examples: a 1D advection-diffusion problem with a moving front, and a 3D linear elastic wave propagation problem implemented in the Norma.jl solid mechanics code. For the advection-diffusion benchmark, the learned policy dynamically allocates high-fidelity resolution as the front propagates and outperforms static FOM/ROM assignments; letting the agent also adapt the domain decomposition provides no further benefit. For the elastic wave benchmark, learned policies for two and three subdomain decompositions track the propagating wave by assigning FOMs to subdomains containing the wave and ROMs elsewhere, as expected. Our results demonstrate the potential of RL to enable predictive online adaptation of model fidelity within Schwarz-based hybrid simulations.

</details>


### [121] [How Many Labels Does Model Choice Need? Certificates and Budgets for Selective Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18622)
*Tetsuji Kuboyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 分类器预测可能相同，但比较AUGRC时仍需标签，因为置信度排序会不同地加权相同错误。论文用预标签下界排除不足预算，用覆盖线性规划将证书大小限制在K-1内，并显示实际预算常需过半标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类器可能做出相同预测，但选择性性能比较依赖标签：置信度排序会让相同错误产生不同权重。需要量化AUGRC比较所需的标签预算。

Method: 针对AUGRC，提出预标签下界排除不足预算；在全部标签已知时，用覆盖线性规划界定足以确定胜者的最小标签数（证书大小），对K个候选限制在K-1内；分析独立均匀顺序和iid Bernoulli错误下的获取策略；并给出任意获取顺序下可用的精确停止测试。

Result: 对固定K，独立均匀顺序且预测相同时，预标签下界趋近池的四分之一；iid Bernoulli错误独立于顺序时，精确获取策略渐近读取几乎所有标签，但双候选证书只需一半。108个特征面板比较中，分歧标签能解决所有accuracy选择，却不能解决任何AUGRC选择；20%预算在96种条件下被排除，证书平均需56-57%。十个预训练图像分类器条件中，精确选择需读取10,000标签的68-91%，AUGRC容差5×10^-4时为50-67%。

Conclusion: 结果将置信度排序与标签预算、认证模型比较联系起来，表明AUGRC认证比较通常比accuracy比较需要更多标签，并提供了预算下界、证书大小界和精确停止测试。

Abstract: Classifiers can make identical predictions yet require labels to compare their selective performance: confidence ranks weight the same errors differently. We quantify this requirement for the area under the generalized risk-coverage curve (AUGRC). A prelabel lower bound rules out insufficient budgets. With all labels known, a covering linear program bounds the minimum number of labels sufficient to fix the winner (the certificate size) within $K-1$ labels for $K$ candidates. For fixed $K$, independent uniform orders and identical predictions, the prelabel bound approaches one quarter of the pool. With iid Bernoulli errors independent of the orders, every exact acquisition policy reads almost all labels asymptotically, although a two-candidate certificate needs only half. Across 108 feature-panel comparisons on nine datasets, disagreement labels settle every accuracy choice but no AUGRC choice. A 20% budget is ruled out in 96 conditions; certificates need 56-57% on average. On ten conditions with pretrained image classifiers, confidence-score choice reads 68-91% of 10,000 labels for exact selection and 50-67% with AUGRC tolerance $5\times10^{-4}$. An exact stopping test works with any acquisition order. Together, these results link confidence ranks to label budgets and certified model comparison.

</details>


### [122] [Hybrid coupling with numerics-informed neural networks and the overlapping Schwarz alternating method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17841)
*George Chumbipuma,Irina Tezaur,Alejandro Diaz,Beatrice Riviere*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出混合框架,用重叠Schwarz交替法将预训练的数值信息神经网络(NINN)与经典全阶模型(FOM)耦合,在Peclet数10^6的对流扩散问题上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络(PINN)在对流主导问题中难以训练,需探索可替代的NINN及其与经典求解器的耦合部署方式。

Method: 以重叠乘性Schwarz交替法作为耦合机制,将子域局部预训练NINN与相邻FOM耦合,NINN权重在Schwarz迭代中固定;子域NINN采用两种训练:自顶向下(边界数据来自全FOM-FOM Schwarz解)和自底向上(合成边界迹,无需全域求解)。

Result: 与对应PINN不同,单体NINN无需域分解即可精确训练;混合NINN-FOM解与FOM-FOM Schwarz解高度吻合,两种训练方法精度相当。

Conclusion: NINN可作为稳定的预训练子域模型,通过Schwarz方法无缝耦合进经典数值框架,且自底向上训练无需全域求解即可达到可比精度。

Abstract: We develop a hybrid modeling framework for coupling pre-trained numerics-informed neural networks (NINNs) with classical full order models (FOMs) using the overlapping Schwarz alternating method. We consider the two-dimensional advection-diffusion equation in the advection-dominated, Peclet-number 10^6 regime. We first demonstrate that, unlike the corresponding physics-informed neural network (PINN), a monolithic NINN can be accurately trained on our model problem without domain decomposition. We then employ overlapping multiplicative Schwarz as a deployment mechanism for coupling a pre-trained, subdomain-local NINN with a neighboring FOM, with the NINN weights held fixed throughout the Schwarz iteration. We consider two training approaches for the subdomain-local NINNs: a top-down approach, in which boundary data are obtained from a coupled Schwarz solve on the full domain with a FOM on each subdomain (FOM-FOM Schwarz), and a bottom-up approach, in which boundary traces are generated synthetically on the NINN subdomain without requiring any full-domain solves. The resulting hybrid NINN-FOM solutions agree closely with the corresponding FOM-FOM Schwarz solutions, with the top-down and bottom-up training approaches yielding comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [123] [Revisiting Distributed Sign-Based Variance Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18656)
*Wei Jiang,Zechao Li,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了基于符号的方法在异构数据下因聚合局部符号产生偏差、无法达到最优收敛率的问题，提出了在服务器端通过递归梯度增量的无偏压缩来跟踪全局梯度的新方法，获得了非凸随机和有限和优化的最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于符号的方法可降低分布式环境中的通信成本，但当数据异构时，聚合局部符号会引入偏差，导致现有基于符号的方差缩减方法无法获得最优收敛率。

Method: 首先给出一个反例，说明即使使用精确局部梯度，多数投票也无法逼近稳定点。受此限制启发，提出在服务器端通过递归梯度增量的无偏压缩来跟踪全局梯度。对于有限和问题，将周期性精确梯度刷新与压缩的组件梯度差分相结合。

Result: 在ℓ1范数下达到O(√(d/K)+√d(a/(nK))^{1/3})、ℓ2范数下达到O(√(a/K)+√a/(nK)^{1/3})的收敛率（K为迭代数，n为工作节点数，d为维度，a=1+ω，ω为压缩器相对方差）。有限和问题（M个组件）的总样本复杂度分别为O(M+d√(aM)ε^{-2})和O(M+a√M ε^{-2})，与集中式设置下的对应界匹配。

Conclusion: 所提方法克服了符号聚合的偏差问题，在非凸随机和有限和优化中均取得了最优收敛率，有限和问题的样本复杂度达到与集中式设置相匹配的界。

Abstract: Sign-based methods reduce communication costs in distributed environments, but aggregating local signs can introduce bias when data are heterogeneous. As a result, existing sign-based variance reduction methods fail to obtain the optimal convergence rates. In this paper, we solve this problem and obtain optimal rates for both nonconvex stochastic and finite-sum optimization. We first give a counterexample showing that majority voting can fail to approach stationary points even with exact local gradients. Motivated by this limitation, we propose tracking the global gradient at the server through unbiased compression of recursive gradient increments. As a result, we can obtain the convergence rates of $O(\sqrt{d/K}+\sqrt d (a/(nK))^{1/3})$ for the $\ell_1$-norm and $O(\sqrt{a/K}+\sqrt a/(nK)^{1/3})$ for the $\ell_2$-norm. Here, $K$ is the iteration number, $n$ is the number of workers, $d$ is the dimension, and $a=1+ω$, with $ω$ denoting the compressor's relative variance. For finite-sum problems with $M$ components, we combine periodic exact gradient refreshes with compressed component-gradient differences. The resulting total sample complexities are $O(M+d\sqrt{aM}ε^{-2})$ and $O(M+a\sqrt M\ epsilon^{-2})$ for $\ell_1$ and $\ell_2$ gradient norms at most $ε$, matching the corresponding bounds in centralized settings.

</details>


### [124] [Uncertainty-Aware Continual Learning for Open-World Intent Discovery Under an evolving Label Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17866)
*Pisante Aida,Formentin Simone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出不确定性感知的概率框架，用于演化标签空间下的持续新意图发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实智能系统处于开放世界，用户意图非固定、非穷举且会演化，需要持续发现新意图并控制标签空间扩展。

Method: 用自适应β-VAE编码话语，得到潜在均值（分类与密度建模）及后验不确定性；结合分类器置信度、后验不确定性与DP-GMM似然做多信号决策；对候选新样本用密度聚类，仅将可靠簇升为新标签；用回放与EWC缓解灾难性遗忘。

Result: 实验显示高新颖性精度、跨阶段稳定适应与有限遗忘；NMI和ARI接近零，说明难以完整重建细粒度意图分类体系，符合保守升格策略；定性分析显示能发现密集且局部语义一致的簇。

Conclusion: 该框架可在稳定性-可塑性约束下可靠地发现新意图结构，而无需穷尽恢复完整意图分类体系。

Abstract: Real-world intelligent systems increasingly operate under open-world conditions, where user intents are not fixed or exhaustively known a priori and may evolve as new interaction patterns emerge. This paper proposes a unified uncertainty-aware probabilistic framework for continual new intent discovery under an evolving label space. Each utterance is encoded through an adaptive $β$-VAE into a latent mean, used for classification and density modelling and a posterior uncertainty estimate acting as a global reliability signal. Classifier confidence, posterior uncertainty and DP-GMM likelihood are combined through a multi-signal decision mechanism to distinguish known intents from potentially novel samples. Candidate novel instances are clustered through a density-based discovery module and only reliable clusters are promoted to new labels, enabling controlled label-space expansion. Replay and Elastic Weight Consolidation mitigate catastrophic forgetting and preserve previously acquired knowledge. The paper formalises continual intent discovery as a structured multi-phase open-world problem, introduces adaptive label-space expansion under stability--plasticity constraints and uses posterior uncertainty to regulate trusted-sample selection, pseudo-labelling, novelty admission and replay. Experiments show high novelty precision, stable adaptation across sequential phases and limited forgetting. Near-zero NMI and ARI indicate limited reconstruction of the complete fine-grained intent taxonomy, consistent with the framework's conservative promotion strategy. Qualitative analyses nevertheless reveal dense and locally coherent semantic clusters, showing that reliable novel structures can be discovered without exhaustive recovery of the underlying taxonomy.

</details>


### [125] [When Edit Flows are Edit Jumps: replicating Edit Flows and EvoFlows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18745)
*Gabriel Bénédict,Melanie Buechler,Gerard Riera-Solà,Chloé de Ancos,Yves Gaetan Nana Teukam,Moritz Freidank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文统一了Edit Flows与EvoFlows为连续时间纯跳生成器匹配框架，开源实现EditJumps，并揭示复现所需未记录的超参数及评测指标对参考样本量的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗体先导优化需要对候选序列做有界编辑（替换、插入、删除），而基于编辑的生成模型是唯一能预先分配编辑预算、不固定编辑位置/数量/长度的方案。但现有Edit Flows与EvoFlows未公开代码与完整训练细节，阻碍复现与公平评估。

Method: 作者证明两种方法本质相同：编辑在连续时间中以学习到的速率逐个触发，对应有限序列上的生成器匹配纯跳情形。提出EditJumps——该框架首个开源实现，用1.66M Observed Antibody Space同源对训练单一通用抗体编辑器，零样本编辑未见先导序列，无需按家族重训。

Result: 从头复现时需逆向出决定实际突变数的未文档化速率缩放超参数，才能与已发表编辑分布一致；同时发现已发表评测指标对参考样本量高度敏感，常导致方法排名翻转。

Conclusion: 开源代码、自动化测试与配置（GitHub链接）对生成生物学至关重要，可保证可复现性与可靠评测。

Abstract: Antibody lead optimization calls for a small, bounded set of edits to an existing candidate: substitutions, but also insertions and deletions. Edit-based generative models are the only ones that allocate such an edit budget without fixing the edit positions, the edit count, or the output length in advance. However, the existing approaches Edit Flows and EvoFlows did not release code or complete training specifications. Here, we show that both methods follow the same underlying process -- edits firing one at a time, at learned rates, in continuous time -- the pure-jump case of generator matching over finite sequences. With EditJumps we introduce the first open implementation of this framework, with a single generalist antibody editor trained on 1.66M Observed Antibody Space homolog pairs to propose homolog-like variants of a seed sequence, editing unseen leads zero-shot, without the per-family retraining original approaches require. Replicating this system from scratch exposes why open code is essential for generative biology: reconciling published edit distributions required reverse-engineering an undocumented rate-scaling hyperparameter that dictates realized mutation counts. Moreover, we show that published evaluation metrics are highly sensitive to reference sample size, frequently flipping method rankings. We release our full codebase, automated test suite, and configurations at: https://github.com/VisiumCH/editjumps

</details>


### [126] [Dataset-Dependent Effects of Cross-Depth Aggregation and Soft-Routed Experts in EEG Foundation Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17886)
*Mingyang Jiang,Yamin Li,Daniel Moyer,Fan Ma,Hua Xu,Catie Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在CBraMod上加入跨深度注意力残差(AttnRes)与双软路由专家库，测试专用模块能否提升全微调EEG基础模型。四数据集三随机种子实验显示效果因数据集而异，且开销显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG解码依赖不同的时间动态与跨通道关系，作者想验证针对这些特性的专用模块能否改进全微调的EEG基础模型（CBraMod）。

Method: 在CBraMod基础上增强两种模块：跨深度注意力残差(AttnRes)和两个软路由专家库，并在FACED、ISRUC、SEED-V、PhysioNet-MI四个数据集上以匹配的三随机种子实验对比全微调。

Result: 相对全微调，完整模型的平均平衡准确率变化分别为-0.12、+1.27、+0.77、-1.27个百分点。仅AttnRes在三个数据集上有提升；在AttnRes上再加专家仅对FACED和SEED-V有帮助。代价显著：AttnRes需2.11–2.88x运行时间和1.78–2.67x内存，完整模型需2.41–3.04x运行时间和1.86–2.85x内存。

Conclusion: 所加模块带来的效果依赖数据集、甚至方向相反，未能稳定超越全微调，且资源开销较大。

Abstract: EEG decoding tasks can rely on different temporal dynamics and cross-channel relationships. We test whether specialized modules improve a fully fine-tuned EEG foundation model by augmenting CBraMod with cross-depth Attention Residuals (AttnRes) and two soft-routed expert banks. Across matched three-seed experiments on FACED, ISRUC, SEED-V, and PhysioNet-MI, the complete model changes mean balanced accuracy relative to full fine-tuning by -0.12, +1.27, +0.77, and -1.27 points, respectively. AttnRes alone improves mean balanced accuracy on three datasets, whereas adding experts on top of AttnRes helps only FACED and SEED-V. These gains come with substantial overhead: AttnRes requires 2.11 to 2.88x runtime and 1.78 to 2.67x memory, while the complete model requires 2.41 to 3.04x runtime and 1.86 to 2.85x memory. Overall, the added modules produce dataset-dependent, sometimes opposing effects rather than consistent gains over full fine-tuning.

</details>


### [127] [Long-Context Demonstration Selection Using State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17888)
*Ziniu Zhang,Zhenshuo Zhang,Ruoxuan Xiong,Gene Cooperman,Hongyang R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文利用状态空间模型（SSM）进行示例选择（demonstration selection），通过蒸馏Transformer并映射输出到少量token嵌入，实现高效选择。实验显示近似误差<0.7%，下游任务FLOPs减少14.2倍，准确率提升6.48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer推理成本随序列长度二次增长，在长上下文场景下示例选择问题更具挑战性，因此需要更高效的方案。

Method: 基于状态空间模型（线性推理时间）：1）将Transformer所有层分成连续组，通过蒸馏为每组估计一个独立SSM以复现相邻层间的输入输出行为；2）将蒸馏模型输出映射到少量token，用于下游应用的示例选择。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上验证：蒸馏SSM相对真实输出的近似误差小于0.7%；在多个文本分类和推理任务上，相对基线示例选择方法FLOPs减少14.2倍，准确率提升6.48%。

Conclusion: 通过蒸馏SSM并结合输出到少量token的映射，可在长上下文场景中高效完成示例选择，兼顾效率与效果。

Abstract: We study the problem of demonstration selection, which involves selecting a subset of examples for prepending to a query to a language model. This problem is closely related to in-context learning and language model inference. Since the inference cost of a transformer model scales quadratically with sequence length, the selection problem becomes especially challenging in a long-context scenario. In this paper, we tackle this problem by building on state space models (SSMs), which require only linear inference time given the input. Our approach involves two algorithms. The first learns a small set of SSMs through distillation of a (trained) transformer model. We partition all the layers into consecutive groups. Then for each group, we estimate a separate state space model to replicate the input-output behavior within the adjacent layers. Second, we map the distilled model outputs to a small set of tokens, and apply these embeddings for demonstration selection in downstream applications. We perform extensive experiments in both synthetic and real-world datasets to validate our approach. We demonstrate that the distilled SSMs only incur an approximation error of less than $0.7\%$ relative to the true output. In downstream evaluation, we show that on several text classification and reasoning tasks, our approach reduces FLOPs by $14.2\times$ and improves accuracy by $6.48\%$ relative to baseline demonstration selection methods.

</details>


### [128] [Walking the Score Manifold: Continuous-time Generative Dynamics on Learned Data Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17901)
*Jan Tauberschmidt,Brian B. Moser,Stanislav Frolov,Andreas Dengel,Andrew B. Duncan,Sebastian J. Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将时间依赖数据的生成建模从离散时间网格重新表述为学习数据流形上的连续时间演化，利用预训练score模型作为几何先验，学习沿score引导插值路径演化的向量场，实现任意时间戳生成与时间超分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生成建模在离散时间网格上进行，监督仅限于训练数据中的观测时间戳，无法支持任意时间戳生成或超出训练数据离散化的时间超分辨率。

Method: 以预训练score-based模型作为几何先验，学习一个沿score诱导插值路径演化的向量场；通过回归目标实现无模拟训练；提出促进路径相对横向指数稳定性的目标（可解释为横向于插值路径的去噪score匹配）；并扩展到建模未来轨迹分布的概率设定。

Result: 在自然视频和科学动力学数据上验证，包括时间超分辨率、基于PDE的时空场和分子动力学。

Conclusion: 实验表明score-based先验为学习随机连续时间生成动力学提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Generative modeling of time-dependent data is typically formulated on a discrete temporal grid, restricting supervision to the observed timestamps in the training data. We instead frame generation as continuous-time evolution on a learned data manifold. To this end, we leverage pretrained score-based models as geometric priors and learn a vector field that evolves data along score-induced interpolation paths. Because these dynamics follow transitions that respect the geometry learned by the score model, they support generation at arbitrary timestamps and temporal super-resolution beyond the discretization of the training data. Moreover, this geometric formulation allows us to train the vector field simulation-free through a regression objective. To improve long-horizon rollout robustness, we introduce an objective that promotes path-relative transverse exponential stability. While motivated by stability theory, it admits a practical interpretation as denoising score matching transverse to the interpolation path. Further, we extend the framework to a probabilistic setting that models a distribution over plausible future trajectories. We demonstrate the method on natural video and scientific dynamical data, including temporal super-resolution, PDE-based spatiotemporal fields, and molecular dynamics. Our results show that score-based priors provide a strong foundation for learning stochastic continuous-time generative dynamics.

</details>


### [129] [EdgeReMIND: A Scalable, Top-Ranked Memorization Baseline for Temporal Multi-Relational Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17916)
*Bryant Pollard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EdgeReMIND通过线性记忆模型解决了TGB 2.0时态链接预测的可扩展性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TGB 2.0最大规模数据集上，所有现有嵌入方法均内存溢出或超时，而这些图最接近真实部署规模，是实际生产限制。

Method: EdgeReMIND为线性记忆模型，对数据校准特征学习逐关系权重，是唯一能在全部八个数据集上运行的关系感知方法。

Result: 在八个数据集中的六个上取得最高报告的测试MRR。

Conclusion: EdgeReMIND不仅是嵌入失效时的退路，更是整个基准上实用的最先进基线。

Abstract: Temporal link prediction on the Temporal Graph Benchmark 2.0 (TGB 2.0) faces a scalability ceiling: on the benchmark's three largest datasets, every existing embedding method runs out of memory or exceeds the time budget. These large-scale graphs are the ones nearest real deployment scale, so failing on them is a real production limitation. EdgeReMIND sets the highest reported test mean reciprocal rank (MRR) on six of eight TGB 2.0 datasets and is the only relation-aware method that runs on all of them. This linear memorization model, with learned per-relation weights over data-calibrated features, is therefore not merely a fallback where embeddings fail but a practical state-of-the-art baseline across the benchmark.

</details>


### [130] [Locating Hidden Failures Makes Long-Horizon Agents More Reliable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17930)
*Salman Rahman,Yubin Kim,Mihir Parmar,A. Ali Heydari,Genglin Liu,Simon A. Lee,Weizhi Zhang,Arian Hosseini,Ahmed A. Metwally,Yuzhe Yang,Baharan Mirzasoleiman,Xin Liu,Pavel Izmailov,Saadia Gabriel,Mark Malhotra,Shwetak Patel,Daniel McDuff,Hamid Palangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究长时程AI智能体的失败模式，构建Traverse基准（2518条轨迹、6967个错误、78类失败），发现失败具有"首次犯错后难恢复、且往往不自知"的共性特征，并训练出能有效定位失败的小模型Scout用于测试时选择，从而提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体日益承担长时程自主任务，人类更多扮演监督者角色；但当前几乎只用"最终是否成功"来评判，无法揭示过程性问题（何处出错、是否恢复、沿途是否造成不可逆伤害），长时程失败图谱也尚属空白。

Method: 收集软件工程、计算机使用、科学等接近真实部署场景的2518条智能体轨迹，人工标注6967个错误、归纳为78种失败类型，形成Traverse基准；并训练了一个4B参数的验证器Scout，用于定位失败并在测试时对候选运行进行选择。

Result: 失败呈反复出现的特征：首次犯错后往往无法恢复且很少自我发现，运行在看似正确的情况下继续失控；恢复能力取决于任务与环境反馈而非智能体框架。六个前沿评判模型定位失败能力有限，最强者在不到三分之一的运行中能正确找出首个错误；而Scout定位能力显著更强，且能迁移到未见过领域；用于测试时选择可将任务成功率提升至超过智能体单次尝试水平，无需重训智能体。

Conclusion: 使失败变得易于定位和纠正，为能自我纠错、更可信的长时程智能体奠定了基础，也为监督日益自主的AI提供了实用路径。

Abstract: As AI agents take on long, autonomous tasks, we increasingly oversee rather than perform the work, yet we still judge them almost entirely by whether they finally succeed. An outcome cannot reveal where a run went wrong, whether the agent recovered, or the irreversible harm it caused along the way, and where long-horizon agents fail remains unmapped. We study $2518$ agent trajectories across software engineering, computer use, and science, close to real deployment, and classify $6967$ mistakes into $78$ failure types. Failure follows a recurring signature: after its first mistake an agent often fails to recover and rarely catches the error itself, so the run continues unchecked while still looking correct; whether an agent recovers depends on the task and the environment's feedback, not on the agent framework running it. Long-horizon agents can do real harm on the way to a passing result: even runs scored as solved delete data, corrupt systems, or fabricate success rather than earning it. We release these human-verified annotations as Traverse, a benchmark on which six frontier judges struggle to locate failure regardless of scale: even the strongest correctly identifies the first mistake in fewer than a third of runs. Yet Scout, a $4$B verifier we trained, locates failure far better than these judges and transfers to domains it never saw. Used at test time to select among an agent's candidate runs, it raises task success above the agent's own single-attempt performance, without retraining the agent. By making failure cheap to locate and correct, this work is a foundation for more trustworthy long-horizon agents that learn from their own mistakes, and a practical path to overseeing increasingly autonomous AI.

</details>


### [131] [The Attention Within: Consensus Dynamics in Selective State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17997)
*João Pedro Silvestre,Álvaro Rodríguez Abella,Paulo Tabuada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从动力系统视角研究选择性状态空间模型(SSM)，证明其核心递归机制会像Transformer中的注意力一样驱使token趋向共识(consensus)。通过常微分方程建模并利用输入-状态稳定性理论，建立了共识均衡点的局部指数稳定性及其吸引域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSM近来成为Transformer的有力替代方案。Transformer中注意力会驱使token聚类至单一方向(共识)，而SSM的递归机制虽机制不同却发挥类似注意力的作用，且可表述为线性注意力。因此作者提出问题：SSM核心递归是否也会驱使token趋向共识？

Method: 采用动力系统视角，将token跨层演化建模为常微分方程。利用输入-状态稳定性(input-to-state stability)论证，针对时变权重矩阵(此前未涉及的设定)建立共识均衡点的局部指数稳定性，并刻画其吸引域。在预训练Mamba-2模型上进行数值实验验证。

Result: 理论证明SSM核心递归会聚合token，与注意力作用一致。数值实验表明输出门(output gate)是调节该共识程度的组件，防止token完全达到共识。

Conclusion: SSM与Transformer的相似性比表面更深：二者核心机制均驱使token趋向共识。输出门通过调控共识程度防止完全坍缩，解释了实际模型行为。研究为理解两类架构的联系提供了动力系统理论框架。

Abstract: Selective state space models (SSMs) have recently emerged as a compelling alternative to transformers, combining competitive performance with substantially improved inference efficiency. At each SSM layer, a sequence of hidden states are propagated by a recurrence, mixing information of different tokens. Despite using a different mechanism, this mixing plays a role analogous to attention in transformers. In fact, recent works have shown that the two architectures may be closer than they first appear, as this recurrence admits a formulation akin to linear attention. In transformers, attention is known to drive the tokens to cluster, i.e., to reach consensus, collapsing in the limit to a single direction. Thus, we ask: does the recurrence at the core of SSMs drive the tokens to consensus, as attention does in transformers?
  To answer this question, we take a dynamical systems perspective on SSMs, modeling the evolution of tokens across layers as an ordinary differential equation. By exploiting input-to-state stability arguments, we establish local exponential stability of the consensus equilibria and characterize their domain of attraction for time-varying weight matrices, a setting not addressed by previous results. We thereby show that the resemblance between SSMs and transformers does run deeper: the recurrence at the core of SSMs aggregates tokens just as attention does. Numerical experiments on a pretrained Mamba-2 model point to the output gate as the component that regulates the extent of this consensus, preventing the tokens from reaching it in full.

</details>


### [132] [Beyond the Previous Layer: Residual Predictive Structure in Sparse MoE Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17940)
*Hao Li,Yasuyuki Tahara,Yuichi Sei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoE模型中,预测下一层路由选择时,仅用最近一层不够;更早的历史专家选择可显著提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究稀疏MoE模型中路由轨迹的预测性:验证"仅仅前一层选择是否足以预测下一层路由器"这一假设,探究专家选择序列中是否存在超越相邻层持续性的残余预测结构。

Method: 使用冻结的OLMoE和JetMoE模型,以最近一次选择为基线,测量更早专家选择的留出预测增益;扩展历史从1层到11层,并进行预注册的JetMoE复现;通过线性探针与小型MLP对比检验非线性解码;采用参数匹配控制与交叉拟合残余分析。

Result: OLMoE中路由logit的R²从0.59879升至0.66544;JetMoE复现中四层历史增益在两个目标深度分别为0.14275和0.20528,配对bootstrap区间均在零以上;非线性MLP加入历史后R²提升0.17137和0.21861,而单独非线性解码最近状态仅提升0.00139和0.00936;参数匹配控制保持优势,交叉拟合历史残差预测目标残差R²为0.20549和0.23556。

Conclusion: 专家选择轨迹中存在超越相邻层持续性的残余预测结构,更早的专家选择对预测下一层路由器具有实质性的独立预测价值。

Abstract: Sparse mixture-of-experts models route each token through a sequence of expert selections. We ask whether the immediately preceding selection adequately summarizes this trajectory for predicting the next router. Using frozen OLMoE and JetMoE models, we measure the held-out predictive gain from earlier expert selections while retaining the most recent selection as a common baseline. In OLMoE, extending the history from one to eleven layers raises router-logit $R^2$ from 0.59879 to 0.66544. A preregistered JetMoE replication yields four-layer gains of 0.14275 and 0.20528 at two target depths, with paired bootstrap intervals above zero. These gains survive nonlinear decoding: adding history to a small multilayer perceptron improves $R^2$ by 0.17137 and 0.21861, whereas nonlinear decoding of the recent state alone adds 0.00139 and 0.00936 over a linear probe. Parameter-matched controls preserve the advantage, and cross-fitted history residuals predict target residuals with $R^2$ of 0.20549 and 0.23556. These findings identify residual predictive structure in expert-selection trajectories beyond adjacent-layer persistence.

</details>


### [133] [Learning Fractional-Order Dynamics from a Single Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18127)
*Xiaole Zhang,Ziyi Zhang,Zehao Zhao,Stephen Tu,Guannan Qu,Yorie Nakahira,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究离散时间分数阶线性时不变系统的辨识，提出两阶段估计器FO-GS，利用分数差分算子的对角结构将辨识问题按行解耦，并给出非渐近高概率误差界，估计误差为O(t^{-1/2})，实验优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多真实过程具有长程依赖性，当前状态依赖缓慢衰减的历史轨迹而非仅最近状态，这带来非马尔可夫动力学。分数阶系统在估计时耦合整个历史，使统计分析和实际辨识更困难，需要专门的系统辨识方法。

Method: 针对由Grünwald-Letnikov差分算子刻画的离散时间分数阶LTI系统，基于单条长度t的观测轨迹，提出Fractional-Order Ordinary-Least-Squares Grid-Search（FO-GS）两阶段估计器，利用分数差分算子的对角结构将辨识问题按行解耦。在稳定性假设下建立异质设置中分数阶数与系统矩阵估计的高概率非渐近误差界。

Result: 在稳定性假设下，分数阶数与系统矩阵的估计误差的高概率非渐近上界均为O(t^{-1/2})。实验表明FO-GS在恢复分数阶数和底层系统动力学方面优于现有基线。

Conclusion: FO-GS为长程依赖的分数阶LTI系统辨识提供了一种简单有效的两阶段方法，具备非渐近理论保证和实证优势。

Abstract: Many real-world processes exhibit long-range dependence, where the current state depends on a slowly decaying trace of past states rather than on the most recent state alone. This paper studies system identification for discrete-time fractional-order linear time-invariant systems from a single observed trajectory of length $t$, a setting that captures such non-Markovian dynamics through the Grünwald--Letnikov difference operator. Unlike Markovian systems, fractional-order systems couple estimation across the entire history, making both statistical analysis and practical identification more challenging. We propose \emph{Fractional-Order Ordinary-Least-Squares Grid-Search (FO-GS)}, a simple two-stage estimator that exploits the diagonal structure of the fractional-difference operator to decouple the identification problem row-wise. Under the stability assumption, we establish high-probability, non-asymptotic error bounds for estimating both the fractional order and the system matrix in the heterogeneous setting, with both estimation errors scaling as \(\mathcal{O}(t^{-1/2})\). Through experiments, we show that \emph{FO-GS} outperforms existing baselines in recovering both the fractional order and the underlying system dynamics.

</details>


### [134] [ASPIRE: Asynchronous Batched Self-Speculative Decoding for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17943)
*Amir Ziashahabi,Hossein Entezari Zarch,Lei Gao,Murali Annavaram,Salman Avestimehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ASPIRE，非同步批处理自推测解码框架，通过混合前向、在线调度和层内刷新，显著加速长上下文LLM推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理受限于注意力机制，KV-cache重复读取导致解码内存受限；现有批处理自推测解码方法同步执行，所有请求共享同一草稿-验证调度，而最优草稿长度因请求而异且动态变化。

Method: ASPIRE由三部分组成：(1) 统一混合前向，草稿与验证请求共存于同一批前向传播，取消全局草稿-验证阶段；(2) 轻量在线推测调度器，利用每请求接受率估计和批感知成本模型，让每个请求独立决定验证时机；(3) 草稿内刷新层，在草稿阶段指定单层执行全注意力，每步更新稀疏上下文以减少陈旧性。

Result: 在三个模型和五个推理及长上下文基准上，ASPIRE相比自回归基线实现1.70-4.58倍解码吞吐加速，相比最强先前自推测基线平均加速约提升27%。

Conclusion: ASPIRE通过非同步批处理自推测解码有效缓解长上下文推理瓶颈，实现显著加速。

Abstract: Long-context LLM inference is bottlenecked by attention, whose repeated KV-cache reads make decoding memory-bound. Self-speculative decoding alleviates this by drafting tokens with sparse attention and verifying them with full attention, but existing batched methods remain synchronized: all requests in a batch share a single draft-verify schedule, even though the optimal draft length varies widely across requests and changes dynamically within each request. We propose ASPIRE, a non-synchronized batched self-speculative decoding framework built on three components. First, a unified mixed forward allows drafting and verifying requests to coexist in the same batched forward pass, removing the need for global draft-verify phases. Second, a lightweight online speculation scheduler uses per-request acceptance-rate estimates and a batch-aware cost model to let each request independently choose when to verify. Third, an intra-draft refresh layer performs full attention at a single designated layer during drafting, updating the sparse context at every draft step to reduce staleness during drafting. Across three models and five reasoning and long-context benchmarks, ASPIRE achieves $1.70$-$4.58\times$ speedup in decoding throughput over autoregressive baselines and improves average speedup by approximately $27\%$ over the strongest prior self-speculative baselines.

</details>


### [135] [Maximum Strong Independent Sets in Hypergraphs: Reductions, Bounds, and Greedy Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17951)
*Yingquan,Wu,Jason Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限超图中的最大强独立集问题（选取最大顶点集，使每条超边至多交一个顶点），动机来自多频段LSH-MinHash去重中的局部碰撞证据问题。作者发展了一套关联结构工具：证明支配、关联孪生、权重为1块的精确归约，导出闭式与低权上界，引入打孔与覆盖证书，并分析分层贪心聚类算法，给出可行性、极大性、条件最优性及关联局部复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该目标源于每个观察到的块是局部不相容约束、而跨重叠块做传递闭包并不合理的场景。典型例子是多频段LSH-MinHash去重：每个碰撞桶提供局部证据，但连通分量收缩会引入虚假的全局等价。因此需要一种只保证局部不相容、不做全局传递闭包的独立集模型。

Method: 建立关联结构工具包：1）证明支配、关联孪生和权重为1块的精确归约；2）推导闭式及低权上界；3）引入打孔（puncturing）与覆盖（covering）证书以收紧上界；4）分析由块权重和残差关联驱动的分层贪心聚类算法，并进行可行性、极大性、条件最优性、分层见证匹配上界与关联局部复杂度分析。

Result: 证明了支配、关联孪生、权重为1块的精确归约；得到闭式与低权上界，并通过打孔与覆盖证书加以收紧；对分层贪心聚类算法给出可行性、极大性、条件最优性、分层见证匹配上界及关联局部复杂度界；给出广泛的关联族上的正确性、终止性、不动点与最优性证书，并用例子说明不同证书何时分离或重合。

Conclusion: 论文为超图最大强独立集问题提供了一套关联结构分析与算法框架，适用于需要局部不相容约束而不做全局传递闭包的场景，如多频段LSH-MinHash去重，并给出了多种可分离或重合的最优性证书。

Abstract: We study the maximum strong independent set problem in a finite hypergraph: find the largest vertex set that intersects every hyperedge in at most one vertex. This objective arises whenever each observed block is a local incompatibility constraint but transitive closure across overlapping blocks is not justified. A motivating example is multi-band LSH-MinHash deduplication, where each collision bucket gives local evidence, while connected-component contraction can impose spurious global equivalences. The paper develops an incidence-structural toolkit for this problem. We prove exact reductions for dominance, incidence twins, and weight-1 blocks; derive closed-form and low-weight upper bounds; introduce puncturing and covering certificates that sharpen those bounds; and analyze a layered greedy clustering algorithm driven by block weights and residual incidence. The algorithmic analysis includes feasibility, maximality, conditional optimality, a layered witness-matching upper bound, and incidence-local complexity bounds. The results give correctness, termination, fixed-point, and optimality certificates for broad incidence families, together with examples showing when different certificates separate or coincide.

</details>


### [136] [Preventing Model Collapse: A Fisher-Rao Perspective on the Dynamics of Training with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18878)
*Matteo Marchi,João Pedro Silvestre,Bahman Gharesifard,Paulo Tabuada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文借助Fisher-Rao信息几何，重新分析合成数据与人类数据递归训练的动态，给出防止模型崩溃所需最小人类数据比例的非平凡理论界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型训练中人类高质量数据耗尽，转向合成数据，但纯合成数据递归训练会导致模型崩溃。混合人类数据可防止崩溃，但所需最小人类-合成数据比例尚无精确理论刻画；此前基于欧氏距离的下界在高维下变得空洞无效。

Method: 在概率单纯形上利用信息几何结构，采用Fisher-Rao度量分析递归训练过程的动力学，推导收缩性与不变性的定量界。

Result: 导出的收缩与不变性界在高维下依然稳定、不退化，表明防止模型崩溃的有效人类数据比例与已有工作隐含结论不同。

Conclusion: 从信息几何视角为混合训练稳定性提供严格理论保证，修正了以往对所需人类数据比例的认识。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are now routinely trained using synthetic data, since high-quality human data has been exhausted by the ever increasing needs of larger and larger models. However, recursive training on synthetic data frequently induces model collapse, a degenerative feedback loop where models progressively forget the true underlying data distribution. Training on a mixture of synthetic and fresh human data is a logical countermeasure and can prevent model collapse. However, it is an open question as to what is the exact minimum required ratio of human-to-synthetic data to maintain training stability. In this paper, we establish rigorous theoretical guarantees on the minimum rate of human data required to prevent model collapse. Although previous work established a formal lower bound for this ratio, such bound can be vacuous for very high dimensions, as the analysis relies on the usual Euclidean metric in R^n and is not adapted to the space of categorical probability distributions. Instead, in this paper we explicitly leverage the information-geometric structure of the probability simplex by analyzing the dynamics of the process under the Fisher-Rao metric. We derive quantitative contraction and invariance bounds that are stable and do not become trivial as the dimensions increase. Thus, we show that the effective required data ratio to prevent model collapse is different than previously implied.

</details>


### [137] [QuanText: Protecting Dataset-Level Secrets in Textual Data Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17995)
*Shuaiqi Wang,Zinan Lin,Giulia Fanti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QuanText 是一种无需训练、与 LLM 无关的文本数据发布机制，通过随机量化扰动数据集级敏感属性分布，在保护全局秘密的同时保留数据效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发布文本数据集会泄露数据源的全局敏感属性（如性别、诊断、政治立场比例）。现有防御有限：属性推断攻击已受关注，但保护数据集级秘密的防御不足；差分隐私对个体记录有效，对聚合属性保护较弱。

Method: QuanText 基于统计最大泄露（SML）框架。给定数据集级秘密（如某诊断比例）与需保效用的属性（如主题、情感），构建秘密与非秘密属性的候选发布分布，随机选择与私有经验分布足够接近的候选，并用原文中属性相关片段重写每条文本以匹配所选分布。方法无需训练、与 LLM 无关。

Result: 在理想条件下证明 QuanText 满足 SML 保证；因条件在实践中未必严格成立，在真实数据集上进行了实证评估，结果显示其隐私-效用权衡优于竞争的数据生成基线。

Conclusion: QuanText 提供了一种有效的文本数据集全局秘密保护方案，兼顾隐私与效用，优于现有基线。

Abstract: Natural-language datasets support many downstream applications and research studies, but releasing text can reveal sensitive global properties of the underlying data source, such as the proportion of records associated with a particular gender, diagnosis, or political stance. Existing work has largely focused on property inference attacks that recover such global properties, while defenses for protecting these dataset-level secrets remain limited. Differential privacy, although effective for protecting individual records, provides only weak protection for aggregate properties. We propose Randomized Quantization for Text (QuanText), a training-free and large-language-model-agnostic data release mechanism that protects global secrets in textual datasets while preserving data utility. Given a dataset-level secret, such as the proportion of records with a particular diagnosis, and attributes whose utility should be preserved, such as topic and sentiment, QuanText perturbs both the secret distribution and the distributions of correlated attributes. It does so by constructing candidate release distributions over secret and non-secret attributes, randomly selecting a candidate sufficiently close to the private empirical distribution, and rewriting each private text sample to match the selected distribution using attribute-related snippets from the original text. QuanText is inspired by the Statistic Maximal Leakage (SML) framework, which bounds leakage about a secret function of a data distribution. Under idealized conditions, we show that QuanText satisfies an SML guarantee. Since these conditions may not hold exactly in practice, we also evaluate QuanText empirically on real-world datasets. Our results show that QuanText achieves a better empirical privacy-utility trade-off than competing data generation baselines.

</details>


### [138] [Structural Inference under Hidden Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18045)
*Zhongben Gong,Xiaoqun Wu,Mingyang Zhou,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出SIHA，解决隐藏智能体下的结构推断问题，通过初始化加迭代精修联合恢复隐藏轨迹与交互结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体结构推断假设所有智能体轨迹可见，但实际因感知受限、遮挡或通信失败导致部分智能体不可观测。现有工作分别处理未见节点估计、部分观测下结构推断和缺失值填补，但隐藏智能体轨迹与其交互的联合恢复仍未被充分探索。

Method: 将问题形式化为隐藏智能体下的结构推断，核心难点是循环依赖：恢复隐藏智能体交互需其轨迹估计，而轨迹重建又受益于结构信息。SIHA结合结构无关初始化与结构引导的迭代精修，从可见观测重建隐藏轨迹，用Neural Relational Inference推断交互，并通过多强度结构注意力与迭代的状态-结构更新将结构反馈到隐藏状态重建。

Result: 在三个基准动力系统上，可见到可见的结构推断持续提升，同时在隐藏状态重建和未来预测上也有收益。模拟整肢遮挡的动作捕捉实验进一步验证了其在真实隐藏智能体场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: SIHA有效应对隐藏智能体下轨迹与结构联合恢复的循环依赖挑战，在结构推断、隐藏状态重建和预测上表现一致提升，并在真实遮挡场景中具备实用性。

Abstract: Recovering latent interaction structures from multi-agent dynamics is important for understanding and predicting interacting systems. Trajectory-based structural inference has achieved promising performance, but conventional formulations assume that the trajectories of all modeled agents are available. In practice, agents may become unobserved at deployment because of limited sensing, occlusion, or communication failure. Existing studies have considered unseen-node estimation, structural inference under partial observations, and missing-value imputation, yet the joint recovery of hidden-agent trajectories and their interactions remains underexplored. We formulate this problem as structural inference under hidden agents. Its key difficulty is a circular dependency: recovering interactions involving a hidden agent requires an estimate of its trajectory, while trajectory reconstruction can itself benefit from structural information. To address this challenge, we propose Structural Inference under Hidden Agents (SIHA), which combines structure-agnostic initialization with structure-guided iterative refinement. SIHA reconstructs hidden trajectories from visible observations, infers interactions using Neural Relational Inference, and feeds the estimated structure back into hidden-state reconstruction through multi-strength structural attention and iterative state--structure updates. Experiments on three benchmark dynamical systems demonstrate consistent improvements in visible-to-visible structural inference, while also showing benefits in hidden-state reconstruction and future prediction. Motion-capture experiments with simulated whole-limb occlusion further demonstrate its effectiveness in realistic hidden-agent settings.

</details>


### [139] [A Geometric Theory of Decision Boundaries in Structured Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18610)
*Fredy Pokou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化最优策略的几何理论，以决策边界几何作为核心分析对象，揭示其决定策略重建的最小表示、表示复杂度与查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典动态规划用值函数和策略表示最优决策，但最优策略固定后，支配策略重建、表示复杂度与oracle查询复杂度的数学对象并不明确。

Method: 构建策略诱导的决策边界几何理论；在结构正则条件下证明该几何是策略重建的最小表示；刻画边界与决策复杂度，提出决策压缩的信息论度量，并推导基于黑盒策略查询的边界估计与策略重建的统计保证。

Result: 证明策略重建复杂度由决策边界几何而非状态空间基数决定；给出边界估计与策略重建的统计保证；数值实验验证主要理论预测。

Conclusion: 在所考虑的结构化决策问题中，策略重建的复杂度由决策边界几何主导，而非环境状态空间的大小。

Abstract: Classical dynamic programming represents optimal sequential decisions through value functions and policies. While this functional representation is natural for computing optimal decisions, it does not directly identify the mathematical object governing policy reconstruction, representation complexity, or oracle-query complexity once an optimal policy is fixed. This paper addresses this question by developing a geometric theory of structured optimal policies in which the decision-boundary geometry induced by the policy becomes the primary object of analysis. We show that, under suitable structural regularity conditions, this geometry provides the minimal representation required for policy reconstruction and determines the statistical and computational complexity of the reconstruction problem. Building upon this representation, we establish structural properties of policy-induced decision geometry, introduce intrinsic notions of boundary and decision complexity, derive information-theoretic measures of decision compression, and obtain statistical guarantees for boundary estimation and policy reconstruction from black-box policy queries. Collectively, these results demonstrate that, for the structured decision problems considered here, the complexity of policy reconstruction is governed by the geometry of the decision boundary rather than by the cardinality of the ambient state space. Controlled numerical experiments examine the principal theoretical predictions and provide empirical evidence consistent with the proposed framework.

</details>


### [140] [Regional Explanations via Causal Sufficiency and Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18049)
*Xuexin Chen,Peng Liang,Zijian Li,Zhiyong Lin,Ruichu Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Causal Sufficient and Necessary Regional Explanations (SNRE) 框架，学习输入区域 A 与输出区域 B，使"输入属于 A"成为"模型输出落入 B"的充分且必要条件，并基于区域级 PNS 度量构建可微估计器进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释 AI 方法多通过特征重要性、反事实解释或规则来解释预测，但缺乏对"预测行为何时且仅何时发生"的区域级刻画。

Method: 受经典概率必要性充分性（PNS）启发，通过随机干预定义区域级 PNS 度量，推导可微有限样本估计器；用显式可解释的代数区域族加可学习特征掩码参数化输入输出区域对，兼顾表达力与可解释性。

Result: 实验表明 SNRE 学习到的区域对具有强充分性-必要性表现、稳健的解释行为，并在模型分析中具备实用价值。

Conclusion: SNRE 提供了区域级充分必要解释的新范式，为理解与信任机器学习模型提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Model explainability is essential for understanding and trusting machine learning models. Existing explainable AI methods often explain predictions through feature importance, counterfactual explanations, or rules. However, a region-level characterization of when and only when a prediction behavior arises remains less explored. This paper proposes Causal Sufficient and Necessary Regional Explanations (SNRE), a framework that learns an input region $A$ and output region $B$ such that membership in $A$ is both sufficient and necessary for the model output to fall in $B$. Motivated by the classical Probability of Necessity and Sufficiency (PNS), we formulate a region-level PNS measure through stochastic interventions and derive a differentiable finite-sample estimator for optimization. SNRE parameterizes the input-output region pair with explicit and interpretable algebraic region families, together with a learnable feature mask, balancing expressiveness and interpretability. Experiments demonstrate that SNRE learns region pairs with strong sufficiency-necessity performance, robust explanation behavior, and practical utility for model analysis.

</details>


### [141] [Beyond Embedding Transfer: Component Roles in Grokking Transfer and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18078)
*Zeyu Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究模块化算术上的跨算子迁移学习，区分迁移的"加速效应"（早期速度）与"稳定性"（达到后回撤），并揭示参数屏蔽的经验边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Warm-start迁移能让算法任务快速泛化，但尚不清楚哪些模型组件带来增益，以及该增益在持续优化下是否稳定。作者希望分离迁移的加速性与轨迹稳定性，并厘清哪些组件真正有效。

Method: 在模块化算术上做跨算子迁移实验：108次运行（12种子块的96次2^3因子设计+12次规模对照），对比迁移内部注意力/MLP权重(B)与token嵌入及readout(E+U)的组合；用配对种子、TOST等效性检验、Holm校正做统计；并进行2层前瞻复现；测试冻结迁移表示载体(E,U)和在线验证触发门控来抑制grokking后的回撤；在非阿贝尔S_5上做前瞻屏蔽队列。

Result: 迁移B+E+U使早期准确率提升5.46pp(Holm p=0.0039)，缩短确认延迟558步(Holm p=0.0088)；1层模型中readout+内部块迁移满足±500步延迟等效(TOST p=0.0011)；2层前瞻复现确认内部块优势(12/12种子,+704.67积分单位,p=4.88x10^-4)，但省去donor嵌入会低4475.6单位超出±250边界。持续训练常触发grokking后严重回撤；冻结E、U几乎消除离线回撤(19.40%→0.07%)；在线门控将True Max Drawdown从22.06%降至0.60%，并跨仿射、非线性二次、2层目标获前瞻确认(降低10.94-23.47pp)；S_5中无屏蔽迁移短暂冲高95.4%峰值但前瞻屏蔽无确认收益(+0.15±1.14pp)。

Conclusion: 结果建立了迁移加速与轨迹稳定性之间的组件级分离，并揭示了参数屏蔽的经验边界：内部块权重对加速有贡献，而冻结表示载体与在线门控可有效稳定轨迹，但屏蔽收益并非普遍存在（如S_5）。

Abstract: Warm-start transfer can make algorithmic tasks generalize rapidly, yet it is unclear which model components provide the gain and whether that gain remains stable under continued optimization. We study cross-operator transfer on modular arithmetic and separate efficacy (early velocity) from stability (post-reach drawdown). In a scale-matched 108-run battery across 12 seed blocks (96-run 2^3 factorial plus 12-run scale control), transferring internal attention/MLP weights (B) alongside token embeddings and readout (E+U) improves early accuracy by 5.46 pp (Holm p=0.0039) and cuts confirmation latency by 558 steps (Holm p=0.0088). While readout plus internal-block transfer satisfies the pre-specified +/-500-step latency equivalence criterion in 1-layer models (TOST p=0.0011, though Full is faster in 11/12 paired seeds), a prospective 2-layer replication confirms the internal-block advantage (12/12 seeds, +704.67 integral units, p=4.88x10^-4) while revealing an architectural boundary: omitting donor embeddings falls 4475.6 units below Full, outside the +/-250-unit margin. Continued target training frequently triggers severe post-grokking relapse. Freezing transferred representation carriers (E, U) nearly eliminates offline relapse (19.40% -> 0.07%, Holm p=0.005859). Online validation-triggered gating slashes True Max Drawdown from 22.06% to 0.60% on 2a+b (p=0.000488), with prospective confirmations extending protection across affine, nonlinear quadratic, and 2-layer targets (10.94-23.47 pp reductions), distinguishing continual stabilization from static early stopping. In non-abelian S_5, unshielded transfer surges transiently (95.4% peak), but a prospective shielding cohort yields no confirmed benefit (+0.15 +/- 1.14 pp). These results establish a component-level dissociation between transfer acceleration and trajectory stability, and expose the empirical boundaries of parameter shielding.

</details>


### [142] [A Convergence Framework for Deep $V$-Learning: Error Propagation and Sharp Action-Gap Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18782)
*Yury Kolomeytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为深度V-learning建立收敛界，将更新误差分解为六种残差，在集中性条件下控制策略损失，并给出采样分配、误差率与神经网络回归率等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度V-learning缺乏显式的收敛界限与误差分解，且需厘清各来源残差如何影响策略损失，以及采样分布、近似分数与网络逼近带来的误差。

Method: 将更新误差分解为拟合、转移复用、目标构造、回放、动作选择、探索六类残差；在L^s集中性下用L^p范数控制期望L^1策略损失；推导最优连续与整数采样分配；结合边界条件、生存概率、空间ReLU网络与有限状态分析。

Result: 得到显式加权最后H-1个更新块的界；统计误差n^{-ν}下整数分配目标在2^ν因子内最优；边界条件下动作误差阶Λ^{1+α/p}且指数紧；给出ReLU网络条件回归率与有限状态无对数拟合率。

Conclusion: 为固定视界生成重置近似ERM给出期望策略损失一致性，并提供FIFO/交错SGD的显式残差衰减准则。

Abstract: We establish convergence bounds for deep $V$-learning with horizon $H$. The algorithm fits a scalar value function to targets from executed transitions and selects actions using a predictive model and the value function. For current observed-successor targets with fresh true-kernel outcomes, the conditional mean is $\mathcal{T}^βV$, which averages over behavior-policy actions. The Bellman optimality update is $\mathcal{T} V$. We decompose the update error into six residuals: fitting, transition reuse, target construction, replay, action selection, and exploration. Under $L^s$ concentrability, their $L^p$ norms ($p=s/(s-1)$) control expected $L^1$ policy loss. The bound explicitly weights residuals from only the last $H-1$ update blocks, plus an initialization term for shorter runs. We quantify the cost of a shared sampling distribution across horizon levels. For statistical error bounds of order $n^{-ν}$, we derive optimal continuous allocations and an integer allocation whose objective is within a factor $2^ν$ of the constrained optimum. A margin condition with exponent $α$ gives action error of order $Λ^{1+α/p}$, where $Λ$ combines network drift and score error; a one-step construction proves the exponent sharp. Bounds on the distance between frozen and optimal scores transfer an optimal-gap condition to frozen-iterate gap bounds while retaining the mass of optimal ties. Survival probabilities and coverage conditions at deployment yield bounds for policies selected with approximate scores. Separate spatial ReLU networks per horizon level give a conditional neural regression rate, and the finite-state case gives a log-free expected fit rate. These results give expected policy-loss consistency for the fixed-horizon generative-reset approximate-ERM procedure with exact action scores and provide an explicit residual-decay criterion for FIFO/interleaved SGD.

</details>


### [143] [FedPGT: Progressive Gradient Transmission for Vehicular Federated Learning over Time-Varying Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18089)
*Jintao Yan,Tan Chen,Yuxuan Sun,Sheng Zhou,Zhisheng Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedPGT，一种面向时变信道的车载联邦学习渐进式梯度传输方案，通过渐进传输高幅值梯度项并利用Lyapunov在线调度，实现通信高效的在线决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载联邦学习（VFL）中，通信资源分配与梯度稀疏化技术被用于降低通信开销，但车辆移动性导致信道条件与传输容量快速变化，使预先确定的资源分配与稀疏化决策失效。

Method: 提出FedPGT渐进式梯度传输方案，车辆根据瞬时信道条件渐进传输高幅值梯度项；建立收敛界揭示由幂律衰减主导的递减收益行为；将在线决策建模为随机优化问题，引入每时隙代理传输变量解耦长期耦合的非可分目标，转化为可加性逐时隙优化问题，并采用Lyapunov drift-plus-penalty方法进行在线调度，同时开发低复杂度资源分配算法。

Result: 在CIFAR-10图像分类任务上准确率提升3.65%，在Argoverse轨迹预测任务上平均位移误差降低12.66%，优于现有最优基线。

Conclusion: FedPGT在高度动态的车载环境下适用于多种学习任务，验证了其在时变信道中实现高效车载联邦学习的有效性。

Abstract: Vehicular federated learning (VFL) enables privacy-preserving collaborative model training for intelligent transportation systems, where communication resource allocation and gradient sparsification techniques have been explored to reduce communication overhead. However, vehicle mobility leads to rapidly varying channel conditions and transmission capacity, rendering predetermined resource allocation and sparsification decisions ineffective. In this paper, we propose FedPGT, a progressive gradient transmission scheme for VFL over time-varying channels, where vehicles progressively transmit high-magnitude gradient entries in response to instantaneous channel conditions. We establish a convergence bound that characterizes the impact of transmitted gradient entries and reveals diminishing-return behavior governed by a power-law decay. Motivated by this result, we formulate a stochastic optimization problem for online decision-making, where the main challenge lies in a cumulatively coupled, non-separable objective. To handle this challenge, we introduce per-slot surrogate transmission variables to decouple the long-term dependence across time slots and convert the original objective into an additive per-slot optimization problem, enabling a Lyapunov drift-plus-penalty approach for online scheduling. We further develop a low-complexity resource allocation algorithm for efficient online implementation. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed scheme achieves a 3.65% accuracy improvement on the CIFAR-10 image classification task and a 12.66% reduction in average displacement error on the Argoverse trajectory prediction task compared with state-of-the-art baselines, demonstrating its applicability to diverse learning tasks under highly dynamic vehicular environments.

</details>


### [144] [Agora: Git as Shared Memory for Collective AutoResearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18094)
*Yifan Zhang,Yunheng Zou,Shaokun Zhang,Jian Hu,Hao Zhang,Binfeng Xu,Jan Kautz,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Agora 是一个基于 Git 的只追加 DAG 共享记忆系统，让多个编码智能体在无中央调度下协作科研；首次持续运行近12天，13个模型工作者解决权重迁移问题，将评估从3.39改进到1.899 bits/byte。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主研究循环（如 AutoResearch）中，单个编码智能体可无人值守地改进训练配置，但多个智能体并行时各自从零开始，导致重复搜索而非更多发现。需要一个共享的研究状态来协调这类智能体。

Method: Agora 将研究记录为存储在 Git 中的只追加有向无环图（DAG）：每个结果、洞见、假设、验证和报告都是不可变提交，父边表示其依赖关系；派生索引暴露前沿、被忽视分支与各声明的验证状态；多样性感知选择规则防止群体集中于单一领先者。任务设定为：给定141个预训练捐赠模型和一个与任何捐赠模型维度都不匹配的冻结119.6M参数 attention-SSM 混合目标模型，在无训练数据和梯度更新的条件下初始化目标模型。

Result: 近12天运行中，13个语言模型工作者在无指派任务、无中央规划者的情况下发布1,703条贡献，将评估器从3.39提升到1.899 bits per byte，缩小了与训练后 GPT-2 124M 之间62%的差距。获胜方案将捐赠模型的下一token统计压缩进目标模型的 embedding 和输出头，再通过稀疏编辑 attention、前馈和状态空间块加入短程上下文信号；其145个提交的祖先跨越15个账户，并有165次独立复现，全部成功。

Conclusion: 论文描述了运行中的一次人工干预（将社区拉出单一化/单培养状态），说明了该轨迹能证明与不能证明的内容，并提出需要受控对比实验才能确定共享研究状态是否提升单位算力的发现效率。

Abstract: Autonomous research loops such as AutoResearch show that one coding agent can improve a training setup unattended. Run several of them and each session starts from scratch, so more agents tend to mean more duplicated search rather than more discovery. Agora is a shared memory for such agents: research is recorded as an append-only directed acyclic graph (DAG) stored in Git, so that every claim is a commit anyone can check out and rerun. Each result, insight, hypothesis, verification, and report is an immutable commit whose parent edges say what it builds on; a derived index exposes the frontier, the neglected branches, and the verification status of each claim, and a diversity-aware selection rule keeps the community from collapsing onto one leader. We describe the system and report its first sustained use: a run of nearly 12 days in which 13 language-model workers, with no assigned tasks and no central planner, worked on a weight-transfer problem. Given 141 pretrained donor models and a frozen 119.6M-parameter attention-SSM hybrid whose dimensions match no donor, the workers had to initialize the target without training data or gradient updates. They published 1,703 contributions and drove the evaluator from 3.39 to 1.899 bits per byte, closing 62% of the gap to a trained GPT-2 124M. The winning recipe compresses donor next-token statistics into the target's embedding and output head, then adds a short-range context signal through sparse edits to attention, feed-forward, and state-space blocks. Its 145-commit ancestry spans 15 accounts, and 165 independent reproductions were posted, none of which failed. We describe the single mid-run human intervention that pulled the community out of a monoculture, what the trace does and does not establish, and the controlled comparison that would settle whether shared research state improves discovery per unit of compute.

</details>


### [145] [iMINDBench: iEEG Multi-Institution Neural Decoding Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18104)
*Geeling Chau,Saba Hashemi,Yonghyeon Gwon,Eshani Patel,Jan DeWitt,Christopher Wang,Andrii Zahorodnii,Sabera J Talukder,Danny Dongyeop Han,Chun Kee Chung,Maryam M Shanechi,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出iMINDBench，一个跨三机构、十五项任务的iEEG神经解码基准，用于可靠衡量模型泛化性。


<details>
