Under a controlled leave-one-defocus-out evaluation, the physics anchor achieved R^2 = 0.778, against -0.001 for the black-box model and 0.750 for the gated hybrid. Under leave-one-group-out cross-validation, the gated hybrid reached R^2 = 0.592 against 0.538 for the black-box model. Retained-set coverage was 92.9% at a mean full width of 3.65 multiples of a uniform distribution (MUD) under grouped cross-validation and 100% at a width of 3.21 MUD under transfer to a withheld +80 mm defocus regime. An illustrative mapping produced a retained BD elastic-modulus span of 127-187 GPa. On nine conditions from a separately built sample set, the framework withheld three, attenuated three, and matched the measured ordering for the rest. Separating data applicability, physics validity, and predictive uncertainty into distinct decisions lets the framework transfer where an unconstrained model does not, and withhold predictions where no model class performs adequately.

</details>


### [181] [NeuroECG: ECGFounder-Based Deep ECG Representation for EEG-Free Neurological Prognostication After Cardiac Arrest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18891)
*Jiaju Gao,Yi Zhao,Chenyang Xu,Yuxi Zhou,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroECG：基于ECGFounder的心电深度表征框架，无需EEG即可辅助预测心脏骤停后神经预后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏骤停后神经预后评估常用EEG，但EEG资源消耗高；而床边ECG标准且低成本，其预测价值尚未被充分挖掘。

Method: 采用预训练ECG基础模型(ECGFounder)进行任务微调，使用渐进解冻策略处理单通道床边监测ECG；将每患者多个ECG片段编码为片段级深度特征，通过分位数池化(q=0.24)聚合，并用PCA压缩；再与静态临床协变量融合。

Result: 在I-CARE多中心数据库412例患者上，微调后的ECGFounder骨干在纯ECG基线中表现最佳，测试AUROC为0.7333；融合临床协变量后，NeuroECG测试AUROC达0.8077，AUPRC为0.8970。

Conclusion: 深度床边ECG表征可作为有用的辅助预后信息来源，与静态临床协变量整合能在无EEG条件下提升预测性能。

Abstract: Neurological prognostication after cardiac arrest commonly relies on electroencephalography (EEG). However, EEG demands high clinical resources. Bedside electrocardiography (ECG) is standard and low-cost. Yet, its value for predicting neurological outcomes remains underexplored. In this study, we propose NeuroECG, an ECGFounder-based deep representation framework for EEG-free auxiliary prognostication. NeuroECG adapts a pretrained ECG foundation model via task-specific fine-tuning. We implement a gradual unfreezing strategy on single-channel bedside monitoring ECG. Multiple ECG segments per patient are encoded into segment-level deep features. These embeddings are aggregated via quantile pooling ($q = 0.24$) and compressed using principal component analysis (PCA). Experiments on 412 ECG-available patients from the multicenter I-CARE database show that the adapted ECGFounder backbone achieves the best performance among ECG-only backbone baselines, with a test AUROC of 0.7333. We further combine the learned deep ECG representation with static clinical covariates. The proposed NeuroECG model achieves a test AUROC of 0.8077 and an AUPRC of 0.8970. These results support deep bedside ECG representations as a useful source of auxiliary prognostic information. Their integration with static clinical covariates improves prediction in an EEG-free setting. The source code is available at https://github.com/goddream66/NeuroECG_

</details>


### [182] [Higher-order pruning of experts in mixture-of-experts language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18916)
*Alex M. Tseng,Prannay Kaul,Luca Zancato,Wei Xia,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HOPE，一种高阶专家剪枝方法，通过考虑专家间协作关系最小化剪枝误差上界，在高剪枝率下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE语言模型参数量大导致内存瓶颈，现有专家剪枝方法对每个专家独立决策并假设贡献纯加性，忽略了专家间的协作性。

Method: 推导HOPE（Higher-Order Pruning of Experts），一个二阶剪枝目标，可证明地最小化剪枝引起的误差上界；并指出REAP（一阶方法）是忽略交互项时HOPE的特例。

Result: 在三种前沿MoE模型（最大122B参数）、两个校准集、多基准（数学、指令跟随、编码、智能体）上测试。50%剪枝率下HOPE平均排名1.58/5（次优REAP为2.42），智能体编码提升最高+6.1%。

Conclusion: HOPE通过保留一阶方法忽视的专家协作结构，实现了激进压缩下的最小性能退化，在复杂长序列任务中尤其有效。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models suffer from large parameter counts, which create a significant memory bottleneck. Expert pruning is the most direct approach for reducing this parameter count, yet existing methods make pruning decisions for each expert independently, and assume experts' contributions are purely additive. In reality, expert usage in MoEs is inherently cooperative. We derive HOPE (Higher-Order Pruning of Experts), a second-order pruning objective which provably minimizes an upper bound on the error resulting from pruning. We show that REAP (a state-of-the-art first-order pruning method) is a special case of HOPE where interaction terms are ignored. Across three frontier MoE models (up to 122B parameters), two distinct calibration sets, and multiple benchmarks (including math, instruction following, coding, and an agentic suite), we demonstrate that HOPE produces better pruning decisions than existing methods, and its advantage is most pronounced at high pruning rates and on challenging agentic workloads. At 50% pruning, HOPE outperforms all baselines and achieves an average rank of 1.58 out of 5 methods (versus 2.42 for the next-best method, REAP), with gains of up to +6.1% on agentic coding. Over all conditions, HOPE again achieves the best average rank and surpasses every other method in the majority of head-to-head comparisons. By preserving cooperative expert structure that first-order methods ignore, HOPE enables aggressive compression with minimal degradation, particularly on complex tasks where diverse expert combinations are invoked over long sequences.

</details>


### [183] [StableEval Arena: A Cost-Aware Agentic Benchmark for Stablecoin Price Stability Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18949)
*Sean Wan,Dongping Liu,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出StableEval Arena，一个成本感知的基准框架，用于评估LLM智能体在稳定币锚定风险预测任务上的表现，涵盖预测质量、可靠性与推理成本的多维度评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测多以单一准确率为标准，忽视了金融风险场景中可信度应同时包含预测质量、运行可靠性（结构化输出、延迟、成本）与真实风险识别能力。稳定币锚定风险预测尤其需要兼顾成本与稳健性，缺乏专门的基准与防泄漏评测方法。

Method: 构建StableEval Arena框架：采用防泄漏历史回放，结合交易所价量数据与市场上下文特征，让LLM驱动的智能体诊断锚定压力并预测未来隐藏七日内的价格偏离。设置两个实验块：120例压力增强验证集与507例自然分布全场景评测集，对六种LLM智能体配置及基线进行对比，测量预测质量、校准标签行为、结构化输出可靠性、延迟、token消耗和推理成本估计。

Result: 结果显示：智能体能以较低成本稳定产出合规的结构化输出，但在识别稀有严重压力和持续脱锚案例上仍大量漏检，暴露出"协议遵循可靠性"与"金融风险可靠性"之间的显著差距。

Conclusion: StableEval Arena将可信度视为预测质量、运行可靠性与计算成本的联合属性，而非单纯按准确率排名。为支持审计与复现，作者在Hugging Face发布基准数据集，在GitHub公开源代码。

Abstract: We introduce StableEval Arena, a cost-aware benchmark framework for evaluating agentic AI systems on stablecoin peg-risk prediction. StableEval Arena evaluates LLM-backed agentic systems on diagnosing peg stress and forecasting deviations from the one-dollar peg over a hidden seven-day horizon, using leakage-safe historical replay with exchange price-volume data and market-context features. We report two complementary experiment blocks: a 120-case stress-enriched validation block and a 507-case natural-distribution full-arena evaluation block. Across six LLM-backed agent configurations and baselines, StableEval Arena measures prediction quality, calibrated-label behavior, structured-output reliability, latency, token consumption, and estimated inference cost. Rather than ranking agents by accuracy alone, the framework treats trustworthiness as a joint property of forecast quality, operational reliability, and computational cost. The results show a gap between protocol-following reliability and financial-risk reliability: agents reliably produce valid structured outputs at modest measured cost, but still miss most rare severe-stress and sustained-depeg cases. To support auditing and replication, we release the benchmark dataset on Hugging Face and the source code on GitHub.

</details>


### [184] [Changepoint-Aware World Models: Detecting Dynamics Shifts and Recovering by Forgetting Stale Replay in Model-Based RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18950)
*Everest Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAWM让DreamerV3智能体通过CUSUM在线检测动力学突变，并在检测到时遗忘过时回放数据、保留表示，从而在机器人动力学变化后快速恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学到的自身动力学模型在动力学变化后失效：执行器磨损、载荷变化、关节变硬等都会使旧经验过时；普通持续训练策略会被满是过时数据的回放缓冲拖累，适应缓慢。

Method: 提出Changepoint-Aware World Models（CAWM）：基于DreamerV3，用自身内部预测误差，通过在线CUSUM检验对滚动基线进行突变检测，只对突变而非缓慢学习漂移做出响应；检测到后遗忘过时回放数据但保留已学表示。

Result: 在模拟运动任务的两种机器人相关突变（重力加倍、执行器增益减半）下，CAWM恢复显著快于被动重训练；也优于在检测时重生全新动力学模型的强基线（模型库方法的深度世界模型类比）。突变触发响应时，三个随机种子在突变后前30k帧获得+95到+153回报，并渐近匹配重生基线；闭环运行检测器在重力突变上重现该增益。

Conclusion: 该方法在两种突变类型下均有效，且当突变严重到旧数据确实过时的时候收益最大。

Abstract: A robot's learned model of its own dynamics is only valid until those dynamics change: actuators wear, payloads shift, and joints stiffen. A model-based agent that keeps training as if nothing happened adapts slowly, dragged back by a replay buffer full of stale experience. We present Changepoint-Aware World Models (CAWM), a DreamerV3 agent that detects an abrupt dynamics shift from its own internal prediction error, using an online CUSUM test against a rolling baseline that fires only on abrupt change rather than on slow learning drift. It then forgets stale replay, keeping the learned representation while flushing obsolete data. On simulated locomotion under two robot-relevant shifts, doubled gravity and halved actuator gain, CAWM recovers substantially faster than passive retraining. It also beats a strong baseline that respawns a fresh dynamics model on detection, the deep-world-model analogue of model-bank methods. With the response triggered at the shift, CAWM gains +95 to +153 return in the first 30k post-shift frames over three seeds, while matching that respawn at asymptote. Running the detector in closed loop reproduces this gain on the gravity shift. The benefit holds across both shift types, and is largest when the shift is severe enough that old data is genuinely obsolete.

</details>


### [185] [FedGuide: Diffusion Prior Alignment and Value Baseline Guidance for Heterogeneous Federated Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18964)
*Zhilin He,Gauri Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedGuide，一种利用扩散先验作为行为模型为异构客户端提供个性化数据分布支持，并通过OT-MoE聚合扩散先验、用DICE价值基线引导本地策略改进的联邦强化学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FRL方法（方差缩减、散度惩罚、动量优化）主要同步策略或价值网络参数，未显式处理异构客户端间的分布不匹配问题。

Method: 用扩散先验作为行为模型，为异构本地策略学习提供个性化数据支持分布；通过最优传输专家混合（OT-MoE）在分布空间聚合扩散先验以保留异构行为模式；并结合分布校正估计（DICE）价值基线，为本地策略改进提供低方差、回报感知的引导。

Result: 在多种异构环境下，FedGuide在客户端平均回报、末轮性能与最差轮鲁棒性上均优于代表性FRL方法，且在更强异构性下保持稳定学习。

Conclusion: FedGuide通过在分布空间聚合扩散先验并引入DICE价值基线，有效应对异构环境下的分布不匹配，为联邦强化学习提供了更鲁棒的协作策略学习方案。

Abstract: Federated Reinforcement Learning (FRL) enables collaborative policy learning across distributed agents with heterogeneous environments. While recent methods based on variance reduction, divergence penalization, and momentum optimization improve FRL under heterogeneous settings, they still primarily synchronize policy or value-network parameters and do not explicitly address distributional mismatch among heterogeneous clients. Therefore, we propose \textbf{FedGuide}, a FRL framework that uses diffusion priors as behavior models to provide personalized data supported distributions for heterogeneous local policy learning. Instead of directly averaging local policies, FedGuide aggregates those diffusion priors through Optimal-Transport Mixture-of-Experts (OT-MoE), preserving heterogeneous behavior modes in distribution space. It further develops a Distribution Correction Estimation (DICE) value baseline to provide low-variance, return-aware guidance for local policy improvement. Experiments across heterogeneous environments show that FedGuide outperforms representative FRL methods in client-average returns, final-round performance, and worst-round robustness, while maintaining stable learning under stronger heterogeneity.

</details>


### [186] [The Automaton Underneath: The Additive Input Pathway Is a Parasitic Attractor for State Tracking in Householder Linear RNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18966)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 可证明表达状态跟踪自动机的线性RNN（DeltaNet类）实际训练后无法长度泛化。本文通过预注册的因果消融发现：删除加性输入注入项 b_t=W_b e_t 后，同一架构能学到精确自动机并在16倍训练长度上泛化；加性通路是"寄生"的，会先破坏后掩盖已正确学到的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带输入依赖Householder乘积转移的线性RNN理论上能表示硬状态跟踪自动机，但训练后无法长度泛化。近期工作将此归因于优化问题，却缺乏因果解释。本文旨在给出因果性说明。

Method: 在架构内进行预注册的因果消融：仅删除加性输入注入项 b_t=W_b e_t，输入仅通过正交转移作用。提出并检验一条表示律：每token所需Householder因子的最小数等于任务生成元在格式钉定表示下的最大反射长度（parity 1、S_4 3、A_5/S_5 4）。另设两个预注册臂定位机制：(i)以W_b=0的验证精确解初始化，观察Adam行为；(ii)检验拟合是否经由b_t路由。

Result: 带b_t时，模型拟合长度32但在分布外崩塌（S_5位置512仅0.20）。无b_t时，同架构学到精确自动机：16倍训练长度上中位精度1.00，且在表示律允许的所有宽度成立。与DeltaProduct的S_4/A_5 (n_h=2, 群元素分类, SO(3)实现)对比，显示该律是表示相对的，任务格式是状态跟踪基准中的隐藏变量。机制臂：(i) Adam使加性通路增长并将模型拉离精确解，而-b对照保持1.00；(ii) 在律最小宽度下推理时将W_b置零可恢复精确泛化（parity 5/5, S_5 5/5, S_4 4/5, A_5 4/5），49个拟合种子在W_b:=0下均保持域内拟合。

Conclusion: 加性输入通路是寄生的：它先破坏、再掩盖已正确学到的自动机。全部202次运行均预注册，所有数值可由工件重现。任务格式是状态跟踪基准的隐藏变量，表示律是表示相对的。

Abstract: Linear RNNs with input-dependent Householder-product transitions (DeltaNet/DeltaProduct-class) can provably represent hard state-tracking automata, yet trained models fail to length-generalize -- a gap recent work attributes to optimization, without a causal account. We give one, in a pre-registered, within-architecture causal ablation: the same model with one term deleted -- the additive input injection $b_t = W_b e_t$. With $b_t$, models fit length 32 and collapse out-of-distribution on parity, $S_4$, $A_5$, and non-solvable $S_5$ word problems (0.20 at position 512 on $S_5$). Without it -- input acting only through the orthogonal transitions -- the same architecture learns the exact automaton: median accuracy 1.00 at 16x the training length, at every width admitted by a representation law we state and test: the minimal number of Householder factors per token equals the maximal reflection length of the task's generators in the format-pinned representation (parity 1, $S_4$ 3, $A_5$ and $S_5$ 4); below it, nothing fits. The contrast with DeltaProduct's $S_4$/$A_5$ at $n_h{=}2$ (group-element classification, $SO(3)$ realization) shows the law is representation-relative: task format is a hidden variable in state-tracking benchmarks. Two pre-registered arms locate the mechanism. (i) Initialized at a verified-exact solution with $W_b{=}0$, Adam grows the additive path and pulls the model off the exact solution; $-b$ controls stay at 1.00. (ii) Our registered prediction that the fit routes through $b_t$ fired its kill criterion: all 49 fitting seeds retain in-domain fit under $W_b{:=}0$ -- and at law-minimal width, zeroing $W_b$ at inference restores exact generalization (parity 5/5, $S_5$ 5/5, $S_4$ 4/5, $A_5$ 4/5). The additive pathway is parasitic: it destabilizes, then conceals, a correctly learned automaton. All 202 runs pre-registered; all numbers regenerate from artifacts.

</details>


### [187] [One Axis, No Brake: Self-Knowledge Limits the Filtering of Harmful Peer Conformity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18998)
*Yibo Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM系统的"刹车"机制（保留有益修正、阻止有害修正）难以构建，因为判断修正是否有害等价于判断原答案是否正确，这归结为模型的自我认知能力，而该能力远非完美（AUROC约0.64–0.89）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统被认为更可靠（可互相纠错），但同伴压力是双刃剑：既能纠正错误答案，也能推翻正确答案。于是想构建一个"刹车"来只保留有益修正。但作者指出这一刹车本质上难以实现。

Method: 将"寻找刹车"的问题转化为可度量的量——模型的自我认知（self-knowledge）。论证任何基于部署时信号的刹车本质上都是正确性探针。并在六个模型族上测量自我认知（AUROC），尝试白盒引导模型自身的正确性方向，以及在群体规模下观察多智能体社会（不同数量、多样性、更强成员）的表现。

Result: 自我认知AUROC约0.64–0.89，远非完美，构成"墙"（wall）上限。白盒引导正确性方向也无法突破：它改变修正频率，但有害与有益修正同向变化。群体规模下"墙"变成"悬崖"（cliff）：多数智能体初始错误时，辩论会将共同错误放大为自信的错误共识；更多智能体、更大模型多样性、更强成员都无济于事。

Conclusion: 应在修正前增加信息，而非修正后过滤。局部一致不等于全局正确。可构建的刹车上限由自我认知决定，因此难以突破。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems are expected to be more reliable because agents can catch each other's mistakes. But peer pressure cuts both ways: the same correction that fixes a wrong answer can overturn a right one. The tempting safeguard is a brake that keeps the beneficial revisions and blocks the harmful ones. We show this brake is hard to build, for a simple reason: a revision is harmful exactly when the original answer was right, so deciding whether to block it is the same as knowing whether the model was already correct. This turns the open-ended hunt for a brake into one measurable quantity, the model's self-knowledge: any brake built from a deploy-time signal is a correctness probe in disguise, and self-knowledge is far from perfect (AUROC $\approx 0.64$--$0.89$ across six model families). We call this ceiling the wall. Even white-box steering of the model's own correctness direction does not breach it: it changes how often the model revises, but harmful and beneficial revisions move together. At population scale the wall becomes the cliff: when most agents start wrong, debate amplifies the shared mistake into a confident, wrong consensus. In our multiple-choice societies, more agents, more model diversity, and a stronger member do not fix it. What helps is adding information before the revision, not filtering after it. Local agreement is not global correctness.

</details>


### [188] [Capability Emergence Can Be Forecast: Per-Seed, In Advance, With Calibrated Intervals, Certified False Alarms, and a Blind Pre-Registered Gate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19000)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将"涌现能力预测"从早期预警升级为可校准的定量预测框架，在grokking系统与小型语言模型中实现了按运行、提前、带不确定性的涌现时机预测，并通过预注册盲测严格验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作只提供涌现的"早期预警指标"，但从未将其作为预测来评分：没有在受控误报率下的提前时间、没有校准、没有负样本、没有盲测。作者希望为涌现预测建立严格的评估纪律。

Method: 在30个同配置Transformer中，用前一token head的形成时间预测各随机种子的induction head涌现；采用共形区间进行校准；设计trap-language测试攻击自身规则；通过gap-origin研究揭示乘法定律（t_event≈1.19×t_anchor）；所有冻结规则均先于数据、记录在公开提交链上，并进行预注册盲测。

Result: 前一token head以Spearman rho=0.977、中位提前975步（约15%训练量）预测induction head涌现；共形区间覆盖15/15留出种子，并在两个未见配置通过盲测（10/10、9/10）。机制组合规则在两类语言中0误报且rho=1.000，而单一前兆在trap任务中误报10/10。乘法规则在第三方未见配置通过盲测（5/5），误报经33个负样本验证；前兆在Pythia、OLMo、OLMo-2等家族中均领先。

Conclusion: 涌现时机在逐运行层面是可预测的，且可用校准不确定性量化。研究通过预注册、盲测、负样本和公开提交链建立了严格的预测评估范式，并揭示了锚点与涌现时间之间的乘法定律。四条预注册的kill criteria被触发并如实报告。

Abstract: Emergent capabilities are widely treated as unpredictable: loss improves smoothly while abilities appear abruptly. Prior work offers early-warning indicators but never scores them as forecasts: no lead time at controlled false-alarm rate, no calibration, no negatives, no blind tests. We supply that discipline and show that, in grokking model systems and small language models, emergence timing is forecastable per run, in advance, with calibrated uncertainty. Across 30 transformers at identical configuration, the formation time of the previous-token head forecasts each seed's induction-head emergence at Spearman rho=0.977 with median lead 975 steps (~15% of training); a best-case loss rule ties the ranking with 50-step lead (a nowcast). Conformal intervals covered 15/15 held-out seeds, and the frozen rule passed blind pre-registered gates on TWO never-seen configurations (10/10 and 9/10 coverage). A trap-language rung then attacked our own rule as pre-registered: where previous-token context pays for the task itself, the bare precursor false-alarms on 10/10 capability-blocked runs, while the mechanism-composed conjunction is certified in both language classes (0 false alarms) and times emergence at rho=1.000. Finally, a gap-origin study broke the fixed offset (both lr and batch move the gap ~2.3x; no external clock owns it) and revealed the law beneath: across 80 valid-anchor runs the anchor fires at 0.843 of time-to-emergence -- t_event ~= 1.19 x t_anchor -- and this multiplicative rule passed its own blind gate (5/5) at a third unseen configuration. False alarms are certified against 33 manufactured negatives. The precursor leads across 3 public model families (Pythia, OLMo, OLMo-2; 7 suites), with OLMo-2 at 1B tokens showing precursor formed, capability absent. Four pre-registered kill criteria fired and are reported. Every freeze precedes its data in a public commit chain.

</details>


### [189] [CompileRover: Revolutionizing Virtual Machine Compiler Optimization with a Tri-Role LLM-Driven Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19004)
*Mingqiao Mo,Yunlong Tan,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CompileRover框架，通过三角色协作机制优化虚拟机编译器的冗余计算、低效循环与次优函数实现，性能超越现有虚拟机编译器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚拟机编译器输出常存在冗余计算、低效循环结构和次优函数实现，影响执行效率，亟需专门的优化框架。

Method: CompileRover采用referee、advisor、operator三角色协作机制，结合控制流分析、代码结构变换和动态执行模式识别等综合优化算法。

Result: 在多种基准测试上持续超越最先进的虚拟机编译器，显著提升执行性能，降低执行开销，改善数据流一致性。

Conclusion: CompileRover是有效且可靠的虚拟机编译器优化方案，能稳健提升编译器性能。

Abstract: Code optimization plays a crucial role in the development of virtual machine compilers, with optimization frameworks significantly enhancing the performance of generated assembly code. However, existing virtual machine compiler outputs frequently exhibit redundant computations, inefficient loop structures, and suboptimal function implementations, which collectively impair execution efficiency. To address these shortcomings, we propose CompileRover, an advanced optimization framework specifically designed for virtual machine compilers. CompileRover employs a sophisticated three-role collaboration mechanism, comprising a referee, an advisor, and an operator, effectively overcoming performance bottlenecks by leveraging comprehensive optimization algorithms and novel methodologies, including control flow analysis, code structure transformations, and dynamic execution pattern recognition. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that CompileRover consistently surpasses state-of-the-art virtual machine compilers, achieving significant improvements in execution performance across various benchmarks. Furthermore, performance analyses validate that the introduced optimizations notably reduce execution overhead, improve dataflow consistency, and robustly enhance compiler performance, showcasing CompileRover as an effective and reliable approach to optimizing virtual machine compilers.

</details>


### [190] [Tabular Deep Learning vs Classical Machine Learning for Urban Land Cover Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19010)
*Muntasir Tabasum,Tanpia Tasnim,Md. Ekramul Islam,Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在ULC数据集上统一比较经典机器学习与表格深度学习模型，发现树集成是强基线，而TDL在非线性交互显著且不平衡处理得当时可媲美或超越，两者互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市土地覆盖分类对城市规划、环境监测和可持续发展至关重要，但该任务面临高维、异构特征和类别不平衡等遥感典型挑战，需系统比较经典机器学习与表格深度学习方法。

Method: 使用UCI的ULC数据集（来自高分辨率航空影像的表格特征，九类），在统一可复现流程中基准测试经典模型（Logistic Regression、SVM、Random Forest、XGBoost、CatBoost）与TDL模型（TabNet、FT-Transformer、TabTransformer、TabSeq、1D CNN）；用加权交叉熵损失处理类别不平衡。

Result: 树集成仍是强通用基线；当非线性交互显著且不平衡处理有效时，TDL模型可匹配或超越其表现。

Conclusion: 两类方法为城市土地覆盖制图提供互补优势，TDL是经典方法的有力补充。

Abstract: Urban Land Cover (ULC) classification plays a crucial role in urban planning, environmental monitoring, and sustainable development. We study this task using the ULC dataset from the UCI Machine Learning Repository, which includes tabular features derived from high-resolution aerial imagery across nine classes (e.g., roads, trees, grass, water). The dataset presents typical remote sensing challenges, including high dimensionality, heterogeneous features, and class imbalance. In a unified, reproducible pipeline, we benchmark classical machine learning models (e.g., Logistic Regression, SVM, Random Forest, XGBoost, CatBoost) against Tabular Deep Learning (TDL) models (TabNet, FT-Transformer, TabTransformer, TabSeq, and 1D CNNs). To address class imbalance, we employ weighted cross-entropy loss for TDL models and evaluate performance using accuracy, macro-precision, macro-recall, macro-F1, AUC-ROC, and confusion matrices. Our results show that while tree ensembles remain strong general baselines, TDL models can match or exceed their performance when non-linear interactions are significant and imbalance handling is effective, providing complementary advantages for urban land cover mapping. See code: https://github.com/mtesha/tdl-vs-ml-urbanlandcover

</details>


### [191] [TwinMark: A Unified Watermark for Provable Survival Under Feature and Logit Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19011)
*Redwanul Karim,Tobias Feigl,Christopher Mutschler,Felix Ott*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TwinMark 提出一种水印方案，用两个互补线性泛函从同一个 SHAKE128 密钥读取比特，覆盖分类器 API 和只读表示两种提取场景，并对蒸馏攻击提供可测证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印难以同时覆盖分类器 API 与仅表示宿主，且缺乏对知识蒸馏攻击检测能力的可证下界，因此需要一种共享密钥、双通道读出的鲁棒水印。

Method: 通过 cov-Feat（对载体集协方差的投影）和 cc-FALC（由类均值 logits 解码的 Fisher 对齐线性载体）两条线性泛函共享同一比特向量；两通道用受限于场景的 OR 规则结合，测试统计量按暴露面选择，并分别给出算子范数与类间 logit 间隔证书。

Result: 在 m=100 类、K=1024 比特（10.24 倍过编码）下比特投票达 z=23.0σ，教师精度代价仅 +0.9±0.2 个百分点；在 CIFAR-10/100 和 Mini-ImageNet 的 13 种攻击中，凡保留任务效用的模型均验证成功，并跨架构蒸馏到 ResNet-18/50、VGG-16、MobileNet-V3，迁移至 GNSS 小样本、VOC 检测、ISIC 分割和 STL-10 SimCLR。

Conclusion: TwinMark 以单密钥双读出实现了对分类 API 和仅表示宿主的统一水印，具备蒸馏后可测的检测下界与广泛跨任务、跨架构泛化能力。

Abstract: We propose TwinMark, a watermarking scheme that reads a single SHAKE128 secret through two complementary linear functionals of model-output summaries: a covariance projector against the carrier-set covariance (cov-Feat) and a class-conditional Fisher-aligned linear carrier decoded from class-mean logits (cc-FALC). The two readouts share one bit vector and cover the two extraction surfaces of a deployed vision model: a classifier API attacked by KL knowledge distillation (KD) (Std. KL-KD), and a representation-only host attacked by feature-matching KD (FM-KD). Each readout admits a teacher-measurable a posteriori certificate that lower-bounds post-distillation detection power, and the two channels combine under a regime-restricted OR rule whose test statistic (calibrated null or bit vote) is selected by the exposed surface. cov-Feat admits a rank-blind operator-norm certificate, cc-FALC admits a centered-logit-gap certificate that decouples bit capacity from class count: at K=1024 in m=100 classes (a 10.24x over-encoding), the bit-vote attains z=23.0 sigma at a teacher-accuracy cost of +0.9+-0.2%p. Across 13 attacks on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Mini-ImageNet, TwinMark verifies on every cell whose post-attack model retains task utility, survives cross-architecture distillation onto ResNet-18/50, VGG-16, and MobileNet-V3, and ports to GNSS few-shot, VOC detection, ISIC segmentation, and STL-10 SimCLR.

</details>


### [192] [Integrated Optimization of Automated Warehouse Operations and Last-Mile Transport for Differentiated On-Demand Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19048)
*Xiaozhu Sun,Bilal Farooq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AGV智能仓储与末端多式联运的联合优化方法，用深度强化学习实现两系统动态连接，显著提升配送效率与准时率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在差异化按需货物配送场景下，仓储运营与末端运输相互割裂，难以实现高吞吐连续订单调度，且需同时满足服务、成本与外部运输等多重竞争性目标。

Method: 设计多目标联合调度的深度强化学习算法，建立仓储与运输两系统的动态连接；内部仓储采用多目标多奖励机器引导A*深度Q网络（MORM-AGDQN），综合服务水平、系统成本与外部运输需求；外部运输采用多奖励多头注意力异质容量车辆路径问题框架（MRMH-HCVRP），结合优化后的调度顺序与分组及客户位置、需求、优先级、车辆容量、速度与服务范围。

Result: 框架显著优于传统方法：仓储作业准时交付率达100%；联合优化后末端平均配送时间减少29.3%–53.2%，总运输距离减少46.4%，高优先级服务率提升至92%以上，运营成本与客户满意度保持平衡。

Conclusion: 该联合优化框架能有效连接AGV仓储与末端多式联运，提升系统敏感性与适应性，在效率、成本和服务质量间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: In the context of differentiated on-demand goods delivery services, this study proposes an integrated optimization method for automated guided vehicles (AGVs) based smart warehouse operations and the last-mile multi-modal transport. A deep reinforcement learning algorithm for multi-objective joint scheduling is designed to establish a dynamic connection between two systems, solving key challenges such as achieving high-throughput continuous order scheduling, meeting competing requirements, and improving the overall system sensitivity and adaptability. For warehouse optimization within this framework, we propose an improved algorithm based on multi-objective, Multi-Reward Machines-A* Guided Deep Q-Network (MORM-AGDQN), which combines service level, system cost, and external transportation demand. For external optimization, we propose an improved algorithm based on a Multi-Reward, Multi Head attention-Heterogeneous Capacity Vehicle Routing Problem (MRMH-HCVRP) framework, which incorporates the optimized scheduling order sequence and grouping, combined with customer location, demand, and priority, vehicle capacity, speed, and service range. The results show that the proposed framework significantly outperforms traditional methods, achieving 100% on-time delivery rate for warehousing operations. After joint optimization, the average delivery time for the last mile was reduced by 29.3% to 53.2%, the total transportation distance was reduced by 46.4%, the high-priority service rate was increased to over 92%, and a balance was maintained between operating costs and customer satisfaction.

</details>


### [193] [LightSleepX: A Lightweight, Inception-Based Dual-Modal Network for Sleep Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19062)
*Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出轻量级睡眠分期框架LightSleepX，结合Inception式深度可分离卷积、多尺度增强注意力与Mamba编码器，在低算力下实现高效多模态睡眠分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有睡眠分期方法在真实场景中受限：传统方法依赖手工特征、泛化差；深度学习虽准确但计算量大，难以在资源受限环境部署。

Method: LightSleepX采用Inception式架构+深度可分离卷积+多尺度增强注意力进行EEG/EOG多模态特征提取，并用Mamba编码器进行无规则的长程时序建模。

Result: Sleep-EDF-20上准确率85.9%、macro-F1 0.803；跨被试ISRUC-S3上准确率81.8%、macro-F1 0.796；仅0.049M参数、195.9 MFLOPs。

Conclusion: 该框架在低计算成本下保持较高精度，适合本地部署，兼顾隐私与算力约束，适用于资源受限场景的实用睡眠分析。

Abstract: Automatic sleep staging is fundamental to personal health monitoring, yet many existing approaches are ill-suited for real-world applications. Traditional pipelines often rely on hand-crafted features or shallow machine learning models that struggle to generalize, while state-of-the-art deep learning methods, though accurate, are computationally heavy and impractical for resource-constrained environments. This paper introduces LightSleepX, a lightweight framework designed to deliver robust sleep analysis in resource-constrained environments. LightSleepX combines an Inception-style architecture with depthwise separable convolutions and Multi-scale Enhanced Attention for efficient multi-modal EEG/EOG feature extraction, and a Mamba encoder for rule-free long-range temporal modeling. On public benchmark datasets, LightSleepX achieves 85.9% accuracy and a 0.803 macro-F1 score on Sleep-EDF-20, and 81.8% accuracy and a 0.796 macro-F1 score on the cross-subject ISRUC-S3 dataset. With 0.049M parameters and 195.9 MFLOPs, the framework targets practical local deployment where computational cost and privacy are central constraints.

</details>


### [194] [RLLBC-Lib: An Educational Code Library for Reinforcement Learning and Learning-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19074)
*Bernd Frauenknecht,Emma Cramer,Artur Eisele,Paul Kruse,Lukas Kesper,Jonas Hertrampf,Ramil Sabirov,Jyotirmaya Patra,Johannes Berger,Paul Brunzema,Friedrich Solowjow,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RLLBC-Lib是一个面向学习型控制教育的强化学习代码库，通过表格法与深度RL的统一设计降低学习门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习涉及多对象、多周期的复杂交互，理论抽象难以理解，学习者需要一个易上手的实现来辅助理解其动态过程。

Method: 提供以表格RL为核心的全面代码库；采用相同设计原则实现深度RL库，以体现两者的平行性；并包含展示核心RL原理及对比其他学习型控制方法的实现。

Result: 该库可作为编程作业的理想基础，并支持自动评分。

Conclusion: RLLBC-Lib旨在降低学生及学习型控制领域学习者的入门门槛，兼顾理论理解与教学实践需求。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is an exciting concept as well as a remarkable success story worth sharing. However, RL builds on rather complex interactions between different objects that play out over several cycles. Such dynamics are often best explained with an easily accessible implementation. We present RLLBC-Lib, a carefully crafted code library with the goal of lowering the entry barrier for students and other learners of RL in the context of learning-based control. At its heart, RLLBC-Lib comprises a comprehensive library of tabular RL approaches to enforce a clear understanding of the theoretical foundations. A deep RL library follows the same design principles, underscoring the parallels between simple tabular and state-of-the-art deep RL approaches. Additionally, RLLBC-Lib provides a collection of implementations illustrating core RL principles and contrasting RL to other learning-based control approaches. Finally, RLLBC-Lib provides an ideal basis for creating programming assignments with automated grading.

</details>


### [195] [Double descent is the principle of least action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19076)
*Congzhou M Sha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用统计力学解释神经网络的双下降现象：将随机梯度训练视为粒子在能量景观上的热运动，参数量d通过影响有效温度和权重衰减来调节模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释模型测试误差随参数量d呈现"下降-峰值-再下降"的双下降现象，寻求基于统计力学的理论框架。

Method: 将随机梯度方法的训练轨迹建模为在训练损失能量景观上以温度T游走的粒子；假设平衡态下参数服从玻尔兹曼分布，训练因从初始点出发、时间有限而引入有效权重衰减，使每个参数成为二次自由度；利用能量均分定理分配能量，分析d对温度和玻尔兹曼分布的影响。

Result: 能量均分定理使每个自由度分得T/2能量，故固定训练损失下增加参数会降低温度，使玻尔兹曼分布趋近平稳路径；增加参数只能降低平稳路径的L2范数，使固定损失下采样解更不易变大，等效增强权重正则化。

Conclusion: 双下降现象可由统计力学解释：增加参数量通过降低温度、减小平稳路径范数，起到隐式权重正则化作用，从而先改善后恶化再改善测试误差。

Abstract: The test error of a model plotted against its number of parameters $d$ falls, peaks when the model can just fit the training data, and falls again, exhibiting the double descent phenomenon. We explain the phenomenon with statistical mechanics. The training trajectory of a stochastic gradient-based method is a particle wandering over the energy landscape of the training loss at an induced temperature $T$, and a run that has equilibrated visits every parameter vector of a given training loss equally often, the fundamental postulate of statistical mechanics, with probability given by the Boltzmann distribution. Because training starts at an initial point and has only finite time to diffuse, it carries an effective weight decay, which makes every parameter a quadratic degree of freedom. The equipartition theorem then distributes the energy among the $d$ degrees of freedom in shares of $T/2$, so at a fixed training loss adding parameters lowers the temperature and drives the Boltzmann distribution toward the stationary path. Finally, adding parameters can only lower the $L^2$ norm of the stationary path, so a solution sampled at fixed loss is less likely to be large with increasing $d$, effectively increasing weight regularization.

</details>


### [196] [Probabilistic Linear Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19077)
*Frederic Koriche,Jean-Marie Lagniez,Chi Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于稀疏锚定线性模型的统一概率可解释性框架，适用于二分类与连续回归，兼具有理论保证与高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 溯因解释常因涉及特征过多而超出人类认知极限，而概率化松弛方法大多局限于类别分类，缺乏对回归任务及统一框架的支持。

Method: 将实例映射到布尔超立方体，用稀疏锚定线性模型刻画特征贡献的大小与方向，并施加稀疏预算k；证明最小化相关误差在神经网络下为ClassNP^PP-hard，并将其与可处理的可信度误差建立乘法界关联；采用MIP获得可证明最优解（多项式样本复杂度）与多项式时间IHT算法（具近似保证）。

Result: 理论证明稀疏解释的相关误差受可信度误差局部有界约束；实验表明相比LIME、MAPLE等基线，方法在构造上同时满足锚定与稀疏约束，并实现更低的相关误差。

Conclusion: 该框架将子集式方法严格推广到概率与回归场景，提供了理论根基扎实、计算可行且解释质量更优的可解释性方案。

Abstract: Formal explainability provides mathematically grounded justifications for individual predictions. However, abductive explanations often exceed human cognitive limits by involving too many features, while probabilistic relaxations have remained largely limited to categorical classification. We present a unified framework for probabilistic explainability based on sparse, anchored linear models, applicable to both binary classification and continuous regression. By mapping instances to the Boolean hypercube, our linear explanations strictly generalize subset-based approaches: they capture both the magnitude and direction of feature contributions while enforcing a prescribed sparsity budget $k$. We show that minimizing the relevance error for such explanations is \ClassNPPP-hard when the underlying model is a neural network, and we relate this intractable objective to a tractable surrogate---the fidelity error. For a parameterized family of local distributions, the relevance error of any $k$-sparse explanation is bounded by its fidelity error up to a multiplicative factor that remains small locally. We address the resulting empirical problem using two complementary approaches: a Mixed Integer Programming (MIP) formulation that yields provably optimal empirical solutions while maintaining polynomial sample complexity, and a polynomial-time Iterative Hard Thresholding (IHT) algorithm with provable approximation guarantees. Empirical evaluations show that, unlike state-of-the-art baselines such as LIME and MAPLE, our explanations satisfy both the anchoring and sparsity constraints by construction, while consistently achieving lower relevance error.

</details>


### [197] [Evidence-Grounded Agentic Formulation Development in an Autonomous Laboratory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19099)
*Michael M. Craig,Riley J. Hickman,Yingshan Ma,Rémi Piché-Taillefer,Christine Allen,Pauric Bannigan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Andromeda 2是一个智能体系统，通过结构化内部实验证据推理并调用计算与实验工具，设计并执行连续制剂批次，在紫杉醇自乳化给药系统优化中显著优于概率模型和传统DoE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEDDS可提高难溶性药物的口服生物利用度，但筛选高性能配方仍高度依赖实验，成本高、通量大受限。

Method: 提出Andromeda 2智能体系统，在结构化内部实验证据上推理，并调用计算与实验工具设计执行连续配方批次；在匹配预算的微型自动化实验室中，与Andromeda 1概率优化模型及湿实验室DoE对比。

Result: 紫杉醇任务中，Andromeda 2高绩效命中率50%，Andromeda 1为17%，DoE为2%；满足全部四项TPP目标配方数为12、6、0；中位AUC10-240分别为70.1、12.0、3.5 mg·min/mL；选定全TPP配方首次FaSSIF测得表观有效载药19±5% w/w，约为已发表S-SEDDS 5.7% w/w的3.3倍；消融实验显示获取结构化内部实验证据使平均AUC提高34%。

Conclusion: Andromeda 2能显著提升SEDDS配方发现效率与性能，结构化实验证据对性能提升具关键作用。

Abstract: Self-emulsifying drug delivery systems (SEDDS) can improve the oral bioavailability of poorly soluble drugs, but identifying high-performing formulations remains experimentally intensive. We present Andromeda 2, an agentic system that reasons over structured in-house experimental evidence and invokes computational and experimental tools to design and execute successive formulation batches. Using a miniaturized automated laboratory at a matched budget, we benchmark it against Andromeda 1, a probabilistic optimization model deployed across dozens of live development projects, and a wet-lab design-of-experiments (DoE) campaign. For paclitaxel, Andromeda 2 achieved a 50% high-performance hit rate versus 17% for Andromeda 1 and 2% for DoE, and identified 12 formulations meeting all four target product profile (TPP) objectives versus 6 and 0, respectively. Median $AUC_{10-240}$ was 70.1, 12.0, and 3.5 mg$\cdot$min/mL, while maximum AUC was comparable between Andromeda 2 and Andromeda 1. A selected full-TPP formulation achieved an apparent effective paclitaxel loading of $19 \pm 5\%$ w/w at the first FaSSIF measurement, approximately 3.3-fold higher than the 5.7% w/w loading reported for a published paclitaxel S-SEDDS. A controlled ablation showed that access to structured in-house experimental evidence increased mean AUC by 34%.

</details>


### [198] [How Model Growth, Recursion, and Boundary Operators Influence Scaling Exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19107)
*Zixi Chen,Akshay Vegesna,Samip Dahal,Andrew Gordon Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战传统观念，证明架构干预（如循环transformer与模型增长）可以在预训练中改变缩放指数，带来随计算量指数级的性能提升，从而实现更高效的规模扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统缩放定律认为损失随计算量按固定幂律下降，架构设计难以改变这一指数的增长速度。作者希望探究架构干预是否能突破这一限制，实现更高效的扩展。

Method: 以循环transformer（递归深度）为锚点，通过训练中增加循环次数实现模型增长（含共享与不共享权重两种方式）；同时提出在标准transformer中使用边界算子，对较早的块进行归一化并注入。

Result: 权重共享与否的模型增长带来最大的缩放指数变化：7.4B模型增长架构在CORE上以约20倍更少计算量匹配GPT-3 13B，且计算效率增益随规模增大。边界算子也带来递增的效率增益但幅度较小。在数据受限的多轮训练场景中，标准循环具有正则化作用，且随规模增大增加循环次数是计算最优的。

Conclusion: 从计算深度视角看，给定计算预算下增加transformer的有效深度可带来随规模递增的效率增益，架构干预能显著改善缩放行为。

Abstract: Scaling laws predict how loss decreases with increases in computation. We show, contrary to conventional wisdom, that architectural interventions can modify scaling exponents in pre-training, leading to exponential improvements in performance with increases in computation. As an anchoring point, we consider the architectural formulation of looped transformers. Although not typically used in this way, looping, also known as recursive depth, provides a mechanism for model growth, by increasing the number of loops during training. Model growth, with and without shared weights, provides the biggest changes to the scaling exponents. In particular, a 7.4B model growth architecture matches GPT-3 13B on CORE with roughly $20\times$ less compute, and has compute efficiency gains that increase with scale. Moreover, simply using a boundary operator in a vanilla transformer, which normalizes and injects an earlier block, also provides increasing compute-efficiency gains, although to a lesser extent. In the data-constrained, multi-epoch setting, standard looping has a useful regularizing effect, where we find it is compute-optimal to increase the number of loops with scale. These results can be understood through the lens of computational depth: for a given computational budget, we wish to increase the usable depth of the transformer, which can lead to efficiency gains that increase with scale.

</details>


### [199] [Exponential Hardness of Off-Policy Evaluation under History-Dependent Logging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19135)
*Pranaya Jajoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明：当记录器（logger）依赖历史时，即使日志数据频繁覆盖所有隐状态，也可能对目标策略价值指数级无用。构造显示评估已知确定性目标策略需 Θ((3/2)^H log(1/δ)) 条日志样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhang 和 Jiang (2025) 提出了基于模型、历史依赖记录情形下的可学习性问题。本文旨在证明该情形本质上是难处理的（intractable），即覆盖率条件不足以保证有效评估。

Method: 对每个 H≥3 构造两个 POMDP，至多每阶段两个隐状态、三个动作，共享一个三记忆状态的记录器。动作覆盖、信念覆盖及两个行为边际结果揭示条件均有与 H 无关的常数。利用"重置擦除未知转移"的机制，精确刻画统计实验并给出匹配的最优估计器；用双车道有向网格世界实现该构造并做轨迹仿真验证。

Result: 在置信度 1-δ（0<δ≤1/4）下，即使两个候选模型已知，评估确定性目标策略到精度 1/8 仍需 Θ((3/2)^H log(1/δ)) 条日志样本。仿真结果与有限样本预测一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明：在行为边际揭示定义下，历史依赖记录、基于模型的情形具有内在难处理性，为 Zhang 和 Jiang (2025) 的问题给出了下界。

Abstract: Can a logged dataset visit every hidden state frequently and still be exponentially uninformative about a target policy's value? We show that it can when the logger depends on history. For every horizon $H \ge 3$, we construct two POMDPs with at most two latent states per stage, three actions, and a common logger with three memory states. Action coverage, belief coverage, and two behavior-marginal outcome-revealing conditions all have constants independent of $H$. Nevertheless, evaluating a known deterministic target policy to accuracy $1/8$ requires $Θ((3/2)^H \log(1/δ))$ logged episodes at confidence $1-δ$, for $0 < δ\le 1/4$, even when both candidate models are known. The mechanism is simple: a reset erases the unknown transition that determines the target value. We characterize the resulting statistical experiment exactly and obtain a matching optimal estimator. A directed two-lane gridworld realizes the construction, and trajectory simulations agree with its finite-sample prediction. The result establishes intractability for the history-dependent-logging, model-based case posed by Zhang and Jiang (2025, arXiv:2503.01134), under their behavior-marginal definition of revealing.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [AI and Human Approaches to Mathematical Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17779)
*Yang Ding*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究比较了AI与人类数学家对11个数学难题的研究方式：AI侧重解决核心问题与跨领域整合，人类更注重阐明方法、界定假设局限并生成后续问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统已开始报道对长期数学问题的解决、反证和实质性进展，引发其是否以与人类数学家相同方式从事研究的疑问。

Method: 将公开的AI研究陈述与人类文献中11个相关问题的58篇论文进行对比，构建31组问题内比较，并采用六项经验证的文本指标（问题解决、方法阐述、不确定性与边界界定、后续问题生成、一般性、跨学科整合）进行测量。

Result: AI更强调解决焦点问题和跨领域连接；人类论文显著更关注解释方法、说明假设与局限以及提出后续研究问题；一般性上未发现精确差异；逐一移除各数学问题后结果方向不变。

Conclusion: 存在两种不同研究画像：AI集中于“封闭”和“重组”问题，而数学论文更充分记录使结果成为累积知识的过程、局限与研究机会。评估研究型AI需关注探究的组织方式，而非仅看目标是否被解决。

Abstract: AI systems have begun to report solutions, disproofs, and substantive advances on long-standing mathematical problems, raising questions about whether they approach research in the same way as mathematicians. This study compares public AI research accounts with the human literature on 11 such problems. The human corpus contains 58 papers that directly addressed the same mathematical targets later reported by AI sources as resolved, disproved, or substantially advanced; 31 within-problem comparisons were constructed from these materials. Six validated text-based measures capture problem resolution, method articulation, uncertainty and boundary specification, successor-question generation, generality, and cross-disciplinary integration. AI accounts place greater emphasis on resolving the focal problem and connecting ideas across fields. Human papers devote significantly more attention to explaining methods, specifying assumptions and limitations, and identifying questions for subsequent research. No precise difference is detected in generality. The estimated directions remain unchanged when each mathematical problem is removed in turn. The findings reveal two distinct research profiles: AI accounts concentrate on closing and recombining problems, whereas mathematical papers more extensively document the procedures, limits, and research opportunities through which results become cumulative knowledge. Evaluating research AI therefore requires attention to the organization of inquiry, not only whether a target is solved.

</details>


### [201] [Toward a Time-Aware Assessment Framework for the Carbon Cost of AI-Enabled Decarbonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18029)
*Chenrui Xu,Burcu Akinci,Christopher McComb*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个时间感知的评估框架，将AI避免的排放与AI自身产生的排放建模为有限时间范围内的离散时间流，用于判断是否以及何时部署AI脱碳干预措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI脱碳评估多只报告物理系统节省的排放，忽略AI侧（开发、训练、使用）的能耗与CO2e排放；同时很少考虑AI成本发生时间与脱碳收益实现时间之间的错配，而这对基础设施级项目可能影响巨大。

Method: 提出时间感知评估框架，将避免排放与AI诱导排放建模为有限时间范围内的离散时间流。用四类时间特征不同的代表性干预（多项目低碳混凝土设计支持、AI辅助施工物流、自主式HVAC控制、预测性维护）进行演示。

Result: 折现会改变相对于时间不变总量的偏好排序；框架支持排序敏感性分析、折现回收期筛选和盈亏平衡折现率分析，并提供支持go/no-go筛选、时机决策和最低"性价比"阈值的决策指南。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献了一个轻量级框架，用于在明确时间偏好下决定是否以及何时部署AI赋能的脱碳干预措施。

Abstract: AI is increasingly used to support decarbonization decisions across the built environment, yet the development, training, and use of AI consume energy and induce CO2e emissions. However, existing assessments often report physical-system savings while omitting AI-side emissions. Moreover, they rarely account for the mismatch between when AI costs occur and when decarbonization benefits materialize, which may be substantial for infrastructure-scale projects. To address these issues, we present a time-aware assessment framework that models avoided emissions and AI-induced emissions as discrete-time streams over a finite time horizon. In demonstrating this process, we seek to show that time-aware assessment can support temporal decision-making, identify cases in which accounting for time value of carbon can change preferred rankings relative to time-invariant totals, and explore how decisions may vary with slightly different governance priorities. Using four representative interventions with intentionally different temporal profiles (multi-project low-carbon concrete design support, AI-assisted construction logistics, agentic HVAC control, and predictive maintenance), we demonstrate how discounting can change preferred rankings relative to time-invariant totals and supports ranking sensitivity analysis, discounted payback screening, and break-even discount-rate analysis. We also provide decision guidelines that support go/no-go screening, timing decisions, and minimum "bang-for-your-buck" thresholds. Ultimately, this work contributes a lightweight framework for deciding whether and when to deploy AI-enabled interventions for decarbonization under explicit time preference.

</details>


### [202] [Understanding AI Provider Recommendations in Local Service Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18341)
*Hazem Ibrahim,Yasir Zaki*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文审计了AI助手在四个注册服务领域中的推荐行为，发现无搜索时模型大量虚构推荐且匹配率极低（4%-11%），启用搜索后匹配率升至64%-71%，并改变推荐对象与地域分布。AI推荐可信度主要取决于检索配置而非底层模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI助手常被用于询问"该看哪位医生""该信任哪家公司"等推荐性问题，但用户难以判断这些推荐是否真实可信。论文希望系统性审计AI推荐在真实注册数据下的准确性及其与检索配置的关系。

Method: 在美国100个最大都市区的四个有注册数据支持的服务领域，将AI每次推荐与官方注册库（Medicare医生与机构记录、SEC投顾披露）逐一比对，设置三种条件：开源权重模型、无搜索的专有模型、带搜索的同一专有模型。另用餐厅（可分别衡量质量与知名度）作对照。

Result: 无搜索时匹配率仅4%（开源）和11%（专有），且开源匹配多为同名巧合，匹配医生并不更可能是基层医生。带搜索后匹配率升至64%-71%。无搜索时推荐投顾公司SEC违规披露率为基线的3.6倍（调整规模后依旧），带搜索则显著低于基线。餐厅呈现3-5倍评论数溢价但评分溢价最多0.1星。带搜索后大都市规模惩罚基本消失。

Conclusion: AI推荐是否可信高度依赖检索配置而非仅取决于底层模型；无检索生成的答案往往不提示其推荐从未被核实，存在严重的可信度隐患。

Abstract: When someone asks an AI assistant which doctor to see or which firm to trust with their savings, the answer is a referral. We audit AI provider recommendations in four registry-backed service domains across the 100 largest U.S. metropolitan areas, matching every recommendation against the official registry for its domain (Medicare clinician and facility records, and SEC adviser disclosures), under three conditions: an open-weight model, a proprietary model without web search, and the same proprietary model with search. Without search, both models largely fabricate recommendations in the domains the web covers thinly. Only 4% of the open-weight model's recommended doctors and 11% of the proprietary model's match a clinician in the queried city, and the open-weight matches are name coincidences: its matched clinicians are no likelier to be primary-care doctors than names drawn at random from the registry. With search, 64-71% of recommendations in the same domains match a real provider. Search also changes who is recommended. Without it, recommended advisory firms carry SEC misconduct disclosures at 3.6 times the registry base rate, even after adjusting for firm size; with search, significantly below it. Restaurants, where quality and visibility are separately measurable, show a 3-5x review-count premium but a rating premium of at most a tenth of a star. Finally, search largely removes the metro-size penalty: without it, real recommendations concentrate in the largest metros; with it, match rates are similar across metro-size terciles. Whether an AI referral is trustworthy depends strongly on its retrieval configuration rather than on the underlying model alone, yet an answer produced without retrieval often carries no sign that its recommendations were never verified.

</details>


### [203] ["If I Had to Buy Just ONE: Galaxy S26 Ultra": Auditing AI-Generated Product Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18729)
*Lucas G. Uberti-Bona Marin,Thales Bertaglia,Giovanni Astante,Bram Rijsbosch,Gijs van Dijck,Anikó Hannák,Gerasimos Spanakis,Konrad Kollnig*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 对主流 AI 聊天机器人进行商业建议审计，发现其推荐存在首人称偏好、重复请求结果不稳定、来源展示差异巨大，且 API 与消费者界面不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费者日益依赖 AI 聊天机器人获取购物建议，而 OpenAI、Google 等公司通过广告变现，引发对建议偏见与公正性的担忧，因此需要审计这些 AI 商业建议的真实表现。

Method: 构建包含 2,528 条真实商业建议查询的数据集（ConsumerQ），评估 ChatGPT、Google Gemini、Google Search（AI Overviews）共 1,536 条回复，并对比聊天界面与 API 的差异。

Result: ChatGPT 在 79% 的产品推荐回复中表达首人称偏好，远高于 Gemini 的 7% 和 AI Overviews 的 2%，且推荐产品在重复请求间常有变化；同一查询下 ChatGPT 与 Gemini 界面平均仅共享 5.4% 域名，76.7% 的对比无共同域名；API 与对应界面域名重叠平均仅 12.0%（ChatGPT）和 14.8%（Gemini）。

Conclusion: 孤立的单一回复或 API 观测无法代表消费者实际遇到的商业建议，独立审计需考虑重复回复、消费者界面条件和所观测的来源层。

Abstract: Consumers increasingly use AI chatbots for advice on what to buy. With companies like OpenAI and Google monetising their AI through advertising, this raises difficult questions about the bias and impartiality of such advice. In response, we conduct an AI audit of popular chatbots using real commercial-advice queries. First, we curate a dataset of 2,528 real commercial-advice queries (ConsumerQ). Then, we evaluate 1,536 responses to product queries from popular AI chatbots: ChatGPT (chatbot and API), Google Gemini (chatbot and API), and Google Search (AI Overviews). We find that ChatGPT expresses a first-person product preference in 79% of product-recommending responses, compared with 7% for Gemini and 2% for AI Overviews, while the products recommended often change across repeated requests. Displayed sources vary strongly: for the same query, the ChatGPT and Gemini interfaces share only 5.4% of domains on average, with no domain in common in 76.7% of comparisons. APIs provide a different view from their corresponding interfaces, with mean domain overlaps of 12.0% for ChatGPT and 14.8% for Gemini, and also differ in the types and layers of source information they expose. Our findings show that neither isolated responses nor API observations can be assumed to represent the commercial advice consumers encounter. Independent audits of AI-mediated commercial advice should therefore account for repeated responses, consumer-facing conditions, and the source layer being observed.

</details>


### [204] [Control-Theoretic Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18822)
*Benedetta Tessa,Serena Tardelli,Marco Avvenuti,Anna Monreale,Stefano Cresci*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文将内容审核建模为全局、自适应、序列决策问题，提出基于反馈控制的理论框架，用于组合多种干预措施以平衡平台层面的多重竞争目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献多聚焦于局部、单个审核决策的效果，缺乏对如何将局部决策组合成有效平台级审核策略的全局研究。

Method: 借鉴反馈控制理论，构建通用控制论框架，根据干预措施对不断演化平台的预期效果进行组合；在大规模、基于实证的仿真中实例化，并将两种控制论审核者与多种基线和局部策略进行对比。

Result: 当审核目标是在期望水平附近维持相互竞争的平台级属性时，控制论方法整体表现最佳；同时更选择性地使用严厉干预，并在外部有害性激增后恢复更有效。

Conclusion: 将内容审核视为全局、自适应、序列决策问题具有明显优势。

Abstract: A sizable literature studies content moderation locally, at the level of individual moderation decisions, for example by measuring or predicting the effects of specific interventions. However, the problem of how such decisions should be combined into effective platform-level moderation strategies is comparatively unexplored. We address this latter problem by formulating content moderation as a global, adaptive, and sequential decision process in which heterogeneous interventions must jointly balance multiple competing objectives. Drawing on feedback control, we introduce a general control-theoretic framework for composing moderation actions according to their expected effects on an evolving platform. We instantiate the framework in large-scale, empirically grounded simulations and compare two control-theoretic moderators against several baselines and local strategies. When moderation aims to maintain competing platform-level properties around desired conditions, the control-theoretic approaches achieve the best overall performance. They also use severe interventions more selectively and recover more effectively after external surges of harmfulness. These results demonstrate the advantages of studying content moderation as a global, adaptive, and sequential decision problem.

</details>


### [205] [Could Underwater Data Centers Pose a Risk to AI Treaty Verification?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18824)
*James Teague,Ashmita Rajmohan,Yannick Muehlhaeuser*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文评估水下数据中心（UDC）作为规避前沿AI算力限制的可行性，发现其虽技术上可建造，但面临互连和人工维护的严重障碍，且在多模态监测下可探测性较高，因此被认为是相对不太可能的规避途径，但残余风险仍非零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 限制前沿AI发展的国际协议依赖核查机制，其中检测未申报的算力设施是核心挑战。有人提出水下数据中心可能成为规避限制的途径，但其在前沿规模下的可行性和可探测性尚未被认真评估，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者考察现有UDC部署，评估其相对于陆基设施的建造与维护复杂性，分析在海底运行10万块H100等效算力的训练任务的可行性，并从热信号、声学、光学和合成孔径雷达（SAR）四种监测手段评估其可探测性。

Result: 供电与冷却可行，但互连和大型训练所需的人工维护是严重障碍——只有资源充足的国家行为体、以隐蔽而非效率为目标、并接受巨大成本和进度代价时才可能克服。热探测仅在浅水静水条件下有效；声学探测略好但难以归因；光学/SAR监测在建造和维护阶段最有效，因为压力容器制造基地和铺缆船队会通过AIS追踪留下独特信号。

Conclusion: 相比地下或工业伪装陆基设施，UDC是相对不太可能的规避途径，但其残余风险并非零，值得将本文讨论的探测手段付诸实际运行。

Abstract: Proposals for international agreements that limit frontier AI development depend on verification, and a central challenge is detecting undeclared compute facilities used to evade restrictions. Underwater data centers (UDCs) have been suggested as one such evasion vector, but their feasibility at frontier scale and their detectability have not been seriously assessed. We examine current UDC deployments, evaluate construction and maintenance complexity relative to land-based facilities, and analyse the feasibility of a 100,000 H100-equivalent training run underwater. We find that power delivery and cooling are tractable, but interconnect and the hands-on maintenance that large training runs require are severe obstacles - surmountable only by a well-resourced state actor accepting large cost and schedule penalties, and only where concealment, rather than efficiency, is the objective. We then assess detectability through thermal, acoustic, optical and synthetic-aperture-radar (SAR) surveillance. Thermal detection of an operational pod is unlikely outside shallow, calm water; acoustic detection is marginally more effective, but faces limitations in attribution; and optical/SAR monitoring is most powerful during construction and maintenance, when the pressure-vessel fabrication base and the cable-laying fleet create distinctive signatures for AIS-tracking. We conclude that UDCs are a comparatively unlikely evasion route relative to underground or industrially disguised land-based facilities, but the residual risk is non-zero and warrants operationalising the detection modalities discussed.

</details>


### [206] [Prepared Or Unprepared? Evaluating Healthcare Workforce Readiness for Clinical Adoption of Artificial Intelligence in Nigeria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19096)
*Abbas M. Rabiu,Abdulrazaq A. Zubair,Um-mulkhairi Ibrahim,Tolulope Olusuyi,Shaheeda Farouq,Safwan M. Dafi,Adaobi C. Emegoakor,Yewande Gbadamosi,Maruf Adewole*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 尼日利亚761名医护人员调查显示，AI认知度高(92.6%)但知识与准备不足，采纳意愿强，主要障碍为培训缺乏、基础设施差与成本高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI正日益融入全球医疗系统，但其临床成功应用依赖劳动力准备度，尤其在中低收入国家(LMICs)存在基础设施与培训缺口。本研究旨在评估尼日利亚医护人员对AI的认知、态度、准备度及采纳障碍。

Method: 横断面研究，2025年12月至2026年3月间使用结构化、经验证的问卷，调查尼日利亚多个学科与实践场景的761名医护人员，并进行卡方检验、方差分析等统计分析。

Result: AI总体认知度高(92.6%)，但40.9%知识水平低或极低，仅63.0%自认准备充分。92.5%愿意接受培训，78.7%支持将AI教育纳入本科课程。主要障碍：缺乏培训(84.7%)、基础设施差(71.1%)、成本高(61.0%)、担心失业(60.6%)、伦理(52.9%)与隐私(52.7%)。准备度在地缘政治区间差异显著(χ²(5)=24.28, p<0.001)，认知在不同专业组间差异显著(χ²(6)=68.38, p<0.001)，态度在专业组间差异显著(F=3.32, p=0.003)，且自认准备充分者态度更积极(均值3.74 vs 3.46)。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了高认知与实际准备度之间的关键脱节，强调在资源受限环境中需针对性培训、基础设施投资和清晰实施框架，以弥合AI技术潜力与临床现实之间的差距。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly integrated into healthcare systems worldwide, yet its successful clinical adoption depends critically on workforce readiness, particularly in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) where infrastructural and training gaps persist. This cross-sectional study evaluated awareness, attitudes, preparedness, and barriers to AI adoption among 761 healthcare professionals across multiple disciplines and practice settings in Nigeria. Data were collected between December 2025 and March 2026 using a structured, validated questionnaire. Overall awareness of AI in healthcare was high (92.6%); however, objective knowledge and self-reported preparedness remained limited, with 40.9% reporting low or very low knowledge and only 63.0% feeling adequately prepared. Willingness to adopt AI was high: 92.5% expressed interest in training, and 78.7% supported inclusion of AI education in undergraduate curricula. Key barriers included lack of training (84.7%), poor infrastructure (71.1%), high cost of AI tools (61.0%), fear of job displacement (60.6%), ethical concerns (52.9%), and data privacy concerns (52.7%). Significant differences in preparedness were observed across geopolitical zones (chi-square (5) = 24.28, p < 0.001), and awareness differed across professional groups (chi-square (6) = 68.38, p < 0.001). Attitudes toward AI differed significantly across professional groups (F = 3.32, p = 0.003), with professionals who felt prepared demonstrating more positive attitudes (mean = 3.74) compared to those who did not (mean = 3.46). These findings reveal a critical disconnect between high awareness and actual readiness, underscoring the need for targeted training, infrastructure investment, and clear implementation frameworks to bridge the gap between AI technological potential and clinical reality in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [A continuous-time dynamic contracting problem with limited liability and finite horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18287)
*Andrea Bovo,Tiziano De Angelis,Stéphane Villeneuve*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带有代理人有限责任的连续时间Holmström-Milgrom委托-代理模型，用概率方法证明委托人值函数是全非线性全退化PDE的唯一有界经典解，并保证强形式最优控制存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 著名的Holmström-Milgrom模型在连续时间下的扩展，加入代理人有限责任约束，此类动态契约问题中强形式最优控制的存在性极为罕见，值得深入研究。

Method: 发展了一套概率方法，处理带Cauchy-Dirichlet边界条件、定义在[0,T]×[0,∞)上的全非线性全退化PDE，证明值函数是其唯一有界经典解，并证明内部解无限次连续可微；进一步用另一个非线性退化PDE刻画最优控制映射。

Result: 证明值函数是全非线性全退化PDE的唯一有界经典解，解在区域内部无限次连续可微，并由此确保强形式最优控制的存在，同时给出最优控制映射的细致性质和PDE刻画。

Conclusion: 该概率方法论成功刻画了带有限责任的连续时间委托-代理问题的正则性，罕见地保证了强形式最优控制的存在性，为动态契约问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: We perform a detailed study of a principal--agent problem in a continuous time version of the celebrated Holmström--Milgrom model (Econometrica 55 (2), 1987) where we add limited liability for the Agent. We develop a probabilistic methodology to prove that the Principal's value function is the unique bounded classical solution to a fully nonlinear and fully degenerate partial differential equation (PDE) with Cauchy-Dirichlet boundary conditions on $[0,T]\times[0,\infty)$. Indeed, we also prove infinite continuous differentiability of the solution in the interior of the domain. The strength of our regularity result is such that we can ensure existence of optimal controls in strong form---a rare occurrence in dynamic contracting---and we obtain fine properties of the optimal control map, including a characterisation via a further nonlinear degenerate PDE.

</details>


### [208] [Fenchel-Young Duality Gaps: Certified Early Stopping for Regularized Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17629)
*Pierre-Cyril Aubin-Frankowski,Yohann de Castro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究正则化逆问题的可计算误差界与认证早停，基于精确对偶间隙恒等式分解间隙为数据保真与正则项的Fenchel–Young损失，并给出无oracle的误差界与早停准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则化逆问题中缺乏不依赖解的误差界与可靠的早停准则。

Method: 用精确对偶间隙恒等式分解总间隙；在保真几何下做近端步构造对偶可行代理；以GBL（含Beurling–Lasso及多种损失）为示例；并认证Lion-K、Muon等优化器。

Result: 得到可计算、无oracle的间隙界；在源条件下给出估计与预测误差的先验界，尺度由不可约模型噪声误差控制；给出正则项Fenchel–Young损失低于ε的早停规则；二次情形下余项消失。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了可认证的误差界与早停，覆盖GBL等广泛情形，并可用于认证深度学习优化器；后续工作据此建立非退化源条件下GBL的精确支撑恢复。

Abstract: We study computable error bounds and certified early stopping for regularized inverse problems, where a data-fidelity term is traded against a regularizer. The analysis relies on an exact duality-gap identity that splits the total gap of $F(Φμ)+λR(μ)$ into a data-fidelity Fenchel--Young loss and a regularizer Fenchel--Young loss, $ Δ(μ,h)=L_F(Φμ\parallel h)+λL_R(μ\parallelη),\qquad η=-Φ^\star h/λ, $ valid for any primal point $μ$ and any dual point $h$. The data-fidelity term $F$ is strictly convex, so wherever $F^\star$ is differentiable the loss $L_F(Φμ\parallel h)$ is the Bregman divergence of~$F$ between the prediction $Φμ$ and $\nabla F^\star(h)$, and it vanishes exactly at \emph{Mirror Alignment} $h=\nabla F(Φμ)$. Evaluated at a dual-feasible point $\tilde h$, the gap~$Δ(μ,\tilde h)$ is computable and \emph{oracle-free}, meaning that it uses no knowledge of the solution, and it bounds the suboptimality of $μ$. Under the source condition, the same Fenchel--Young losses give \emph{a priori} bounds on the estimation and prediction errors. Their scale is the irreducible model and noise error $L_F(Φμ^\star\parallel h^\star)$, which vanishes exactly when Mirror Alignment holds at the certificate. This gives an early-stopping rule: run the algorithm until the regularizer Fenchel--Young loss falls below a tolerance $ε$. A constructive version of the Brøndsted--Rockafellar theorem then turns the current pair into an exact \emph{dual-feasible} one, and this proxy lifts to an exact primal certificate. We build the proxy by a proximal step in the geometry of the fidelity, with Bregman kernel $F^\star$ and tilted by the prediction $Φμ$: it recovers the Euclidean step of Carlier when $F$ is the squared error, and it reduces the duality gap by the regularizer Fenchel--Young loss, up to a second-order remainder that vanishes in the quadratic case. Our running example is the Generalized Beurling--Lasso (GBL), where $R$ is the total-variation norm on signed measures. It contains the classical Beurling--Lasso, obtained with the squared error, and also covers robust, logistic, entropic and inverse-optimal-transport losses. The same duality gap certifies deep-learning optimizers such as Lion-K and Muon, in their proximal form, as solvers of the regularized program. A companion paper by the same authors builds on these error bounds to establish exact support recovery for the GBL under a non-degenerate source condition.

</details>


### [209] [Optimal Resource Extraction under Distribution Learning and Infinite-Horizon Stochastic Hamilton--Jacobi Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17717)
*Ulrich Horst,Jinniao Qiu,Yang Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究未知总储量下可耗竭资源最优开采的无限期随机控制问题，信息内生（持续开采）与外生流动交互导致非马尔可夫、时间不一致；证明存在等价的时间一致表述，用随机HJB方程的粘性解刻画值函数并证明唯一性，最后给出确定性基准及重标度极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可耗竭资源最优开采中总储量未知，持续开采产生内生信息，同时存在外生信息流，二者交互使问题非马尔可夫、时间不一致，需寻找可处理的时间一致表述与刻画。

Method: 建立等价的时间一致表述；将值函数刻画为带随机系数的随机Hamilton-Jacobi方程的粘性解；用基于Snell包络的严格接触论证证明无限期比较原理，并通过布朗正则化与BSDE修正独立建立唯一性，避免分段马尔可夫近似。

Result: 证明问题存在具有相同最优控制的等价时间一致表述；值函数为随机HJB方程的唯一粘性解（比较原理+唯一性成立）；识别确定性基准，并在持续储量不确定性下证明重标度随机值函数收敛到路径确定性问题。

Conclusion: 长期最优开采率由极限储量分布的渐近风险率决定。该框架为带内生/外生信息交互的非马尔可夫时间不一致资源开采问题提供了严格的粘性解刻画与极限分析。

Abstract: We study an infinite-horizon stochastic control problem for the optimal exploitation of an exhaustible resource with unknown total reserves. Information is generated both endogenously through continued extraction without depletion and exogenously through an external information flow. This interaction makes the natural problem non-Markovian and time-inconsistent. We show that it nevertheless admits an equivalent time-consistent formulation with the same optimal controls. The associated value function is characterized as the unique viscosity solution of a stochastic Hamilton--Jacobi equation with random coefficients. We prove comparison on the infinite horizon through a Snell-envelope-based strict-contact argument and establish uniqueness by an independent Brownian regularization and a BSDE correction, avoiding piecewise Markovian approximations. Finally, we identify the deterministic benchmark and show that, under persistent reserve uncertainty, the rescaled stochastic value function converges to a pathwise deterministic control problem, with the long-run optimal extraction rate determined by the asymptotic hazard rate of the limiting reserve distribution.

</details>


### [210] [Derivative-Free Structured Updates for Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17759)
*Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出无导数Muon框架，用结构化有限差分构造类Muon更新，探讨四种变体及其在精度与函数评估次数间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon依赖梯度与导数，在梯度不可用或不可靠时受限，故需无导数替代方案。

Method: 用结构化有限差分构造Muon式更新，考虑四种变体：逐元素全恢复、随机低秩代理、基对齐秩一探测、直接结构搜索；并证明穷尽基对齐探测等价于坐标有限差分（在理想极正交化前相差正缩放）。

Result: 矩阵回归实验显示随机秩一探测可显著减少函数评估次数但更新精度下降；带噪梯度实验（回归与神经网络）说明精确函数值可弥补不可靠梯度；CartPole小实验考察固定episode预算下正交秩一探测。

Conclusion: 结构化探测是特定黑箱问题的实用选项，但未建立通用收敛保证，也未证明优于精确廉价的梯度。

Abstract: Muon updates matrix-valued neural-network parameters by orthogonalizing a gradient-based momentum matrix. Its reliance on derivatives limits its use when gradients are unavailable or unreliable. We develop a derivative-free framework that constructs Muon-style updates from structured finite differences. Four variants are considered: full entrywise recovery, random low-rank surrogates, basis-aligned rank-one probing, and direct structured search. Exhaustive basis-aligned probing is equivalent, up to positive scaling before ideal polar orthogonalization, to coordinate finite differences. Matrix-regression experiments show that random rank-one probing can reduce the number of function evaluations substantially, at the cost of less accurate updates. Controlled noisy-gradient experiments on regression and a neural network illustrate when accurate function values can compensate for an unreliable gradient oracle. A small CartPole study further examines orthogonal rank-one probes under a fixed episode budget. These results support structured probing as a practical option for selected black-box problems; they do not establish a general convergence guarantee or an advantage over accurate, inexpensive gradients.

</details>


### [211] [The Complexity of Finding Stationary Points in Nonsmooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17780)
*Guy Kornowski*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在Lipschitz函数上寻找(δ,ε)-Goldstein稳定点的一阶算法下界为Ω(δ^{-1}ε^{-3})梯度查询，并证明该下界是紧的，从而解决了非光滑非凸优化收敛到稳定点的复杂度问题。此外还给出了(λ,ε)-稳定性的紧下界Ω(λ^{1/2}ε^{-7/2})，揭示非光滑稳定性的收敛速率不受梯度随机性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非光滑非凸优化中收敛到稳定点的复杂度此前未被完全解决，需要确定紧的上下界来刻画一阶算法的固有极限。同时需研究新提出的松弛稳定性概念及梯度随机性对收敛速率的影响。

Method: 通过构造最坏情形下的一阶算法下界证明，分析(δ,ε)-Goldstein稳定点的查询复杂度，并与已知算法进行匹配分析；进一步推导(λ,ε)-稳定性概念的下界。

Result: 证明了(δ,ε)-Goldstein稳定点的下界为Ω(δ^{-1}ε^{-3})，且上界匹配至常数因子，为紧界；给出(λ,ε)-稳定性的紧下界Ω(λ^{1/2}ε^{-7/2})。

Conclusion: 该结果完整解决了非光滑非凸优化收敛到稳定性的复杂度问题，并表明非光滑稳定性的收敛速率与梯度随机性无关，这与光滑优化形成鲜明对比。

Abstract: We prove that first-order algorithms require $Ω(δ^{-1}ε^{-3})$ gradient queries (in the worst case) to find a $(δ,ε)$-Goldstein stationary point of a Lipschitz function, at which there is a convex combination of gradients within distance $δ$ whose norm is at most $ε$. This lower bound is tight, matching known algorithms up to absolute constants, therefore resolving the complexity of convergence to stationarity in nonsmooth nonconvex optimization. We further prove a tight lower bound of $Ω(λ^{1/2}ε^{-7/2})$ for finding points satisfying the recently proposed relaxed notion of $(λ,ε)$-stationarity, which allows combining further-away gradients. Our results reveal that convergence rates to nonsmooth stationarity are not affected by gradient stochasticity, in sharp contrast to smooth optimization.

</details>


### [212] [Regularity of a Multidimensional Principal-Agent Problem with Separable Effort Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18770)
*Shuaijie Qian,Guan Qiao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究连续时间委托-代理模型中值函数的正则性，在可分离非二次努力成本下，通过引入正则化噪声和有界努力，证明值函数局部属于Sobolev空间 $W^{2,1}_{\infty,\mathrm{loc}}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型的HJB方程完全非线性且退化，系数可能无界；二次成本下的经典解论证不适用于可分离非二次努力成本，需扩展正则性分析。

Method: 基于输出与代理人续期效用为状态变量的随机控制问题，加入独立正则化噪声并约束努力。建立经典正则性，证明最优努力唯一、正且落在固定紧子集内（对控制限制和正则化参数一致），先移除控制限制，再令附加噪声消失。

Result: 正则化值函数收敛到原始值函数，后者局部属于 $W^{2,1}_{\infty,\mathrm{loc}}$。

Conclusion: 将正则性分析推广到可分离非二次努力成本情形，克服了HJB退化和无界系数困难。

Abstract: This paper studies the regularity of the value function arising from a multidimensional continuous-time principal-agent model with separable, nonquadratic effort costs. The associated stochastic control problem has the output and the agent's continuation utility as state variables, and its Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation is fully nonlinear and degenerate, with potentially unbounded coefficients. We address these difficulties by adding an independent regularization noise and bounding the effort. For the resulting problem, we establish classical regularity of the value function and show that the optimal effort is unique, positive and remains in a fixed compact subset, uniformly with respect to both the control restriction and the regularization parameter. These estimates allow us first to remove the control restriction and then to let the additional noise vanish. Consequently, we prove that the regularized value function converges to the original value function and conclude that the latter belongs locally to the Sobolev space $W^{2,1}_{\infty, \mathrm{loc}}$, thereby extending the regularity analysis to separable nonquadratic effort costs, for which the arguments yielding classical solutions in the quadratic-cost setting no longer apply.

</details>


### [213] [Centroids for weak optimal transport with barycentric cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17784)
*Friedemann Krannich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将Wasserstein重心理论从Wasserstein距离推广到带重心代价的弱最优传输问题，给出多边际表述及其对偶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Wasserstein重心仅基于Wasserstein距离，本文希望将其推广到更一般的弱最优传输框架，并探索新的多边际结构。

Method: 将重心代价引入弱最优传输，建立多边际表述并推导对偶问题。

Result: 获得了一个新的多边际问题：其前M个边际不固定，只需在凸序意义下支配前M个给定测度；后N-M个边际则在凸序意义下被后N-M个给定测度支配。

Conclusion: 该多边际问题本身即为新问题，区别于经典多边际最优传输，为弱最优传输重心理论提供了新框架。

Abstract: We generalize the theory of Wasserstein barycenters between N given measures from the Wasserstein distance to the weak optimal transport problem with barycentric cost. We find a multi-marginal formulation and its dual problem. The multi-marginal problem is new in itself; contrary to the classical multi-marginal optimal transport problem, the first M marginals of its competitors are not fixed but instead only need dominate the first M given measures in convex order, while the last N-M marginals of its competitors are dominated in convex order by the last N-M given measures.

</details>


### [214] [Random tilts to find stationary points in stochastic convex optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17798)
*Felipe Areces,John C. Duchi,Malo Sommers*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究随机凸函数及相关变分不等式的驻点求解问题，证明带随机倾斜扰动的正则化经验风险最小化可达到 √(d/n) 的驻点残差，并给出 √(log d / n) 的极小极大下界说明维度依赖的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找随机凸函数与变分不等式的驻点，并探究其是否像标准随机优化/经验风险最小化那样可摆脱维度依赖。

Method: 结合正则化经验风险最小化与随机倾斜（random tilting）扰动；同时构造极小极大下界进行分析。

Result: 对 d 维问题、n 个观测，驻点残差达到 √(d/n) 阶；下界为 √(log d / n)，表明必要的维度依赖。

Conclusion: 与标准随机优化不同，驻点问题存在固有维度依赖，需要 √(log d / n) 级别的下界。

Abstract: We consider the problem of finding stationary points of stochastic convex functions and related variational inequalities. For each, we show that regularized empirical risk minimization, coupled with a random tilting perturbation, obtains stationarity residual order $\sqrt{d/n}$ for $d$-dimensional problems given $n$ observations. We present a few complementary results that show that some dimension dependence is necessary, in distinction from standard stochastic optimization and empirical risk minimization, by providing minimax lower bounds scaling as $\sqrt{\log d / n}$.

</details>


### [215] [Bounded Integer Quadratic Programming through Parallelepiped Covers and Discrete Convic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17889)
*Cinar Ari,Robert Hildebrand*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 给出在有理多面体整数点上最小化任意有理二次多项式的精确算法，时间复杂度为 \(2^{O(n\log(n+1))}(m+1)^{O(n)}\beta^{O(n)}(1+L)^{O(1)}\)，并推广到无界多面体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解整数二次规划（在整数点上最小化二次多项式）的精确算法，改进并推广 Goemans–Rothvoss 的整数平行体覆盖方法。

Method: 细化 Goemans–Rothvoss 的整数平行体覆盖为具有受控位移集的单元；每个单元上要么存在负二次值的整数位移排除全局最优，要么由二次恒等式给出整数目标下水平集凸包的分离预言机；再用 Hildebrand–Goess 的整数查询可行性定理求单元最小值；通过缩放格点解决位移搜索，并用局部化论证分离约束矩阵/二次部分与其余数据的编码依赖。

Result: 得到精确算法，运行时间为 \(2^{O(n\log(n+1))}(m+1)^{O(n)}\beta^{O(n)}(1+L)^{O(1)}\)，其中 \(L\) 的多项式次数为绝对常数，\(b,c,\gamma\) 的编码仅进入固定次数因子。结合 Ari–Hildebrand 的无界性检验可扩展到任意有理多面体。

Conclusion: 该工作为有界及无界有理多面体上的整数二次最小化提供了新的精确算法，将编码依赖良好分离，并扩展了整数平行体覆盖技术。

Abstract: We give an exact algorithm for minimizing an arbitrary rational quadratic polynomial $x^T Q x + c^T x + γ$ over the integer points of a bounded rational polyhedron $\{x : Ax \le b\}$. For $n$ variables and $m$ inequalities, the running time is $$2^{O(n \log(n+1))} (m+1)^{O(n)} β^{O(n)} (1+L)^{O(1)},$$ where $β$ is one plus the maximum binary encoding length of an entry of $A$ or $Q$, and $L$ is the full input encoding length. The polynomial degree in $L$ is absolute; in particular, the encoding of $b$, $c$, and $γ$ enters only this fixed-degree factor. Our algorithm refines the integral parallelepiped cover of Goemans and Rothvoss into cells with controlled displacement sets. On each cell, either an integer displacement of negative quadratic value rules out every point as a global minimizer, or the quadratic identity supplies a separation oracle for the convex hull of each integer objective sublevel set. The integer-query feasibility theorem of Hildebrand and Goess then finds the cell minimum. A refinement through scaled lattices solves the displacement search, and a localization argument separates the encoding dependence of the constraint matrix and quadratic part from that of the remaining data. Combining the bounded algorithm with the unboundedness test of Ari and Hildebrand extends the method to arbitrary rational polyhedra.

</details>


### [216] [Day-ahead Coordination of Virtual Power Plants within Active Distribution Networks using Deterministic Bi-Level Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17927)
*Laura M. Barajas-Arguello,Rafael A. Núñez-Rodríguez,Daniel Gebbran,Clodomiro Unsihuay-Vila*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一个确定性双层优化框架，用于含VPP的主动配电网协调运行，保留完整AC-OPF并转为单层MPEC求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双层VPP调度模型多对下层子问题线性化以得到MILP，损失了精度，因此需要保留AC-OPF的非线性特性。

Method: DSO为上层领导者，最小化费用、有功损耗与电压偏差的加权组合，受非线性AC潮流约束；每个VPP为下层跟随者，在统一价格信号下最大化利润。用KKT条件与强对偶定理将下层替换，得到单层MPEC，再用Fortuny-Amat big-M法线性化互补条件。

Result: 在IEEE 33节点馈线上进行24小时验证，四个分布式资源聚合为一个VPP。相比单一调度对照固定分时电价，聚合使有功损耗降低10.5%，累积电压偏差降低18.3%，低于0.95 p.u.的节点小时数从132降到29；代价是社会成本增加0.31%、DSO支出增加0.26%，而聚合商收益得以保持。

Conclusion: 该确定性双层MINLP/MPEC框架在保留AC-OPF精度的同时实现了VPP聚合的显著网络效益，且对聚合商收益影响小。

Abstract: This paper proposes a deterministic bilevel optimization framework for the coordinated operation of Virtual Power Plants (VPPs) embedded in an active distribution network. The Distribution System Operator (DSO) acts as the upper-level leader, minimizing a weighted combination of expenditure, active losses and voltage deviation subject to nonlinear AC power flow constraints, while each VPP operates as a lower-level follower that maximizes its profit under the uniform price signal issued by the DSO. Unlike most existing formulations, which linearize the lower-level subproblem to obtain a Mixed-Integer Linear Program (MILP), the proposed model retains the full AC Optimal Power Flow (AC-OPF) equations, producing a bilevel Mixed-Integer Nonlinear Program (MINLP). The lower-level problem is replaced by its Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) optimality conditions and the Strong Duality Theorem, yielding a single-level Mathematical Program with Equilibrium Constraints (MPEC). Complementarity conditions are then linearized via the Fortuny-Amat big-M transformation. The framework is validated on the IEEE 33-bus feeder over a 24-hour horizon, with four distributed resources aggregated into a single VPP. Compared with individual dispatch against a regulated time-of-use tariff, aggregation reduces active losses by 10.5 %, the accumulated voltage deviation by 18.3 %, and the bus-hours below 0.95 p.u. from 132 to 29. These gains cost 0.31 % in social cost and 0.26 % in DSO expenditure, while the rent of the aggregator is preserved.

</details>


### [217] [The BAR-SOT Method: Long-term Average Cost Control as Stochastic Optimal Self-Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17966)
*Sharan Srinivasan,Berke M. Turkay,Harsha Honnappa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将平均代价（遍历）马尔可夫跳跃过程控制重构为有限时域随机最优传输问题（BAR-SOT），联合优化受控演化与起始/返回的边缘分布（自传输），最优值为长期平均代价率的常数缩放。给出三种等价表述，证明最优对偶为平稳势加时间线性项，并设计了熵正则Sinkhorn迭代与PINN/图注意力对偶参数化，在队列与交换机任务上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均代价马尔可夫控制通常用遍历/庞加莱不等式或强化学习求解，缺乏以边缘分布为视角的传输几何结构，也难以获得对偶势的显式刻画。作者希望借助随机最优传输（SOT）框架统一刻画平均代价控制，并利用对偶结构设计可学习、可扩展的算法。

Method: 从自传输（起点与终点边缘相同）角度将平均代价控制写为有限时域BAR-SOT，用基本伴随关系（BAR）约束边缘流与受控生成元；给出三种等价形式：受控过程、Fokker-Planck约束、松弛边缘测度；推导对偶为平稳势加时间线性项；对控制加相对熵罚得到代价倾斜薛定谔桥，所有转移率可控时用Sinkhorn型迭代求解，罚趋于零时收敛到无正则最优；理论先覆盖有限MDP再推广到一般受控马尔可夫跳跃过程；用PINN编码Harrison等效工作量对偶，并用图注意力参数化对偶。

Result: 任意时域T下BAR-SOT最优值是长期平均代价率的常数缩放；对偶斜率等于该费率。PINN对偶在数值上匹配Dai-Gluzman(2022)强RL基线，加入次主导横向修正后同时优于该基线与最优优先级启发式；在输入排队交换机上，图注意力对偶参数化优于已知最强匹配启发式，且参数量不随交换机规模增长。

Conclusion: 平均代价控制可由BAR-SOT自传输问题精确刻画，对偶结构（平稳势+时间线性项）为算法设计提供统一指导：熵正则给出可计算的Sinkhorn迭代，神经/图注意力对偶参数化在队列与交换机场景中达到或超越强基线，并具备良好扩展性。

Abstract: We reformulate average-cost (ergodic) control of a Markov jump process as a finite-horizon stochastic optimal transport (SOT) problem that jointly optimizes over the controlled evolution and the marginal law from which it starts and returns to (i.e., a self-transport).
