The constraint relating the marginal flow to the controlled generator is the basic adjoint relationship (BAR), so we call the resulting problem BAR-SOT. For any time horizon T>0, its optimal value is a constant scaling of the long-run average-cost rate. We give three equivalent formulations (through controlled processes, a Fokker--Planck constraint, and relaxed marginal measures) and show that the optimal dual is a stationary potential plus a term linear in time, with slope equal to the rate. A relative-entropy penalty on the control yields a cost-tilted Schrodinger bridge problem, computable by a Sinkhorn-type iteration when every transition rate is controlled, whose value converges to the unregularized optimum as the penalty vanishes. We develop the theory for finite Markov decision processes and then for general controlled Markov jump processes. A neural parametrization of the dual, trained as a physics-informed neural network (PINN), that encodes Harrison's equivalent-workload formulation (see Harrison 2000) matches the strong reinforcement-learning baseline of Dai and Gluzman (2022); numerically conditioning the fit of a sub-dominant transverse correction then improves on both that baseline and the best priority heuristic. On the input-queued switch, a graph-attention parametrization of the dual improves on the strongest matching heuristic we are aware of, with a parameter count that does not grow with the switch size.

</details>


### [218] [Matching Multi-Loop Complexities with a Single Loop: Optimal Optimization Stationarity and Best-Known Game Stationarity in Nonconvex--Concave Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17973)
*Minghao Zhang,Zi Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出单循环投影阻尼外推梯度法（结合投影外推、对偶动量、移动近端中心），在优化稳定性与博弈稳定性准则下达到单循环一阶方法最优复杂度，并匹配多循环方法复杂度，且在特定条件下达到最优ε依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对光滑非凸-凹极小极大优化，现有单循环一阶方法复杂度次优，多循环方法复杂度更优但结构复杂，本文旨在用单循环框架在两类稳定性准则下达到最优复杂度。

Method: 提出投影阻尼外推梯度法：结合投影外推更新、对偶动量和移动近端中心；并加入固定中心预热阶段以改进初始化依赖。

Result: 优化稳定性：梯度复杂度O(L²D_Y Δ̄₀ ε⁻³)，预热后改进为O(L²D_Y Δ_φ ε⁻³)，并证明该类投影零尊重一阶方法下界Ω(L²D_Y Δ_φ ε⁻³)，说明预热版本最优。博弈稳定性：O(L^{3/2}D_Y^{1/2}Δ_φ ε^{-5/2})，匹配多循环最优复杂度。对偶强凹下：O(√κ L Δ_φ ε⁻²)，且ε⁻²依赖最优。

Conclusion: 该单循环框架在两类稳定性准则下均达到最优或匹配最优复杂度，并通过对偶强凹条件获得最优ε精度依赖，同时给出下界证明最优性。

Abstract: We introduce a new single-loop algorithmic framework for smooth nonconvex--concave minimax optimization. The resulting projected damped extragradient method combines projected extragradient updates, dual momentum, and a moving proximal center. Under both the optimization-stationarity and game-stationarity criteria, our method achieves the best-known complexity among single-loop first-order methods. For optimization stationarity, our method achieves a gradient complexity of $O(L^2D_Y\barΔ_0\varepsilon^{-3})$, where $L$ is the gradient Lipschitz constant, $D_Y$ bounds the diameter of the dual feasible set, and $\barΔ_0$ is an initialization quantity involving the value-function gap and the initial gradients. Moreover, by incorporating a fixed-center warm-up phase, the complexity can be improved to $O(L^2D_YΔ_φ\varepsilon^{-3})$, up to an additive lower-order cost, where $Δ_φ:=φ(x_0)-\inf_xφ(x)$. We further establish a lower bound of $Ω(L^2D_YΔ_φ\varepsilon^{-3})$ for optimization stationarity over projected zero-respecting first-order methods. This lower bound proves that the warm-started version of our algorithm is optimal up to a constant factor for optimization stationarity within this oracle class. For game stationarity, our method achieves $\mathcal{O}\!(L^{3/2}D_Y^{1/2}Δ_φ\varepsilon^{-5/2})$ gradient complexity. This matches the best-known complexity of multi-loop first-order methods, thereby establishing the same complexity with a single-loop algorithmic structure. Under dual strong concavity, the proposed framework achieves $O\!(\sqrtκ\,LΔ_φ\varepsilon^{-2})$ leading complexity for both stationarity criteria, where $κ=L/μ$ is the dual condition number, up to an additive initialization cost. The $\varepsilon^{-2}$ accuracy dependence is optimal under fixed regularity and initialization bounds.

</details>


### [219] [Prediction with Five Experts and Geometric Stopping: A Probabilistic Construction and Analytic Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17986)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Ibrahim Ekren,Nikolaos Kolliopoulos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 求解带几何停止的五专家预测问题，给出极小极大遗憾的精确渐近：45π²/(512√(2δ))+o(δ^(-1/2))。借助斜反射布朗运动与PDE显式求解并证明全局C²正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将五专家预测问题在几何停止下的极限遗憾精确刻画，延续并推进Bayraktar-Ekren-Zhang四专家构造。

Method: 随机分析与PDE：贴现边界局部时期望表示退化的斜反射布朗运动；导出并显式求解边界迹的双曲系统；用等式方向、双曲比较原理与控制平均验证HJB方程，把48个区域控制不等式归约为低维边界问题。

Result: 极小极大期望遗憾为45π²/(512√(2δ))+o(δ^(-1/2))；对抗性秩控制在每个状态最大化极限Hamiltonian；证明跨区域界面与秩序变化处全局C²正则性；交替COMB控制在最高两分数相等且第三、第四高分数相等时恰好达到极限Hamiltonian最大值。

Conclusion: 成功解决五专家几何停止预测问题，给出极限遗憾的显式渐近与最优对抗控制结构，并建立严格正则性理论。

Abstract: We present a solution of the limiting five-expert prediction problem with geometric stopping, based on stochastic calculus and partial differential equations. If $δ$ is the stopping probability, the minimax expected regret from tied initial scores is $45π^2/(512\sqrt{2δ})+o(δ^{-1/2})$ as $δ\downarrow0$. The adversarial rank control selecting the best and third-best experts maximizes the limiting Hamiltonian at every state. Following the four-expert construction of Bayraktar, Ekren, and Zhang (2020), we represent the value correction as a discounted boundary-local-time expectation for a degenerate obliquely reflected Brownian motion. The hyperbolic systems governing its boundary traces are derived and solved explicitly. To verify the nonlinear Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman equation, we combine equality directions, hyperbolic comparison principles, and averaging across controls. These arguments reduce the 48 regional control inequalities to lower-dimensional boundary problems, with the remaining signs established by explicit monotonicity arguments. We prove global $C^2$ regularity across both regional interfaces and changes in rank ordering. We also show that the alternating COMB control attains the limiting Hamiltonian maximum exactly when the two highest scores coincide and the third- and fourth-highest scores coincide.

</details>


### [220] [Optimal control via chemotactic sensitivity of a logistic Keller-Segel model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18030)
*Yacouba Simpore,Mahamadi Warma,Luis P. Yapu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究带控制趋化通量的两方程Keller-Segel最优控制问题，证明适定性与最优控制存在性，推导一阶最优性条件，并用样条配点+Runge-Kutta数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Keller-Segel系统在趋化敏感度受控制调制时的最优控制问题尚缺乏在低正则性假设下的完整分析与数值验证。

Method: 对趋化通量∇·(f u ∇v)引入控制f；通过ε正则化、Schauder不动点、ε无关的正性与先验估计、ε→0极限及唯一性论证证明状态方程适定；用Lagrange乘子框架建立伴随系统与变分不等式；数值上用空间样条配点与Runge-Kutta时间步进。

Result: 在低正则性下状态方程适定；对含轨迹跟踪、趋化惩罚、控制正则与终端目标的代价泛函，证明最优控制存在并得到一阶最优性条件；数值实验模拟保守与非保守情形下的自由及受控动力学。

Conclusion: 建立了受控Keller-Segel系统的最优控制理论框架并给出可行的数值方案，可用于保守/非保守情形的模拟。

Abstract: We study an optimal control problem for a two-equations Keller-Segel system on a bounded domain with Neumann boundary conditions, where the chemotactic sensitivity is modulated by a control $f(x,t)$ acting on the taxis flux $\nabla\!\cdot(f\,u\,\nabla v)$. We prove the well-posedness of the state equation under low regularity assumptions. The analysis is based on the study of an $\varepsilon$-regularized problem, the application of Schauder's fixed point theorem, $\varepsilon$-independent, positivity and a priori estimates, the passage to the limit as $\varepsilon \to 0$, and a uniqueness argument. For a cost functional combining trajectory tracking, taxis penalization, control regularization, and terminal objectives, we establish the existence of optimal controls and derive the first-order optimality conditions through a Lagrange multiplier framework, leading to an adjoint system and a variational inequality for the optimal control. A numerical scheme based on spline collocation in space and Runge-Kutta time-stepping is set and implemented. Numerical experiments are performed to simulate the free and controlled dynamics in conservative and non-conservative cases.

</details>


### [221] [Optimal Network Dependence in Distributed Stochastic Optimization via Tree Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18101)
*Runze You,Shi Pu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将图直径与逆谱隙联系起来，指出仅依赖拓扑的最优性不存在普适缩放律，提出基于直径的极小极大基准，并设计 Tree-RGT 算法实现直径最优的网络依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式优化的通信瓶颈常以混合矩阵的谱隙衡量，但谱隙同时依赖链路权重与拓扑，掩盖了网络本身的固有影响，因此需要厘清拓扑效应与权重/矩阵选择效应的区别。

Method: 建立图直径与逆谱隙的普适单边紧界，并通过多类图的构造说明不存在仅依赖拓扑的逆命题或普适双边缩放律；据此提出按图类评估的视角，并设计 Tree-Routed Gradient Tracking (Tree-RGT)：在根节点最短路径生成树上流水化模型广播与梯度聚合，每轮仅需一跳通信与一次随机梯度评估。

Result: 在光滑性与无偏有界方差oracle下，Tree-RGT 在给定规模与直径的连通图上达到一致最优的网络依赖性；经 O(nD_G^2) 次瞬态迭代后恢复集中式随机缩放，其中 n 为智能体数、D_G 为图直径；该瞬态界优于或匹配代表性分布式方法。

Conclusion: 拓扑感知的路由可在不依赖预设混合矩阵的情况下获得直径最优保证，表明网络最优性应按图类与算法类共同刻画。

Abstract: Communication is a central bottleneck in distributed optimization, but its effect is often summarized by the spectral gap of a chosen mixing matrix. Since this gap depends on link weights as well as topology, it can obscure the intrinsic effect of the network. We clarify this distinction by relating graph diameter to the inverse spectral gap. We establish a universal one-sided bound that is tight up to constant factors, while constructions across several graph families show that no topology-only converse or universal two-sided scaling law exists. This motivates a class-based view of network optimality: diameter-dependent guarantees can be assessed uniformly over graph classes, whereas spectral-gap optimality requires both the graph--matrix pair class and the admissible algorithm class to be specified. Based on the resulting diameter-based minimax benchmark, we introduce Tree-Routed Gradient Tracking (Tree-RGT), a distributed method for stochastic nonconvex optimization. The method pipelines model dissemination and gradient aggregation over a rooted shortest-path spanning tree. Each iteration uses one round of one-hop communication and one stochastic-gradient evaluation per agent, without inner consensus or multi-gossip. Under smoothness and unbiased bounded-variance oracles, Tree-RGT achieves uniformly optimal network dependence over connected graphs of prescribed size and diameter. It recovers centralized stochastic scaling after $\mathcal{O}(nD_{\mathcal{G}}^2)$ transient iterations, where $n$ is the number of agents and $D_{\mathcal{G}}$ is the graph diameter. This network-dependent transient-iteration bound improves upon or matches those reported for representative distributed methods. Thus, topology-aware routing attains diameter-optimal guarantees without relying on a prescribed mixing matrix.

</details>


### [222] [Sample average approximation of multiobjective stochastic optimization problems with application in insurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18159)
*Bogdan Norkin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种求解多目标随机优化问题的技术：将随机系统建模为向量(input-random output)系统，把随机输出转化为确定性的性能与风险指标向量，通过样本平均近似和离散样本近似将原问题近似为一系列确定性多目标优化问题，并给出收敛性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标随机优化问题中，输出具有随机性，且需要同时考虑多个性能与风险指标，直接求解困难。需要在随机环境下找到对应Pareto最优输出指标的输入决策。

Method: 使用向量(input-random output)系统作为随机系统的广义模型；将随机输出转化为确定性性能与风险指标向量；通过样本平均近似目标函数、离散样本近似可行域，把问题近似为一系列确定性多准则优化问题；以弱Pareto有效解（在向量容差意义下）定义近似最优解。

Result: 建立了通用近似格式的收敛性，并给出在适当调节采样参数下以概率1收敛的条件。以保险业务多准则优化支持系统为案例进行了说明。

Conclusion: 所提技术可有效将多目标随机优化转化为可求解的确定性多目标问题序列，并在采样参数合理调节下保证收敛性，具有实际应用价值（如保险业务决策支持）。

Abstract: The article describes a technique for solving multiobjective stochastic optimization problems. As a generalized model of a stochastic system to be optimized a vector (input-random output) system is used. Ran- dom outputs are converted into a vector of deterministic performance and risk indicators. The problem is to find those inputs that correspond to a Pareto-optimal values of output indicators. The problem is approximated by a sequence of deterministic multi-criteria optimization problems, where, for example, the objective vector function is a sample average approximation of the original one and the feasible set is a discrete sample approximation of the feasible inputs. Approximate optimal solutions are defined as weakly Pareto-efficient ones within some vector tolerance. Convergence analysis includes establishing convergence of the general approx- imation scheme and establishing conditions of convergence with probability one under proper regulation of sampling parameters. The proposed technique is illustrated on a computer system for supporting multi-criteria optimization of insurance business.

</details>


### [223] [Near-Optimal Deterministic Exact-Value Complexity for Smooth Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18230)
*Wendao Wu,Haihan Zhang,Chenheng Zhang,Yanyi Li,Chunyuan Zheng,Cong Fang,Haoxuan Li,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究光滑凸优化中仅用精确函数值的确定性oracle复杂度，证明在中等精度区域，查询复杂度为Θ(d√(βR²/ε))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅用精确函数值（value oracle）优化时，单次实数查询可编码任意多信息，使得下界证明极其困难，此前缺乏匹配的确定性下界。

Method: 用坐标有限差分结合抗误差加速投影法给出上界；用Moreau平滑的有偏max链、精确前缀屏蔽机制和批量延迟旋转构造单一固定硬实例来证明下界。

Result: 上界O(d√(βR²/ε))；下界Ω(d·min{√(βR²/ε),(d/log(ed))^{1/3}})；由此在βR²(log(ed)/d)^{2/3}≤ε≤cβR²范围内得到极小极大复杂度Θ(d√(βR²/ε))。

Conclusion: 确定性有界查询算法在中等精度下，平方根复杂度分支是最优的；所构造技术可保持与完整自适应查询记录的一致性。

Abstract: We study the deterministic oracle complexity of smooth convex optimization when the algorithm receives only exact function values. The objective is a globally $β$-smooth convex function, all queries and the final output are restricted to the Euclidean ball of radius $R$, and the unique minimizer lies in the ball of radius $R/2$. We establish an upper bound of $O(d\sqrt{βR^2/ε})$ using coordinate finite differences together with an error-robust accelerated projected method. Our main contribution is a matching lower bound, up to the high-accuracy saturation of the construction: any deterministic adaptive value-oracle algorithm requires $Ω\!\left(d\min\{\sqrt{βR^2/ε},(d/\log(ed))^{1/3}\}\right)$ queries. Consequently, the minimax oracle complexity is $Θ(d\sqrt{βR^2/ε})$ throughout the moderate-accuracy regime $βR^2(\log(ed)/d)^{2/3}\leqε\leq cβR^2$ for a universal constant $c>0$. The lower bound must account for the fact that a single exact real value can encode arbitrarily much information. To overcome this difficulty, we construct a single fixed smooth convex hard instance using a Moreau-smoothed biased max chain, an exact prefix-shielding mechanism, and batched delayed rotations. These techniques preserve consistency with the full adaptive transcript and establish the optimality of the square-root complexity branch for deterministic bounded-query algorithms.

</details>


### [224] [Bounded Cubic Integer Programming in Fixed Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18266)
*Cinar Ari,Robert Hildebrand*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明：在固定维数下，有理三次多项式在有理多面体整数点上的精确最小化可在多项式时间内求解，将二维有界结果推广到任意固定维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维有界三次整数最小化已知多项式可解，而二维四次已 NP-hard；本文旨在将三次结果推广到任意固定维数。

Method: 结合三次多项式的两个精确恒等式与 Ari-Hildebrand 固定维整数二次规划工具：对称位移覆盖、二次型负位移搜索、整数查询凸可行性。用负 Hessian 方向生成线性曲率割，无该方向时用端点-Hessian 恒等式生成线性目标割，构成整数查询分离预言机，再用椭球与格算法做整数凸可行性，避免计算格中心点。

Result: 对任意固定维数的有界情形给出精确多项式时间算法，并通过实代数局部化界推广到具有有界实改进下水平集的无界多面体（含所有强制目标）。

Conclusion: 三次是该方法自然边界：三次的对称二阶差分恰为其方向 Hessian 且关于基点仿射，四次则出现额外四阶项，故无法直接推广。

Abstract: We consider the exact minimization of a rational cubic polynomial over the integer points of a rational polytope in fixed dimension. Del Pia, Hildebrand, Weismantel, and Zemmer proved polynomial-time solvability in dimension two, while quartic polynomial minimization is already NP-hard in dimension two. We show that the bounded cubic result extends to every fixed dimension. The proof combines two exact identities for cubic polynomials with three ingredients from the recent fixed-dimensional algorithms for integer quadratic programming of Ari and Hildebrand: symmetric displacement covers, negative-displacement search for quadratic forms, and integer-query convex feasibility. At an integer query point, a negative Hessian direction yields a linear curvature cut valid for every global minimizer in the current cell, while absence of such a direction yields a linear objective cut through an exact endpoint-Hessian identity. These cuts define an integer-query separation oracle for the convex hull of the curvature-admissible sublevel points. Integer convex feasibility via ellipsoids and lattice algorithms then gives exact optimization without computing lattice centerpoints. A real-algebraic localization bound extends the result to unbounded polyhedra with a bounded real improving sublevel, including all coercive objectives. The argument also explains why degree three is a natural boundary for this approach: the symmetric second difference of a cubic is exactly its directional Hessian, which is affine in the basepoint; for quartics an additional fourth-order term appears.

</details>


### [225] [Rapid drone-based wildfire detection at a fraction of current prevention spending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18556)
*Romain Puech,Danique de Moor,Ana Trišović,Dimitris Bertsimas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出联合优化监测基础设施部署与自主无人机路径规划的定量框架，用于大规模野火早期检测，并以加州2021-2024年3,693次点火数据做样本外验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏量化框架来规模化分配检测基础设施，早期野火检测对防止小火酿成大灾至关重要。

Method: 在现实运营约束下，联合优化监测基础设施的选址与自主无人机的路径规划。

Result: 在1000万美元五年预算下，优化无人机网络可检测97.3%火灾，其中74%在首小时内；年均约2000万美元，约为加州年度野火防治支出的5%。当前技术成本下，无人机监测比静态地面传感器更具成本效益。

Conclusion: 检测能力主要由空间覆盖决定，而路径策略主要决定检测速度；无人机监测是经济高效的大规模早期野火检测方案。

Abstract: Early wildfire detection is critical to prevent small ignitions from escalating into large-scale disasters, yet current monitoring systems lack a quantitative framework for allocating detection infrastructure at scale. We jointly optimize the placement of monitoring infrastructure and the routing of autonomous drones under realistic operational constraints to quantify the investment required for rapid, large-scale wildfire detection. Evaluated out-of-sample on 3,693 California ignitions from 2021-2024, an optimized drone network operating at a $100 million five-year budget detects 97.3% of fires, including 74% within the first hour. Amortized over five years, that budget is about $20 million per year, roughly 5% of California's annual wildfire-prevention expenditure. Under current technology costs, drone-based monitoring is substantially more cost-effective than static ground sensors. Detection is governed primarily by spatial coverage, while routing strategy mainly determines detection speed.

</details>


### [226] [Predictive Online Disturbance-Action Control for Linear Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18307)
*Xia Jiang,Jianping Li,Lihua Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出预测在线控制(POC)算法，用于带时变代价函数的线性动力系统，利用短期预测并考虑记忆效应，在LiDAR自适应感知任务上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时自适应控制（如电机化LiDAR感知系统）需在线调整方向，在变化环境条件下平衡定位精度、扫描效率与平滑执行，传统在线控制未充分利用未来代价预测且忽视系统动力学的记忆效应。

Method: 基于扰动-动作控制器(DAC)参数化，将在线控制问题转化为带记忆的在线凸优化(OCO-with-memory)形式；设计窗口化滚动时域更新，融合短期预测并引入全变差正则项抑制策略突变。

Result: 理论上证明POC的动态策略遗憾界随比较序列的路径长度增长，并给出对数级预测与记忆时域下遗憾保证的充分条件；在电机化LiDAR感知任务上提升定位精度，并在感知精度与扫描完整性间取得良好折衷。

Conclusion: POC通过结合短期预测与记忆效应建模，实现了具有理论保证的预测在线控制，适用于时变环境下的实时自适应控制任务。

Abstract: This paper studies predictive online control for linear dynamical systems with time-varying cost functions, motivated by applications such as real-time adaptive control of motorized LiDAR sensing systems, where the controller must adjust the sensing direction online to balance localization accuracy, scanning efficiency, and smooth actuation under changing environmental conditions. We propose a predictive online control (POC) algorithm that leverages short-term predictions of future cost functions while accounting for the memory effect induced by system dynamics. Using the disturbance-action controller (DAC) parameterization, the online control problem is transformed into an OCO-with-memory formulation over policy parameters. We develop a windowed receding-horizon update that incorporates short-term predictions and accommodates a total-variation regularizer to suppress abrupt policy variations. Theoretically, we prove that POC achieves a dynamic policy regret bound scaling with the path length of the comparator sequence, and provide sufficient conditions in terms of logarithmic prediction and memory horizons for the regret guarantee. The proposed method is evaluated on a motorized LiDAR sensing task, demonstrating improved localization accuracy together with a favorable trade-off between sensing accuracy and scanning completeness.

</details>


### [227] [A sequential regularized piecewise affine algorithm for nonconvex nonsmooth multicomposite optimization in RNN training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18325)
*Lingzi Jin,Xiao Wang,Xiaojun Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究训练RNN的非凸非光滑多重复合优化问题，提出SRPA算法，可收敛到二阶d-平稳点并具有O(ε^{-2})复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练RNN常涉及非凸非光滑的多重复合优化问题，现有方法难以保证收敛到二阶平稳点且计算成本高。

Method: 首先给出易验证条件，保证近似一阶d-平稳点即为近似二阶d-平稳点；随后提出序列正则化分段仿射算法SRPA，每次迭代最小化正则化分段仿射函数，并引入主动集策略降低单次迭代计算成本。

Result: 基于二阶d-平稳性条件，证明SRPA全局收敛到二阶d-平稳点，并获得求取ε-近似二阶d-平稳点的O(ε^{-2})复杂度界；在合成和真实数据集上训练RNN的实验表明SRPA性能优于现有先进算法。

Conclusion: SRPA为训练RNN的非凸非光滑优化提供了具有二阶收敛保证和低复杂度的有效方法。

Abstract: This paper focuses on a class of nonconvex nonsmooth multicomposite optimization problems for training RNNs (Recurrent Neural Networks). We first establish easily verifiable conditions under which an approximate first-order d-stationary point of the problem is guaranteed to be an approximate second-order d-stationary point. Subsequently, we propose a sequential regularized piecewise affine algorithm, SRPA, that minimizes a regularized piecewise affine function at each iteration, where the idea of active-set strategy is incorporated to reduce the computational cost per iteration. Leveraging the aforementioned conditions for second-order d-stationarity, we establish the global convergence of SRPA to second-order d-stationary points and the complexity bound of $ \mathcal{O}(ε^{-2}) $ for obtaining an $ ε$-approximate second-order d-stationary point. Finally, numerical experiments for training RNNs on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate the promising performance of SRPA compared with state-of-the-art algorithms.

</details>


### [228] [Gradient Descent with Stochastic Subspaces via Persistence of Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18416)
*Subhroshekhar Ghosh,Clement Z. Q. Ng,Pierre-Louis Poirion,Akiko Takeda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出"记忆持久性"技术改进随机子空间方法，通过弱相关引导向量指导下降方向，并给出首个稀疏函数SSD理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机子空间（SSD）方法在大规模优化尤其是分布式场景中流行，但存在改进空间，尤其缺乏对稀疏函数的理论分析。

Method: 引入"persistence of memory"思想，利用一个与梯度弱相关的向量作为引导结构，指导随机子空间的生成过程；该引导向量可在多次迭代中固定，仅间隔性刷新。在稀疏或minibatch结构中可高效获取引导向量；在最优解邻域内证明梯度估计与Hessian低层特征向量对齐。

Result: 建立了首个针对稀疏函数的经典SSD方法理论分析；证明梯度估计与Hessian低层特征向量的对齐现象，使引导向量可一次性计算，提升计算效率。

Conclusion: "记忆持久性"显著扩展并改进了随机子空间方法，在稀疏/结构化问题及非结构化目标中均具计算优势。

Abstract: Stochastic subspace methods have gained popularity as gradient descent based techniques for large scale optimisation problems, especially in distributed settings. In this paper, we introduce the technique of "persistence of memory" to greatly extend and improve the random subspace methods. To this end, we leverage a vector that is only weakly correlated with the gradient in order to provide a guiding structure to the generative process of the random subspace along which the descent is going to take place. This guidance vector may be fixed for a large number of iterations, only to be refreshed at wide intervals (on whose size we can provide guarantees in terms of problem parameters). In important machine learning settings, such as optimisation problems embodying sparsity or a minibatch structure, we show that the guidance vector can be obtained in an effective and computationally inexpensive manner by leveraging the structured properties of the problem. En route, we establish to our knowledge the first theoretical analysis of classical SSD methods for sparse functions. In a local neighbourhood of the optimum, we demonstrate an alignment phenomenon of our gradient estimates with a low-lying eigenvector of the Hessian, allowing a once-for-all computation of the guidance vector which renders the method computationally favourable even in scenarios with unstructured objectives.

</details>


### [229] [A Robust Perceptron Cycling Theorem and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18342)
*Tobias Harks*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文证明经典感知机循环定理的鲁棒变体：对增量取自有限集且0在其凸包中的加性轨迹，若每步为B-近似最小化器，则轨迹范数一致有界；由此得到仿射强单调变分不等式上Frank-Wolfe的O(k^{-1})最后迭代收敛率并应用于博弈、鞍点与交通分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Block-Levin感知机循环定理要求更新与当前状态内积非正，适用范围受限；作者希望推广到更一般的加性轨迹与近似最优更新，并将该纯组合/几何结果转化为约束优化算法（如Frank-Wolfe）的收敛保证。

Method: 考虑z_{k+1}=z_k+u_k，增量u_k取自有限集U，0∈conv U；令A具有正定对称部分（不要求对称），B≥0。若每个u_k是u↦⟨Az_k,u⟩在U上的B-近似最小化点，则证明存在与初值、B及更新选择无关的常数C，使得sup_k‖z_k‖≤C(1+‖z_0‖+B)。将该轨迹界应用于多面体上具有相对内点解的仿射强单调变分不等式的调和顶点回返Frank-Wolfe。

Result: 得到加性轨迹的一致范数界；作为算法推论，证明仿射强单调变分不等式上调和顶点回返Frank-Wolfe的最后迭代范数具有O(k^{-1})界，将Bach等关于二次Frank-Wolfe/herding的保证推广到非对称仿射算子。进一步应用于强稳定线性种群博弈的经验最佳响应、二次正则化双线性鞍点问题，以及具有强制性仿射非对称代价映射的交通分配。

Conclusion: 论文建立了感知机循环定理的鲁棒扩展，并揭示了其在近似最小化轨迹稳定性与Frank-Wolfe最后迭代收敛率之间的桥梁，尤其覆盖非对称仿射单调问题，拓展了原有对称/二次情形的理论边界。

Abstract: The classical perceptron cycling theorem of Block and Levin \cite{BlockLevin1970} bounds correction sequences whose selected updates come from a finite set and have nonpositive inner product with the current state. We prove a robust variant for additive trajectories $z_{k+1}=z_k+u_k$, for all integers $k\geq0$, with increments in a finite set $U\subset E$, where $E$ is a finite-dimensional real inner-product space and $0\in\conv U$. Let $A\colon E\to E$ have positive-definite symmetric part, without requiring symmetry, and let $B\geq0$. If each $u_k$ is a $B$-approximate minimizer of $u\mapsto\ip{Az_k}{u}$ over $U$, then $\sup_{k\geq0}\norm{z_k}\leq C(1+\norm{z_0}+B)$, with $C=C(E,U,A)$ independent of the initial state, $B$, and all admissible update choices. Our central algorithmic consequence is an $O(k^{-1})$ last-iterate norm bound for harmonic vertex-returning Frank--Wolfe for affine strongly monotone variational inequalities on polytopes with relatively interior solutions. It extends the quadratic Frank--Wolfe/herding guarantee of Bach, Lacoste-Julien, and Obozinski \cite[Section~4.2]{BachEtAl2012} to nonsymmetric affine operators. We apply this bound to empirical best responses in strongly stable linear population games, quadratically regularized bilinear saddle problems, and traffic assignment with coercive affine asymmetric cost maps.

</details>


### [230] [A Parameter-Free Adaptive Reflected Gradient Method for Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18355)
*Yekini Shehu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文分析单调Lipschitz变分不等式的一步一次求值方法，证明常数步长下的弱收敛性、自适应步长规则的无条件收敛性，并给出仿射情形的最优步长界与强单调下的R-线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对变分不等式(VI)求解，现有方法通常需要Lipschitz常数或额外求值，且收敛性依赖有界性等条件。作者希望设计无需L、无需额外求值的自适应步长规则，并在常数步长下精确刻画可容许步长范围与收敛性质。

Method: 采用每次迭代仅一次求值的外推格式x_{k+1}=P_C(x_k-λB(u_k))，其中u_k由θ_k、β_k外推。对常数步长与可和过滤(sum β_k<∞)构造Lyapunov函数并给出精确的有理耗散预算；提出带安全机制的自适应步长规则，用数据驱动的Lyapunov权重消去对L的依赖。

Result: 常数步长下证明弱收敛，对可和算子误差稳健；C有界时步长范围达λ<(√2-1)/L，无界时给出先验有界性，经耗散交换认证λL=0.387。仿射B在polyhedral C上距离趋于0且平方和可和，C有界时强收敛并有R-线性速率。自适应规则无需L与额外求值，无条件弱收敛。仿射B、C=H时可证λL=1/√3的锐性（旋转下界），无约束非线性B同一常数亦锐。低于1/(√3 L)的无条件收敛归结为边缘极点绝对稳定性。强单调下证明R-线性收敛并认证Ω(L)加速。

Conclusion: 所提一步一次求值方法在常数与自适应步长下均有稳健收敛保证，自适应规则消除了对Lipschitz常数的依赖；仿射情形的最优步长界与强单调加速被严格刻画，数值实验验证理论增益。投影情形下的无条件收敛仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We analyze a one-evaluation-per-iteration method for $\mathrm{VI}(C,B)$ with monotone $L$-Lipschitz $B$: $x_{k+1}=P_C(x_k-λB(u_k))$, $u_k=x_k+θ_k(x_k-x_{k-1})+β_k(x_k-u_{k-1})$, $θ_k+β_k=1$. For constant step and summable filter ($\sum_kβ_k<\infty$) we prove weak convergence via a Lyapunov function with exact rational dissipation budgets, including Malitsky's reflected gradient method. It is robust to summable operator errors; if $C$ is bounded, the constant-step range reaches $λ<(\sqrt2-1)/L$. For unbounded $C$ this reduces to an a priori boundedness statement, certified to $λL=0.387$ by dissipation trading; for affine $B$ on polyhedral $C$, $\dist(x_n,S)\to0$ with $\sum_n\dist^2(x_n,S)<\infty$, strong convergence for bounded $C$, and $R$-linear rates after face identification. The main result is a safeguarded adaptive step-size rule needing no $L$ and no extra evaluations, proved weakly convergent unconditionally: a data-driven Lyapunov weight removes $L$ from the dissipation budgets. For affine $B$ with $C=\mathcal H$, sharpness of $λL=1/\sqrt3$ via a rotation lower bound; the same constant is sharp for unconstrained nonlinear $B$, with convergence for square-summable operator values. Unconditional convergence below $1/(\sqrt3\,L)$ reduces to a marginal-pole absolute-stability statement; the projected case remains open. Under strong monotonicity we prove $R$-linear convergence with explicit contraction, and certify $Ω(L)$ speedups over constant steps. Numerics confirm the gains.

</details>


### [231] [The sharp step-size constant for one-call reflection splittings on monotone inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18373)
*Yekini Shehu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 证明了 forward-reflected-backward splitting 的步长上界 1/(2L) 是紧的，并给出更一般斜旋转族的精确稳定阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Malitsky-Tam 方法在 λ∈(0,1/(2L)) 时弱收敛，但该上界是否紧一直作为公开问题（见 \cite{GS}）悬而未决。

Method: 构造匹配斜实例 A=LJ, B=LJ（J 为 R² 中逆时针 π/2 旋转），并推广到斜旋转族 A=γJ, B=J，分析其迭代稳定性阈值。

Result: 对 A=LJ, B=LJ，当 λ≥1/(2L) 迭代不收敛，λ>1/(2L) 时发散；一般斜旋转族的精确稳定阈值为 λ*(γ)=1/√((1+γ)(3-γ))，在 γ=1 取最小值 1/2，γ=0 时恢复 reflected gradient 常数 1/√3。该实例同时是 Cevher-Vũ 的 reflected-forward-backward 方法的反例。

Conclusion: 步长上界 1/(2L) 是紧的，且所得反例也适用于在线性算子上与 forward-reflected-backward 重合的 reflected-forward-backward 方法。

Abstract: The forward-reflected-backward splitting of Malitsky and Tam converges weakly for step sizes $λ\in(0,\tfrac{1}{2L})$, where $B$ is monotone and $L$-Lipschitz, and the question whether this bound is tight has been recorded as open \cite{GS}. We show that it is: for the matched-skew instance $A=LJ$, $B=LJ$ ($J$ the counterclockwise rotation by $π/2$ in $\R^2$), the iterates fail to converge for every $λ\ge\tfrac{1}{2L}$ and diverge for $λ>\tfrac{1}{2L}$. More generally, for the skew--rotation family $A=γJ$, $B=J$, the exact stability threshold is $λ^\star(γ)=1/\sqrt{(1+γ)(3-γ)}$, which attains its minimum $1/2$ at the matched skew $γ=1$ and recovers the reflected gradient constant $1/\sqrt3$ at $γ=0$. The same instance is a counterexample for the reflected--forward--backward method of Cevher and Vũ, which coincides with forward-reflected-backward on linear operators.

</details>


### [232] [The Projected Hessian Quantification Theorem: Exact Duality For Constrained Eigenvalues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18538)
*Meiling Wang,Yong Xia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立约束对称特征值问题的定量惩罚理论，给出约化Hessian极值特征值的精确刻画、惩罚路径收敛分析及矩阵无关的Penalty–Split–Merge算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典投影Hessian引理（源自Finsler定理）仅能通过二次惩罚刻画约束零空间上的定性（正定性），但无法量化对应的约束特征值及惩罚路径，缺乏对惩罚参数选择的定量指导。

Method: 提出定量惩罚理论，用全空间惩罚特征值问题精确刻画约化Hessian的极值特征值；给出基于正交分解、Schur补分析和半定规划对偶的三种证明；分析有限精确恢复及无有限恢复时的渐近收敛与一阶误差展开（含显式主系数）；利用Hellmann–Feynman灵敏度关系预测惩罚参数；设计矩阵无关的Penalty–Split–Merge方法（惩罚延拓、Split–Merge迭代、deflation与投影认证）。

Result: 精确刻画了惩罚路径上的极值特征值恢复性质，得到渐近收敛的一阶误差展开与显式主系数，并据此提出惩罚参数预测策略；数值实验验证了预测的惩罚区间、投影认证的有效性，并在中等规模基准与大规模矩阵无关实例上评估了计算性能。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典定性惩罚引理推广为定量理论，为约束特征值问题提供了完整的惩罚路径刻画与实用算法，兼顾理论精确性与大规模矩阵无关计算的可扩展性。

Abstract: The classical Projected Hessian Lemma, originating from Finsler's theorem, characterizes definiteness over a constraint null space through quadratic penalties. However, it does not quantify the corresponding constrained eigenvalues or the associated eigenvalue penalty path. This work develops a quantitative penalty theory for constrained symmetric eigenvalue problems and establishes exact characterizations of the extremal eigenvalues of the reduced Hessian via full-space penalized eigenvalue problems. Three proofs are provided based on orthogonal decomposition, Schur complement analysis, and semidefinite programming duality. We characterize finite exact recovery along the extremal-eigenvalue penalty paths and, in the absence of finite recovery, establish asymptotic convergence with a first-order error expansion and an explicit leading coefficient. The associated Hellmann--Feynman sensitivity relation leads to a strategy for predicting penalty parameters. Based on these results, we develop a matrix-free Penalty--Split--Merge method for successive constrained extremal eigenpairs using penalty continuation, Split--Merge iterations, deflation, and projected certification. Numerical experiments illustrate the predicted penalty regimes, evaluate projected certification, and assess computational performance on moderate-scale benchmarks and large-scale matrix-free test instances.

</details>


### [233] [Nonlinear parameter-varying embeddings for nonlinear state estimation with application to a two-link robot manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18603)
*Jiaxin Ji,Shivaraj Mohite,Jan Heiland*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出非线性参数变化(NLPV)观测器设计方法，通过将非线性系统嵌入含线性结构的NLPV类，减少调度参数数量，并以双机械臂为例与EKF和滚动时域估计对比性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性系统观测器设计在系统与控制领域既重要又具挑战性。作者希望借鉴LPV嵌入的线性结构优势，同时保留部分非线性结构以减少调度参数数量。

Method: 采用将系统嵌入非线性参数变化(NLPV)系统类的思路，保留线性结构并保留部分非线性结构；给出针对一般非线性系统的NLPV观测器设计流程，并提出若干改进；以双机械臂模型为应用实例进行验证。

Result: 在数值研究中，将NLPV设计性能与扩展卡尔曼滤波(EKF)及滚动时域估计(MHE)等已有标准非线性方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: NLPV嵌入方法能够兼顾线性结构的便利性与非线性结构的表达力，减少调度参数，适用于一般非线性系统的观测器设计。

Abstract: Observer design for nonlinear systems is a relevant and challenging task in systems and control design. In this work, we follow the idea of embedding the system in the class of nonlinear parameter-varying systems to benefit from linear structures as in a standard LPV embedding while keeping some nonlinear structures and, thus, reducing the numbers of scheduling-parameters in the representation. We lay out the NLPV observer design procedure for general nonlinear systems, propose a number of improvements, and exemplify the application for a two-arm robot model. In a numerical study, we compare the performance of the NLPV design to established standard nonlinear approaches such as the \emph{extended Kalman filter} and the \emph{moving horizon estimation}.

</details>


### [234] [A $\mathcal{VU}$-calculus for composite functions and the $\mathcal{U}$-Hessian of partly smooth functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18683)
*Shuai Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为凸外层复合非光滑函数建立有限维 VU-演算，给出链式法则及 V、U 子空间与 U-梯度的分解，并用 U-Hessian/H_ε 刻画倾斜稳定性与强度量正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合非光滑函数的变分分析缺乏系统的 VU 空间分解与可计算的二阶信息，需要一套有限维演算以统一处理链式法则、梯度与 Hessian，并联系稳定性理论。

Method: 构建凸外层复合函数的有限维 VU-演算：链式法则、可分凸和、lsc 项的严格可微扰动、模型 δ_X+f_0+θ∘F 及有限最大；在凸链式法则成立集上以因子表示 Vf、Uf、正交标架与 U-梯度，并推广 ε-VU 链式法则。

Result: 在 C^1-部分光滑下 U-梯度等于 U_f^T∇_M f；在 C^2-部分光滑且 0∈ri∂f 时，凸 U-Hessian H_U 或局部矩阵 H_ε 等于 Gram 矩阵 U_f^T∇^2_M f U_f，且沿活动流形连续依赖基点。

Conclusion: 在 prox-正则与次微分连续性下，H_ε≻0 等价于倾斜稳定性及 ∂f 的强度量正则性，且 Lipschitz 模等于 ‖H_ε^{-1}‖；结果在 hinge 复合与两个 H_U≻0 测试中验证。

Abstract: We assemble a finite-dimensional \(\mathcal{VU}\)-calculus for composite nonsmooth functions whose outer function is convex: the chain rule, separable and convex sums, a strictly differentiable perturbation of a lower semicontinuous (lsc) term, the model \(δ_X+f_0+θ\circ F\), and a finite maximum of \(C^1\) functions. The same algebra yields an \(\varepsilon\)-\(\mathcal{VU}\) chain rule for a proper outer approximation of the subdifferential. On the set \(\mathcal{R}_{h,F}\) of points at which the convex chain rule holds, the subspaces \(\mathcal{V}f\) and \(\mathcal{U}f\), an orthonormal frame of \(\mathcal{U}f\), and the \(\mathcal{U}\)-gradient \(\bar g_u\) are written in terms of the factors. If \(f\) is \(C^1\)-partly smooth at \(\bar{x}\), that gradient is \(\bar g_u=U_f^\top\nabla_{\mathcal{M}}f(\bar{x})\). If \(f\) is \(C^2\)-partly smooth and \(0\in\ri\partial f(\bar{x})\), the convex \(\mathcal{U}\)-Hessian \(H_U\) (when \(f\) is convex) or the local matrix \(H_\varepsilon\) (when \(f\) is prox-regular at \(\bar{x}\) for \(0\)) equals the Gram matrix \(U_f^\top\nabla^2_{\mathcal{M}}f(\bar{x})\,U_f\); the same matrix, written along the active manifold in a continuous frame, depends continuously on the base point. If in addition \(f\) is \(C^2\)-partly smooth at \(\bar{x}\), then under prox-regularity and subdifferential continuity at \(\bar{x}\) for \(0\), \(H_\varepsilon\succ 0\) is equivalent to tilt stability of \(\bar{x}\) and to strong metric regularity of \(\partial f\) at \((\bar{x},0)\), with \(\lip\bigl((\partial f)^{-1}\bigr)(0\mid\bar{x})=\|H_\varepsilon^{-1}\|\). The calculus and the test are illustrated on a hinge composite and two elementary tests of \(H_U\succ 0\).

</details>


### [235] [Near-Optimal Exact-Value Zeroth-Order Complexity for Smooth Strongly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18728)
*Wendao Wu,Haihan Zhang,Chenheng Zhang,Yanyi Li,Chunyuan Zheng,Cong Fang,Haoxuan Li,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究 $\beta$-光滑、$\mu$-强凸函数的确定性自适应优化查询复杂度，给出 minimax 复杂度上下界，高精度区间仍存在 $(1+\log(\mu R^2/\epsilon))$ 的差距未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在仅用精确标量函数值（无梯度）的确定性自适应优化下，光滑强凸函数的查询复杂度精确上下界，填补 minimax 复杂度中 $d$ 维度依赖项的理解空白。

Method: 下界构造"精确屏蔽光滑链"与"批量延迟旋转"；上界结合有限差分、加速方法与重启策略；以常数 $D_d=(d/\log(ed))^{1/3}$ 刻画维度效应。

Result: 得到 $N_\epsilon$ 的 minimax 上下界：$N_\epsilon\ge cd\min\{\sqrt Q,\sqrt\kappa,D_d\}$，$N_\epsilon\le Cd\min\{\sqrt Q,\sqrt\kappa[1+\log_+(Q/\kappa)]\}$。当 $\min\{Q,\kappa\}\le D_d^2$ 时，在精度主导区 $Q\le\kappa$ 及常数相对精度 $\epsilon=\Theta(\mu R^2)$ 处上下界匹配至常数。

Conclusion: 在 $\kappa\le D_d^2$ 的任意高精度区间，上下界最多相差 $1+\log(\mu R^2/\epsilon)$；一般情况下最优精度依赖仍待解决。

Abstract: We study deterministic adaptive optimization of globally $β$-smooth, $μ$-strongly convex functions using exact scalar function values. Queries and outputs lie in $B_2^d(R)$, and the minimizer lies in $B_2^d(R/2)$. Set $κ=β/μ$, $Q=βR^2/ε$, and $D_d=(d/\log(ed))^{1/3}$. For sufficiently large $d$ and $0<ε\le c_εβR^2$, the minimax value complexity $N_ε$ satisfies \[ \begin{aligned} N_ε&\ge c d\min\{\sqrt Q,\sqrtκ,D_d\},\\ N_ε&\le C d\min\left\{ \sqrt Q,\sqrtκ[1+\log_+(Q/κ)] \right\}, \end{aligned} \] where $c,C,c_ε>0$ are universal constants and $\log_+(t)=\max\{0,\log t\}$. The lower bound uses an exactly shielded smooth chain and batched delayed rotations; the upper bound combines finite differences, acceleration, and restart. When $\min\{Q,κ\}\le D_d^2$, these bounds match up to constants in the accuracy-dominated regime $Q\leκ$ and at constant relative accuracy $ε=Θ(μR^2)$. For arbitrarily higher accuracy in the range $κ\le D_d^2$, the bounds differ by at most $1+\log(μR^2/ε)$; the optimal accuracy dependence remains unresolved in general.

</details>


### [236] [Optimizing Lyapunov Certificates via Stability-Preserving Quadratization for Polynomial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18871)
*Yubo Cai,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出最优耗散二次化(ODQ)，联合设计增益与规范，用SDP全局最小化谱范数界，扩大多项式系统的吸引区认证面积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式系统的吸引区(ROA)证书随状态维度和阶数增长而计算昂贵：直接SOS公式需要组合增长的单项式基。二次化可将多项式向量场精确表示在二次系统的不变流形上，从而用二次Lyapunov函数认证ROA。但增益和规范都影响谱范数证书，而先前工作先用可行性启发式固定增益再优化规范。

Method: 对固定单项式提升、参考延拓、镇定器分解和Lyapunov权重Q=I，联合设计增益与规范。增益位于预设紧Hurwitz盒内；在每个增益处用精确半定规划(SDP)全局最小化规范上的谱范数界。采用残差感知界得到认证的闭Lyapunov次水平集，考虑浮点残差。

Result: 在理想化外层搜索下，所有聚点满足盒式Clarke平稳性；有限运行返回最佳独立验证候选(不声称增益搜索全局最优)。平面五次系统上，优化增益使认证面积比匹配零增益规范提高2.238倍。16个异构多项式系统上ODQ优于固定增益提升基线；relay基准36次ODQ运行全部完成，9个完全配对案例的27次重复比较均优于固定二次Lyapunov的SOS基线(方向代理和构造时间)。与直接SOS方法的更广泛比较结果mixed。

Conclusion: ODQ通过联合优化增益和规范有效扩大ROA认证面积，在多种基准上优于固定增益提升基线，但与直接SOS方法相比整体结果尚不一致。

Abstract: Region-of-attraction (ROA) certificates for polynomial systems become expensive as state dimension and degree grow: direct sum-of-squares (SOS) formulations require combinatorially growing monomial bases. Quadratization represents a polynomial vector field exactly on an invariant manifold of a quadratic system, allowing a quadratic Lyapunov function to certify the ROA. For a fixed lift, stabilizer gains shape the off-manifold extension and transverse dynamics, while representation gauges change the matrix representation but not the vector field. Both affect the spectral-norm certificate, yet prior work fixes the gain by a feasibility heuristic before optimizing the gauge. We formulate optimal dissipative quadratization (ODQ), jointly designing gains and gauges for a fixed monomial lift, reference extension, stabilizer factorization, and Lyapunov weight $Q=I$. Gains lie in a prescribed compact Hurwitz box. At each gain, an exact semidefinite program globally minimizes the spectral-norm bound over the gauge. Residual-aware bounds yield a certified closed Lyapunov sublevel set, accounting for the floating-point Lyapunov residual. Under our stated assumptions, every accumulation point of the idealized outer search is box-Clarke stationary. A finite run returns the best independently verified candidate; global optimality of the gain search is not claimed. On a planar quintic, optimizing the gain increases the certified area by a factor of $2.238$ over a matched zero-gain gauge. Across 16 heterogeneous polynomial systems with stabilizer freedom, ODQ improves on both fixed-gain lifted baselines. All 36 ODQ runs on the relay benchmark complete, and all 27 repeat-level comparisons across nine fully paired cases favor ODQ over an SOS baseline with a fixed quadratic Lyapunov function in both the fixed-direction proxy and construction time. Broader comparisons with direct SOS methods remain mixed.

</details>


### [237] [From Optimal Transport to Optimal Quantization: A Variational Study of the Witsenhausen Counterexample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18992)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文用最优传输重构标量Witsenhausen反例：将首个控制吸收进传输映射，把问题化为在目标律Q上最小化"Wasserstein传输代价+MMSE"的变分问题，并分析其存在性、Euler-Lagrange条件、量化约化及k大小渐近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Witsenhausen反例是分散控制中的经典难题，其非凸性与"控制贵时反而偏好非线性控制"的反直觉性使解析求解困难。作者希望借助最优传输为标量版本提供新的变分视角与结构性理解。

Method: 将第一控制器视为把先验P推到目标律Q的单调重排（传输映射），问题等价为J=inf_Q{k^2 W_2^2(P,Q)+mmse(Q)}。证明最优Q^的存在性与绝对连续性，导出Euler-Lagrange条件与Fisher信息表示，给出高斯基准及线性最优阈值；将Q限制为有限支撑得到MMSE正则化最优量化，其平稳条件耦合质心与Voronoi单元，退化到Lloyd-Max条件，并用关于控制罚k的确定性退火同伦求解非凸问题。

Result: 获得最优目标律的存在性与绝对连续性；导出Euler-Lagrange方程与等价的Fisher信息形式；给出半闭式高斯基准及线性最优的显式阈值；得到有限支撑量化的平稳条件（含Lloyd-Max特例）；推导J在小k与大k下的渐近，并识别极限控制器：控制昂贵时为线性控制，控制廉价时为两电平信令量化器；数值验证两区域行为。

Conclusion: 最优传输为标量Witsenhausen问题提供了统一框架：控制代价两端由线性控制与两级量化分别主导，中间区域体现非凸性；该表述还可推广到量化、信息论与分散控制的分析。

Abstract: We study the scalar Witsenhausen counterexample through optimal transport. Absorbing the first control into a transport map recasts the problem as the variational problem $J^=\inf_Q{k^2W_2^2(P,Q)+\operatorname{mmse}(Q)}$ over target laws $Q$. This formulation balances quadratic Wasserstein transport cost against minimum mean-square estimation error, with the optimal first controller recovered as the monotone rearrangement that pushes the prior $P$ to a minimizing law $Q^$. We establish existence and absolute continuity of $Q^$, derive an Euler-Lagrange condition, give an equivalent Fisher-information representation of the estimation cost, and obtain a semi-closed-form Gaussian benchmark with an explicit threshold for linear optimality. Restricting the target law to finite support yields an MMSE-regularized optimal quantization problem. Its stationarity conditions couple centroid levels with Voronoi decision cells and reduce to the classical Lloyd-Max conditions when control is expensive. We solve this nonconvex problem using a deterministic-annealing homotopy in the control penalty $k$. Finally, we derive the small- and large-$k$ asymptotics of $J^$ and identify the limiting controllers: a linear controller when control is expensive and a two-level signaling quantizer when it is cheap. Numerical results illustrate both regimes.

</details>


### [238] [Capacity-Constrained Wasserstein Barycenters: Existence, Duality, and Entropic Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19054)
*Chamila Gamage*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出带容量约束的Wasserstein重心问题，证明存在性与强对偶，并研究了熵正则化版本及其收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Wasserstein重心未限制运输计划的容量，本文引入容量约束以刻画更现实的运输限制，并研究其正则化理论性质。

Method: 在每个从输入测度到重心的运输计划受给定容量测度约束的框架下，于紧域上证明容量约束重心的存在性与强对偶公式；进一步在容量密度有界假设下研究熵正则化，推导其对偶的显式capped-exponential惩罚项及容量裁剪的Gibbs公式。

Result: 证明容量约束重心存在性与强对偶；正则化问题有唯一最优运输计划元组与唯一重心，对偶含显式capped-exponential惩罚，最优密度满足容量裁剪Gibbs公式；得到最优值的O(ε)估计，并证明正则化计划收敛到无正则化问题的最小熵最优解。

Conclusion: 容量约束Wasserstein重心在理论与正则化框架下均具良好性质，熵正则化以O(ε)精度逼近原问题并收敛到最小熵解。

Abstract: We introduce a capacity-constrained Wasserstein barycenter problem in which each transport plan from an input measure to the barycenter is bounded by a prescribed capacity measure. On compact domains, we prove existence of capacity-constrained barycenters and establish a strong duality formula. We then study an entropy-regularized version under bounded capacity-density assumptions. The regularized problem has a unique optimal tuple of transport plans and a unique barycenter, while its dual involves an explicit capped-exponential penalty. Whenever dual maximizers exist, the optimal densities satisfy a capacity-clipped Gibbs formula. Finally, we prove an $O(ε)$ estimate for the optimal values and show that the regularized plans converge to the minimum-entropy optimal solution of the unregularized problem.

</details>


### [239] [Trajectory Manifolds for Nonlinear Data-Enabled Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19079)
*Arda Bayer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为确定性非线性系统的轨迹流形表示建立几何基础，证明终端状态增强的有限时域行为是一个$C^r$嵌入子流形，维度为$n+Nm$，并给出全局光滑坐标与编码-解码表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为数据驱动预测控制中的数据启用方法提供严格的确定性几何基础，明确轨迹行为的流形结构。

Method: 针对离散时间系统$x_{k+1}=f(x_k,u_k)$（$C^r$，$r\geq 1$），考虑预测时域$N$内所有容许轨迹构成的终端状态增强有限时域行为，通过微分几何分析证明其流形性质。

Result: 行为是环境轨迹空间的$C^r$嵌入子流形，内在维度为$n+Nm$；展开映射$(x_0,\mathbf u)$为到该流形的$C^r$微分同胚，提供全局光滑坐标与规范精确编码-解码表示，并推出任何精确可微潜表示维度至少为$n+Nm$。

Conclusion: 该几何结果无需可控性、可镇定性或可逆性假设，同样适用于零阶保持采样的连续系统和固定步长数值转移映射，为后续数据驱动近似与预测控制奠定基础。

Abstract: This note establishes a geometric foundation for trajectory-manifold representations of deterministic nonlinear systems in a behavioral setting motivated by data-enabled predictive control. For a discrete-time system $x_{k+1}=f(x_k,u_k)$ with measured state and a $C^r$ transition map, $r\geq 1$, we consider the terminal-state-augmented finite-horizon behavior consisting of all admissible state-input trajectories over a prediction horizon $N$. We prove that this behavior is a $C^r$ embedded submanifold of the ambient trajectory space with intrinsic dimension $n+Nm$, where $n$ and $m$ are the state and input dimensions. Moreover, the rollout map from the admissible initial-state and input coordinates $(x_0,\mathbf u)$ is a $C^r$ diffeomorphism onto the behavior manifold, providing explicit global smooth coordinates. This yields a canonical exact encoder--decoder representation and implies that any exact differentiable latent representation of the full behavior must have latent dimension at least $n+Nm$. The geometric result does not require controllability, stabilizability, or invertibility of the dynamics. Corresponding results are given for zero-order-hold sampled continuous-time systems and fixed-step numerical transition maps. These results provide the deterministic geometric foundation for subsequent data-driven approximation and predictive-control development.

</details>


### [240] [Level-Set Geometry and the Theoretical Performance of PDHG for Conic Linear Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19097)
*Zikai Xiong,Robert M. Freund*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析重启原始-对偶混合梯度法(rPDHG)在一般凸锥线性优化(含LP)上的理论收敛性,建立了由原始-对偶子水平集几何性质(直径与"锥半径"之比)决定的收敛率界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: rPDHG配合启发式增强和GPU实现在超大规模LP上非常成功,但其在更一般的凸锥优化问题上的应用与理论基础研究不足,缺乏对收敛性能的理论刻画。

Method: 对一般凸锥线性优化(及作为特例的LP)分析rPDHG的理论性能,建立原始-对偶δ-(次)水平集W_δ的几何性质与收敛率之间的关系,证明收敛率界依赖于W_δ到最优解集的Hausdorff距离及直径与"锥半径"之比。

Result: 证明当存在满足(i)W_δ在Hausdorff距离上接近最优解集、(ii)直径与锥半径之比较小这两个条件的原始-对偶子水平集时,收敛率界得到改进。LP特例中,性能仅由对应最佳非最优极点的(次)水平集的该比值界定;该比值随问题实例可能取极端值,导致rPDHG表现极好或极差。

Conclusion: rPDHG的性能本质上由问题实例的原始-对偶子水平集几何结构(尤其直径/锥半径比)决定,这解释了其理论上和实践中表现差异巨大的现象,为理解其适用范围提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We consider solving (convex) conic linear optimization problems, at the scale where matrix-factorization-free methods are attractive or necessary. The restarted primal-dual hybrid gradient method (rPDHG) -- with heuristic enhancements and GPU implementation -- has been very successful in solving huge-scale linear optimization problems (LPs). However, its application to more general conic convex optimization problems is not so well-studied. We analyze the theoretical performance of rPDHG for general (convex) conic linear optimization, and LP as a special case thereof. We show a relationship between the geometry of the primal-dual $δ$-(sub-)level sets ${W}_δ$ and the convergence rate of rPDHG. Specifically, we prove a bound on the convergence rate of rPDHG that improves when there is a primal-dual (sub-)level set ${W}_δ$ for which (i) ${W}_δ$ is close to the optimal solution set in Hausdorff distance, and (ii) the ratio of the diameter to the ``conic radius'' of ${W}_δ$ is small. And in the special case of LP, the performance of rPDHG is bounded only by this ratio applied to the (sub-)level set corresponding to the best non-optimal extreme point. Depending on the problem instance, this ratio can take on extreme values and can result in excellent or poor performance of rPDHG both in theory and in practice.

</details>

### [241] [SAiFE-gym: Model-based Environments for Automated Market Making with Concentrated Liquidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17788)
*Georgios Chionas,Charalampos Kleitsikas,Stefanos Leonardos,Leandro Sánchez-Betancourt,Carmine Ventre*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文提出 SAiFE_gym，一个用于研究集中流动性恒定乘积市场（CPM）交易问题的 Python 仿真环境集合，采用向量化设计以支持高维强化学习工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中流动性（CL）使流动性提供者（LP）能精细控制资金分配并动态调整提供区间以影响收益，但缺乏可交互、可扩展的仿真工具来研究此类序贯决策问题。

Method: 将 CL 型 CPM 的微观结构分解为可交互组件以复现多种经济设定，并采用向量化方法优化环境，使其适用于高维强化学习与序贯决策。

Result: 通过在市场参数不确定条件下评估 RL 智能体在 CL 型 CPM 中的表现，展示了该环境的实用价值。

Conclusion: SAiFE_gym 为研究人员和从业者提供了可扩展的仿真平台，用于研究集中流动性市场中的交易与流动性提供策略。

Abstract: We present SAiFE_gym, a Python module that provides a collection of simulation environments for studying trading problems in Constant Product Markets (CPMs) with Concentrated Liquidity (CL). These markets give Liquidity Providers (LPs) granular control over how their capital is allocated and enable them to adjust their range of liquidity provision dynamically based on market conditions, which in turn, dictates how they earn fees. We decompose the microstructure of CPMs with CL in interactive components that allow researchers and practitioners to capture various economic settings. We employ a vectorized approach to optimize our environments, making them scalable for high dimensional Reinforcement Learning (RL) workflows that best describe sequential decision problems. We demonstrate the benefits of our environments by evaluating the performance of RL agents in CPMs with CL under uncertainty in market parameters.

</details>


### [242] [Sniper Cohorts and Algorithmic Filter Rejections in Solana Memecoin Markets: Two-Window Replication of Lifecycle-Stage Population Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18975)
*Arati Uday Kamat*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 研究检验Solana模因币市场中"狙击手队列检测"与"算法过滤器拒绝"两条独立检测流是否存在代币种群重叠，发现重叠率不足1%，生命周期阶段分离是结构性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往Solana模因币市场的实证研究常将毕业前（bonding-curve）与毕业后（公开市场）代币群体混为一谈。作者要验证同一市场中两条算法检测流（狙击手队列检测与过滤拒绝流）所针对的代币种群是否重叠。

Method: 使用两个不重叠观测窗口（2026-06-11至06-25、2026-06-29至07-16）和两个独立版本的队列检测管线（v1、v2），分别计算每个窗口内队列检测mint与拒绝流mint的集合重叠，并系统性排除四种替代解释：时间窗口伪影、数据源不匹配、DEX场所不对称、管线版本伪影。

Result: 两窗口重叠均低于队列种群的1%，尽管检测器选择性（v1: 20,162 mints；v2: 623 mints）与拒绝流覆盖（v1窗口83 mints；v2窗口1,743 mints）差异显著。跨窗口队列重叠为零。生命周期阶段分离是结构性的：队列检测作用于pump.fun bonding-curve阶段，拒绝过滤作用于PumpSwap等毕业后场所。

Conclusion: 毕业前与毕业后代币种群在结构上分离，不可混同。该结论对过滤器设计、协同行为研究及跨阶段代币监控具有启示意义。所有数据集以CC-BY-4.0发布以供复现。

Abstract: Prior empirical work on Solana memecoin markets has often conflated pre-graduation (bonding-curve) and post-graduation (open-market) token populations. We test the assumption of population overlap between two independent algorithmic detection streams operating on the same underlying market: sniper-cohort detection and algorithmic filter rejection. Using two non-overlapping observation windows (June 11 to June 25, 2026, and June 29 to July 16, 2026) and two independent versions of the cohort-detection pipeline (v1 and v2), we compute set overlap between cohort-detected mints and rejection-stream mints in each window. Overlap remains under 1 percent of the cohort population in both windows despite substantial variation in cohort detector selectivity (v1: 20,162 mints; v2: 623 mints) and rejection-stream coverage (v1 window: 83 mints; v2 window: 1,743 mints). The lifecycle-stage separation is structural rather than statistical: cohort detection operates at the pump.fun bonding-curve stage, while rejection filtering operates on PumpSwap and other post-graduation venues. Cross-window cohort overlap is zero, confirming that different time windows detect distinct token populations. We systematically rule out four alternative explanations: time-window artifact, data-source mismatch, DEX venue asymmetry, and pipeline-version artifact. Implications for filter design, coordinated-behaviour research, and cross-stage token surveillance are discussed. All datasets are released under CC-BY-4.0 for replication.

</details>


### [243] [Model-Free Passive Execution via Order-Level Shadowing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18019)
*Vincent Maciejewski*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Shadow-PPOV，一种无模型的被动 POV 执行算法：通过跟踪第三方挂单，在同价同交易所下自己的限价单，并以标识符驱动撤单。在 CME ES 期货全年回放中评估其滑点与延迟敏感性，并作为被动挂单的无模型基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动执行算法（TWAP、VWAP、POV、Implementation Shortfall）均属基于模型的调度族，依赖显式模型、预测或控制规则。作者希望探索一种不依赖订单簿模型和成交预测的被动挂单方式，作为评估预测模型的基准。

Method: 提出 Shadow-PPOV：挂单速率由观测到的订单流而非成交量决定。观察到第三方挂单时，在同价同交易所发出自己的限价单，并将对方交易所标识符与自身订单建立单一关联；撤单由标识符驱动——对方取消时立即撤单，成交时经短暂宽限窗口后撤单。价格与交易所直接读取自被观测订单，因而决策为无模型，但信息继承自订单流。

Result: 在确定性市场回放模拟器中，用芝加哥商品交易所 ES 期货完整日历年数据进行评估，报告滑点与延迟敏感性，并与激进型 POV 对比。

Conclusion: Shadow-PPOV 可作为被动挂单的无模型基准，用于对预测性挂单模型打分。算法与订单簿模拟器已在开源项目 kaspar-hft 中实现。

Abstract: Automated execution algorithms are organized into schedule-based and liquidity-seeking families. This paper concerns the first, whose members -- Time-Weighted Average Price (TWAP), Volume-Weighted Average Price (VWAP), Percentage of Volume (POV) and Implementation Shortfall -- are all model-based: each derives its decisions from an explicit model, forecast, schedule or control rule. We introduce Shadow-PPOV, a passive POV whose order-placement rate is set from observed order flow rather than from traded volume. Placing a passive order to fill efficiently conventionally involves an order-book model and a fill prediction. Shadow-PPOV replaces that prediction with tracking: on observing a third-party add, it may transmit its own limit order at the same price on the same venue, recording a single association between the observed order's exchange identifier and its own. Cancellation is then identifier-driven -- the shadow is withdrawn when the order it follows ends, at once on a cancel and after a brief grace window on a trade. The placement decision is thus model-free: price and venue are read off the observed order. Model-free is not information-free. Shadow-PPOV reads every order-book message and places only where a participant has just committed capital, while computing nothing from what it reads. Information is inherited from the flow rather than derived from a model. We evaluate Shadow-PPOV on a full calendar year of replayed Chicago Mercantile Exchange (CME) ES futures in a deterministic market-replay simulator, reporting its slippage and latency sensitivity and comparing it against the aggressive equivalent POV. We propose it as a model-free benchmark for passive-order placement, against which a predictive placement model can be scored. The algorithm and the order-book simulator are implemented in the open-source kaspar-hft project.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [Making AI-Assisted Claims Independently Challengeable: Publication Authority and a Protocol for Falsifiable Publication Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17631)
*Torsten Olivi Tiltack,Yifei Dong,Kun Yu,Xu Wang,Wei Liu,Jianlong Zhou,Ren Ping Liu,Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"出版权威"(Publication Authority)——一种精确状态、不可转让、单次使用的出版能力，并在PAC-2026(AIJIM协议候选)中实例化，用形式化与实证方式评估其第四版语义冻结(SF-4)的内部一致性与有界安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助的主张可能因证据、分析、人类授权、呈现与更正历史各自指向不同状态而显得权威。现有溯源、证明与透明机制虽能暴露历史，却无法单独界定此处所考察的"出版状态转换"，因此需要一种可精确指定该转换的出版能力。

Method: 定义Publication Authority为精确状态、不可转让、单次使用的出版能力；在PAC-2026机器可读规范中实例化，冻结为SF-4固定配置文件。设定六项义务(证据；运行与工件；测量披露；授权；表层对应；生命周期连续性)，每项产生目标绑定的见证、局部反例或局部不可验证性，互不补偿。仅当产生全新、完整、全通过的记录时才派生出许可，供一次原子出版转换消耗。采用身份向量、对抗案例、有限模型与历史实现进行评测。

Result: 10个模型探索110,764个安全可达状态；76个不安全配置均产生预期违规或观察者反模型。表层通过对应性检查者若无被接受记录仍不能授权出版。SF-4区分证据视界与验证时刻，拒绝"真实但因果无效"的授权。历史前身路径复现17个冻结的授权-后继结果；后期内部实例盲测试匹配全部183项评分预期，同主机包执行复现240条存档观测。

Conclusion: 结果支持内部一致性、有界安全性、故障敏感性与有限可构造性；但不支持事实真实性、一般性精化、盲互操作性、现场有效性或标准地位。

Abstract: AI-assisted claims can appear authoritative when evidence, analysis, human authorization, presentation, and correction history refer to different states. Provenance, attestation, and transparency expose history but alone do not specify the publication transition examined here. We develop Publication Authority as an exact-state, non-transferable, single-use publication capability and instantiate it in PAC-2026 (Publication-Accountability Calculus), a machine-readable AIJIM Protocol candidate. We evaluate its fourth bounded semantic freeze (SF-4), a fixed-profile specification designed for replaceable bindings. Six obligations govern evidence, runs and artifacts, measurement disclosure, authorization, surface correspondence, and lifecycle continuity. Each yields a target-bound witness, localized counterexample, or localized unverifiability; none can compensate for another. Only a fresh, complete all-pass record derives the permit consumed by one atomic publication transition. We use identity vectors, adversarial cases, finite models, and historical implementations. Ten models explored 110,764 safe reachable states; 76 unsafe configurations produced the expected violation or observer countermodel. A reader surface passing its correspondence check cannot authorize publication unless the accepted record admits that surface. SF-4 separates evidence horizon from verification time and rejects an authentic but causally invalid authorization. A historical predecessor path reproduced 17 frozen authorization-successor outcomes. A later in-house, instance-blind test of known case classes matched all 183 scored expectations; same-host package execution reproduced its 240 archived observations. Results support internal coherence, bounded safety, fault sensitivity, and limited constructibility, but not factual truth, general refinement, blind interoperability, field efficacy, or standards status.

</details>


### [245] [Recursive Reasoning or Statistical Extrapolation? In-Context Learning in Multi-Agent Interdependent Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18591)
*Yu Liu,Wenwen Li,Yifan Dou,Guangnan Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM智能体在多智能体不完全信息博弈中的上下文学习（ICL），发现其行为更符合统计外推而非策略推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICL能让LLM智能体利用交互历史改进决策，但其改进源于真正的内部推理还是单纯的统计模式外推尚不明确，尤其缺乏在需要递归信念推理的策略环境中的研究。

Method: 构建公共物品博弈，操纵历史反馈的统计结构，并以历史无关的理性预期均衡（REE）为基准评估决策质量。

Result: 当历史统计模式被破坏时，更长上下文的收益基本消失，决策质量降至无上下文基线，且该退化在策略相互依赖更强时被显著放大。

Conclusion: 在策略环境中，ICL行为更符合统计外推而非策略推理；工作将ICL机制研究拓展到策略多智能体场景，引入REE作为诊断工具，并提供可复用框架来探测LLM递归信念推理的边界。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) enables large language model (LLM) agents to improve decisions using interaction history, yet it remains unclear whether such improvement reflects refined internal reasoning or mere extrapolation of statistical patterns. To disentangle these mechanisms, we study LLM agents in multi-agent incomplete-information games that require recursive belief reasoning. By constructing a public goods game and manipulating the statistical structure of historical feedback, we evaluate decision quality against a history-independent rational expectations equilibrium (REE) benchmark. Our experiments reveal that when historical statistical patterns are disrupted, the benefits of longer context largely vanish, degrading decision quality to the no-context baseline in a way sharply amplified by stronger strategic interdependence. These results suggest that, in such strategic environments, ICL behavior is more consistent with statistical extrapolation than with strategic reasoning. Our work extends the mechanistic study of ICL to strategic multi-agent settings, introduces REE as a diagnostic tool for distinguishing reasoning from extrapolation, and provides a reusable framework for probing the boundaries of LLM reasoning in recursive belief tasks.

</details>


### [246] [EvolveTrade: Experience-Driven Policy Refinement for Self-Evolving LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17632)
*Sehee Kim,Yumin Choi,Minki Kang,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvolveTrade将LLM交易代理的系统提示视为文本参数化策略，通过Policy Agent基于决策轨迹与组合反馈周期性更新该策略，在固定骨干LLM下提升交易表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM交易代理的工具使用策略常为部署前固定的手写规则，难以在变化的市场环境中自适应地收集证据、调用工具、验证信号和管理风险。

Method: 提出自演化框架EvolveTrade，把工具使用代理的系统提示作为可文本参数化的策略；每个更新间隔后，Policy Agent利用累积决策轨迹和已实现的组合反馈修订策略，骨干LLM保持不变，更新后的策略用于下一批交易决策。

Result: 在多种市场状态和两个LLM骨干上，EvolveTrade在多数评估设置中相较于固定策略的LLM基线提升了Sharpe Ratio和Cumulative Return；行为分析显示自演化策略增加代码介导的分析并激活与市场状态相关的计算，案例级策略到收益归因揭示了配置变化对收益差异的贡献。

Conclusion: 适应性调整支配工具使用的可复用程序是构建更稳健LLM交易代理的关键方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) trading agents can combine market data, news, and executable analysis, but their behavior is often controlled by static hand-written tool-use policies that are fixed before deployment. This limits their ability to adapt how they gather evidence, invoke tools, verify signals, and manage risk under changing market regimes. We introduce EvolveTrade, a self-evolving framework that treats the system prompt of a tool-using trading agent as a text-parameterized policy. After each update interval, a Policy Agent revises this policy using accumulated decision traces and realized portfolio feedback, while keeping the backbone LLM fixed. The updated policy is then used for the next batch of trading decisions, enabling the agent to refine its information-acquisition and portfolio-construction procedure over time. Experiments across multiple market regimes and two LLM backbones show that EvolveTrade often improves Sharpe Ratio and Cumulative Return over fixed-policy LLM baselines, achieving the improved SR and CR in most evaluated settings. Behavioral analyses further show that self-evolved policies increase code-mediated analysis and activate regime-relevant computations; case-level policy-to-return attributions trace how policy-induced allocation changes contribute to realized return differences. These results suggest that adapting the reusable procedure governing tool use is a key direction for building more robust LLM trading agents.

</details>


### [247] [One Color Preprocessing Improves DSATUR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17633)
*Adam Nouira,Lucas Isenmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SSLD，用SDP预处理首个颜色类，再交给DSATUR完成图着色，在1600+基准上匹配或超越DSATUR，但慢约195倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图着色问题（GCP）是NP-hard问题。DSATUR是求解最快的启发式之一，但所用颜色数通常多于最先进算法。作者希望通过预处理一个固定颜色类来改进DSATUR。

Method: SSLD（Semidefinite Spectral Learning with DSATUR）：先用类似计算Lovász theta数的半定规划（SDP）预处理得到一个优质颜色类，再让DSATUR完成剩余图的着色。这是首个通过固定颜色类预处理来改进DSATUR的方法，并与朴素1颜色类预处理基线（GISD）比较。

Result: 在DIMACS实例、随机图（Erdős–Rényi、Watts-Strogatz、Barabási–Albert）、频率分配和作业车间调度实例上，超过1600个基准中几乎全部匹配或超越DSATUR，且优于朴素GISD基线，验证了SDP引导选择首个颜色类的价值。代价是运行时间约慢195倍。

Conclusion: SDP引导的首个颜色类预处理是改进图着色的有前景的方向，值得未来进一步研究。

Abstract: The Graph Coloring Problem (GCP) is NP-hard and DSATUR stands as one of the fastest heuristics for it despite producing colorings that typically use more colors than state-of-the-art coloring algorithms. We propose SSLD (Semidefinite Spectral Learning with DSATUR), which improves DSATUR by preprocessing a first good color class before letting DSATUR complete coloring the rest of the given graph. We obtain this color class from a Semidefinite Programming (SDP), similar to an SDP used to compute the Lovász theta number. To the best of our knowledge, SSLD is the first approach to improve DSATUR by preprocessing through fixed color classes. We evaluate SSLD against DSATUR and against a naive 1-color-class preprocessing algorithm on DIMACS instances, random graphs (Erdős--Rényi, Watts-Strogatz, Barabási--Albert), Frequency Assignment and Job Shop Scheduling instances. SSLD matches or beats DSATUR in almost every case across over 1600 benchmark instances, and out performs the naive GISD baseline, allows us to confirm the value brought by the SDP-guided choice of the first color class. This quality comes at a runtime cost of roughly 195 times slower that DSATUR, but demonstrating that SDP-guided preprocessing of a first color class is a direction for future improvements.

</details>


### [248] [Physics-Constrained Digital Twins for Sensor Integrity in Urban Pedestrian Flow: Detecting Stealthy False Data Injection with Conformal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17635)
*Oscar Mogollon Gutierrez,Fatemeh Ghasemi,Mohammadhossein Homaei,Andres Caro,Mar Avila*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将城市行人计数数据视为可能被攻击的非真值，研究城市尺度行人感知中的隐蔽假数据注入，提出数字孪生+守恒律检测方法并量化物理约束带来的攻击裕度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市行人计数系统支撑经济指标、规划决策与安全运营，但基于其构建的数字孪生默认输入流为真值；当数据被恶意注入时后果严重，而行人感知的相对观测矩阵比电力/水务网络更加秩亏，已有隐蔽性刻画不适用。

Method: 形式化城市尺度行人感知的隐蔽假数据注入；构建数字孪生在行人街道图上估计有向流，通过学习的图局部增益同化计数；以耦合计量与非计量路段的流守恒残差进行训练；检测结合新息与残差，阈值由自适应保形校准设定；以白盒对手直接优化穿越孪生，定义攻击裕度（估计流场最坏污染的相对下降）进行度量。

Result: 在六年的墨尔本数据上，单个设备被攻陷时攻击裕度达0.54，三分之一设备被攻陷时降至0.19；网络仅1.18%可步行路段被计量；将街道图替换为距离图后裕度崩塌至0.09，表明增益来自守恒律而非局部性。

Conclusion: 物理守恒律可显著提升城市行人感知数字孪生对隐蔽假数据注入的鲁棒性，攻击裕度随被攻陷比例上升而下降；增益来源是流守恒而非图局部性。

Abstract: City pedestrian counting systems now feed economic indicators, planning decisions and safety operations, yet the twins built on top of them treat the incoming stream as ground truth. We study what happens when it is not. We formalise stealthy false data injection for city-scale pedestrian sensing, where the map from latent flow to observation is far more rank deficient than in the power and water networks for which stealth has been characterised. Our twin estimates directed flows on the pedestrian street graph, assimilates counts through a learned graph-localised gain, and is trained against a flow conservation residual that couples metered and unmetered segments. Detection combines the innovation with that residual, and the alarm threshold is set by adaptive conformal calibration rather than by hand. To measure what the physics buys, we define the attack margin, the relative reduction in worst-case corruption of the estimated flow field, achieved against a white-box adversary that optimises directly through the twin. On six years of Melbourne data the margin reaches 0.54 against a single compromised device and falls to 0.19 when a third of the fleet is compromised, on a network where only 1.18 per cent of walkable segments are metered. Replacing the street graph by a distance graph collapses it to 0.09, which shows that the gain comes from the conservation law rather than from locality.

</details>


### [249] [What You Can't See Is Still What You Learn: A Preregistered Sixty-Society Confirmation That Evidence Masking Drives Compositional Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17637)
*Narcis Marincat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在冻结语言模型骨干、通过连续数据包通信的系统中,限制模块可读取的证据(masking)大幅提升组合泛化准确率,预注册行为标准全部通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究"限制模块可读取的信息是否能改进系统学到的计算能力"这一假设,并进行预注册验证。

Method: 64个四单元系统共享冻结语言模型骨干,通过学习到的连续数据包通信;五种条件变化证据掩蔽、所有权标记、以及用中性填充替换外来证据;跨越6个初始化簇、每簇2种数据顺序,在一个全新任务世界上进行预注册确认实验。

Result: 在两种条件下均有标记可用时,掩蔽使留出的二操作与三操作组合准确率中位配对差分别提升 0.846 和 0.859,全部12对均超过所需边际,完整预注册行为标准通过,无标记复制也通过;无全局可见系统通过标记跟随检验,角色信息效应仍不明确;填充条件产生7个完全泛化者但分解标准不确定;18个审计掩蔽系统的数据包干预符合预测的中间值变化,但审计为有限且以成功为条件,不构成中介证明。

Conclusion: 结果确认所测掩蔽机制具有显著优势,但其更细粒度的归因与普适性仍未解决;协议、结果与检查点已公开。

Abstract: Restricting what a module can read may improve what a system learns to compute. We test this in a preregistered confirmation with sixty four-cell systems sharing a frozen language-model backbone and communicating through learned continuous packets. Five conditions vary evidence masking, ownership markers, and replacement of foreign evidence with neutral filler, across six initialization clusters, each with two data orders, on one fresh task world. With markers available in both regimes, masking improved accuracy on held-out two- and three-operation compositions by median paired differences of 0.846 and 0.859; all twelve pairs cleared the required margins, and the full preregistered behavioral criterion passed. The unmarked replication also passed. No globally visible system passed the marker-following check, so the effect of usable role information remains unresolved. The filler condition yielded seven full generalizers, but its decomposition criteria were inconclusive. Packet interventions in all eighteen audited masked systems followed the predicted intermediate-value changes on eligible cases; these finite, success-conditioned audits do not establish mediation. The results confirm a large advantage of the tested masking regime, while leaving its finer attribution and generality open. Protocols, results, and checkpoints are public.

</details>


### [250] [CapMem: A Benchmark for Caption-Based Episodic Memory in Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17688)
*Dingli Liang,Yiqiao Xie,Yukai Huang,Zhaokai Wang,Weitong Cai,Guangwen Feng,Jifei Song,Zhensong Zhang,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Captions as episodic memory (CapMem) 基准与caption-guided retrieve-and-verify方法，验证文本字幕作为可复用情节记忆在长视角视频QA中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴助手需要对第一人称视频进行情节记忆，但现有视觉语言模型受限于帧预算、视觉token成本增长以及长上下文检索失败。研究探索在现实约束下文本字幕能否作为可复用的情节记忆。

Method: 定义Episodic Memory Video Caption QA任务，构建CapMem人工标注基准（75个视频共33.7小时，16个场景下1000道选择题）。对比full-coverage CaptionQA（30s/60s窗口）与直接VideoQA；设置匹配帧数的对照实验（6个Qwen模型）；提出caption-guided retrieve-and-verify框架。

Result: 长视频（>20min）上，30s和60s窗口的CaptionQA分别优于直接VideoQA（10/12和8/12模型）。匹配帧对照下平均准确率提升3.22和2.55分。caption引导检索-验证框架进一步提升准确率最高达5.3分。

Conclusion: 文本字幕记忆对长视角视频的情节推理有效，字幕可作为可复用的情节记忆替代方案。

Abstract: Wearable assistants require episodic memory over egocentric video, yet current vision-language models face bounded frame budgets, growing visual-token costs, and long-context retrieval failures. Under these practical constraints, we study whether textual captions can serve as reusable episodic memory. We define the Episodic Memory Video Caption QA task and introduce CapMem, a human-annotated benchmark with 75 videos totaling 33.7 hours, and 1,000 multiple-choice questions across 16 scenarios. On long videos (>20 min), full-coverage CaptionQA with 30s and 60s caption windows outperforms direct VideoQA for 10/12 and 8/12 models, respectively. On the same video subset, a matched-frame control across six Qwen models retains mean accuracy gains of 3.22 and 2.55 points, respectively. Our caption-guided retrieve-and-verify harness further improves accuracy by up to 5.3 points. These results support the effectiveness of caption memory for episodic reasoning over long egocentric video.

</details>


### [251] [GraphEcho: Structural Redundancy and Evidence Provenance in LLM Graph Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17695)
*Sikun Wang,Yixi Zhou,Lei Fan,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphEcho基准测试发现LLM图智能体易将重复遇到的信息误认为额外证据，即"冗余路径偏见"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在图上能走更多路径，但重复遭遇同一证据并不等于获得更多独立证据。论文想检验智能体是否会将重复路径误判为额外佐证。

Method: 提出GraphEcho基准，在固定证据内容的前提下改变路径数量和证据来源，评估判断与主动探索；并用"来源感知后训练"(PAPT)进行干预。

Result: 受控合成实验显示判断偏移依模型而异，但冗余支持路径普遍提高重复游走比例。PAPT减少重访、提升合成准确率，却覆盖更少独立来源；在科学声明上减少重复的同时准确率下降。

Conclusion: 揭示了高效探索与有效证据利用之间的鸿沟：智能体可学会不重复，却忽视真正需要的信息。GraphEcho为同时评估结论与探索是否触及独立来源提供了受控方法。

Abstract: A large language model (LLM) agent can follow more graph paths without acquiring more independent evidence. GraphEcho tests whether agents mistake these repeated encounters for additional corroboration. The benchmark varies path counts and evidential origins while holding evidence content fixed, and evaluates both judgments and active exploration. Controlled synthetic experiments reveal model-dependent judgment shifts, but redundant supporting paths increase the share of repeated walks across all evaluated frozen agents. Provenance-aware post-training (PAPT) reduces revisits and improves synthetic accuracy, yet covers fewer distinct sources. On scientific claims, it continues to reduce repetition while accuracy declines. These findings expose a gap between efficient exploration and effective evidence use: an agent can learn to stop repeating itself while overlooking information it needs. GraphEcho provides a controlled way to evaluate both what graph agents conclude and whether their exploration reaches distinct evidential sources.

</details>


### [252] [GVD: Governed Versioning and Deduplication for Document Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17696)
*Mohammadreza Sediqin,Shivali Dalmia,Sumukha Thoppanahalli,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GVD框架，统一跨文档版本链接与规则级冲突消解，本地运行无需大模型，在120篇企业文档上版本族构建F1达0.97、规则一致性F1达0.94。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档库持续演化，指南与政策被修订、替换、重传，同一内容以不同措辞重现，新版本细化或矛盾旧版本。这些不一致属于整个集合而非单个文档，但现有工作将版本化、重复检测、矛盾检测作为孤立的成对任务处理，配对标注后即停止。

Method: GVD框架：通过双向规则对齐将新来文档分配到版本族；将其规则与族记忆比较，识别重复、矛盾、非对称细化及新知识；用反事实跨度探测(CSP)解决被推理误判为中性的相关对；按关系特定策略抑制重复、仅升级重要变更以供审核，保留版本谱系作为审计轨迹；全流程本地运行，不使用大模型。

Result: 在120篇企业文档、59个版本族、共140次摄入上，版本族构建F1为0.97，规则级一致性F1为0.94；CSP将规则一致性从0.90提升至0.94。

Conclusion: GVD将版本链接与规则级冲突消解统一于可审计的更新策略下，超越孤立成对处理，实现高精度且可本地部署的文档治理。

Abstract: Document repositories evolve continuously. Guidelines and policies are revised, superseded, and re-uploaded, so the same content recurs in different wording and newer versions refine or contradict earlier ones. These inconsistencies belong to the growing collection rather than to any single document, yet existing work treats versioning, duplicate detection, and contradiction detection as isolated pairwise tasks and stops once a pair is labeled. We present GVD (Governed Versioning and Deduplication), a framework that unifies cross-document version linking with rule-level conflict resolution under an auditable update policy. Incoming documents are assigned to version families through bidirectional rule alignment, and their rules are compared against the family memory to identify duplicates, contradictions, asymmetric refinements, and new knowledge, with Counterfactual Span Probing (CSP) resolving related pairs that inference misclassifies as neutral. Relation-specific policies suppress duplicates and escalate only consequential changes for review, retaining version lineage as an audit trail. The pipeline runs fully locally, with no large language model. On 120 enterprise documents processed as 140 ingestions across 59 version families, GVD reaches an F1 of 0.97 for version-family construction and 0.94 for rule-level consistency, with CSP raising rule consistency from 0.90 to 0.94.

</details>


### [253] [NeMo Data Designer: An Extensible Framework for Multimodal Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17699)
*Johnny Greco,Nabin Mulepati,Andre Manoel,Eric Tramel,Kirit Thadaka,Mike Knepper,Dhruv Nathawani,Dane Corneil,Yev Meyer,Alex Watson,Maarten Van Segbroeck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出开源通用多模态合成数据生成框架 NeMo Data Designer (NDD)，通过声明式配置定义数据列，支持文本、代码、结构化输出、图像、嵌入及统计采样器，并以插件系统扩展。核心内建"预览-修改"迭代循环，运行时自动解析依赖、调度模型端点调用并重试失败请求。案例覆盖结构化、智能体、多模态与领域专用任务，已用于 Nemotron 模型开发及企业生产部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据生成（SDG）本质上是一个迭代过程，但现有工具在易用性、可复现性和多模态支持上存在不足。作者希望提供一个直观、通用且可检查的框架，让人或智能体用户能够灵活定义并可控地生成多模态数据集，同时支持工作流共享与复现。

Method: NDD 采用声明式配置格式，用户为每个数据列指定列类型——涵盖文本、代码、结构化输出、图像、嵌入以及用于调控数据多样性的统计采样器；通过灵活的插件系统引入新列类型与功能。配置本身是可检查的工件，支持工作流共享与复现。框架将"预览-修改"循环内建于核心工作流：先小批量生成并检视，再细化规格并全量重跑。运行时负责解析依赖、调度对用户提供的模型端点的调用，并重试失败请求。

Result: 作者描述了 NDD 的架构与编程模型，并通过案例研究展示其能力，涵盖结构化、智能体（agentic）、多模态及领域专用任务，其中包括用于 Nemotron 模型开发的数据集以及生产环境企业部署中的数据集。

Conclusion: NDD 是一个开源、通用、直观的多模态合成数据生成框架，凭借声明式配置、插件扩展和内置迭代工作流，兼顾灵活性与可复现性，并已在 Nemotron 模型研发和实际企业生产部署中得到验证。

Abstract: We present NeMo Data Designer (NDD), an open-source, general-purpose framework for multi-modal synthetic data generation (SDG). Designed to be intuitive to use, NDD provides a declarative configuration format in which human and/or agent users define each dataset column, with column types spanning text, code, structured outputs, images, embeddings, and statistical samplers that are explicitly configured to steer dataset diversity. Additional column types and functionality can be introduced using the framework's flexible plugin system. NDD's configuration is an inspectable artifact, supporting workflow sharing and reproducibility. SDG is an inherently iterative process. NDD therefore builds a preview-and-revision loop into its core workflow, allowing users to generate and inspect a small number of records, refine the specification, and rerun generation at full scale. At runtime, NDD resolves dependencies, schedules calls to user-provided model endpoints, and retries failed requests. We describe NDD's architecture and programming model and present case studies spanning structured, agentic, multimodal, and domain-specialized tasks, including datasets used in Nemotron model development and in production enterprise deployments.

</details>


### [254] [A Systematic Evaluation of the COTQ Provincial Land Cover Product: Structural Consistency, Spectral Separability, and Relative Positioning Against ESA, ESRI, and Google Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17731)
*Étienne Clabaut,Samuel Foucher,Yacine Bouroubi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了魁北克省级10米土地覆盖产品COTQ，与三个全球10米LULC数据集（ESA WorldCover、ESRI LandCover、Google DynamicWorld）对比，采用结构、光谱及人工判读多准则方法，在代表魁北克各生物气候带的八个Sentinel-2瓦片上进行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sentinel-2衍生的高分辨率LULC产品广泛应用于环境监测，但在生态梯度复杂、地表异质性强的区域性能不稳定。魁北克因此开发了省级10米产品COTQ，需对其行为与一致性进行客观评估。

Method: 不提出新的制图方法，而是对COTQ与其他三个全球产品进行系统评估：统一图例后，采用结构指标（对象尺寸分布、形状复杂度、调整兰德指数、交并比）、基于Sentinel-2反射率的光谱可分性指标，以及针对分歧区域的人工判读，覆盖八个代表魁北克主要生物气候带的Sentinel-2瓦片。

Result: COTQ在结构和光谱特征上与ESA WorldCover最为相似，同时显示出与类别定义和主题优先级相关的系统性差异，尤其在城市、湿地以及岩石或隐花植被表面。

Conclusion: 该多准则评估为COTQ产品提供了客观刻画，明确了其相对于现有全球土地覆盖数据集在魁北克业务化土地监测中的定位。

Abstract: High-resolution land use and land cover (LULC) products derived from Sentinel-2 imagery are widely used for environmental monitoring and land management, yet their performance can vary across regions with complex ecological gradients and heterogeneous surface conditions. In Quebec, these limitations motivated the development of a provincial 10-m land-cover product, the COTQ, designed to support annual monitoring of land occupation and soil artificialisation.
