Method: 规划者选择哪些单位获得补贴，同时允许均衡价格内生调整；在广泛且可解释的条件下证明福利函数超模，从而利用互补性进行精确多项式时间优化，并建立需求与供给估计不确定性下的 plug-in 分配的统计保证。

Result: 证明福利函数超模性使优化可解；刻画普遍或定向补贴最优的条件；揭示市场条件与异质性如何塑造最优分配；给出 plug-in 分配的统计保证；并用菲律宾家庭支出数据校准的优惠券分配问题进行了说明。

Conclusion: 均衡溢出效应会显著影响普遍与定向分配之间的权衡，但所提出的超模结构使规划者问题在存在单位间互动时仍具计算可行性，并为估计不确定性下的分配提供统计保障。

Abstract: This paper develops a framework for individualized treatment allocation when interventions shift equilibrium prices and generate spillovers across treated and untreated units. The planner chooses which units receive a subsidy while allowing equilibrium prices to adjust endogenously. We show that the resulting welfare function is supermodular under broad and interpretable conditions, implying complementarity across treatment assignments and enabling exact polynomial-time optimization. This structure clarifies how equilibrium spillovers shape the trade-off between universal and targeted distribution and makes the planner's problem computationally tractable despite interactions across units. We characterize when universal or targeted subsidies are optimal and show how market conditions and heterogeneity shape the optimal allocation. We further establish statistical guarantees for plug-in allocation under estimation uncertainty in demand and supply. Finally, we illustrate the framework in a coupon allocation problem calibrated with household expenditure data from the Philippines.

</details>


### [328] [Conditionally linear, matrix normal state space models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18734)
*Drew D. Creal,Marcelo C. Medeiros,Rodrigo Sarlo*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出一类针对矩阵值时间序列的线性状态空间模型，状态为潜在矩阵正态过程，推导了矩阵版卡尔曼滤波、似然与平滑器，并用于美国50州宏观经济面板数据分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维宏观经济数据呈矩阵结构（州×变量），传统向量化状态空间模型参数膨胀、难以扩展；需要能同时处理混合频率、异方差和异常值的统一框架。

Method: 构建状态为潜在矩阵正态过程的线性状态空间模型；推导矩阵形式的卡尔曼滤波、对数似然与平滑器以估计状态与参数；提供从潜在状态矩阵联合后验抽样的贝叶斯推断算法。

Result: 应用于美国50州大规模宏观经济时间序列面板；框架统一处理混合频率、异方差与异常值；少数潜在因子即可刻画各州与各变量间的联合动态。

Conclusion: 矩阵值状态空间框架为高维宏观系统提供了一种简约且可扩展的建模方法。

Abstract: We develop a class of linear state space models for matrix-valued time series data where the state is a latent matrix normal process. We derive matrix versions of the Kalman filter, log-likelihood, and smoother enabling estimation of the latent state matrix as well as the model's parameters. To conduct Bayesian inference, we provide algorithms that draw from the joint posterior distribution of the latent state matrices conditional on the observed data and parameters. We apply these methods to a large panel of U.S. macroeconomic time series across the 50 U.S. states. The proposed framework accommodates mixed-frequency data, heteroskedasticity, and outliers within a unified matrix-valued structure. Empirically, we find that a small number of latent factors captures the joint dynamics across states and variables, providing a parsimonious and scalable approach to modeling high-dimensional macroeconomic systems.

</details>


### [329] [Shrinkage Bayesian Causal Forest with Instrumental Variable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18903)
*Lennard Maßmann,Jens Klenke*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 提出SBCF-IV方法，在高维稀疏场景下发现并估计异质性服从者平均因果效应(CACE)子群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工具变量分析中，发现可解释的、其服从者效应偏离平均值的子群是核心目标，但现有基于树的方法在大多数协变量与效应无关时会退化。

Method: 在估计条件意向治疗效应和服从者比例的贝叶斯加性回归树的划分概率上施加稀疏诱导的Dirichlet先验，将后验质量集中于少数调节效应的协变量；后验划分频率作为下游CART的变量级成本，引导划分朝向相关调节变量，形成可解释的协变量空间划分。

Result: 蒙特卡洛实验表明，随着无关协变量比例增加，SBCF-IV在树级和单元级比非稀疏前身BCF-IV更可靠地恢复真实划分，并在BCF-IV区间退化时保持名义覆盖率。应用于俄勒冈健康保险实验和401(k)资格数据。

Conclusion: SBCF-IV能在高维稀疏设置下有效发现和估计异质性CACE子群，提供优于BCF-IV的划分恢复与区间覆盖表现。

Abstract: Discovering interpretable subgroups whose complier effects deviate from the average is a central goal of instrumental variable analysis under imperfect compliance, yet existing tree-based methods degrade when most covariates are irrelevant to the effect. We propose Shrinkage Bayesian Causal Forest with Instrumental Variable (SBCF-IV) for discovering and estimating subgroups with heterogeneous Complier Average Causal Effects (CACE) in sparse high-dimensional settings. SBCF-IV places a sparsity-inducing Dirichlet prior on the splitting probabilities of the Bayesian Additive Regression Trees that estimate the conditional intention-to-treat and the complier share, concentrating posterior mass on the few covariates that moderate the complier effect and thereby regularizing effect estimation. The posterior split frequencies additionally enter a downstream CART as variable-level costs that steer the partition toward relevant moderators, providing an interpretable division of the covariate space. Monte Carlo experiments show that, as the share of irrelevant covariates grows, SBCF-IV recovers the true partition more reliably than its non-sparse predecessor BCF-IV at the tree and unit level, and retains nominal coverage where BCF-IV's intervals deteriorate. We apply the method to the Oregon Health Insurance Experiment and the 401(k) eligibility data.

</details>


### [330] [Identification and Estimation of Optimal Continuous Treatment Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19031)
*Fangzhou Yu*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文为"以有界结果权重为基元"的一类加权平均导数效应，建立了同方差与异方差下最优有效估计量的识别与估计理论，并给出DML估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续处理效应的平均导数估计依赖病态的条件密度得分函数，且经典理论要求严格重叠与密度平滑条件，在边界处和处理"沙漠"（interior treatment deserts）处失效。近期以有界结果权重为基元的方法避免了密度估计，但其最优有效估计量的识别与估计理论尚不完整。

Method: 为该类效应中的最优有效估计量建立识别理论；在同方差与异方差下分别推导其识别条件；给出有效影响函数（EIF），并构造去偏机器学习（DML）估计量。

Result: 证明这些估计量放松了标准识别条件：在尖锐边界处以及经典严格重叠与密度平滑条件所排除的内部处理沙漠区域仍然有效；并推导出同方差与异方差下的有效影响函数。

Conclusion: 该框架在不进行条件密度估计的前提下，实现了对连续处理效应的最优有效估计，显著扩展了识别条件的适用范围。

Abstract: Estimating continuous treatment effects is hard because average-derivative estimators rely on an ill-posed conditional-density score. Recent work makes a bounded outcome weight the primitive, characterizing a class of weighted average derivative effects without density estimation. In this paper, we develop the identification and estimation theory for the optimally efficient estimands of this class under homoskedasticity and heteroskedasticity. On identification, we show that these estimands relax standard conditions, remaining valid at sharp boundaries and at interior treatment deserts that the strict overlap and density-smoothness conditions of classical theory rule out. On estimation, we derive the efficient influence function, and develop Debiased Machine Learning estimators.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [331] [Barrier Functions Against Safety Drift in Shared Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17701)
*M. Yusuf Uzun,Yildiray Yildiz*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种将安全约束锚定于无辅助飞行员轨迹的共享控制仲裁框架，防止不安全自动化导致的功作负荷约束漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享控制仲裁机制若依赖同一自动化辅助轨迹演化的量，会结构性失效。当辅助不安全（故障或设计缺陷）时，仲裁逻辑会适应被污染的轨迹而非抵抗它，导致功作负荷约束漂移。

Method: 以无自动化辅助的基线模型生成可接受的功作负荷包络；引入辅助诱导偏差屏障与闭式失配界，关联实际与基线功作负荷演化；构建基于控制屏障函数（CBF）的二次规划，保证增广安全集的前向不变性且始终可行。

Result: 在F-16俯仰跟踪问题仿真中，所提方法防止了不安全辅助下的功作负荷约束漂移。

Conclusion: 通过将安全约束锚定于无辅助轨迹，可有效避免仲裁逻辑适应被污染轨迹的结构性失败，保证安全性。

Abstract: Shared-control arbitration mechanisms can fail structurally when they are driven by quantities that evolve on the same automation-assisted trajectory that unsafe automation can corrupt. Under unsafe assistance, arising from faults or flawed design, the arbitration logic can adapt to the corrupted trajectory instead of resisting it. Focusing on workload regulation, this paper proposes a framework that prevents this problem by anchoring the safety constraints to an unassisted pilot trajectory. A baseline model evolving without automation assistance generates the acceptable workload envelope, while an assistance-induced deviation barrier and a closed-form mismatch bound relate actual and baseline workload evolution. The resulting control barrier function based quadratic program is always feasible and guarantees forward invariance of an augmented safe set. Simulations on an F-16 pitch-tracking problem show that the proposed method prevents workload-constraint drift under unsafe assistance.

</details>


### [332] [Synthetic Electric Vehicle Charging Session Generation Using a Conditional Variational Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17808)
*Graeme Kelly,Emilio J. Palacios-Garcia,Barry P. Hayes*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出基于条件变分自编码器(CVAE)的方法，从真实交易级充电数据生成合成电动汽车充电会话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车普及给住宅配电网带来额外需求，规划与仿真研究需要真实充电数据集，但真实EV充电数据受隐私限制、记录不完整和可获取性受限等因素影响难以获得。

Method: 采用CVAE，训练于插桩时长、充电时长、传输能量、充电延迟和周期性周内时间等特征，以星期几和受控充电状态为条件；使用高斯负对数似然(NLL)重构损失建模各特征异方差不确定性，并用KL散度正则化隐空间。

Result: 通过分布度量评估统计保真度，并通过TSTR协议评估下游任务性能，结果显示生成的合成充电会话在保留原始数据集关键统计特性同时支持预测建模任务。

Conclusion: 该方法可生成高质量合成EV充电会话，缓解真实数据获取难题，适用于配电网规划与仿真研究。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of electric vehicles (EVs) is expected to place significant additional demand on residential distribution networks, creating a need for realistic charging datasets for planning and simulation studies. However, access to real-world EV charging data is often limited due to privacy constraints, incomplete records, and restricted availability. This paper proposes a conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) for the generation of synthetic EV charging sessions from real transaction-level charging data. The model is trained on engineered session features describing plug-in duration, charging duration, delivered energy, charging delay, and cyclical time-of-week, while conditioning on day of week and managed charging status. A Gaussian negative log-likelihood (NLL) reconstruction loss is employed to model feature-wise heteroscedastic uncertainty, and the latent space is regularised using a Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence term. The statistical fidelity of the generated data is evaluated using distributional metrics and downstream task performance through the Train-on-Synthetic-Test-on-Real (TSTR) protocol. Results demonstrate that the proposed approach produces synthetic EV charging sessions that preserve key statistical properties of the original dataset while supporting predictive modelling tasks.

</details>


### [333] [Multi-Scale Datacenter Power Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17809)
*Akshay Sreekumar,Nicolas Christianson,Fiodar Kazhamiaka,Ram Rajagopal*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该论文研究云数据中心的功率调控问题，将其建模为网络化混合动力系统的有限时域控制，提出分层滚动时域控制器协调快速的worker节流与慢速的服务器状态转换，在超15000台服务器的真实场景中满足功率上限且服务影响远低于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心需响应时变电网与基础设施约束来调节功耗。功率降低可通过即时的连续worker节流（但损害服务容量）和延迟生效的离散服务器转换（更深节能但演化滞后）。协调二者形成高维混合整数动态优化问题，规模上难以求解。

Method: 提出分层滚动时域控制器，将慢速服务器重配置与快速节流应对分离。固定服务器状态时，节流层化为服务级凸优化问题，用对偶分解高效求解；服务器层采用排序前缀搜索，通过规划时域内的应对值评估候选配置。

Result: 在超过15000台服务器、200000个worker、1400项服务的真实场景实验中，控制器无违反地满足时变功率上限，服务影响显著低于仅快速或仅慢速基线，且相对标准优化求解器大幅加速，在20秒实时控制间隔内算出近优方案。

Conclusion: 分层滚动时域控制可有效协调快慢两种功率调节机制，在近优解质量与实时计算速度间取得平衡，为大规模数据中心的功率调控提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cloud datacenters must increasingly modulate power in response to time-varying grid and infrastructure constraints. We study this problem as finite-horizon control of a networked hybrid dynamical system, where datacenter power and service capacity depend on interactions between servers, workers, and hosted services. Power can be reduced through fast continuous worker throttling, which acts immediately but degrades service capacity, and slow discrete server transitions, which provide deeper savings but evolve with delay. Coordinating these mechanisms yields a high-dimensional mixed-integer dynamic optimization problem which is intractable to solve at scale. We propose a hierarchical receding-horizon controller that separates slow server reconfiguration from fast throttling recourse. For fixed server states, the throttling layer reduces to a service-level convex recourse problem solved efficiently by dual decomposition. The server layer then uses a ranked-prefix search that evaluates candidate configurations through the recourse value over the planning horizon. Experiments on realistic instances with over 15,000 servers, 200,000 workers, and 1,400 services show that the controller satisfies time-varying power caps with no violations and substantially lower service impact than fast-only or slow-only baselines. Our method offers significant speedups compared to standard optimization solvers, computing near-optimal plans within a 20 second real-time control interval.

</details>


### [334] [Hybrid Sequential Feedback Optimization for Wind Farm Power Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17859)
*Shijie Huang,Sergio Grammatico*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该文提出一种混合顺序反馈优化（SFO）方法，通过带可求和递减权重的凸组合引入修正项，用于稳态输入输出映射及其灵敏度计算昂贵时的非线性离散系统最优稳态运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优稳态运行中，稳态输入输出映射及其灵敏度计算代价高昂，纯SFO依赖精确计算，导致早期瞬态性能差。

Method: 提出混合SFO扩展：用可求和递减权重的凸组合，将基于模型的SFO梯度与修正项结合；研究两种变体——基于递归最小二乘（RLS）的灵敏度估计和基于极值搜索控制（ESC）的梯度估计；在收缩性与光滑性假设下分析收敛性。

Result: 在收缩性与光滑性假设下，两种混合方案均保持SFO收敛至最优稳态邻域；通过中等保真度风电场功率最大化问题验证，早期瞬态性能优于纯SFO。

Conclusion: 混合SFO在保持收敛性的同时改善了早期瞬态性能，为计算昂贵的稳态优化提供有效方案。

Abstract: This paper considers feedback optimization for optimal steady-state operation of nonlinear discrete-time systems when the steady-state input-output map and its sensitivity are expensive to compute. We propose a hybrid extension of sequential feedback optimization (SFO) that augments the model-based SFO gradient with correction terms through a convex combination with summable diminishing weights. Two variants are studied: one based on recursive least-squares (RLS) sensitivity estimation, and another on extremum seeking control (ESC) gradient estimation. Under contractivity and smoothness assumptions, we show that both hybrid schemes preserve the convergence of SFO to a neighborhood of the optimal steady state. The proposed methods are validated through a wind farm power maximization problem using a medium-fidelity model, demonstrating improved early transient performance compared to pure SFO.

</details>


### [335] [Predicting Viral Evolution from a Single Early Measurement Using the Target Cell Limited Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17862)
*Rahal Nanayakkara,Paulo Tabuada*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文基于Target Cell Limited模型，通过坐标变换将非线性病毒动力学转化为单调系统，从单次早期病毒载量观测推导出峰值病毒载量的上界与感染时间下界的解析公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期病毒载量可被精确量化，但宿主内部生物状态不可观测，如何从单次带噪测量预测感染峰值与传染起始时间等临床指标是一个关键难题。

Method: 采用Target Cell Limited（TCL）模型，引入坐标变换将非线性病毒动力学转为单调系统，利用单调系统理论并考虑变换系统的不变子空间，推导解析表达式。

Result: 给出峰值病毒载量的严格上界以及感染起始时间（time to infectiousness）的保证下界，仅需单次早期病毒观测即可计算。

Conclusion: 该工作为从单次早期噪声测量预测个体感染轨迹提供了具有理论保证的解析工具。

Abstract: Recent advances in diagnostic techniques have enabled the accurate quantification of early-stage viral loads. A key problem of interest is translating these measurements into predictive clinical insights, such as forecasting a patient's onset of infectiousness and peak infection severity. In this work, we address this problem using the Target Cell Limited (TCL) model. Because the host's internal biological states are practically unobservable, predicting the viral trajectory from a single noisy viral load measurement is highly nontrivial. To overcome this, we introduce a novel coordinate transformation that converts the nonlinear viral dynamics into a monotone system. By leveraging monotone systems theory and taking into account invariant subspaces of the transformed system, we derive explicit analytical formulae that establish a strict upper bound on the peak viral load and a guaranteed lower bound on the time to infectiousness using a single early viral observation.

</details>


### [336] [Mixed-Integer Nonlinear Differentiable Predictive Control for Underground Pumped Hydro Energy Storage Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17964)
*Honghui Zheng,Ján Boldocký,Yury Dvorkin,Ján Drgoňa*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文将混合整数可微预测控制(MI-DPC)扩展到多模态离散决策与非凸多项式动力学,用于地下抽水蓄能(UPHES)日前调度,实现近乎最优且极大加速的在线求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UPHES调度涉及大规模混合整数最优控制问题,含非线性机组性能曲线与容积-水头耦合,传统求解在线计算代价高,需可微学习方法兼顾精度与速度。

Method: 训练神经网络策略,将问题参数映射为连续设定点与整数模式选择(Gumbel-Softmax层),通过非线性动力学模型对有限时域控制目标期望微分,自监督学习。三项贡献:保持梯度幅度的并行可微仿真器、捕捉长时依赖的Transformer编码器、正则化组合搜索的Gumbel-Softmax温度退火策略。

Result: 相对分段混合整数二次规划基线,MI-DPC次优性仅1.6%,在线调度时间加速五个数量级。

Conclusion: MI-DPC能有效处理含离散决策与非凸动力学的大规模混合整数最优控制,在UPHES日前调度中兼具近优精度与显著在线效率。

Abstract: This paper extends Mixed-Integer Differentiable Predictive Control (MI-DPC) to multi-modal discrete decisions and nonconvex polynomial dynamics arising in Underground Pumped Hydro Energy Storage Systems (UPHES). A neural policy mapping problem parameters to continuous setpoints and integer mode selections via a Gumbel-Softmax layer is trained in a self-supervised manner by differentiating the expectation of the finite horizon control objective through the nonlinear dynamics model. Three methodological contributions enable this extension: a parallel differentiable simulator that preserves gradient magnitude, a Transformer encoder that captures long-range temporal dependencies, and a Gumbel-Softmax temperature annealing schedule that regularizes the combinatorial search. We demonstrate the framework on day-ahead scheduling of a UPHES, a large-scale mixed-integer optimal control problem with nonlinear unit performance curves and volume-head coupling. MI-DPC achieves only 1.6% suboptimality relative to a piecewise mixed-integer quadratic programming baseline, while providing five orders of magnitude speedup in online scheduling time.

</details>


### [337] [On the Optimal Co-location of Data Centers with Renewable Energy Sources in SIDS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17980)
*Shankar Ramharack,Patrick Hosein,Rajiv Sahadeo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出基于PyPSA的容量扩展框架，用于加勒比小岛屿发展中国家数据中心与可再生能源的协同选址，结合表后储能、负荷灵活性（70/30）与多母线输电拓扑，并以特立尼达和多巴哥、牙买加为案例，配合飓风鲁棒性压力测试与10因素选址框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心快速扩张为加勒比SIDS带来经济机遇与系统性风险，而当地缺乏数据中心专属分区法规，且能源最优位置可能在社会指标（噪声、淡水压力、分区准备度）上表现不佳，亟需面向小岛屿的综合规划工具。

Method: 采用PyPSA构建容量扩展模型，纳入表后电池储能、70%/30%非灵活/灵活负荷划分及多母线输电拓扑；开展两个案例研究，并加入后验分布鲁棒飓风压力测试与新型10因素选址框架。

Result: 牙买加最优方案配置405 MW太阳能、278 MW风电、800 MWh电网级BESS与84 MWh表后BESS，可再生能源渗透率达42.5%；特立尼达以天然气为主的方案不新建可再生能源，敏感性分析显示太阳能需气价高于65–70美元/MWh、碳价25–50美元/tCO2或太阳能CAPEX为800美元/kW才具备经济性。

Conclusion: 能源最优选址在社会性指标上可能评分较低，说明小岛屿情境下需要整合能源与社会维度的综合规划工具。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of data centers presents both economic opportunities and systemic risks for Caribbean Small Island Developing States~(SIDS). This paper develops a PyPSA-based capacity expansion framework for optimal co-location of data centers with renewable energy infrastructure, incorporating behind-the-meter battery storage, workload flexibility (70\%/30\% inflexible/flexible split), and multi-bus transmission topology. Two case studies are examined: Trinidad~\&~Tobago (single-bus, 92\,MW Brechin Castle solar, 5\,MW data center) and Jamaica (4-bus 138\,kV JPS network, 15\,MW data center at Kingston). Jamaica's optimum deploys 405\,MW solar, 278\,MW wind, 800\,MWh grid-scale BESS, and 84\,MWh behind-the-meter BESS, achieving 42.5\% renewable penetration; Trinidad's gas-anchored optimum builds no new RE, and a sensitivity analysis shows solar entering at gas costs above \$65--70/MWh, a carbon price of \$25--50/tCO$_2$, or solar CAPEX of \$800/kW. A post-hoc distributionally robust stress test evaluates hurricane resilience, and a novel 10-factor siting framework addresses the regulatory gap in SIDS that lack data-center-specific zoning. Results show that energy-optimal locations may score poorly on societal criteria---noise, freshwater stress, zoning readiness. This demonstrates the need for integrated planning tools in small island contexts.

</details>


### [338] [Chaotically Paced Transit of an Embedded-Leader Swarm with Local Spring-Damper Formation Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18014)
*Bo Tu,Mohammad Rasouli*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 论文研究无地面站参考信号时的平面集群沿固定路线转移，用嵌入领导者结合Chua振荡器饱和坐标生成机载参考，并通过仿真验证混沌速率相对恒定速率的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集群沿固定路线转移通常依赖地面站实时提供参考，一旦飞行中缺少地面站信号流，参考生成与到达时间约束就难以保证，因此需要一种机载、可分析的参考生成机制。

Method: 一个嵌入式领导者存储部署点和目的地，机载生成参考；用Chua振荡器的饱和坐标驱动进度率，饱和使速率保持在规定正区间并给出参考到达时间显式界。该领导者将标量速率广播给其他领导者，各领导者将其作为速度前馈并基于位置误差单点牵制一致性保持站位；跟随者仅通过弹簧-阻尼网络使用相对位置和速度反馈。分析针对固定图、理想信息交换下的运动学领导者和双积分跟随者。

Result: 领导者误差指数衰减，跟随者误差有界；只要最终队形严格位于目的地内部，整个集群最终停留在目的地区域内。30个智能体的十组仿真表明：参考到达满足解析界；混沌速率在三个测试时域上均提高恒定速度预测器的误差，代价是跟随者误差更大但有界。

Conclusion: 该方案可在无地面站飞行参考下实现集群转移，并提供到达时间与误差的有界性保证；但分析未涉及内部链路故障或模型感知观测器。

Abstract: This paper treats planar swarm transit along a fixed route when no ground station streams the reference in flight. One embedded leader stores the deployment point and the destination, generates the reference onboard, and drives its progress rate with a saturated coordinate of a Chua oscillator; the saturation keeps the rate inside a prescribed positive band, which gives explicit bounds on the reference arrival time. This leader broadcasts the one scalar rate to the other leaders; every leader adds it as a velocity feedforward and holds its station by single-pinned consensus on position error, while followers use only relative position and velocity feedback through a spring-damper network. For kinematic leaders and double-integrator followers on fixed graphs with ideal information exchange, the leader errors decay exponentially, the follower errors are bounded, and the whole swarm eventually stays inside the destination area whenever the final formation fits strictly inside it. Ten simulations with 30 agents compare the chaotic rate with a constant rate. The reference arrivals satisfy the analytical bounds, and the chaotic rate raises the error of a constant-velocity predictor at all three tested horizons, at the cost of a larger but bounded follower error. The analysis does not address internal link failures or model-aware observers.

</details>


### [339] [Agents in the Scene: An Agentic Framework for Resource-Efficient Site-Specific Base Station Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18027)
*Zihao Zhou,Zhaolin Wang,Yuanwei Liu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种agentic框架，用于无线网络规划中的自主站点级基站部署，在有限RT评估预算下实现无人工干预的部署优化，性能显著优于启发式、学习型及LLM辅助方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基站部署依赖人工现场勘测或大量射线追踪仿真，人工干预多、成本高；需在有限RT预算下实现资源高效的自主网络规划。

Method: 基于三维无线数字孪生构建连续、几何落地的部署动作空间；由agent团队执行有状态的"感知—推理—反思"循环，包括双模式（经验引导+探索）的Placement Agent生成候选部署，经RT评估后由Reflection Agent结合场景几何解释结果，诊断遮挡、覆盖重叠、盲区等问题并转化为后续部署指导。

Result: 在两个真实城市场景中：1）显著优于启发式、学习型和LLM辅助方法；2）覆盖性能接近最优解，且发射机级RT评估次数大幅减少；3）站点特定反思有效将RT反馈转化为改进指导，双模式机制保持多样性并帮助跳出局部最优。

Conclusion: 该agentic框架可实现无人工干预、资源高效的站点级基站自主部署，反思与双模式机制是关键，具实际城市网络规划应用价值。

Abstract: An agentic framework is proposed for autonomous site-specific base station (BS) deployment in wireless network planning. In contrast to conventional approaches that rely on manual site surveys or extensive ray-tracing (RT) simulations with significant human intervention, the proposed framework autonomously explores and optimizes BS deployment under a limited RT evaluation budget, enabling resource-efficient network planning. To this end, a continuous, geometry-grounded deployment action space is first constructed from three-dimensional (3D) wireless digital twins. Within this action space, an agent team operates through a stateful perception--reasoning--reflection loop. Specifically, a Placement Agent first generates candidate BS deployments in two complementary modes: an experience-guided mode that refines promising solutions, and an exploration mode that avoids getting stuck in local optima. After the candidate deployments are evaluated through RT, a Reflection Agent interprets the RT results together with the scene geometry, identifies performance-limiting factors such as blockage, overlapping coverage, and uncovered areas, and converts these diagnoses into guidance for subsequent deployment. Through this iterative process, site-specific experience is accumulated and deployment plans are optimized without human intervention. Numerical results in two realistic urban scenarios show that: 1) the proposed approach substantially outperforms heuristic, learning-based, and large language model (LLM)-assisted methods; 2) it achieves highly competitive coverage against the optimal solution while requiring substantially fewer transmitter-level RT evaluations; 3) site-specific reflection effectively turns raw RT feedback into refinement guidance, whereas the dual-mode mechanism preserves diversity and facilitates escape from local optima.

</details>


### [340] [PESTO: Formally Correct Registration of LiDAR Point Clouds with Limited Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18082)
*Valen Yamamoto,Matteo Marchi,Paulo Tabuada*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出PESTO算法，利用四面体作为LiDAR点云配准的通用特征，在有遮挡环境中具竞争力，并给出误差上界证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LiDAR点云配准（对齐）问题，寻找与环境无关的通用特征以适应不同部署场景，尤其应对遮挡环境。

Method: 提出PESTO算法，将四面体作为LiDAR数据的"通用特征"，即对环境无关的特征，用于点云对齐。

Result: 实验表明PESTO在LiDAR点云对齐上具竞争力，尤其在遮挡环境中表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过证明最坏情况下的对齐误差上界，建立了PESTO的形式化正确性。

Abstract: In this paper we tackle the problem of aligning LiDAR point clouds also known as the point cloud registration problem. We propose a new algorithm, PESTO, that exploits tetrahedra as "universal features" for LiDAR data, i.e., features that are agnostic to the environment where the LiDAR sensors are deployed. We show empirically that PESTO is competitive with existing solutions for aligning LiDAR point clouds, especially in environments with occlusions. Moreover, we establish PESTO's formal correctness by proving worst-case bounds on the alignment error.

</details>


### [341] [Sliding Mode Control of Cardiac Rhythms in the Sinoatrial Node using Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18116)
*Gabriel da Silva Lima,Marcelo Amorim Savi,Wallace Moreira Bessa*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于滑模的鲁棒控制器，结合高斯过程回归补偿建模不确定性，用于窦房结心律控制，将病理心律诱导为正常心律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 窦房结异常会导致心律紊乱或心律失常。开发针对自然起搏点的心律控制策略，可为处理及避免某些心脏病理提供有效途径。

Method: 采用滑模鲁棒控制器，内嵌高斯过程回归器预测并补偿建模不确定性与扰动；使用包含窦房结、房室结和希氏-浦肯野复合体的三振荡器延迟耦合网络模型（与实验测量高度吻合），并进行类Lyapunov稳定性分析以研究有界性与收敛性。控制器设计者不掌握心脏模型，模型仅用于仿真评估。

Result: 结果表明，应用所提控制方案可避免异常心律，使ECG更接近预期正常行为，并防止危重心律反应，同时评估了控制器应对患者间差异的能力。

Conclusion: 基于滑模并嵌入高斯过程回归的鲁棒控制策略能有效稳定窦房结心律，在模型不可知条件下仍具应对个体差异的性能，可避免异常心律。

Abstract: The Sinoatrial node (SA), also called natural pacemaker, is responsible to initiate the heart electrical activity, usually represented by electrocardiograms (ECGs). Abnormalities at the SA node can produce disordered heart rhythms or, in other words, cardiac arrhythmia that are visualized in the ECGs. The development of control strategies to stabilize the cardiac rhythm at the natural pacemaker can provide efficient ways to deal with and avoid some heart pathology. This paper investigates the use of a robust controller based on sliding modes for cardiac rhythms at the SA node in order to induce normal rhythms from pathological responses. Embedded into this controller, a Gaussian process regressor is utilized to predict and compensate modeling uncertainties and disturbances. A mathematical model that presents close agreement with experimental measurements is employed to represent the heart functioning. The adopted model comprises a network of oscillators formed by sinoatrial node, atrioventricular node (AV) and His-Purkinje complex (HP). Three nonlinear oscillators are employed to represent each one of the nodes that are connected by delayed couplings. The boudedness and convergence properties are investigated with a Lyapunov-like stability analysis. In order to evaluate the ability of the control law to deal with interpatient variability, the heart model is assumed to be not available to the controller designer, being used only in the simulator to assess the control performance. The results show that, by applying the proposed control scheme, abnormal rhythms can be avoided, turning the ECG closer to the expected normal behavior and preventing critical cardiac responses.

</details>


### [342] [Indicators of resilience for autonomous control systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18264)
*Jasper van Beers,Da-Hwi Kim,Prashant Solanki,Coen de Visser*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种为非线性控制系统系统化设计的"泛型韧性指标"，基于临界慢化（critical slowing down）理论，可在自主系统真正失稳前提供早期预警，并通过典型机器人仿真与四旋翼螺旋桨逐步损坏的实飞实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统广泛应用于日常生活，其安全运行至关重要。现有故障预测与预防技术可能与真实系统状态错位——系统会因部分故障或自然磨损发生意外变化，从而微妙地削弱稳定性。这类细微变化对稳定性的影响难以被传统方法察觉，因此需要能在失稳前就探测到韧性退化的早期预警手段。

Method: 借鉴用于预测自然系统灾难性临界点的"临界慢化"泛型指标，将其系统化地设计到非线性控制系统中，用于反映闭环稳定性损失；在典型机器人系统的仿真中直接操控其接近不稳定的程度进行验证；并通过四旋翼实飞实验，逐步损坏螺旋桨叶片使其逐渐趋近不稳定，检验指标的实际预警能力。

Result: 在典型机器人系统的仿真中，所设计的泛型韧性指标能够反映稳定性损失；在四旋翼实飞实验中，随着螺旋桨叶片被逐步损坏，系统趋近不稳定，而韧性退化对闭环稳定性的影响在失稳真正出现之前就已明显显现，指标可提供早期预警。

Conclusion: 基于临界慢化的泛型韧性指标可被系统化地设计用于非线性控制系统，并能比传统方法更早地揭示韧性退化与闭环稳定性下降，为自主系统的安全预警提供了一种有效途径。

Abstract: As modern societies rely more on autonomous systems to facilitate daily life, assuring their safe operation is paramount. Naturally, there are many techniques available to predict and prevent system failures. However, the safety afforded by such schemes may become misaligned with the true system, which can change in unexpected ways - from partial faults to natural wear-and-tear - that subtly degrade its stability. The implications that such subtle changes have on autonomous system stability can be observed through generic indicators of resilience derived from critical slowing down, popular for anticipating catastrophic tipping points in natural systems. Here, we show how one can systematically design these generic indicators for nonlinear control systems and show how these can reflect loss of stability though simulations of canonical robotic systems wherein their proximity to instability is manipulated directly. These results are affirmed through real-world flight experiments of a quadrotor that is nudged towards instability by progressively damaging its propeller blades. Our results show that the implications of degraded resilience on closed-loop stability are evident well before they appear, for which the indicators of resilience derived here can provide an early warning.

</details>


### [343] [A Recursive CBF Framework for Safety under State Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18268)
*Rahal Nanayakkara,Aaron D. Ames,Paulo Tabuada*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出"递归CBF"框架，通过保证"存在鲁棒安全控制输入的safe子集"的前向不变性，解决状态不确定性下CBF的递归可行性缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒CBF方法处理状态不确定性时常常缺乏递归可行性保证，在不确定性较高时会失效，使系统进入不存在任何安全控制输入的歧义区域。

Method: 提出递归CBF（recursive CBFs）框架：不再仅仅保证原始安全集的不变性，而是强制保证安全区域中一个子集的前向不变性——在该子集内可保证存在鲁棒安全的控制输入。这是一个整体性框架。

Result: 该框架确保系统永不进入歧义区域，无论状态不确定性的水平如何，都能持续提供可行性与安全性保证。

Conclusion: 通过强制存在安全控制输入的safe子集前向不变，递归CBF为不确定性下的安全关键控制提供了持续可行且安全的新方案。

Abstract: The practical implementation of Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) for safety-critical control is often hindered by uncertainty in the knowledge of the state. While existing robust CBF methods address state uncertainty, they often lack recursive feasibility guarantees or fail when uncertainty levels are high, allowing the system to enter regions where no safe control input exists. To resolve this, we propose a novel framework of enforcing recursive CBFs. Rather than merely ensuring the invariance of the original safe set, this approach enforces the forward invariance of a subset of the safe region where a robustly safe control input is guaranteed to exist. This holistic framework ensures that the system never strays into ambiguous regions, providing continued feasibility and safety guarantees, regardless of the level of state uncertainty.

</details>


### [344] [Macroscopic Motion Patterns from Generalized Velocity Rigidity in Multi-Agent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18276)
*Ronghai He,Changhuang Wan*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出"广义速度刚性"新范式，将刚性约束从位置空间转移到速度空间，使多智能体编队能通过图的代数约束实现复杂宏观运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图刚性理论在位置空间施加结构约束，只能将多智能体编队限制为静态几何形状，缺乏运动灵活性。

Method: 将刚性约束直接施加于智能体的连续时间速度空间，提出广义速度刚性概念；把网络加速度剖面严格限制在速度刚性矩阵的零空间内，将零空间的平凡运动数学映射为物理轨迹，并分析速度空间奇异性。

Result: 证明平移、旋转、缩放平凡运动分别对应连贯曲线群集、同步螺旋与圆形SO(d)环绕、指数空间均匀伸缩；速度一致性引发有效拓扑收缩而非结构失效；数值仿真验证了理论映射。

Conclusion: 仅通过速度空间的代数约束即可编排复杂宏观机动，为多智能体集群解锁了前所未有的空间形状灵活性。

Abstract: Traditional graph rigidity theory enforces structural constraints in the position space, effectively restricting a multi-agent formation to a static geometric shape. In this paper, we introduce a fundamental paradigm shift by applying rigidity constraints directly to the agents' continuous-time velocity space. We propose the concept of generalized velocity rigidity, demonstrating that the macroscopic physical motion patterns of a multi-agent network are entirely dictated by the underlying static graph topology. By strictly confining the network's acceleration profile to the null space of the velocity rigidity matrix, we mathematically map the trivial motions of this null space into exact physical trajectories. Specifically, we prove that translational, rotational, and scaling trivial motions seamlessly integrate into cohesive curved flocking, synchronized helical and circular $\mathrm{SO}(d)$ orbiting, and exponential spatial homothety, respectively. Furthermore, we analyze velocity-space singularities, showing that velocity consensus induces a valid topological contraction rather than a structural failure. Finally, we provide comprehensive numerical simulations to validate these theoretical mappings, demonstrating that complex macroscopic maneuvers can be orchestrated purely through algebraic constraints in the velocity space, unlocking unprecedented spatial shape flexibility for multi-agent swarms.

</details>


### [345] [A Stochastic Mean-CVaR Framework for BESS Multi-Market Bidding Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18349)
*Younes Zahraoui,Jun Cao,Samir Kouro,Pedro Rodriguez Cortes*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个风险感知的两阶段随机优化框架，用于BESS在日前能量市场与mFRR市场的协同竞价，采用KDE生成联合价格场景，并引入CVaR构建Mean-CVaR目标以平衡收益与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BESS运营商参与多市场时面临价格波动与备用激活不确定性的复杂耦合；传统确定性调度忽略极端市场情形带来的"尾部风险"，可能导致技术不可行或严重经济损失。

Method: 通过非参数核密度估计（KDE）生成保留欧洲平衡市场经验特征的联合价格场景，建模多维不确定性；采用两阶段随机规划进行DA能量市场与mFRR市场的协同优化；在Mean-CVaR目标函数中集成CVaR以控制高影响价格事件的财务风险。

Result: 仿真结果表明，相比确定性基准方法，所提出的联合CVaR方法显著提升了收益稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架允许决策者调节风险厌恶程度，在盈利性与稳健性之间识别有效前沿，为BESS多市场参与提供风险感知的优化工具。

Abstract: Battery Energy Storage Systems (BESS) operators face significant challenges when participating in multiple electricity markets due to the complex coupling of price volatility and stochastic reserve activation. Traditional deterministic dispatch models neglect the "tail risks" associated with extreme market realizations, potentially leading to technical infeasibility or severe economic losses. This paper proposes a risk-aware stochastic optimization framework for the co-optimization of BESS participation in the Day-Ahead (DA) energy market and the manual Frequency Restoration Reserve (mFRR) market. The model explicitly captures multi-dimensional uncertainties by utilizing non-parametric Kernel Density Estimation (KDE) to generate joint price scenarios that preserve the empirical characteristics of European balancing markets. A two-stage stochastic programming approach is employed.To manage the financial exposure to high-impact price events, the Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) metric is integrated into a Mean-CVaR objective function. This allows decision-makers to tune their risk-aversion levels and identify an efficient frontier between profitability and robustness. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed joint CVaR approach significantly enhances revenue stability compared to deterministic benchmarks.

</details>


### [346] [Optimization Design and Simulation Validation of a Variable Stiffness Actuator Based on a Crossed Four-Bar Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18456)
*Yuanlong Ji,Ruizhe Jiang,Xiangyu Xie,Junheng Lin,Dongrun Jin,Xingbang Yang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受交叉韧带启发的拮抗式变刚度驱动器，采用两个交叉四杆柔顺传动弹性单元，通过多目标优化平衡力矩精度、等效惯量与质量，实现轻量化非线性弹性分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拮抗式变刚度驱动器难以同时实现非线性弹性整形与低结构惯量，现有机制在这两方面存在矛盾。

Method: 受前后交叉韧带在膝关节屈曲时的交叉约束行为启发，设计基于两个交叉四杆柔顺传动弹性单元（CFB-CTE）的拮抗式VSA；利用几何传动、弹性储能与双侧拮抗布局塑形输出力矩和等效刚度；建立多目标优化模型平衡力矩跟踪精度、等效惯量与质量；并构建ADAMS多体模型验证结构响应。

Result: 所选折中设计方案力矩RMSE为0.883 N·mm，总质量50.2 g；平均等效惯量较仅优化力矩的独立设计降低约78%；ADAMS仿真在单输入、反向输入和同向输入条件下验证了结构响应。

Conclusion: 结果证明所提出的交叉四杆弹性单元可作为拮抗式VSA的轻量化非线性弹性分支，方案可行。

Abstract: This paper presents a bio-inspired antagonistic variable stiffness actuator (VSA) based on two crossed four-bar compliant transmission elastic units (CFB-CTEs). The design addresses the difficulty of combining nonlinear elastic shaping with low structural inertia in antagonistic VSA mechanisms. Inspired by the crossed constraint behavior of the anterior and posterior cruciate ligaments during knee flexion, the proposed actuator uses geometric transmission, elastic energy storage, and bilateral antagonistic arrangement to shape the output torque and equivalent stiffness. A multi-objective optimization model is established to balance torque tracking accuracy, equivalent inertia, and mass. The selected compromise design achieved a torque root-mean-square error (RMSE) of $0.883~\mathrm{N\,mm}$ and a total mass of $50.2~\mathrm{g}$. Its average equivalent inertia was reduced by about 78% compared with an independent torque-only optimized design. An ADAMS multibody model was further built to verify the structural response under single-input, opposite-input, and same-input conditions. The results support the feasibility of the proposed crossed four-bar elastic unit as a lightweight nonlinear elastic branch for antagonistic VSAs.

</details>


### [347] [A Closed-Loop Model of an Anion Exchange Membrane Electrolyser Based on Operational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18638)
*Maiken Borud Omtveit,Lisa Marie Dannappel,Shi You,Kjetil Uhlen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文基于DTU PtX实验室Enapter AEM电解槽实测数据，建立了极化曲线与电流动态模型，用于电力系统研究，并验证了AEM在风电平滑和频率支撑等服务的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AEM电解槽技术成熟度（TRL）较低，公开实验数据支持的运行参数文献有限，现有模型难以准确反映其动态行为和功耗，影响绿色制氢系统拓扑与控制策略的评估。

Method: 利用丹麦技术大学Power-to-X实验室Enapter模块的实测运行数据，直接辨识AEM系统模型参数，模型由极化曲线和运行中的电流动态模型组成，并与运行数据进行了验证。

Result: 模型能够捕捉AEM电解槽的真实动态特性与功耗，验证结果表明白AEM技术适用于平滑风电场功率输出及提供频率平衡等系统服务。

Conclusion: 设计灵活负荷运行方案时需考虑过程约束，并重视功率控制与过程控制系统之间的交互作用。

Abstract: The main contribution of this work is to identify model parameters for an Anion Exchange Membrane (AEM) system directly from measured operational data, enabling their use in power system studies. Due to the relatively low technology readiness level (TRL) of AEM electrolysers, only limited literature reports operational parameters supported by openly available experimental data. By capturing the realistic dynamic behaviour and power consumption of the AEM electrolyser, this model allows for more accurate evaluation of system topologies and control strategies for green hydrogen production. This paper presents a model of an AEM electrolyser based on experimental data from Enapter modules installed in the Power-to-X (PtX) Laboratory at the Technical University of Denmark [1]. The model comprises a polarisation curve and a model of current dynamics during operation. It is validated against operational data and can be applied in grid-balancing studies as well as in assessments of how electrolyser dynamics influence the power system. The results suggest that the AEM technology is suitable for services such as smoothing wind farm power output and provide system services to support frequency balancing. The findings highlight the importance of considering the constraints of the process when designing for flexible load operation, and consideration of the interaction between the power control and process control systems.

</details>


### [348] [GNN-Accelerated Mixed-Integer Dual MPC for Interactive Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18679)
*Yidan Zhu,Shuhao Qi,Luyao Zhang,Sofie Haesaert,Jonas Mårtensson*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合整数对偶MPC，用于多车道多反应型对手的自动驾驶交互，并利用GNN预测整数机动决策以降低求解负担，实现主动探测并加速2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对偶MPC可通过信息探索动作降低对手行为不确定性，但现有自动驾驶应用仅限于单车道单一对手，无法应对多车道多对手的复杂交互场景。

Method: 构建基于场景树的混合整数对偶MPC，联合优化整数机动决策（换道与安全区选择）和连续运动；用图神经网络预测最优整数机动决策，在求解前固定高置信度预测以缩减问题规模。

Result: 仿真显示复杂交互场景中涌现主动探测行为；GNN引导固定了76.3%的整数决策，平均求解时间降低2.5倍，且最优性退化可忽略。

Conclusion: 该方法将双对偶MPC扩展到多车道多对手场景，并通过GNN加速实现了高效求解，同时保持了近优性能。

Abstract: In interactions with uncertain opponents, dual model predictive control (MPC) can improve performance through information-seeking actions that reduce uncertainty about opponents' behavior. Its recent applications to autonomous driving, however, are limited to scenarios involving a single opponent on a single lane. This paper presents a mixed-integer dual MPC for multiple reactive opponents on multi-lane roads, jointly optimizing integer-valued maneuver decisions (lane changes and safe-region selections), and continuous motion over a scenario tree that samples plausible interactions with the opponents. As interaction complexity increases, solving the resulting mixed-integer nonlinear program becomes increasingly expensive. To reduce this computational burden, a graph neural network (GNN) predicts the optimal maneuver decisions, and high-confidence predictions are fixed before the reduced problem is solved. Simulations show that active probing behavior emerges in complex interactive scenarios, and that GNN guidance fixes $76.3\%$ of the integer decisions and reduces the solve time by $2.5\times$ on average, with negligible degradation of optimality.

</details>


### [349] [Forgetting While Remembering, an Invariant Online Data-Driven Predictive Control Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18827)
*Alessandro Chiuso,Florian Dörfler,Keith Moffat*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一个贝叶斯在线DPC框架，基于ARX模型并引入外部先验，用卡尔曼滤波传播参数后验，实现低信噪比下线性时变系统的鲁棒控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线数据驱动预测控制(DPC)在线性时变系统中面临低信噪比(SNR)数据的核心挑战，此时性能易退化。需要一种机制在数据质量差时仍能保障控制性能。

Method: 采用带外生输入的ARX模型，引入外部提供的先验（编码平滑动态与稳定性等归纳偏置）。用卡尔曼滤波将ARX参数后验向前传播，状态方程含自适应速率超参数并在线调整以跟踪系统动态变化速率。后验均值和协方差用于构造DPC的最终控制误差代价函数（二次代价的后验期望）。关键设计：过程方程使先验分布随时间保持不变，从而在遗忘旧数据的同时保留先验。

Result: 在时变二阶系统上的DPC跟踪实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯在线DPC框架通过"遗忘数据但保留先验"的机制，在低信噪比下为线性时变系统提供了有效的跟踪控制性能。

Abstract: Low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) data is a core challenge of online Data-Driven Predictive Control (DPC) for linear, time-varying systems. This paper proposes a Bayesian, online DPC framework based on autoregressive models with exogenous inputs (ARX) that uses an externally-provided prior, which encodes inductive bias such as smooth system dynamics and stability, to safeguard performance when SNR is low. The posterior estimate of the ARX parameter is propagated forward in time using a Kalman filter with a state equation defined by an adaptation-rate hyperparameter, which is adjusted online to track the rate at which the underlying system dynamics evolve. The Kalman filter's posterior mean and covariance determine the DPC's Final Control Error cost function, which is the posterior expectation of the quadratic cost function. Critically, the Kalman filter's process equation is chosen so that, regardless of the hyperparameter adaptation, the prior distribution is invariant over time. Thus, while old data is forgotten, the prior is not. DPC tracking experiments on a time-varying second-order system demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed method.

</details>


### [350] [Quantum Computing in Next-Gen Smart Grid Operations: A Comprehensive Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18847)
*Md Habib Ullah*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文对量子计算在智能电网运行中的应用进行了结构化综述，涵盖监测估计、规划、运行控制、安全可靠性、稳定性、数据智能与数字孪生等方向，并梳理了量子软硬件生态、基准测试与计算优势证据及未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网边缘分布式能源的快速扩张使现代电力系统运行复杂度显著上升，传统计算方法在大规模优化控制、不确定性管理、非线性动力学及组合决策方面面临可扩展性与计算效率瓶颈，量子计算作为潜在补充范式受到关注。

Method: 采用结构化综述方法，基于关键词文献检索，对现有研究按监测与估计、系统规划、运行与控制、安全可靠性与韧性、稳定性评估、数据驱动智能及数字孪生等类别进行分类与评估，同时介绍量子计算基础概念与关键算法，并梳理量子硬件、软件框架、模拟器、云服务及硬件无关生态。

Result: 从实现环境、基准测试实践、应用规模和计算优势证据等维度对文献进行了考察，并在证据校准的基础上总结了各应用方向的研究现状。

Conclusion: 论文讨论了实际落地的主要挑战并展望未来研究方向，为量子计算支撑下一代智能电网运行提供了整合且经证据校准的视角。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of grid-edge distributed energy resources has significantly increased the operational complexity of modern power systems. Consequently, conventional computational techniques face growing scalability and computational-efficiency challenges in addressing large-scale optimization and control, uncertainty management, nonlinear dynamics, and combinatorial decision-making in smart grid operations. Quantum computing has therefore emerged as a promising computational paradigm that can complement classical methods in addressing selected computationally intensive problems. In this context, this paper presents a comprehensive structured review of quantum computing applications in smart grid operations. Following a transparent keyword-based literature search, the paper classifies and assesses existing studies across monitoring and estimation, system planning, operation and control, security, reliability and resilience, stability assessment, data-driven intelligence, and digital twin technologies. The paper also describes fundamental quantum-computing concepts and key algorithms, highlighting their relevance for power system applications. Furthermore, it reviews the current state of quantum hardware, software frameworks, simulators, cloud services, and emerging hardware-agnostic ecosystems that support cross-platform application development and deployment. The reviewed studies are examined by implementation environment, benchmarking practices, application scale, and evidence of computational advantage. Finally, the principal challenges associated with practical implementation are discussed, and future research directions are outlined. This paper provides a consolidated and evidence-calibrated perspective on the current state and future potential of quantum computing for next-generation smart grid operations.

</details>


### [351] [Towards Interaction Regulation from Human Feedback via Free Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18853)
*Maria Paula Diaz Monfort,Cinzia Tomaselli,Michael Richardson,Giovanni Russo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 受计算神经科学自由能原理启发，提出控制论框架将人类偏好在线整合到智能体策略中，并构建开放控制架构，通过VR人机回环漫游车实验验证了人机交互调节的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制与学习领域的核心挑战是设计调节人类与自主智能体交互的机制。

Method: 基于自由能原理引入控制论框架，实现在线整合人类偏好；将其实现为开放控制架构；搭建VR人机回环实验平台，操作者与漫游车共享传感信息，并通过手势提供偏好（带来合作与竞争性交互）。

Result: 实验显示人机交互得到有效调节，验证了所提方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该控制论框架与开放架构能够有效整合人类偏好并调节人机交互，为自主智能体与人类的协作提供新途径。

Abstract: A central challenge across control and learning is the design of mechanisms regulating the interactions between humans and autonomous agents. Inspired by the free energy principle from computational neuroscience, we introduce a control-theoretical framework to integrate human preferences online into an agent policy. We turn the framework into an open control architecture and validate our approach using a human-in-the-loop experimental testbed involving a rover navigating via onboard sensing. The human, remotely located and equipped with virtual reality headsets, shares the same sensory information as the rover. Human preferences are provided to the rover via gestures which introduce both cooperative and competitive interactions between the agent goal and the preferences. The experiments show that interactions are regulated, validating the proposed approach.

</details>


### [352] [Learning to Solve Two-Stage Stochastic Unit Commitment Problems with Quality Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18859)
*Andrea Fusco,Andrea Lodi,Lavanya Marla*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出用输入凸神经网络学习两阶段随机混合整数规划的凸替代值函数，配合神经Benders校正循环恢复精确最优解，在随机机组组合基准上实现零最优性间隙和最高214倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段随机混合整数线性规划是电力系统不确定性优化的标准工具，但其扩展形式规模随场景数线性增长，在日前时间约束下计算上很快变得不可行。需要一种在保持凸性的前提下加速第一阶段优化、并能保证解质量的方法。

Method: 用输入凸神经网络（ICNN）学习第二阶段值函数的凸代理，按构造保持凸性；将该代理与神经Benders校正循环耦合，对第一阶段解进行事后精化。无论网络高估还是低估追索成本，都能恢复精确最优解，且解质量认证独立于代理精度。

Result: 在IEEE随机机组组合基准（case9–case118）上评估，涵盖连续和整数追索。对所有测试算例及场景维度K∈{10,50,100}，在K场景实例上实现零最优性间隙；整数设定下相对扩展形式加速最高达214倍，求解时间在不同场景实现间稳定，符合日前时间窗口。

Conclusion: 该方法通过凸代理加速与Benders校正保证精确性，兼顾速度与解质量认证，适用于日前电力系统随机优化，且对整数追索同样有效。

Abstract: Two-stage stochastic Mixed-Integer Linear Programs are a canonical modeling tool to optimize power system operations under uncertainty, yet their extensive-form counterparts scale linearly with the number of scenarios and quickly become computationally prohibitive under day-ahead time constraints. We propose an Input Convex Neural Network architecture to learn a convex surrogate of the second-stage value function, enabling fast first-stage optimization while preserving convexity by construction. We couple the surrogate with a Neural-Benders correction loop that refines the first-stage solution a posteriori, recovering the exact optimum whether the network overestimates or underestimates the recourse cost, and certifying solution quality independently of surrogate accuracy. We evaluate the method on IEEE Stochastic Unit Commitment benchmarks (case9-case118) under both continuous and integer recourse. The proposed approach achieves solutions with zero optimality gap on the $K$-scenario instance across all test cases and scenario dimensions $K \in \{10, 50, 100\}$, with speedups up to $214\times$ over the Extensive Form in the integer setting. Solve times are stable across scenario realizations and compatible with day-ahead time windows on the tested benchmark instances.

</details>


### [353] [Time-Optimal Operation of a Load-Hoisting Gantry Crane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18872)
*Eric Mountain,Tarunraj Singh*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究二维龙门起重机点到点运动的时间最优控制，考虑吊装绳长匀速变化，得到基于贝塞尔函数的闭式解，分析切换结构并提出鲁棒控制器与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 龙门起重机点到点运动需要在吊装过程中同时改变绳长，绳长变化使系统成为线性时变模型，传统定绳长时间最优控制方法不再适用，且初始绳长存在不确定性。

Method: 假设吊装电机以恒定速率完成吊装、且与小车到达终点同时完成，建立线性时变模型；推导时间最优控制的闭式解并用贝塞尔函数参数化；通过切换函数分析bang-off-bang控制中开关的产生与消失机制；针对初始绳长不确定性，构建含状态对初始绳长灵敏度的增广模型以设计鲁棒时间最优控制器。

Result: 给出时间最优控制曲线结构及开关数目变化规律，发现一种非直观结构：小车静止时先改变绳长再启动运动；实验结果验证了时间最优与鲁棒时间最优控制曲线。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于贝塞尔函数的时间最优控制及增广灵敏度模型的鲁棒控制方案有效，可用于绳长变化的龙门起重机点到点控制，并通过实验得到验证。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of designing time-optimal control profiles for point-to-point control of a gantry crane moving in a two dimensional plane. It is assumed that the hoisting motor completes the hoisting maneuver at a constant rate and completes its transition in the same time that it takes for the cart to reach its terminal position. This results in a linear time-varying model and a closed form solution to the time-optimal control problem is shown to be parameterized with Bessel functions. A comprehensive analysis of the structure of the time-optimal control profile is studied by examining the switching function which illustrates the mechanism of introduction and decimation of switches in the bang-off-bang control profile. The variation of the number of switches in the optimal control profile is presented and a non-intuitive control profile structure is noted, one which initiates with a stationary cart, while the hoisting cable length is changed prior to the initiation of motion of the cart. To address the issue of uncertainties in the initial cable length, a model with the sensitivity of the system states with respect to the initial cable length is used to augment the system model and is used for the design of robust time-optimal controllers. Experimental results validate the time-optimal and robust time-optimal control profiles.

</details>


### [354] [Designing Grid-Aware Dynamic Specifications for Large Data Center Loads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18888)
*Ashutossh Gupta,Vassilis Kekatos*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM训练负荷对电网的动态影响，针对启动/终止的突变斜坡和训练中的周期性振荡，分别推导频率最低点/RoCoF解析表达式与频谱约束，为数据中心负荷提供可操作的电网动态规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心负荷日益渗透电网，电网运营商亟需向数据中心业主提供明确的动态规范以保证电网安全运行，而LLM训练负荷具有两类显著动态行为需加以刻画。

Method: 针对突变斜坡负荷，证明节点转子频率可由惯量中心(COI)频率精确近似，推导其最低点和RoCoF解析表达式；针对振荡负荷，推导傅里叶系数的频谱约束，将可行系数集近似为多面体，并用最大体积内接椭球（证明为轴对齐）给出紧凑表示。

Result: 斜坡表达式确定了满足频率限值的斜坡时间与稳态负荷需求的可行组合；振荡负荷的可行系数集可由多面体近似并具轴对齐椭球紧凑表示；在WECC 179节点系统上的数值测试表明规范对高阶非线性动态仍然有效。

Conclusion: 该框架为监管大型数据中心负荷的动态行为、指导数据中心生态内负荷塑形机制提供了可操作的规范。

Abstract: As data center (DC) loads increasingly penetrate the power grid, there is an urgent need for grid operators to provide clear dynamic specifications to DC owners to ensure safe grid operation. To this end, we study two salient behaviors of large language model (LLM) training loads: abrupt ramps at job initiation and termination, which induce transient frequency excursions, and sustained periodic oscillations during training, which result in oscillatory steady-state behavior. For ramping loads, we show that nodal rotor frequencies can be accurately approximated by the center-of-inertia (COI) frequency and derive analytical expressions for its nadir and rate of change of frequency (RoCoF). These expressions determine allowable combinations of ramp times and steady-state load demands satisfying prescribed frequency limits. For oscillatory loads, we derive spectral specifications on their Fourier coefficients and show that the admissible coefficient set can be approximated by a polytope. We further obtain a compact representation via its maximum-volume inscribed ellipsoid, which we show is axis-aligned. Numerical tests on the WECC 179-bus system demonstrate that the resulting specifications remain valid for higher-order nonlinear dynamics. The proposed framework provides actionable specifications for regulating the dynamic behavior of large DC loads and informing load-shaping mechanisms within the data center ecosystem.

</details>


### [355] [On asymptotic stability of the time-varying Kalman filter for unstabilizable linear systems: an optimization perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18925)
*James B. Rawlings,Titus Quah,Matthias A. Müller*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文用状态平滑优化问题替代经典黎卡提方程分析，给出半正定初始协方差、正定过程与量测噪声下时变卡尔曼滤波器渐近稳定的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典文献依赖离散黎卡提方程迭代代数，过程繁琐且难推广到非线性系统，因此需要一种更简洁、可推广的分析框架。

Method: 将卡尔曼滤波等价表述为状态平滑优化问题，并构造类 Lyapunov 函数（modified Q-function）来建立稳定性结论。

Result: 得到了时变卡尔曼滤波器渐近稳定的充分必要条件。

Conclusion: 优化视角避开了繁琐的黎卡提迭代代数，具有更好的泛化性，并可用于非线性系统。

Abstract: This paper establishes the necessary and sufficient conditions for asymptotic stability of the time-varying Kalman filter applied to a linear time invariant system with semidefinite initial state covariance and positive definite process and measurement noise. Rather than analyze the discrete Riccati equation as in the classic literature, the equivalent state smoothing optimization problem is stated and all results are established using properties of this optimization problem. A Lyapunov-like function, termed a modified $Q$-function is derived and used for this analysis. This optimization approach removes the need for the classic but cumbersome Riccati iteration algebra and provides better generalization and application for nonlinear systems.

</details>


### [356] [An Exact Componentwise Voltage-Monotonicity Threshold for Radial Distribution Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18988)
*Marian Mester*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文推导单支路电压灵敏度的精确符号，证明有功单调性反转的临界点 p* = g v，并给出配电网极值点筛查失效的闭式判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网运行工具常假设电压幅值对节点注入单调响应，从而只需检查运行包络的极值点即可验证内部安全；但已有观测表明有功输出上升时电压可能下降，其精确起始点尚无闭式表达。

Method: 推导单支路电压灵敏度精确符号；证明有功分量单调性在 p* = g v 处反转，无功阈值 q* = b_a v 处于短路尺度不可达，二者相差支路 x/r 比；设计预先固定场景网格、容差与验收标准的测试协议，在两个独立网络（1594 单元、26000 有序注入对）上验证，并在第二真实网络与电导扫描上复核。

Result: 所有观测到的违规均出现在低 R/X 变电站支路；逐单元归因区分了阈值穿越与第二种损耗介导耦合（后者在每条支路裕度仍为正时反转灵敏度）；受调压母线后，整个协议通过。

Conclusion: 该阈值可作为逐支路单调性裕度的运行标志，为配电网操作者提供极值点筛查何时失效的闭式标识；任何对注入仿射的潮流模型因构造上分量单调，无法检测此失效。网络尺度充分性条件作为有证据支持的显式假设提出。

Abstract: Distribution-network operating tools routinely assume that voltage magnitudes respond monotonically to nodal power injections, so that checking the extreme points of an operating envelope certifies its interior. That the voltage may instead fall under rising active export is documented -- measured on feeders and explained through phasor loci and P-V maxima -- but its exact onset has not been placed in closed form. We derive the exact sign of the single-branch voltage sensitivity and show that componentwise monotonicity in active power reverses precisely at p* = g v, while the reactive threshold q* = b_a v sits at short-circuit scale and is physically unreachable; the two differ by the branch x/r ratio. A test protocol whose scenario grid, tolerances and acceptance criteria were fixed before the runs, on two independent networks (1594 cells, 26000 ordered injection pairs), corroborated on a second real network and a conductance sweep, places every observed violation at the low-R/X station branch, and a per-cell attribution separates threshold crossings from a second, loss-mediated coupling that reverses sensitivities while every individual branch margin is still positive. Behind a regulated busbar the entire protocol passes, so a network-scale sufficiency condition is stated as an explicit, evidence-supported hypothesis. Because every power-flow model affine in the injections is componentwise monotone by construction, screening built on such models cannot detect this failure. The threshold reads operationally as a per-branch monotonicity margin, giving distribution operators a closed-form flag for where extreme-point screening ceases to be valid.

</details>


### [357] [Detection of Synchronized AI Data Center Load Episodes Using SCADA Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18997)
*Chandan Chaudhary,Abanish Tiwari,Yansong Pei,Mohammed Ben-Idris,Joydeep Mitra*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需新仪表、训练分类器或标注数据的方法，利用滑动样本协方差矩阵的主特征值占比构建"同步指数"，结合CUSUM检验检测AI数据中心同步负荷事件，并在IEEE 39节点RTDS模型上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI数据中心分布式训练负载会在输电网中产生阵发性、空间相关的有功功率扰动，这些同步事件提升了跨变电站负荷相关性，削弱了备用容量规划所假设的多样化收益。传统能量管理系统独立评估每个变电站，无法提取定义同步事件的跨变电站统计结构。

Method: 计算跨变电站滑动样本协方差矩阵的主特征值占比作为"同步指数"；用累积和（CUSUM）序贯检验将指数转化为有延迟上限的事件告警；同一次特征分解还免费产出主特征向量，用于将检测到的事件归因到主导变电站。

Result: 在含三个AI数据中心节点的IEEE 39节点系统RTDS实时数字仿真模型上测试，方法能以远超随机水平的明显裕度区分事件窗口与正常窗口，并实现变电站粒度的参与归因。

Conclusion: 该方法仅依赖标准有功功率遥测即可检测并定位AI数据中心同步负荷事件，无需额外仪表或标注数据，为电网备用规划提供了实用的跨变电站同步性监测手段。

Abstract: AI data centers running distributed training workloads impose episodic, spatially correlated active-power disturbances on the transmission grid. These synchronized episodes increase cross-substation load correlation and limit the diversification benefit that reserve margin planning assumes. Conventional energy management systems evaluate each substation independently and do not extract the cross-substation statistical structure that defines a synchronized episode. This paper develops a detection method that identifies synchronized AI data-center load episodes from standard active-power telemetry without new instrumentation, trained classifiers, or labeled data. The method computes a Synchronization Index, the dominant eigenvalue fraction of a sliding sample covariance matrix across substations. A cumulative-sum (CUSUM) sequential test converts the index into a delay-bounded episode alarm. The same eigen decomposition yields, at no additional cost, a dominant eigenvector that attributes a detected episode to the substations that drive it. Tests on a real-time digital simulator (RTDS) model of the IEEE 39-bus system with three AI data-center buses show that the method separates episode and normal windows with a wide margin over chance and attributes episode participation at substation granularity.

</details>


### [358] [Distributed Secondary Frequency Control and SoC Balance for Droop-Controlled BESSs with A Unified SoC Relative Variation Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19055)
*Yalin Zhang,Zhongxin Liu,Fuyong Wang,Zengqiang Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 针对电池容量退化导致容量参数与下垂系数不匹配的问题，提出基于当前SoC水平比值的功率分配方案，并设计分布式MAS控制器实现SoC均衡、功率共享与频率恢复（渐近与有限时间两种方式）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有下垂控制器基于容量的功率分配在电池容量退化时，容量参数不再等于下垂系数，导致功率共享不精确、频率恢复失效，原有基于容量的下垂控制器和二次控制器不再适用。

Method: 设计基于当前SoC水平比值的功率分配方案以导出新的下垂控制器，使各SoC同时降至0；基于多智能体系统设计分布式额定频率控制器、SoC平均值估计器与功率共享控制器，分别给出渐近与有限时间两种方案，并分析参数对收敛时间的影响。

Result: 渐近方案中SoC估计器稳态性能可调，功率共享与频率恢复误差为零；有限时间方案中分析了参数对收敛时间的影响并给出保守计算方法；在改进IEEE57节点系统上通过时域仿真验证了两种方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方案克服了容量退化带来的控制局限，在渐近与有限时间两种方式下均能有效实现SoC均衡、精确功率共享与频率恢复。

Abstract: The State of Charge (SoC) balance, power sharing, and frequency restoration are common control objectives of battery energy storage systems (BESSs). However, the SoC balance scheme induced by the power allocation through existing droop controllers can cause the capacity parameters of battery cells to be unequal to the droop coefficient, which is the result of battery capacity degradation. Under this limitation, previous capacity based droop controllers and the secondary controllers no longer suitable to address the imprecise power sharing and frequency restoration caused by this problem. Therefore, a power allocation scheme based on the current SoC level ratio is designed to induce a new droop controller and ensure that the SoC simultaneously drops to 0. In order to restore frequency in a distributed manner and obtain SoC level ratio, a distributed nominal frequency controller, SoC average estimator, and power sharing controller are designed based on multi-agent systems (MASs) in both asymptotic and finite time manners. In the asymptotic scheme, the steady-state performance of the SoC estimator is adjustable, and power sharing and frequency restoration are zero errors. In the finite time scheme, the influence of parameters on convergence time is well analyzed, and some conservative calculation methods are provided. Several time-domain simulation examples are designed on an improved IEEE57 bus system to verify the distribution of asymptotic and finite time schemes.

</details>


### [359] [A Marginal Cost Consensus Scheme with Reset Mechanism for Distributed Economic Dispatch in BESSs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19068)
*Yalin Zhang,Zhongxin Liu,Zengqiang Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该论文为并网电池储能系统(BESS)设计了一种基于比例积分(PI)控制与重置机制的分布式边际成本共识经济调度方案，以加速收敛并抑制超调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并网BESS中的内部功耗、容量退化及与电力公司的电力交易不可忽视，需在满足供需平衡的前提下进行经济调度；而现有分布式比例协议控制精度低、响应慢。

Method: 构建涵盖内部功耗、容量退化与电力交易的BESS支出函数；将KKT条件归纳为边际成本(MC)收敛至分时电价的共识问题；基于多智能体系统设计分布式MC共识方案，提出带重置机制的PI控制（比例项过零时积分项重置为0），并给出共识、正则性与无Zeno行为的参数条件。

Result: 仿真案例验证了所设计DED算法的有效性，重置机制可加速MC收敛并抑制超调。

Conclusion: 所提出的带重置PI控制的分布式经济调度方案能有效提升并网BESS经济调度的收敛速度与控制性能。

Abstract: Battery energy storage systems (BESSs) are often integrated into the smart grid as the key equipment for valley filling and peak suppression. However, the internal power consumption and capacity degradation of battery cells can not be ignored. In addition, there are certain electric trading between the owner of the grid-connected BESSs and the electric company (EC). Therefore, an expenditure function for grid-connected BESSs is constructed in this paper, in which internal power consumption, capacity degradation and power trading are covered while meeting the balance of power supply and demand. On this basis, the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) condition of the function is summarized as the consensus problem of marginal cost (MC) converging to time-phased electricity price. Thus, we plan to design a distributed MC consensus scheme for economic dispatch (ED) in BESSs based on multi-agent systems (MASs) on a small-time scale. In view of the low control accuracy and response speed of the existing distributed proportional protocol, this paper proposes a distributed ED (DED) scheme with reset mechanism based on a proportional integral (PI) control. When the proportional term encounters zero crossing, the integral term of the control scheme is reset to 0, which ensures that the signs of the two are aligned, thus accelerating MCs convergence and restraining overshoot. Parameter conditions for consensus, regularity and Zeno-free behavior are given through the relevant theoretical analysis. Several simulation cases are designed to verify the designed DED algorithm.

</details>


### [360] [Distributed Power Allocation Scheme with Prescribed Performance and Intermittent Dynamics for BESSs with Discharge Rate Constraints in Microgrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19069)
*Yalin Zhang,Zhongxin Liu,Fuyong Wang,Zengqiang Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该论文针对多BESS网络中动态负载下的功率共享与SoC平衡状态估计问题，基于多智能体系统设计了具有预设瞬态和稳态性能的分布式动态平均跟踪算法，提出事件触发和自触发两种版本以应对间歇通信，实现近乎零误差的快速时变状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SoC是多电池储能系统的重要参数，其平衡问题在多BESS网络中值得研究。已有功率分配方法依赖实时获取功率共享状态和SoC平衡状态，因此需要一种分布式方式对动态负载下的BESS网络中的这些状态进行实时估计。

Method: 基于多智能体系统(MASs)设计分布式动态平均跟踪算法，融合预设性能控制(PPC)方法，将期望的稳态和动态性能封装进性能函数；设计事件触发与自触发两种方案以应对间歇通信；并将一致性与平均跟踪性能解耦，便于参数调节。

Result: 两种估计方案在不同程度上保证了间歇通信下估计器的性能；实现了对快速时变功率共享状态和SoC平衡状态的近乎零误差估计；在4节点系统上进行了案例研究与仿真验证。

Conclusion: 所提分布式动态平均跟踪算法结合PPC方法，能够有效估计BESS网络中的功率共享与SoC平衡状态，事件触发与自触发方案适应间歇通信，且一致性与平均跟踪解耦便于参数调节，仿真验证了理论分析的正确性。

Abstract: The State-of-Charge (SoC) is an important parameter of a battery energy storage system (BESS), and its balance problem is also an issue worth studying in a multi-BESS network. Recently, some researchers have proposed a power allocation method, claiming that as long as the power sharing state and SoC balance state can be obtained in real-time, it can not only maintain supply and demand balance, but also ensure that any BESS will not exit early due to insufficient energy storage. Considering this, we are attempting to design {a distributed dynamic average tracking algorithm} based on multi-agent systems (MASs) with prescribed transient and steady state performance to estimate the power sharing and SoC balance states \textcolor{blue}{of} a BESSs network with dynamic load in a distributed manner. Two versions of the solution are designed here, where one is event triggered and the other is self triggered. These two schemes ensure the performance of the estimators under intermittent communication to varying degrees, \textcolor{blue}{respectively}. In each constructed estimation scheme, prescribed performance control (PPC) method is implemented to ensure the expected steady-state and dynamic performance, which is encapsulated in a performance function. Thus, it achieves almost zero error estimation of the fast time-varying {power} sharing and the SoC balance states. In addition, consensus and average tracking performance are decoupled, which provides convenience for \textcolor{blue}{parameters} tuning. Finally, to verify the results of \textcolor{blue}{the} theoretical analysis, some cases are studied and relevant simulations are performed on a 4 bus system.

</details>


### [361] [Leader-Follower Formation Control with Prescribed Convergence Rates under Bearing Persistence of Excitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19106)
*Tarek Bouazza,Zhiqi Tang,Soulaimane Berkane,Tarek Hamel*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时变矩阵增益的领航者-跟随者编队控制方法，仅利用相对方位和速度测量，使收敛速率与持续激励（PE）界解耦，并可通过设计参数调节指数收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方位编队控制多用固定增益，其收敛速率依赖期望编队的持续激励（PE）性质，难以独立调节。

Method: 设计随各跟随者可用方位信息演化的时变矩阵增益；证明该增益在方位持续激励编队下的适定性与一致有界性；用可调设计参数刻画指数收敛速率；并推广至双积分器动力学编队。

Result: 在方位持续激励条件下增益一致有界且系统指数收敛，收敛速率与PE界解耦、可由设计参数调节；仿真对比验证了相对固定增益设计的收敛优势。

Conclusion: 时变增益策略实现了收敛速率与PE解耦，提升了方位领航-跟随编队控制的灵活性与可控性。

Abstract: This paper addresses leader-follower formation control using only relative bearing and velocity measurements. Bearing-based leader-follower control strategies commonly use fixed control gains, for which the guaranteed convergence rates explicitly depend on the persistence of excitation (PE) properties of the desired formations. We propose a time-varying matrix gain that evolves according to the bearing information available to each follower and decouples the convergence rate from the PE bound. We establish the well-posedness and uniform boundedness of the proposed gain for bearing-persistently exciting formations, and characterize the exponential convergence rate through a tunable design parameter. The proposed control design is further extended to leader-follower formations with double-integrator dynamics. Simulations illustrate the resulting convergence properties compared with fixed gain designs.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.MF'></div>

# q-fin.MF [[Back]](#toc)

### [362] [Demystifying the Bergomi-Guyon expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17869)
*Florian Bourgey,Jim Gatheral*

Main category: q-fin.MF

TL;DR: 本文重新表述Bergomi-Guyon隐含方差微笑展开，证明匹配条件可化为非线性热方程，给出避免高阶项的递归算法，得到各阶前置因子均为k的ℓ次多项式，且具有普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alòs、Gatheral与Radoičić从累积量生成函数的森林展开推导Bergomi-Guyon隐含方差微笑展开，其系数为钻石树乘积之和，前置因子是log-strike k的多项式。逐阶匹配矩时会产生高于ℓ次的项，这些项会神秘抵消，因此需要一个更清晰的框架来避免这些多余项。

Method: 作者引入合适的变量，将逐阶矩匹配条件表述为非线性热方程；由此得到递归关系，用较少树乘积的系数计算每个树乘积的前置因子，而不会生成高次项。唯一与模型无关的输入是累积量级数，并给出其闭式表达。

Result: 在阶数ε^ℓ，每个前置因子在k中恰好为ℓ次多项式；前置因子是普适的，只需计算一次。论文还提供了代码和系数。

Conclusion: 该工作为Bergomi-Guyon隐含波动率展开提供了新的递归计算框架，消除了神秘的抵消现象，并表明前置因子具有普适性，可通过非线性热方程递归有效计算。

Abstract: Alòs, Gatheral and Radoičić derived the Bergomi-Guyon expansion of the implied variance smile from the forest expansion of the cumulant generating function. Its coefficients are sums of products of diamond trees, with prefactors that are polynomials in the log-strike $k$. Matching moments order by order produces, at order $ε^\ell$, terms of degree greater than $\ell$ in $k$ that mysteriously cancel.
  We show that, in suitable variables, the matching condition can be formulated as a nonlinear heat equation. The resulting recursion computes the prefactor of each product of trees from those of products of fewer trees, without generating the higher-degree terms; its only model-independent input is a cumulant series, which we compute in closed form. Consequently, at order $ε^\ell$ every prefactor has degree exactly $\ell$ in $k$. The prefactors are universal and need only be computed once. Code and coefficients are provided.

</details>


### [363] [Quadratic G-BSDEs for bond pricing with endogenous short-rate feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19094)
*Jaehyun Kim,Hyungbin Park*

Main category: q-fin.MF

TL;DR: 在G-期望框架下研究带内生短期利率反馈的稳健债券定价，将问题化为对数价格的二次G-BSDE，建立有限/无限期解的存在唯一性、比较与稳定性，并应用于逆短期利率设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在波动率不确定性下，短期利率依赖债券价格会产生非线性固定点问题，传统线性定价框架难以刻画这种内生反馈，因此需要稳健的债券估值理论。

Method: 采用G-期望框架，将债券价格与短期利率的相互依赖表示为对数价格的二次G-BSDE；在适当假设下用BSDE方法分析有限期解的适定性，并引入严格单调性条件处理无限期解与有限期逼近的收敛。

Result: 证明了有界有限期解的存在性、唯一性、比较定理与稳定性；在严格单调条件下得到唯一有界无限期解及有限期逼近在紧时间区间上的指数收敛；进一步用于逆短期利率设计，构造贴现率系数以实现给定到期日的平滑债券价格目标，并在长期限下使补偿对数价格指数收敛到指定有界状态依赖曲线，渐近收益率等于目标值。

Conclusion: 该工作为波动率不确定性下的内生反馈债券定价提供了系统的G-BSDE理论工具，并展示了在逆短期利率与长期反馈规则设计中的应用价值。

Abstract: We study robust bond valuation with endogenous short-rate feedback under volatility uncertainty. Within the $G$-expectation framework, the dependence of the short rate on the bond price yields a nonlinear fixed-point problem, represented by a quadratic $G$-BSDE for the logarithmic price. Under suitable assumptions, we establish existence, uniqueness, comparison, and stability for bounded finite-horizon solutions. An additional strict monotonicity condition yields a unique bounded infinite-horizon solution and exponential convergence of finite-horizon approximations on compact time intervals. We apply these results to inverse short-rate design, constructing discount-rate coefficients that reproduce admissible smooth bond-price targets at a fixed maturity. For long maturities, we construct feedback rules under which the compensated logarithmic price converges exponentially to a prescribed bounded state-dependent profile, while the asymptotic yield equals a specified target.

</details>


### [364] [Optimal entry and exit for variance swaps: closed-form rules for the perpetual contract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19102)
*Jun Maeda*

Main category: q-fin.MF

TL;DR: 本文为永续、连续结算的方差互换建立最优进入与退出的闭式阈值规则：在含方差风险溢价的实概率测度下，问题简化为对CIR过程的仿射报酬最优停止，阈值由合流超几何函数的光滑粘贴方程唯一根给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方差互换是交易vega与凸性的便利工具，Cboe已有上市合约，但交易者何时建仓、何时平仓缺乏理论指导；在定价测度下盯市是鞅、问题无意义，需在含风险溢价的实测度下讨论才有内容。

Method: 在永续连续结算合约框架下，将累积方差精确分离，把到期、行权价与成本吸收为单一强迫项（其符号决定exercise区域几何），剩下的是CIR过程上的仿射报酬；用最优停止文献求解，阈值满足合流超几何函数的光滑粘贴方程并取唯一根。

Result: 退出规则可由溢价与买卖价差（均可观测）推出；建仓与平仓并不对称。在风险溢价的经验符号下，只有空头一方值得开仓，且其进入规则仅在carrying charge的某区间内存在，即便存在也只在实概率分布上尾深处触发。

Conclusion: 空头方值得开仓但入场门槛极高：场外交易者不持仓不付成本，故可操作的进入规则需引入退出规则所不需要的闲置资本成本，这刻画了进入与退出的本质不对称。

Abstract: Variance swaps are a convenient instrument for trading vega and convexity, and a listed contract now trades on Cboe. We ask when a trader should put such a position on and when she should take it off, and for a perpetual, continuously settled contract we answer both in closed form: each threshold is the unique root of a smooth-pasting equation in confluent hypergeometric functions.
  Under the pricing measure the question has no content, the mark-to-market being a martingale. Under the physical measure with a variance risk premium it becomes meaningful, and then reduces: the accrued variance separates exactly, the maturity, strike and costs are absorbed into a single forcing term whose sign fixes the geometry of the exercise region, and what is left on the perpetual is an affine reward on a CIR process, which the optimal-stopping literature already solves.
  Entering the position and exiting it are not mirror images. An exit rule follows from the premium and the trading spread, both observable. For the short --- the only side worth opening under the empirical sign of the premium --- an entry rule exists only for an interval of carrying charges, and even there triggers only deep in the upper tail of the physical law: a trader who is out of the market pays nothing to stay out, so an operational entry rule needs a cost of idle capital that the exit rule does not.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [365] [Rényi Tracking Bounds for Langevin Dynamics with Moving Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17577)
*Yuchen Xin,Jingxin Zhan,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究时变目标分布下的Langevin扩散与LMC，在LSI条件下给出Rényi散度非渐近跟踪保证，并应用于基于逐次Moreau包络的非光滑采样，给出平滑参数与步长的显式选择及复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Langevin动力学分析多针对静态目标分布，而实际问题中目标分布常随时间变化，缺少非渐近的Rényi散度跟踪保证。

Method: 在log-Sobolev不等式假设下，分析连续时间Langevin扩散及其离散化（LMC）对当前目标的跟踪；并将其结果应用于逐次Moreau包络的非光滑采样方案。

Result: 导出连续与离散Langevin动力学的非渐近Rényi散度跟踪界；对该非光滑采样方案给出平滑参数和步长的显式选择及相应复杂度界。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对离散目标更新的Langevin动力学非渐近Rényi散度跟踪界，为时变分布采样提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We study Langevin diffusion and Langevin Monte Carlo (LMC) when the target distribution changes over time. Under a log-Sobolev inequality (LSI), we derive non-asymptotic Rényi-divergence guarantees for tracking the current target. The framework covers continuous-time Langevin diffusion and its discretizations. We then apply the results to nonsmooth sampling based on successive Moreau envelopes. For this scheme, we give explicit choices of the smoothing parameters and step sizes, together with corresponding complexity bounds. To our knowledge, these are the first non-asymptotic Rényi-divergence tracking bounds for Langevin dynamics with discrete target updates.

</details>


### [366] [Stability-Constrained Approximation in Spline KANs: Exact Layer Balancing and Budget-Compatible Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17619)
*Aleksander Tankman*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在逐层Lipschitz预算约束下研究深度样条叠加网络的逼近问题，围绕"因子分解稳定性复杂度"与"预算相容逼近复杂度"两个量展开。作者精确求解了有限深度对角平衡问题，给出了保预算的样条离散化定理及极小极大下界，并证明层误差在复合时不会抵消，深度线性累积并非证明假象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度样条叠加网络存在逼近阶与跨深度稳定性之间的张力：加深网络可提升逼近能力，但层误差随深度累积可能破坏数值稳定性。因此需要在严格的逐层Lipschitz预算下，厘清逼近能力与稳定性的内在权衡，并判断误差在复合时是否必然累积。

Method: 1) 对固定非负包络矩阵链，精确求解有限深度对角平衡问题，给出最优均匀层预算的显式公式与一阶最小化构造，并完整处理矩形层、退化与不可达情形；2) 证明保预算的构造性样条离散化定理，给出显式网格阈值，允许受控松弛；3) 对精确保预算的线性样条值算子，在一阶与三阶导数范数同时约束的类上证明预算相容的极小极大下界；4) 构造稳定的深度L塔，使累积误差达到常数比例。

Result: 最优均匀层预算等于 ‖M_{L-1}···M_0‖_{∞→∞}^{1/L}，且可由显式单次最小化达到；该最优值可能远大于网络自身的Lipschitz常数，因为取包络会破坏符号抵消。给出保预算的样条离散化定理及显式网格阈值；证明了一阶/三阶导数双约束下的极小极大下界，排除常见的尺度逃逸；并证明对类中每个算子都存在稳定深度L塔实现累积误差的常数比例，表明上界的深度线性累积并非证明技术造成的假象。

Conclusion: 深度样条叠加网络中逼近阶与稳定性存在本质张力：取包络导致的预算放大、保预算离散化的可控松弛、以及层误差复合时的不可抵消性共同表明，深度线性误差累积是结构性的，而非分析中的保守估计。

Abstract: Deep spline superposition networks face a tension between approximation order and stability across depth. We study approximation under a hard layerwise Lipschitz budget, and organise it around two quantities: the factorisation stability complexity of a given deep factorisation, and the budget-compatible approximation complexity of a discretisation operator.
  First, we solve exactly the finite-depth diagonal balancing problem for a fixed chain of nonnegative envelope matrices: the optimal uniform layer budget equals $\|M_{L-1}\cdots M_0\|_{\infty\to\infty}^{1/L}$, attained by an explicit one-pass minimiser, for rectangular layers, with a complete treatment of degeneracies and non-attainment. The optimum can be arbitrarily larger than the Lipschitz constant of the network itself, because passing to envelopes destroys sign cancellation.
  Second, we give a constructive spline discretisation theorem preserving the budget up to a controlled slack, with an explicit grid threshold. Conversely, for linear spline-valued operators that preserve the budget exactly, we prove budget-compatible minimax lower bounds on classes constrained simultaneously in the first and third derivative norms -- a constraint pair that is forced by the problem and that rules out the usual scaling escapes.
  Finally, we show that the corresponding layer errors need not cancel under composition: for every operator of the class there is a stable depth-$L$ tower realising a constant fraction of the accumulated error, so the linear-in-depth accumulation of the upper bound is not a proof artefact.

</details>


### [367] [METALICA: METAdynamics and repLICA exchange for enhanced diffusion sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17823)
*Alireza Omidi,Jiajun He,Jörg Gsponer,Saifuddin Syed*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: METALICA将元动力学(metadynamics)与副本交换(Replica Exchange)结合到预训练扩散模型上，用于采样蛋白质构象中的稀有状态。它按扩散时间层级维护副本，通过沿集体变量积累偏置势排斥已采样点，并对样本重加权回无偏分布，实现长链采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多蛋白质通过构象状态间的转换发挥功能，但在平衡系综上训练的扩散模型很少采样到稀有状态，因此需要更好的采样方法。

Method: METALICA在预训练扩散模型上实现基于副本交换的元动力学：沿集体变量积累偏置势，通过有偏采样使新样本远离已采样样本，并将样本重加权到无偏分布。每个扩散层级持有一个副本，形成通过跨副本通信演化的马尔可夫链，随着偏置增长原位精修。它是序列控制的"对偶"——序列蒙特卡洛在粒子批次上并行，而METALICA在扩散时间表层级上并行，从而生成来自长链的样本。

Result: 在具有已知自由能的双峰目标上验证成功；应用于蛋白质去折叠时，在序列控制无法得到去折叠结构的预算下，METALICA成功填充该盆地并分辨出第二个自由能极小值。

Conclusion: METALICA能够高效采样稀有事件，其精度由运行长度而非可用内存决定，为蛋白质构象稀有态采样提供了有效手段。

Abstract: Many proteins function through transitions between conformational states, yet rare states are rarely sampled by diffusion models trained on an equilibrium ensemble, demanding better sampling methods. We introduce METALICA, which implements Metadynamics on a pretrained diffusion model via Replica Exchange. It accumulates a bias potential along a Collective Variable, repels new samples from previous ones through biased sampling, and reweights samples onto the unbiased distribution. METALICA holds one replica per diffusion level, forming a Markov Chain that evolves through inter-replica communication and is refined in place as the bias grows. METALICA is the dual of sequential control, in which Sequential Monte Carlo parallelizes the sampler over a batch of particles. Parallelism over the levels of the diffusion-time schedule instead allows METALICA to generate samples from long chains, essential for the discovery of rare events, with accuracy set by run length rather than by the memory available. We validate on a bimodal target with known free energies, then apply METALICA to the unfolding of a protein.
